### [1] [Dynamics of planar integrable Kepler billiards with a focused hyperbolic branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18705)
*Daniel Jaud,Lei Zhao*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究以双曲线（hyperbola）一支为边界、且该支以开普勒中心（Kepler center）为焦点的平面开普勒台球（Kepler billiard）的可积动力学；利用焦点焦散圆（foci-caustic circle）的存在性构造动力学线性化的椭圆曲线（elliptic curve），并分析 n-周期轨道的凯莱条件（Cayley's condition），并通过取极限讨论抛物弧与直线边界情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为作者前期工作的续篇（sequel），本文旨在将焦点焦散圆方法推广至以双曲线一支为边界的平面开普勒台球，并统一处理相关极限情形下的可积动力学。

Method: 借助焦点焦散圆（foci-caustic circle）的存在性，确定一条使动力学线性化（linearized）的椭圆曲线（elliptic curve），并在该框架下分析 n-周期轨道的凯莱条件（Cayley's condition）；同时通过取各种极限（limits）处理边界为聚焦抛物弧及直线的情形。

Result: 在双曲线边界情形下，动力学可在所构造的椭圆曲线上线性化，且 n-周期轨道满足相应的凯莱条件；通过取极限，该方法同样适用于聚焦抛物弧（focused parabolic arc）边界及直线边界的平面开普勒台球。

Conclusion: 平面开普勒台球在双曲线、抛物弧及直线等边界下具有统一的可积描述；对于直线边界情形，该方法提供了与 Felder 工作中不同的椭圆曲线推导途径。

Abstract: We consider the integrable dynamics of a planar Kepler billiard in the plane bounded by a branch of a hyperbola focused at the Kepler center. As a sequel to our previous work \cite{JZ2}, we use the existence of the foci-caustic circle to identify an elliptic curve on which the dynamics is linearized and we analyze Cayley's condition on $n$-periodic orbits in this setting. By taking various limits also we discuss the dynamics of planar Kepler billiards with boundary being a focused parabolic arc, as well as a straight line. In this last case, our approach offers a different way to deduce the elliptic curve as in \cite{Felder}.

</details>

### [2] [Infrared Universality of Scalar Induced Gravitational Waves Beyond Second Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17928)
*Chen Yuan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文为标量诱导引力波（scalar induced gravitational waves）红外标度（infrared scaling）提供物理解释：近乎同频声波的拍频效应持续源出长波张量扰动，使其红外标度仅由背景状态方程（equation of state）$w$ 决定，并证明该标度对高阶修正与非高斯性（non-Gaussianities）稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞暗物质（primordial black hole dark matter）由增强的原初密度涨落坍缩形成，并伴随标量诱导引力波；其红外标度在领头阶仅依赖背景状态方程 $w$。但该特征标度在任意微扰阶修正及非高斯性下是否稳健，尚缺乏物理层面的理解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用物理图像分析：考察近乎等频声波（sound waves）的叠加，指出其应力产生缓慢拍频（slow beat），在长波张量模进入视界（horizon entry）前持续充当源项；并进一步分析高阶相互作用所生成的自由传播波（freely propagating waves）在视界深处的行为。

Result: 高阶相互作用虽产生自由传播波，但其最终在视界深处导致相同的缓慢拍频；由于非高斯性不改变自由传播波的速度，故不修正红外标度。缓慢拍频源项在红外张量模进入视界前有足够时间累积，从而记录背景膨胀信息。

Conclusion: 红外标度仅依赖膨胀背景，因而对微扰阶数与非高斯性均稳健，可作为宇宙学状态方程（cosmological equation of state）的可靠探针。

Abstract: Primordial black hole dark matter is formed from the collapse of enhanced primordial density fluctuations. The formation of primordial black holes is accompanied by scalar induced gravitational waves whose infrared scaling only depends on the equation of state, $w$, of the background at leading order. Establishing whether this characteristic scaling remains robust under corrections of arbitrary perturbative order, including in the presence of non-Gaussianities, requires a physical understanding of the infrared scaling. In this work, we provide such a physical interpretation. We find that the stress of sound waves with nearly equal frequencies produces a slow beat. Although the incoming waves oscillate rapidly, this slow beat continues to source the long wavelength tensor perturbation before its horizon entry, thus generating an infrared scaling that only depends on the expanding background. We show that higher order interactions generate freely propagating waves that finally lead to the same slow beat deep inside the horizon. Since non-Gaussianities would not change the speed of the freely propagating waves, they do not modify the infrared scaling. As the source with a slow beat has enough time to accumulate before the horizon entry of the infrared tensor mode, it records the background expansion and thus the infrared scaling could provide a robust probe of the cosmological equation of state.

</details>


### [3] [Cosmological anatomy of interacting dark energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18288)
*Miguel A. Sabogal,Sunny Vagnozzi,Eleonora Di Valentino*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文提出对相互作用暗能量（interacting dark energy, IDE）模型进行三级递进式比较，以区分其真实宇宙学特征与参数固定化带来的假象。结果表明，在固定角尺度和辐射-物质相等红移后，IDE 的复合前演化与 ΛCDM 几乎一致，CMB 温度功率谱难以区分；剩余特征主要体现在 CMB 透镜效应与物质功率谱上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IDE 模型近年重获关注，但其真实宇宙学特征研究不足，已有比较多在固定宇宙学参数下与 ΛCDM 进行，易混淆相互作用效应与参数偏移效应。论文旨在建立受控比较策略以剥离这些混杂因素。

Method: 针对能量交换率正比于暗能量密度的模型，设计三级比较序列：第一级为朴素的固定参数比较；第二级补偿角尺度 θ_s 与辐射-物质相等红移 z_eq 的偏移；第三级进一步扣除修改背景演化带来的影响。并与具有相同背景的非相互作用 wCDM 模型对比。

Result: 固定 θ_s 与 z_eq 后，复合前的 Weyl 势（Weyl potential）、光子单极（photon monopole）与重子速度演化与 ΛCDM 几乎相同，CMB 温度功率谱基本不可区分。相比同背景 wCDM，IDE 在 CMB 透镜（CMB lensing）中呈现小尺度依赖特征；在物质功率谱（matter power spectrum）中差异更大，源于暗区划分不同导致的 Ω_m 差异，但比较 Ω_m^2 P_m(k) 后仅剩百分级差异。

Conclusion: 控制变量式比较策略能有效揭示 IDE 模型的真实可观测特征，并强调高精度测定 Ω_m 的重要性；该策略可推广至 ΛCDM 之外的广泛模型。

Abstract: Interacting dark energy (IDE) models, featuring interactions between dark matter (DM) and dark energy (DE), have received significant renewed interest. However, little attention has been devoted to understanding their genuine cosmological signatures, with comparisons against $Λ$CDM often carried out at fixed cosmological parameters. Considering the widely studied model with energy exchange rate proportional to the DE density, we carry out a three-level sequence of comparisons, starting from the naïve fixed-parameter comparison, then compensating for shifts in $θ_s$ and $z_{\text{eq}}$, and then removing effects due to the modified background. We show that fixing $θ_s$ and $z_{\text{eq}}$ leads to pre-recombination evolutions of the Weyl potential, photon monopole, and baryon velocity which are nearly identical to their $Λ$CDM counterparts, and hence virtually indistinguishable Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) temperature power spectra. Comparing IDE to a non-interacting $w$CDM model with the same background, we find small scale-dependent signatures in CMB lensing. We find larger differences in the matter power spectrum, reflecting the different values of $Ω_m$ resulting from the different partition of the dark sector into DM and DE, with only percent-level differences remaining once we compare $Ω_m^2P_m(k)$, indicating the importance of high-fidelity determinations of $Ω_m$. Our controlled comparison strategy can be a powerful tool for a wide range of models beyond $Λ$CDM.

</details>

### [4] [Adaptive hybrid coupling with operator inference, the overlapping Schwarz alternating method and reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17837)
*Trishit Mondal,Irina Tezaur,Anthony Gruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于强化学习（reinforcement learning, RL）的方法，用于在重叠 Schwarz 交替法（overlapping Schwarz alternating method, O-SAM）耦合的混合模型中在线自适应地选择各子域使用全阶模型（full order model, FOM）还是降阶模型（reduced order model, ROM）。深度 Q 网络（deep Q-network, DQN）离线训练后可预测性部署，在移动前沿与弹性波传播两个算例上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有混合域分解（domain decomposition）框架通常假定仿真过程中各子域的模型分配固定不变，这限制了局部特征随时间迁移、需高保真分辨率区域不断变化的瞬态问题。因此需要一个能在仿真中在线调整模型保真度分配的机制。

Method: 将 FOM 与预训练算子推断（Operator Inference, OpInf）ROM 通过 O-SAM 耦合：子域局部求解并通过重叠界面上的传输边界条件交换解信息。使用深度 Q 网络离线训练，依据兼顾精度、成本与模型切换频率的奖励函数，在子域局部 FOM 与 ROM 之间进行选择；训练完成的策略无需参考 FOM 解即可在未见问题上预测性部署。在 1D 对流-扩散（advection-diffusion）移动前沿问题与 Norma.jl 固体力学代码实现的 3D 线弹性波（linear elastic wave）传播问题上进行验证。

Result: 1D 对流-扩散算例中，学习策略随前沿传播动态分配高保真分辨率，优于静态 FOM/ROM 分配；进一步允许智能体自适应域分解并未带来额外收益。3D 弹性波算例中，两子域与三子域分解的学习策略均按预期将 FOM 分配给含波子域、其余使用 ROM，从而跟踪传播中的波。

Conclusion: 结果表明强化学习（RL）有潜力在基于 Schwarz 的混合仿真中实现模型保真度的预测性在线自适应。

Abstract: Hybrid domain decomposition methods provide a flexible framework for coupling full order models (FOMs) and reduced order models (ROMs), but typically assume the model assigned to each subdomain is fixed throughout a simulation. This is limiting for transient problems in which localized features propagate through the domain and the regions requiring high-fidelity resolution change over time. We introduce a reinforcement learning (RL)-based approach for online adaptation of FOM-ROM models coupled via the overlapping Schwarz alternating method (O-SAM), an iterative domain decomposition method that solves subdomain-local problems while exchanging solution information through transmission boundary conditions on overlapping interfaces. Deep Q-networks (DQNs) are trained offline to select among subdomain-local FOMs and pre-trained Operator Inference (OpInf) ROMs using a reward balancing accuracy, cost, and model-switching frequency. Once trained, the policies are deployed predictively on problem instances not seen during training, without requiring a reference FOM solution. We demonstrate the approach on two examples: a 1D advection-diffusion problem with a moving front, and a 3D linear elastic wave propagation problem implemented in the Norma.jl solid mechanics code. For the advection-diffusion benchmark, the learned policy dynamically allocates high-fidelity resolution as the front propagates and outperforms static FOM/ROM assignments; letting the agent also adapt the domain decomposition provides no further benefit. For the elastic wave benchmark, learned policies for two and three subdomain decompositions track the propagating wave by assigning FOMs to subdomains containing the wave and ROMs elsewhere, as expected. Our results demonstrate the potential of RL to enable predictive online adaptation of model fidelity within Schwarz-based hybrid simulations.

</details>


### [5] [Hybrid coupling with numerics-informed neural networks and the overlapping Schwarz alternating method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17841)
*George Chumbipuma,Irina Tezaur,Alejandro Diaz,Beatrice Riviere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合建模框架，利用重叠 Schwarz 交替法（overlapping Schwarz alternating method）将预训练的数值信息神经网络（numerics-informed neural networks, NINNs）与经典全阶模型（full order models, FOMs）耦合，用于求解对流占优的二维对流-扩散方程（Peclet 数 10^6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对对流占优（advection-dominated）高 Peclet 数问题中单一神经网络训练困难、且需与已有高保真模型协同使用的需求，探索将预训练 NINN 作为子域求解器嵌入传统 FOM 的耦合方案。

Method: 采用重叠乘性 Schwarz（overlapping multiplicative Schwarz）作为部署机制，在 Schwarz 迭代中将子域局部预训练 NINN 的权重固定，与相邻 FOM 耦合。子域 NINN 的训练包含两种途径：自顶向下（top-down，边界数据来自全域 FOM-FOM Schwarz 解）与自底向上（bottom-up，边界迹在 NINN 子域上合成生成，无需全域求解）。

Result: 首先表明与对应的物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural network, PINN）不同，单体（monolithic）NINN 可在无域分解情况下于该模型问题上精确训练。混合 NINN-FOM 解与相应的 FOM-FOM Schwarz 解高度吻合，且自顶向下与自底向上两种训练方式精度相当。

Conclusion: 重叠 Schwarz 交替法可有效将预训练 NINN 与经典 FOM 耦合，且边界数据可由合成方式生成而不依赖全域求解，为混合神经-数值求解提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We develop a hybrid modeling framework for coupling pre-trained numerics-informed neural networks (NINNs) with classical full order models (FOMs) using the overlapping Schwarz alternating method. We consider the two-dimensional advection-diffusion equation in the advection-dominated, Peclet-number 10^6 regime. We first demonstrate that, unlike the corresponding physics-informed neural network (PINN), a monolithic NINN can be accurately trained on our model problem without domain decomposition. We then employ overlapping multiplicative Schwarz as a deployment mechanism for coupling a pre-trained, subdomain-local NINN with a neighboring FOM, with the NINN weights held fixed throughout the Schwarz iteration. We consider two training approaches for the subdomain-local NINNs: a top-down approach, in which boundary data are obtained from a coupled Schwarz solve on the full domain with a FOM on each subdomain (FOM-FOM Schwarz), and a bottom-up approach, in which boundary traces are generated synthetically on the NINN subdomain without requiring any full-domain solves. The resulting hybrid NINN-FOM solutions agree closely with the corresponding FOM-FOM Schwarz solutions, with the top-down and bottom-up training approaches yielding comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [6] [Physics-based prediction, uncertainty quantification and decision-making for IN718 crystallographic texture intensity across LPBF defocus regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18863)
*Yisheng Lu,John Riris,Jie Song,Yao Fu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究面向激光粉末床熔融（laser powder bed fusion, LPBF）中 Inconel 718 的 <001> || BD 织构预测，提出一个两阶段物理基模型：第一阶段由工艺变量映射熔化模式与熔池几何，第二阶段以经验物理模型叠加随机森林残差模型，并在支撑不足或超出传导区间时抑制预测。受控评估中物理锚定模型显著优于黑箱模型，并具备可迁移的预测区间与预测抑制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠预测 LPBF 织构对于关联工艺条件与各向异性响应及工艺合格性至关重要。黑箱模型在分布偏移（shift）下可能失效，且无法区分数据支撑薄弱与物理有效性丧失。

Method: 构建两阶段物理基框架：Stage 1 将工艺变量映射至熔化模式与熔池几何；Stage 2 结合经验物理模型与随机森林（random-forest）残差模型。采用 k 近邻（k-nearest-neighbor）权重在支撑不足时衰减残差修正，并以研究特定的面束功率密度（areal beam-power-density）准则在传导（conduction）包络之外拒绝预测。使用共形区间（conformal intervals）在保留的物理有效集上评估，并以 SHAP 与 Sobol 分析考察残差敏感性。

Result: 在受控的 leave-one-defocus-out 评估中，物理锚定模型 R^2 = 0.778，黑箱模型为 -0.001，门控混合模型为 0.750；在 leave-one-group-out 交叉验证下，门控混合模型 R^2 = 0.592，黑箱模型为 0.538。分组交叉验证中保留集覆盖率为 92.9%（平均全宽 3.65 MUD），迁移至被留出的 +80 mm 离焦（defocus）区间时为 100%（宽 3.21 MUD）。示例映射得到保留的 BD 弹性模量跨度为 127-187 GPa。在独立样本集的九个条件中，框架拒绝三个、衰减三个，其余条件的实测次序吻合。

Conclusion: 将数据适用性、物理有效性与预测不确定性拆分为独立决策，使框架能够在无约束模型无法迁移的场景中实现迁移，并在无任何模型类表现充分时拒绝给出预测。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of crystallographic texture in laser powder bed fusion is critical for linking process conditions with anisotropic response and for qualification. However, black-box models may fail under shift and cannot distinguish weak data support from loss of physical validity. This study develops a two-stage physics-based model for <001> || BD (build direction) texture in Inconel 718. Stage 1 maps process variables to melting mode and melt pool geometry. Stage 2 predicts texture by combining an empirical physics model with a random-forest residual model. A k-nearest-neighbor weight attenuates residual corrections for poorly supported queries, while a study-specific areal beam-power-density criterion withholds predictions outside the adopted conduction envelope. Conformal intervals are evaluated on the retained physics-valid set, and SHAP and Sobol analyses assess residual sensitivity. Under a controlled leave-one-defocus-out evaluation, the physics anchor achieved R^2 = 0.778, against -0.001 for the black-box model and 0.750 for the gated hybrid. Under leave-one-group-out cross-validation, the gated hybrid reached R^2 = 0.592 against 0.538 for the black-box model. Retained-set coverage was 92.9% at a mean full width of 3.65 multiples of a uniform distribution (MUD) under grouped cross-validation and 100% at a width of 3.21 MUD under transfer to a withheld +80 mm defocus regime. An illustrative mapping produced a retained BD elastic-modulus span of 127-187 GPa. On nine conditions from a separately built sample set, the framework withheld three, attenuated three, and matched the measured ordering for the rest. Separating data applicability, physics validity, and predictive uncertainty into distinct decisions lets the framework transfer where an unconstrained model does not, and withhold predictions where no model class performs adequately.

</details>

### [7] [Parity anomaly governs the thermal Hall response of chiral superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene above and below $T_c$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18954)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作指出，菱面体（rhombohedral）四层与五层石墨烯中手性超导（chiral superconductivity）的热霍尔（thermal Hall）响应由 (2+1) 维场论的宇称反常（parity anomaly）精确锁定，给出无自由参数的公式 κ_xy/T = (π²k_B²/6h) C_BdG tanh[Δ(T)/(2k_BT)]，并预测信号在配对形成温度 T* 而非 T_c 起始。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验已在接近 BCS–BEC 交叉（BCS–BEC crossover）的菱面体四层、五层石墨烯中确认手性超导，但缺乏理论给出台阶预期的热霍尔响应，也无法解释信号为何能在相位相干温度 T_c 之上持续存在。

Method: 基于 (2+1) 维场论的宇称反常，推导全温区适用的定量公式；用 BCS–BEC 双能隙关系 Δ² = Δ_sc² + Δ_pg² 统一凝聚态与赝能隙（pseudogap）两个区间；以 Coleman–Hill 非重整化（non-renormalization）与 c_1 = 0 定理保证结果不受相互作用与有限尺寸效应影响；并以三类独立数值方法验证。

Result: 数值验证包括：FHS Chern 数、Wilson 环 c_1 = 0 检验（在机器精度上确认拓扑输入），以及 28 个收敛基态的 DMRG 多体计算，其中实空间 p+ip 特征 arg A_y − arg A_x = −π/2 精确到 10^{-14}。

Conclusion: 理论给出立即可检验的判据，且无需新实验装置：T_c 以下 κ_xy/T 的符号必须等于 T_c 以上已测得反常霍尔电阻（anomalous Hall resistance）R_xy 的符号；另有可在现有器件上用稀释制冷机纳米量热学（nano-calorimetry）检验的四项预测。

Abstract: Chiral superconductivity has recently been confirmed in rhombohedral tetra- and pentalayer graphene near the BCS--BEC crossover, but no theory tells the experimentalist what thermal Hall response to expect, or why any signal should persist above the phase-coherence temperature $T_c$. We show that the parity anomaly of $(2{+}1)$-dimensional field theory fixes the answer exactly, at all temperatures, with no free parameters: $κ_{xy}/T = (π^2 k_B^2/6h)\,C_{\rm BdG}\,\tanh[Δ(T)/(2k_BT)]$, where $C_{\rm BdG}$ is the BdG Chern number and $Δ(T)$ is the fermionic excitation gap. The BCS--BEC two-gap relation $Δ^2 = Δ_{\rm sc}^2 + Δ_{\rm pg}^2$ makes the same formula govern both the condensate and pseudogap regimes, so the signal onsets at the pair-formation temperature $T^{*}$ rather than at $T_c$. Coleman--Hill non-renormalization and the $c_1 = 0$ theorem protect this result against interactions and finite-size artefacts. Three independent numerical validations confirm the topological input at machine precision (FHS Chern numbers, Wilson-loop $c_1 = 0$ test) and at the many-body level (DMRG on 28 converged ground states, including the real-space $p+ip$ signature $\arg\mathcal{A}_y - \arg\mathcal{A}_x = -π/2$ recovered to $10^{-14}$). The theory delivers an immediate falsifiable test that requires no new experimental apparatus: the sign of $κ_{xy}/T$ below $T_c$ must equal the sign of the anomalous Hall resistance $R_{xy}$ already measured above $T_c$, and four further predictions accessible by dilution-refrigerator nano-calorimetry on existing devices.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state properties of a two-component attractive Fermi gas confined to a spherical surface, demonstrating how curvature and finite-size effects lead to qualitatively different behavior compared to the planar limit. In the strongly attractive regime, the system forms a Bose-Einstein condensate (BEC) of tightly bound dimers, yielding a gap and chemical potential that approach flat-space results with leading-order geometric corrections. However, as the attraction decreases, the system crosses over into a Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) regime where the Cooper pair size becomes constrained by the sphere radius, producing pronounced deviations from flat-space behavior. We identify strong shell effects at magic particle numbers set by the single-particle spectrum, revealing the geometry-driven suppression of fermionic pairing at weak interactions. Our results highlight a fundamental interplay among geometry, quantum statistics, and interactions, motivating future experiments with shell-trapped ultracold fermions and inspiring geometry-controlled condensed matter devices.

Abstract: Recent quantum gas cavity-QED experiments demonstrated simultaneous coupling of photons to single atom transitions as well as correlated pairs of ultracold fermions trapped inside optical cavities. This enables simultaneous control over density ordering and pairing. Using extensive numerical simulations, we show that in a transversely driven, two-component degenerate Fermi gas, the interplay of cavity-induced and bare atom--atom interactions controls not only the power threshold for self-organization, but also the type of spatial ordering in the $\mathbb Z_2$-symmetry-broken superradiant state. In the repulsive interaction regime, unpaired or weakly paired fermions first self-organize through a charge-density-wave instability, and finite-momentum pairing only occurs at much stronger pump strengths. In contrast, an attractive superfluid undergoes a joint density--pairing instability, directly entering into intertwined phase with charge-density-wave and pair-density-wave orders. At strong pumping the photon-enhanced pair interaction generates localized density and pairing order even when the bare contact interaction is repulsive. In this cavity-dominated regime strong spatial localization suppresses the long range superfluid coherence. Our results identify the role of cavity-induced atomic interactions in supporting intertwined fermionic orders and pave the way for exploring exotic states with multiple orders in highly controlled hybrid light-matter systems.

### [10] [Chern Insulators on a Twisted Klein Bottle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18189)
*Rong Xiao,Y. X. Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该工作指出，动量空间非点式手性对称性（momentum-space nonsymmorphic chiral symmetry）可在二分晶格上由晶体对称性与子晶格对称性共同作用自然涌现，无需精细调节跃迁相位，并由此产生扭曲的克莱因瓶（twisted Klein bottle）与双宇宙（dicosm）等商流形，显著改变拓扑分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有动量空间非点式对称性（momentum-space nonsymmorphic symmetries）的实现通常依赖对跃迁相位（hopping phases）的精细调节以满足投影对称代数（projective symmetry algebras），缺乏自然来源，限制了其作为拓扑相设计原理的普适性。

Method: 在二分晶格（bipartite lattices）上，通过晶体对称性与子晶格对称性的相互作用构造对称代数；借助 Atiyah–Segal 形式化（Atiyah–Segal formalism）分析对称算符自由作用（free actions）所给出的商流形及其扭曲性质。

Result: 所得对称算符可与哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）反对易，构成动量空间非点式手性对称性；其自由作用将布里渊环面（Brillouin torus）约化为更基本的流形，但在 Atiyah–Segal 形式化下这些流形是扭曲的：手性滑移反射（chiral glide reflection）与螺旋旋转（screw rotation）分别在二维与三维给出扭曲克莱因瓶与 dicosm。扭曲显著改变拓扑分类——非扭曲克莱因瓶因不可定向性（nonorientability）迫使陈数（Chern number）为零，而扭曲克莱因瓶可承载非零陈数，对应于环面上的偶数陈数；在开放边界下，相应拓扑不变量体现为具有滑移反射结构的能量—动量空间手性边缘态（chiral edge states）。

Conclusion: 动量空间非点式手性对称性构成一种新的组织原理（organizing principle），可用于发现与设计超越常规晶体学分类（crystalline classifications）的拓扑相。

Abstract: Recently, momentum-space nonsymmorphic symmetries have attracted considerable attention. However, their realizations typically rely on fine-tuning hopping phases to satisfy the required projective symmetry algebras. Here, we show that such symmetry algebras arise naturally on bipartite lattices through the interplay of crystalline and sublattice symmetries. Unlike previously studied cases, these symmetry operators can anticommute with the Hamiltonian and are therefore referred to as momentum-space nonsymmorphic chiral symmetries. Although the free actions of these symmetries reduce the Brillouin torus to more elementary manifolds, the resulting manifolds are twisted in the Atiyah--Segal formalism. In particular, chiral glide reflection (screw rotation) gives rise to a twisted Klein bottle (dicosm) in two (three) dimensions, and this twisting dramatically alters the topological classification. Unlike the untwisted Klein bottle, whose nonorientability forces the Chern number to vanish, the twisted Klein bottle can host nonzero Chern numbers, corresponding to even Chern numbers on the torus. At open boundaries, the corresponding topological invariant manifests itself through chiral edge states exhibiting a glide-reflection structure in energy--momentum space. Our results establish momentum-space nonsymmorphic chiral symmetry as a new organizing principle for discovering and engineering topological phases beyond conventional crystalline classifications.

</details>


### [11] [Robust Topology and Tunable Geometry in Generalized BHZ Model with Fractional Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19060)
*Coleen Adrianne Panganiban,Kristian Hauser Villegas*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文将 Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang（BHZ）模型分数化，研究分数阶色散对能带拓扑与量子几何的影响，发现分数调制重塑布里渊区内的量子几何分布，但陈数保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数阶量子力学（fractional quantum mechanics）的最新进展，探究分数阶色散（fractional dispersion）如何影响能带拓扑（band topology）与量子几何（quantum geometry）。

Method: 提出 BHZ 模型的分数阶推广；在低能极限下归约为分数阶狄拉克哈密顿量（fractional Dirac Hamiltonian）并保持动量空间周期性；通过傅里叶级数变换直接构造对应的实空间紧束缚模型（tight-binding model），并利用布洛赫本征态（Bloch eigenstates）计算量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor）及其分量（贝里曲率与量子度规）。

Result: 分数阶调制使量子几何量在布里渊区（Brillouin zone）内重新分布，色散指数变为分数时局部能带几何出现显著改变；但陈数（Chern number）保持不变量。

Conclusion: 全局拓扑相（topological phase）对分数阶形变具有鲁棒性，且该不变性对更广泛的一类分数阶模型依然成立。

Abstract: With recent developments in fractional quantum mechanics, we introduce a fractional generalization of the Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang (BHZ) model to investigate the effects of fractional dispersion on band topology and quantum geometry. In the low-energy limit, the model reduces to a fractional Dirac Hamiltonian while preserving momentum-space periodicity, thereby ensuring a compact Brillouin zone and a well-defined topological invariant. We further show that the corresponding real-space tight-binding model can be constructed directly through a Fourier-series transformation, providing a simpler and more general alternative to the methods typically employed for fractional lattice systems. This approach readily extends beyond the generalized BHZ model considered here. Using the Bloch eigenstates, we compute the quantum geometric tensor and analyze its components, namely the Berry curvature and quantum metric. We find that fractional tuning redistributes these quantities throughout the Brillouin zone as the dispersion exponent becomes fractional, leading to pronounced modifications of the local band geometry. In contrast, the Chern number remains invariant, demonstrating the robustness of the global topological phase against fractional deformation. We further argue that this invariance persists for a broad class of fractional models.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究构建分层贝叶斯（hierarchical Bayesian）框架，将引力波定位与活动星系核（AGN）星表的空间关联信息及引力波源内禀性质建模统一于单一似然函数，用于约束AGN宿主引力波事件占比 f_agn。结合 GWTC-5.0 与 Quaia AGN 星表，无法排除AGN对观测并合率的贡献，并对不同热辐射光度阈值给出 f_agn 的95%可信水平上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）观测到的恒星级双黑洞（BBH）起源尚不确定，其中AGN吸积盘通道可提升并合率并可能在质量上隙产生快速自旋黑洞。既有约束依赖空间关联或源内禀性质建模两类彼此独立的途径，缺乏统一分析。

Method: 提出分层贝叶斯框架，将引力波定位与AGN星表的空间相关性、以及引力波源族群内禀性质的建模统一进关于 f_agn 的单一似然；使用最新引力波暂现星表 GWTC-5.0 与电磁 Quaia AGN 星表进行分析。

Result: 在基准模型下，对于光学与红外波段未被遮蔽、热辐射光度 L_bol ≥ {10^44.5, 10^45.5, 10^46.5} erg/s 的AGN，在95%可信水平上排除 f_agn ≥ {0.52, 0.41, 0.61}；同时无法排除AGN对观测并合率的贡献。

Conclusion: AGN通道对LVK并合族群的贡献尚不能被排除，仅能给出依赖光度阈值与基准模型的上限。该方法与结果为将完整引力波后验（posterior）与电磁观测结合、以推断引力波族群性质与AGN吸积盘性质提供了路径。

Abstract: The origin of the stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) observed in gravitational wave (GW) events by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) detectors remains uncertain. One promising formation pathway is the active galactic nucleus (AGN) channel, in which BBHs embedded in AGN accretion discs experience enhanced merger rates and may produce rapidly spinning black holes in the upper mass gap. Previous constraints on the AGN contribution to the LVK merger population have relied either on spatial correlations between GW localisations and AGN catalogues or on modelling the intrinsic properties of GW sources. We develop a hierarchical Bayesian framework that unifies these approaches within a single likelihood for the fraction of AGN-hosted GW events, $f_{\rm agn}$. This framework, combined with the latest gravitational wave transient catalogue GWTC-5.0 and the electromagnetic Quaia AGN catalogue, cannot exclude an AGN contribution to the observed merger rate. For unobscured AGN in the optical and infrared bands with bolometric luminosities $L_{\rm bol}\geq\{10^{44.5},10^{45.5},10^{46.5}\}\,\mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$, we exclude $f_{\rm agn} \geq \{0.52,0.41, 0.61\}$ at the 95 percent credible level under our fiducial model. The methodology and results presented in this work lay down the pathway for combining the full GW posterior with electromagnetic observations to infer the properties of GW populations and AGN discs.

</details>

Assuming the remnant from binary neutron star merger is a supramassive neutron star, as suggested by the modeling of the electromagnetic counterparts of GW170817, we examine the maximum mass of nonrotating neutron stars ($M_{\rm TOV}$) while accounting for the uncertainty in the remnant's lifetime ($t_{\rm c}$). Our results show that $M_{\rm TOV}$ varies from $2.09^{+0.11}_{-0.09}\,M_\odot$ for $t_{\rm c}=0.1$ s to $2.18^{+0.10}_{-0.09}\,M_\odot$ for $t_{\rm c}=1$ s. This result is consistent with that independently inferred from the re-construction of the equation of state of neutron star matter, i.e., $M_{\rm TOV,exc}=2.16^{+0.10}_{-0.07}M_\odot$, particularly if the very massive neutron stars with masses measured indirectly have been removed in constructing the prior distribution of $M_{\rm TOV}$. ### [14] [On Logarithmic Donaldson-Thomas invariants for local Calabi-Yau $4$-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17720)
*Xiaolong Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究含简单正规交叉除子的局部对数 Calabi-Yau 4-流形上的对数 Donaldson-Thomas 不变量，通过移位拉格朗日类构造相对与族对数 DT₄ 理论并证明退化公式，最终计算了局部曲面 snc 对的零维对数 DT₄ 不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为局部对数 Calabi-Yau 4-流形（local log Calabi-Yau 4-folds）建立对数 Donaldson-Thomas 不变量（logarithmic Donaldson-Thomas invariants）理论框架，特别是处理带简单正规交叉除子（simple normal crossing divisors）的情形，并发展相应的退化公式（degeneration formula）与计算方法。

Method: 利用 Khan-Kinjo-Park-Safronov 宣布的移位拉格朗日类（shifted Lagrangian classes）的族版本，为曲线的对数 Hilbert 概形（logarithmic Hilbert schemes）构造相对与族对数 DT₄ 理论并证明退化公式；对点的对数 Hilbert 概形构造虚拟类（virtual classes）并在 Chow 群中证明退化公式。最后结合退化公式与对数配边（logarithmic cobordism）进行计算。

Result: 对曲线的对数 Hilbert 概形，构造了相对与族对数 DT₄ 理论并证明退化公式；对点的对数 Hilbert 概形，构造了虚拟类并证明 Chow 群中的退化公式，且该结果不依赖于移位拉格朗日类；结合退化公式与对数配边，显式计算了局部曲面 snc 对（local surface snc pairs）的零维对数 DT₄ 不变量。

Conclusion: 本文建立了局部对数 CY 4-流形上对数 DT₄ 不变量的理论工具与退化公式，其中点的情形可独立于移位拉格朗日类成立，并通过对数配边实现了局部曲面 snc 对零维不变量的显式计算，为后续对数枚举几何研究提供了框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study logarithmic Donaldson-Thomas invariants for local log Calabi-Yau $4$-folds with simple normal crossing divisors. Using the family version of shifted Lagrangian classes announced by Khan-Kinjo-Park-Safronov, we construct relative and family logarithmic $\mathsf{DT}_4$-theories for logarithmic Hilbert schemes of curves, and prove a degeneration formula. For logarithmic Hilbert schemes of points, we construct virtual classes and prove a degeneration formula in Chow groups; in particular, it is independent of shifted Lagrangian classes. Finally, combining our degeneration formula with logarithmic cobordism, we explicitly compute the zero-dimensional logarithmic $\mathsf{DT}_4$-invariants for local surface snc pairs.

</details>

### [15] [Theoretical--operational modelling of complex experiments: parameter robustness and degeneracy in muon--electron conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17581)
*Vitaly Pronskikh*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 论文提出“理论—操作模型（theoretical--operational model, TOM）”，将复杂实验的向前预测表述为带类型（typed）的因子分解，并研究各类模型变形如何投影到物理参数生成的局部流形上。该框架把模型变形分解为可被参数吸收的等价分量与无法吸收的残差分量，从而给出参数推断的鲁棒性、精确简并（exact degeneracy）与部分简并的局部判据，并在μ--e转换实验（Mu2e）场景中加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂实验通过物理模型与数据约简（data reduction）的耦合链条推断理论参数，但模型变形对参数推断的影响缺乏统一的局部理论描述。该工作旨在刻画制备、现象建模、读出、本底与分析等环节的变化如何映射到物理参数所生成的局部流形，从而给出鲁棒性与简并性的判据。

Method: 将TOM表述为向前预测的带类型因子分解（typed factorization）；对平滑预测与局部可识别（locally identifiable）的加权最小二乘估计，构造响应映射（response map），把每个模型变形分离为参数等价分量与残差分量。方法随后应用于铝中带电轻子味破坏（charged-lepton-flavour-violating）的μ--e转换，包括单箱转换率模型、双模板弹性和非弹性谱模型，以及基于已发表Mu2e谱的数值算例。

Result: 单箱转换率模型存在精确的归一化简并（normalization degeneracy）。在双模板弹性和非弹性谱模型中，共同的信号归一化可被拟合的转换率吸收而不改变对算符敏感的核响应比，而弹性和非弹性之间的相对效率变化在一阶上精确等价于该比值的变化。基于已发表Mu2e谱的Run-I数值算例显示，100 keV/c的动量尺度失配仅为部分参数等价：其在由弹性归一化R0和对数非弹性—弹性响应比ρ生成的局部切空间上的投影比为η=0.331，而大部分加权谱变形仍以残差形状形式存在。

Conclusion: TOM在粒子/核现象学与实验实现之间的界面上，提供了关于参数鲁棒性与简并性的局部理论描述。

Abstract: Complex experiments infer theory parameters through a coupled chain of physical models and data reduction. We formulate the theoretical--operational model (TOM) as a typed factorization of this forward prediction and study how changes of preparation, phenomenon modelling, readout, backgrounds, and analysis project onto the local manifold generated by the physics parameters. For a smooth prediction and a locally identifiable weighted least-squares estimate, the resulting response map separates each model deformation into a parameter-equivalent component and a residual component that cannot be absorbed by a change of the fitted physics parameters. This gives local criteria for robustness, exact degeneracy, and partial degeneracy of parameter inference. The construction is applied to charged-lepton-flavour-violating muon--electron conversion in aluminium. A one-bin conversion-rate model exhibits an exact normalization degeneracy. In a two-template model of the elastic--inelastic spectrum, a common signal normalization is absorbed by the fitted conversion rate without changing an operator-sensitive nuclear-response ratio, whereas a relative elastic--inelastic efficiency change is exactly parameter-equivalent to a change of that ratio at first order. A numerical Run-I example based on published Mu2e spectra shows that a \(100\,\mathrm{keV}/c\) momentum-scale mismatch is only partially parameter-equivalent: its projection ratio onto the local tangent space generated by the elastic normalization \(R_0\) and the logarithmic inelastic-to-elastic response ratio \(ρ\) is \(η=0.331\), while most of the weighted spectral deformation remains as a residual shape. TOM thereby provides a local theoretical description of parameter robustness and degeneracy at the interface of particle/nuclear phenomenology and experimental realization.

</details>

### [16] [The suppression thesis in Riemann's Habilitationsvortrag](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18923)
*Victor Tapia*

Main category: math.HO

TL;DR: 本文针对黎曼（Riemann）1854年就职演讲（Habilitationsvortrag）中缺少解析推导（analytical developments）这一现象，反驳“压制说”（suppression thesis），主张真正原因是其思想过于新颖而缺乏可用的数学语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解释（据戴德金（Dedekind）记载）认为，黎曼为顾及哲学院非数学教授的理解而刻意省略分析性推导，即“压制说”。作者质疑该解释的合理性，试图给出更可信的替代说明。

Method: 采用历史文献比较与反证法：以1851年就职学位论文（Inauguraldissertation）为对照，该文面向同一批非数学教授却包含大量解析推导，从而检验“压制说”的内在一致性。

Result: 1851年的论文包含大量分析展开且未被省略，说明非数学教授在场与解析推导的有无并无关联，故“压制说”不成立；1854年演讲中流形（manifold）、线元（line element）、曲率（curvature）等概念当时过于新颖，尚无恰当语言可表达，黎曼因此被迫采用非技术性叙述风格。

Conclusion: 相较于“压制说”，以思想新颖性与语言缺失来解释1854年演讲缺乏解析推导的解释更具可信度。

Abstract: In 1854 Riemann presented his Habilitationsvortrag {\it Über die Hypothesen welche der Geometrie zu Grunde liegen} to the Faculty of Philosophy of the University of Göttingen. The lecture does not contain analytical developments and Riemann never explained the reason for this choice. According to Dedekind this choice was done in order to make the lecture understandable to the non-mathematician professors of the Faculty. This is the suppression thesis.
  Nevertheless, in 1851, three years before, Riemann has presented his Inauguraldissertation {\it Grundlagen für eine allgemeine Theorie der Functionen einer veränderlichen complexen Grösse} to the same professors of the Faculty of Philosophy. The Inauguraldissertation contains many analytical developments but, on that occasion, they were not suppressed. Therefore, the presence of non-mathematician professors is unrelated with the presence or absence of analytical developments in the lectures.
  The ideas contained in Riemann's Habilitationsvortrag --manifold, line element, curvature-- were so new at the time that there was not even a proper language to express them. Therefore, Riemann was forced to adopt a non-technical expository style. This explanation for the absence of analytical developments in Riemann's Habilitationsvortrag is more credible than the suppression thesis.

</details>

### [17] [Uniform High-Frequency Localization on Quantum Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17623)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究紧致度量图（compact metric graph）上拉普拉斯算子（Laplacian）特征函数的高频局域化（high-frequency localization），刻画其渐近局域化常数与边界感知奇异完备（singular completion）之间的关系，并构造反例说明奇异完备原则在一般情形下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解紧致度量图上高频特征函数必须在给定可测观测集上放置的最小 L² 质量，即量化局域化几何，并判定仅由奇异（secular）流形的正则高斯像（Gauss image）所决定的奇异完备是否能完全刻画该变分问题。

Method: 方法上，首先将渐近局域化常数刻画为可达边强度集（edge-intensity set）上线性泛函的最小值；在标准 Kirchhoff 情形下用奇异流形的正则高斯像描述该集合；进而引入边界感知奇异完备锥（boundary-aware singular-completion cone），并结合精确有理分离元（rational separator）与经验证的 Krawczyk 包围（Krawczyk enclosure）构造反例，再通过径向导数恒等式与递推得到精确高频特征序列。

Result: 结果表明：本原环（primitive cycle）与外部到外部路径决定正性阈值而非一般情形下的正数值；奇异完备锥包含树、单环图与秩二闭图的全部正则奇异边能量向量，但存在秩二、带两个 Dirichlet 叶子的图使其失效；在该图上可构造满足 C∞(ω;ℓ) < 1/2 < C_sing(ω;ℓ) 的可测观测集。

Conclusion: 结论是：奇异完备在若干低复杂度图类中能捕捉定量局域化几何，但在固定量子图（quantum graph）上一般不决定高频变分问题，故其适用范围存在本质限制。

Abstract: We study uniform high-frequency localization for the Laplacian on compact metric graphs through the least $L^2$-mass that eigenfunctions must place in a prescribed measurable observation set. We first identify this asymptotic localization constant with the minimum of a linear functional over the attainable edge-intensity set; in the generic standard-Kirchhoff setting, this set is governed by the regular Gauss image of the secular manifold. Primitive cycles and exterior-to-exterior paths consequently determine the positivity threshold, but not, in general, the positive numerical value. We introduce a boundary-aware singular-completion cone and prove that it contains all regular secular edge-energy vectors for trees, unicyclic graphs, and closed graphs of cycle rank two. We then construct a cycle-rank-two graph with two Dirichlet leaves for which this completion principle fails. An exact rational separator, combined with a validated Krawczyk enclosure, yields a nonsingular scalar secular state lying outside every boundary-compatible singular sector. A positive radial derivative identity and recurrence in the compact orbit closure convert this local separation into an exact high-frequency eigensequence for a single fixed metric. For a suitable measurable observation set, the true high-frequency localization constant $C_\infty(ω;\ell)$ and its singular-completion counterpart $C_{\mathrm{sing}}(ω;\ell)$ satisfy $$C_\infty(ω;\ell)<1/2<C_{\mathrm{sing}}(ω;\ell)$$. Thus, singular completion captures the quantitative localization geometry in several low-complexity classes but does not, in general, determine the high-frequency variational problem on a fixed quantum graph.

</details>

Abstract: A qKZ-type discrete flat connection with multiplicative step $p$ has monodromy operators when $p$ is a root of unity. The monodromy operators are commuting endomorphisms of the connection. We construct their eigensections and eigenvalues. The construction is based on the presentation of the discrete flat connection as a discrete Gauss-Manin connection.

</details>

Motivation: 该主题在探测（probing）相关问题中具有现实意义，而此类问题中被积函数（integrand）自然应被视为不连续函数（discontinuous function）。然而现有逆变换公式（inversion formulas）仅在可微函数（differentiable functions）情形下得到证明，二者之间存在不匹配，因而产生为不连续函数建立公式的需求。

Method: 论文设定为：通过对已有针对光滑函数（smooth functions）的证明进行某种修改（modification），以获得所需结果。摘要未给出修改的具体构造细节。

Conclusion: 作者认为，将 Radon 变换（Radon transform）的逆变换结论从可微函数（differentiable functions）推广到不连续函数（discontinuous function）是可行且必要的，其途径在于对既有光滑情形证明的改造。

Abstract: The problem of inverting the Radon integral transform in
  finite-dimensional Euclidean space is considered. The relevance
  of this topic for probing issues is indicated. It is noted that for the latter direction, it is
  natural to consider the integrand as discontinuous function.
  However, the available inversion formulas are only proven for
  differentiable functions. Therefore, the question of obtaining
  formulas for discontinuous functions arises. It is set that the
  required results can be obtained by some modification of available
  proofs for smooth functions.

</details>

Abstract: By using the Dirac and symplectic formalisms, the analysis of the Holst action in the $G \rightarrow 0$ limit is performed. We study the theory's full canonical structure and symmetries. In particular, we focus on the cases $γ= i$ and $γ\neq i$, aiming to obtain Smolin's auto-self-dual model in terms of the Ashtekar variables and a Barbero-type formulation. We then perform the symplectic analysis and discuss the principal insight of these formalisms.

</details>


### [21] [Pathology-Free Real-Space Renormalization Group Theory on an Inverse Limit Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18356)
*Fabio Arz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有限系统逆极限（inverse limits）的新框架，用以严格实现威尔逊（Wilson）原始的重整化群（renormalization group）思想，规避已有严格实现中的病态问题，并为非平凡不动点（non-trivial fixed points）存在性等难题开辟新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自威尔逊提出获诺贝尔奖的重整化群思想逾五十年，数学界虽多次尝试，仍无令人满意的严格实现。既有证明多依赖其他思路，未能覆盖重整化群的全部预测能力；而严格实现中发现的病态现象进一步削弱了数学界的兴趣。

Method: 基于有限系统（finite systems）的逆极限（inverse limits）构造一个全新框架，以严格方式实现威尔逊的原始重整化群思想。

Result: 该框架可规避此前严格实现中出现的病态（pathologies）问题，从而得到一个数学上自洽的威尔逊重整化群框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为解决严格重整化群理论中的若干既有问题（如非平凡不动点（non-trivial fixed points）的存在性）开辟了一条全新路径。

Abstract: It has been over fifty years since Kenneth Wilson had his Nobel-prize-winning ideas on the renormalization group. In this time frame, despite many attempts, no mathematical results have implemented Wilson's vision to a satisfactory degree. Although a number of predictions stemming from the renormalization group framework have been proven to date, these proofs usually rely on alternative ideas and do not cover the full predictive power of Wilson's renormalization group. The discovery of pathologies within rigorous implementations of renormalization group transformations have further reduced the enthusiasm of the mathematics community for this subject. This paper presents a novel framework to implement Wilson's original idea in a rigorous manner based on inverse limits of finite systems. Doing so, one avoids these pathologies and hence obtains a mathematically sound framework for Wilson's renormalization group. This opens up a completely new path to tackle some of the existing problems in rigorous renormalization group theory like the existence of non-trivial fixed points.

</details>

Abstract: In this note we review and compare mathematical procedures aimed at rigorously deriving fluid-dynamic descriptions of classical particle systems in the large-scale limit of Hamiltonian dynamics. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注接触变换在不再保持接触形式的情形下，如何刻画其守恒与耗散结构，并将该框架扩展至奇异光滑映射。

Method: 定义条件施加于拉回的接触一次形式（pulled-back contact one-form），不要求其仍为接触形式；利用多项式构造，从与原接触形式具有相同耗散率的一次形式生成首次积分（first integrals）；通过两种归一化处理关联哈密顿量耗散率不同的映射，并在哈密顿量非零区域构造光滑不变自同态（invariant endomorphism）。

Result: 构造出迹守恒的光滑不变自同态，该构造涵盖原哈密顿量的零点（此处自同态消失）；将张量迹与哈密顿量归一化多项式的系数相联系，得到 2n+1 维下 n 个功能独立迹不变量的上界，并给出一个达到该上界的五维奇异映射。

Conclusion: 该框架统一刻画了接触类正则变换及奇异映射的守恒与耗散性质，示例涵盖阻尼自由粒子、耗散率不等的变换、阻尼谐振子以及带线性摩擦的重力运动。

Abstract: We study conserved and dissipated quantities associated with contact canonoid transformations and their extension to possibly singular smooth maps. The defining condition is imposed on the pulled-back contact one-form, without requiring it to remain contact. A polynomial construction produces first integrals from one-forms having the same dissipation rate as the original contact form. Two normalizations extend this construction to maps whose associated Hamiltonians have different dissipation rates. On the set where the associated Hamiltonian is nonzero, we construct a smooth invariant endomorphism whose traces are conserved. This construction includes zeros of the original Hamiltonian, where the endomorphism vanishes. We relate the tensor traces to the coefficients of the Hamiltonian-normalized polynomial and obtain an upper bound of n functionally independent trace invariants in dimension 2n + 1. A five-dimensional singular map attains this bound. Examples include singular maps for the damped free particle, a transformation with unequal dissipation rates, the damped harmonic oscillator, and motion under gravity with linear friction.

</details>


### [24] [Wehrl-type entropy problem for compact connected semisimple Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18919)
*Haonan Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文求解了任意紧致连通半单李群（compact connected semisimple Lie group）的 Wehrl 型熵（Wehrl-type entropy）问题，证明相干投影算子（coherent projectors）是所有密度矩阵（density matrices）上 Wehrl 熵的唯一极小元，并唯一最大化任意 p>1 阶 Husimi 幂矩（Husimi power moment）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wehrl 型熵问题的既有结果多局限于特定群或表示情形，尚缺乏对一般紧致连通半单李群的统一处理。该工作旨在将该问题的解推广至与最高权（highest weight）λ 对应的任意有限维不可约酉表示（irreducible unitary representation）。

Method: 对极值元（extremizer）沿与 Killing 场（Killing fields）相关联的方向作扰动并计算二阶变分（second-variation），沿用 Frank 与 Lieb 的策略；由此将原问题归约为最高权向量（highest-weight vectors）的极端矩（extreme-moment）性质。

Result: 在 V_λ 上的所有密度矩阵中，相干投影算子是 Wehrl 熵的唯一极小元；同时它们唯一最大化所有 p>1 阶 Husimi 幂矩。

Conclusion: Wehrl 型熵问题及相应 Husimi 幂矩的极值刻画对任意紧致连通半单李群成立，其唯一最优解由相干投影算子给出，证明核心可归约至最高权向量的极端矩性质。

Abstract: This paper solves the Wehrl-type entropy problem for arbitrary compact connected semisimple Lie groups. Let $G$ be a compact connected semisimple Lie group, and let $π:G\to U(V_λ)$ be a finite-dimensional irreducible unitary representation associated with the highest weight $λ$. We prove that coherent projectors are the unique minimizers of the Wehrl entropy over all density matrices on $V_λ$. They also uniquely maximize every Husimi power moment of order $p>1$. The proof uses a second-variation at the extremizer by perturbation in directions associated with Killing fields, similar to the strategy of Frank and Lieb used in \cite{FrankLieb}. Then the problem reduces to the extreme-moment property of highest-weight vectors.

</details>


### [25] [Polynomial rigidity of strong-field magnetic billiards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18937)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在强磁场（strong-field）情形下，若平面严格凸域上的磁台球（magnetic billiard）存在关于速度变量的非平凡多项式首次积分（first integral），则该域必为圆盘，从而消除了早期多项式不可积理论遗留的有限例外磁场强度集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆盘磁台球具有一个关于速度的多项式首次积分，由此产生中心刚性（central rigidity）问题：是否存在其他光滑凸台球也具有此类积分。该问题在早期多项式不可积理论中仅被部分解决，仍遗留有限个例外强场强度。

Method: 设 Ω 为有界严格凸且边界光滑的平面域，Larmor 半径（Larmor radius）r=|B|^{-1} 满足 0<r<r_0(γ)/2。刚性机制分两个逻辑独立阶段：其一，由最高反射模式导出边界缠绕恒等式（boundary winding identity），确定最高系数的次数并证明其全部根严格位于台球内部；其二，将前两个主导反射恒等式延拓至复化边界的归一化（normalization of the complexified boundary），用其在无穷远处的赋值排除坐标函数的共同极点，迫使最高系数为单一线性因子的幂。

Result: 在强场（strong field）条件下，任意有限次数的非常数多项式首次积分均不存在于非圆盘情形：若此类积分存在，则 Ω 必为圆盘。此结论移除了先前理论中遗留的有限例外场强集合；且未施加实解析（real-analytic）边界假设，解析性可由代数强场平行曲线（parallel curves）推出。

Conclusion: 将根位置定理（location theorem）与根坍缩定理（root-collapse theorem）相结合，得到边界切线与径向方向之间的常角关系（constant-angle relation），进而推出边界的圆性（circularity），完成刚性证明。

Abstract: A magnetic billiard describes a charged particle constrained to a planar domain: the particle moves along circular Larmor arcs in the interior and undergoes specular reflection at the boundary. The round disk has an explicit first integral that is polynomial in the velocity, and a central rigidity question asks whether any other smooth convex table can have such an integral.
  We answer this question negatively in the strong-field regime. Let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^2$ be a bounded strictly convex domain with smooth boundary $γ$, let $r=|B|^{-1}$ be the Larmor radius, and assume $0<r<r_0(γ)/2$, where $r_0(γ)$ is the maximal embedded tubular radius. If the magnetic billiard admits a nonconstant first integral polynomial in the velocity variables, of any finite degree, then $Ω$ is a disk. This removes the finite exceptional set of strong field strengths left by the earlier polynomial nonintegrability theory.
  The rigidity mechanism has two logically independent stages. First, the highest reflection mode gives a boundary winding identity. This determines the degree of the top coefficient and places all of its roots strictly inside the table, but it does not show that those roots coincide. Second, after the two leading reflection identities are continued to the normalization of the complexified boundary, their valuations at infinity exclude simultaneous poles of the coordinate functions. The remaining one-sided poles force the entire top coefficient to be one linear factor of multiplicity equal to the Fourier degree. Combining the location theorem with this root-collapse theorem produces a constant-angle relation between the boundary tangent and a radial direction, hence circularity. No real-analytic boundary hypothesis is imposed: analyticity follows from the algebraic strong-field parallel curves.

</details>


### [26] [The Thermodynamic Limit of Short-Range Spin Glasses with Periodic Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19005)
*Hexiang Wang,Keheng Zhu,Mauris Chueng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明离散环面上最近邻 Edwards–Anderson Ising 模型（Edwards–Anderson Ising model）的淬火热力学极限（quenched thermodynamic limit）存在，且自由能密度（free-energy density）几乎必然自平均（almost-sure self-averaging），仅需耦合分布满足一阶矩有限（E|J|<∞）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法多依赖周期性次可加性（periodic subadditivity）或对耦合律施加对称性、中心化等额外假设，限制了对一般无序分布（disorder distribution）的适用性。本文旨在放宽矩条件并摆脱上述结构假设，同时明确几乎必然陈述所需的概率空间设定。

Method: 证明避免使用周期性次可加性：将以环绕键（wrap-around bonds）产生的效应处理为表面阶扰动（surface-order perturbation），并借助铺砌论证（tiling argument）与强大数定律（strong law of large numbers）得到自由边界极限。论证中唯一的矩假设为 E|J|<∞，且不要求耦合律对称或中心化；同时显式构造了一个统一的概率空间（common probability space），以使跨体积的几乎必然陈述有定义。

Result: 建立了淬火热力学极限的存在性与自由能密度的几乎必然自平均性，并在一般矩假设下给出无序平均有限体积修正（disorder-averaged finite-volume correction）的定量界 O(L^{-1})。

Conclusion: 结果表明，环面上最近邻 Edwards–Anderson Ising 模型的热力学极限与自平均性质可在极弱的矩条件（E|J|<∞）及无对称性/中心化假设下成立，且相关几乎必然命题需在明确指定的公共概率空间上表述。

Abstract: For the nearest-neighbour Edwards--Anderson Ising model on the discrete torus, we prove existence of the quenched thermodynamic limit and almost-sure self-averaging of the free-energy density. The only moment assumption in the main argument is $\E|J|<\infty$, and no symmetry or centering of the coupling law is needed. The proof avoids periodic subadditivity: the wrap-around bonds form a surface-order perturbation, while a tiling argument and the strong law of large numbers give the free-boundary limit. We also obtain the quantitative bounds $(O(L^{-1})$ for the disorder-averaged finite-volume correction. A precise common probability space is specified, since an almost-sure statement across volumes is otherwise not well defined.

</details>

### [27] [Electric field effects on electrolytes near rough dielectric surfaces by GPU-accelerated code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17791)
*Isaac Smith,Nicholas Pogharian,Francisco J. Solis,Trung Dac Nguyen,Monica Olvera de la Cruz*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作结合分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）与微扰解析计算，揭示外加电场可调控正弦介电界面诱导的离子电荷密度交替图案，并可将其逆转；同时给出抵消该调制所需的临界电场解析式，并发布经GPU加速的DIELECTRIC软件包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介电界面（dielectric interfaces）广泛存在于离子电子器件、超级电容器与活细胞等人工及自然系统中，这些粗糙介电表面上的离子电荷分布受表面几何与电场共同影响，但电场如何改写界面诱导的离子分布尚需厘清。

Method: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟与微扰解析计算（perturbative analytic calculations）相结合的方法；针对小幅正弦表面高度起伏推导临界电场解析表达式，并采用傅里叶模式叠加处理多模表面；同时更新并验证LAMMPS中的DIELECTRIC软件包。

Result: 正弦界面产生的离子电荷密度交替区域图案可被外加电场修改乃至逆转；确定了抵消介电界面驱动离子密度调制所需的临界电场强度并给出小幅正弦起伏下的解析式；表面高度为傅里叶模式之和时，离子浓度可由各模式贡献线性相加而得，从而可预测粗糙表面附近的离子分布；新版本DIELECTRIC软件包在测试体系上实现2.4-24倍加速，并支持在任意粗糙度介电界面附近施加电场。

Conclusion: 电场可作为调控介电界面附近离子电荷分布的有效手段，小振幅正弦起伏可用解析式刻画临界场，多模叠加使粗糙表面离子分布具备可预测性；配套的GPU并行DIELECTRIC软件包提升了模拟效率并扩展了功能。

Abstract: Dielectric interfaces are ubiquitous in manufactured and natural systems, such as iontronic devices, supercapacitors, and living cells. These, often rough, dielectric surfaces host ionic charge distributions that depend on the surface geometry and the electric fields present. In this work, we study the effect of electric fields on such ionic charge distributions. We demonstrate, by molecular dynamics (MD) and perturbative analytic calculations, that the pattern of alternating regions of ionic charge density created by a sinusoidal interface can be modified and reversed by applying an electric field. We determine the strength of the critical electric field required to cancel the effect of dielectric interface-driven modulation in ion density and develop an analytic expression for that field for small amplitude sinusoidal variations in surface height. We show that ion concentrations near a surface with height given by a sum of Fourier modes can be found by adding the contributions from the concentration modulation due to each mode, allowing the possibility to predict ion distributions near rough surfaces. We updated and validated a LAMMPS package for MD simulation of polarizable surfaces, the DIELECTRIC package, by implementing a new version that achieves a 2.4-24 times speed increase by GPU parallelization on our test system and adds the capability to simulate an applied electric field on simple and complex electrolytes near dielectric interfaces with arbitrary roughness.

</details>


### [28] [Posture selection in active elastic filaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17924)
*Adam Pearl,Ludwig A. Hoffmann,L. Mahadevan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究构建了主动弹性细丝（active elastic filaments）在重力与流体阻力分布载荷下稳定姿态选取的通用理论框架，将力学平衡约束与最优控制理论（optimal control theory）结合，揭示姿态选择是控制代价、功能与动力学稳定性之间的权衡，并应用于固着鳗鱼（sessile eels）以复现实验观测的直立到斜倚姿态转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛇、鳗等细长体的姿态控制源于被动变形、主动内部驱动与任务级约束之间的相互作用，其稳定姿态的选取机制尚缺乏统一理论描述。该研究旨在为主动弹性细丝在外场中如何调控几何以维持功能提供统一视角，兼顾生物学与人工系统意义。

Method: 将力学平衡（mechanical equilibrium）约束与最优控制理论相结合，构建主动弹性细丝在重力与流体阻力（fluid drag）分布力作用下的稳定姿态选择框架。理论最终归结为少数参数的最小化描述，这些参数刻画水动力载荷与重力载荷、弹性（elasticity）与活性（activity）之间的竞争关系。

Result: 姿态选择体现为控制代价、功能与动力学稳定性（dynamical stability）之间的权衡，导致不同解分支（solution branches）共存及分支间的突变式转变。将该理论应用于流动中的固着鳗鱼，复现了实验观测到的从直立（upright）到斜倚（reclining）姿态的转变，并预测了体形与暴露长度的标度律（scaling laws）。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于主动细丝如何在外场中调控几何以维持功能的统一视角，对生物系统与人工系统均具有启示意义。

Abstract: Posture control in slender bodies such as snakes and eels arises from the interplay between passive deformation, active internal actuation, and task-level constraints. We formulate a general framework for the selection of stable postures in active elastic filaments subject to distributed forcing from gravity and fluid drag, by combining the constraints of mechanical equilibrium with optimal control theory. Our theory leads to a minimal description in terms of parameters governing the competition between hydrodynamic and gravitational loading, elasticity, and activity. We show that posture selection reflects a trade-off between control cost, function and dynamical stability, leading to the coexistence of distinct solution branches and abrupt transitions between them. Applying the theory to sessile eels in flow, we recover the experimentally observed transition from upright to reclining postures and predict scaling laws for body shape and exposed length. More generally, our results provide a unified perspective on how active filaments can regulate geometry to maintain function in external fields, with implications for biological and artificial systems.

</details>


### [29] [Prediction of the maximum penetration of a circular intruder in a two-dimensional granular bed from its early trajectory using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17968)
*Patricia Altshuler*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过机器学习（machine learning）检验仅凭圆形侵入体（circular intruder）早期轨迹即可预测其在二维颗粒床（two-dimensional granular bed）中最大穿透深度（maximum penetration depth），结果表明单一特征几乎承载全部预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在验证是否能在不提供侵入体密度（density）信息的前提下，利用早期轨迹预测其在颗粒床中的最大穿透深度。该问题关注早期冲击响应中是否已隐含密度相关信息。

Method: 基于100组密度不同的离散元方法（DEM, discrete element method）模拟，提取前50 ms的运动学特征（kinematic features），并采用岭回归（Ridge）与随机森林（Random Forest）进行最大穿透深度预测。

Result: 岭回归与随机森林均能预测最大穿透深度；其中单一特征——侵入体在50 ms时的高度——几乎集中了全部预测能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，密度信息已编码于侵入体的早期冲击响应之中，故无需显式提供密度即可预测最大穿透深度。

Abstract: We test whether the maximum penetration depth of a circular intruder into a two-dimensional granular bed can be predicted from its early trajectory using machine learning, without giving the intruder density. From 100 DEM simulations with varying density, we extract kinematic features from the first 50 ms. Ridge and Random Forest predict the maximum penetration. A single feature (the intruder height at 50 ms) concentrates almost all predictive power, showing that density information is already encoded in the early impact response.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画大环药物劳拉替尼在不同溶剂环境下的构象行为，并检验基于第一性原理（first principles）与连续溶剂模型（continuum solvent model）的化学位移计算能否重现实验核磁共振（NMR）数据，从而为该类计算方法提供基准验证。

Method: 方法上使用副本交换分子动力学（REMD）在显式水（explicit water）与显式氯仿（chloroform）中采样构象自由能景观，获得代表性构象体；随后以这些构象体为起点，通过第一性原理与连续溶剂模型计算质子化学位移，并按构象布居数加权得到理论谱图，与实验 CDCl₃ 谱图直接比较。

Result: 结果显示溶剂环境显著影响构象分布：在水中仅恢复出单一主导构象盆地（dominant basin），而在氯仿中则分辨出两个构象态（conformational states）。基于这些构象体得到的布居加权谱图被用于与实验 CDCl₃ 谱图对照。

Conclusion: 结论上，该工作将 REMD 构象采样与第一性原理化学位移计算相结合，并以实验 NMR 数据作为基准，展示了溶剂依赖的构象景观及其对谱图预测的影响。

Abstract: We use replica-exchange molecular dynamics (REMD) to map the conformational free-energy landscape of the macrocyclic drug lorlatinib in explicit water and chloroform. In water a single dominant basin is recovered; in chloroform two conformational states are resolved. We use these conformers as the starting point to calculate proton chemical shifts from first principles and a continuum solvent model. The resulting population-weighted spectrum is compared directly to the experimental CDCl$_3$ spectrum, benchmarking the computational approach against measured NMR data.

</details>


### [31] [Transport of Deformable Vesicles Driven by Chiral Active Brownian Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18743)
*Dipak Patra,Anil Kumar Dasanna*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过二维定面积囊泡（vesicle）内手性活性粒子（chiral active particles）的数值模拟，揭示手性（chirality）与形变耦合产生多种集体运动模式，并发现囊泡转速对手性呈非单调依赖，存在最优手性；建立解析理论给出标度不变（scale-invariant）关系并实现数据坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性物质（active matter）产生的机械应力可形变其周围环境，是远离平衡态（far from equilibrium）输运与形貌动力学的途径；含活性粒子的可形变囊泡提供了活性应力与边界力学直接耦合的最小模型。

Method: 采用数值模拟研究二维、定面积囊泡内填充手性活性粒子，粒子间通过排除体积（excluded volume）与极性取向（polar alignment）相互作用；并发展解析理论，将囊泡旋转与手性活性粒子产生的有效力矩（effective torque）相关联。

Result: 手性与囊泡形变的耦合产生多种集体运动模式，如类“跑-停”（run-and-tumble-like）迁移、转子式（rotor-like）动力学与持续自旋（persistent spinning）。囊泡旋转对手性非单调：固定活性下存在使转速最大化的最优手性；理论揭示了该最优手性背后的竞争机制，并预测转速对活性与手性的标度不变依赖，可将不同活性强度下的模拟结果坍缩到一条普适曲线（universal curve）。

Conclusion: 结果确立了手性与集体取向（collective alignment）调控活性应力向可形变边界传递的一般机制，为理解受限活性系统（confined active systems）中的输运与旋转动力学提供框架。

Abstract: Active matter systems can generate mechanical stresses that deform their surroundings, providing a route to transport and shape dynamics far from equilibrium. Deformable vesicles containing active particles offer a minimal setting in which such active stresses are directly coupled to boundary mechanics. Here, we use numerical simulations to investigate a two-dimensional, fixed-area vesicle filled with chiral active particles interacting through excluded volume and polar alignment. We find that the coupling between particle chirality and vesicle deformation produces distinct modes of collective motion, including run-and-tumble-like migration, rotor-like dynamics, and persistent spinning. Most notably, we show that vesicle rotation is non-monotonic in chirality: at fixed activity, an optimal chirality maximizes the rotational velocity. We develop an analytical theory that relates the vesicle rotation to the effective torque generated by the chiral active particles. The theory identifies the competition underlying the optimal chirality and predicts a scale-invariant dependence of the rotational velocity on activity and chirality. This scaling collapses simulation results obtained over different activity strengths onto a universal curve. Our results establish a general mechanism by which chirality and collective alignment regulate the transmission of active stresses to deformable boundaries, providing a framework for understanding transport and rotational dynamics in confined active systems.

</details>


### [32] [Dense HeLa cell monolayers remain liquid-like despite strong crowding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18768)
*Suravi Pal,Nen Saito,Takeshi Kawasaki,Hiroyuki Ebata*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 结合 HeLa 单层细胞体外延时成像与可变形活性细胞模型模拟，研究密度与运动性对集体弛豫的调控，表明未处理条件下致密 HeLa 单层保持类液态并能持续结构弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密细胞单层的集体动力学（collective dynamics）由拥挤（crowding）与细胞运动性（cellular motility）共同决定；密度升高可减慢细胞运动并促成类玻璃行为（glass-like behaviour），但致密 HeLa 单层的动力学状态尚不明确。

Method: 采用体外延时成像（in vitro time-lapse imaging）观测 HeLa 细胞单层，并配合可变形活性细胞模型（deformable active-cell model）的模拟，考察细胞密度与运动性如何调控集体弛豫（collective relaxation）。

Result: 在实验可达的密度与时间范围内，未处理的 HeLa 单层保持类液态（liquid-like）：结构弛豫（structural relaxation）随密度增加而逐渐减慢，但始终可观测；低营养条件下细胞运动性显著降低，结构弛豫明显变慢。模拟定性重现了结构弛豫时间随密度上升的趋势，并显示降低自驱动（self-propulsion）会在高堆积分数下促进长寿命笼效应动力学（long-lived caging dynamics）。

Conclusion: 致密 HeLa 单层在未处理条件下可维持缓慢、异质（heterogeneous）但仍在弛豫的集体动力学；持续的细胞运动性是高密度下维持结构弛豫的重要因素，或有助于理解癌细胞的转移潜能（metastatic potential）。

Abstract: Collective dynamics in dense cell monolayers are governed by the interplay between crowding and cellular motility. Although increasing density can slow cellular motion and promote glass-like behaviour, the dynamical state of dense HeLa monolayers remains unclear. Here, we combine in vitro time-lapse imaging of HeLa cell monolayers with simulations of a deformable active-cell model to examine how cell density and motility regulate collective relaxation. Within the experimentally accessible density and time ranges, untreated HeLa monolayers remain liquid-like: structural relaxation progressively slows down with increasing density but remains observable throughout the investigated range. Under low-nutrient conditions, cell motility is strongly reduced, and structural relaxation becomes substantially slower. To elucidate the mechanisms underlying these experimental observations, we further performed simulations using a deformable-cell model. The model qualitatively reproduces the density-dependent increase in structural relaxation time and further shows that reducing self-propulsion promotes long-lived caging dynamics at high packing fractions. These results show that dense HeLa monolayers can sustain slow, heterogeneous, yet relaxing collective dynamics under untreated conditions, and indicate that persistent cellular motility is an important factor in maintaining structural relaxation at high density, which may provide insight into the metastatic potential of cancer cells.

</details>


### [33] [Geometry-Controlled Relaxation Spectra in Viscoelastic Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18926)
*Niloyendu Roy,Rupayan Saha,Debankur Das,Matthias Krüger,Clemens Bechinger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究证明，宽弛豫谱（relaxation spectrum）未必源于材料微观复杂性，也可由机械激励的几何构型（geometry）本身产生：在驱动胶体二聚体中，扭转驱动将距驱动对象的距离转化为弛豫时间，形成几何控制的弛豫模式层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软材料（soft materials）的弛豫过程常跨越多个时间量级，此类弛豫谱被广泛用于推断内部动力学，并通常被视为微观复杂性、无序或非均匀性的指纹（fingerprints）。本文质疑这一常规解读，探讨几何因素本身能否生成宽弛豫谱。

Method: 在具有约一秒主导体相弛豫时间的蠕虫状胶束（wormlike micellar）流体中，对胶体二聚体（colloidal dimers）实施旋转驱动，并结合粒子测速（particle velocimetry）与连续介质壳层模型（continuum shell model）分析其回弹动力学及受限条件下的行为。

Result: 扭转驱动将距驱动对象的距离转化为弛豫时间，产生几何控制的弛豫模式层级：取向回弹（orientational recoils）可持续数百秒，并在相当长的时间内记录既往扭矩加载历史。粒子测速显示角动量（angular-momentum）在远离探针处快速输运，与所存储扭转应力的缓慢弛豫形成对比；壳层模型可再现观测到的回弹动力学及空间受限下长寿命贡献的选择性抑制。

Conclusion: 几何可将具有简单本征弛豫的材料转变为具备长寿命、空间依赖记忆（memory）的系统，提示可通过机械激励而非材料组分来调控材料动力学，并对微观机械记忆元件（microscopic mechanical memory elements）具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Soft materials store, dissipate and release mechanical stresses through relaxation processes that often span many orders of magnitude in time. Such relaxation spectra are widely used to infer internal material dynamics and are usually regarded as fingerprints of microscopic complexity, disorder, or heterogeneity. Here we show that a broad relaxation spectrum can instead be generated by the geometry of mechanical excitation itself. Using rotationally driven colloidal dimers in a wormlike micellar fluid with a dominant bulk relaxation time of order one second, we demonstrate that torsional driving converts distance from the driven object into relaxation time. This produces a geometry-controlled hierarchy of relaxation modes: orientational recoils persist for hundreds of seconds and encode past torque protocols over comparably long times. Particle velocimetry reveals rapid angular-momentum transport away from the probe, in contrast to the slow relaxation of stored torsional stress. A continuum shell model captures the observed recoil dynamics and the selective suppression of long-lived contributions under spatial confinement. Our results show that geometry can transform a material with simple intrinsic relaxation into a system with long-lived, space-dependent memory, suggesting a route to tune material dynamics through mechanical excitation rather than composition, with potential implications for microscopic mechanical memory elements.

</details>

### [34] [Machine learning kinetics from molecular dynamics data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17736)
*Jonathan Weare,Aaron R. Dinner*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了从分子模拟中估计 committor（提交概率）及相关动力学统计量的现代方法，重点介绍无需标注射击数据的自监督学习路径，并以统一的算子视角连接多种理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数分子转变（molecular transitions）的时间尺度远超直接分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations）的可及范围，因此需要有效的动力学统计量。Committor 作为核心动力学统计量，可提供与机制无关的反应坐标（reaction coordinate），并为过渡路径理论（transition path theory）与速率计算奠定基础。

Method: 综述采用统一的算子视角（operator viewpoint），将基于生成元（generator）的偏微分方程、变分原理（variational principles）、马尔可夫状态模型（Markov state models）、动力学伽辽金近似（dynamical Galerkin approximation）与神经网络（neural networks）相互关联。重点考察自监督方法（self-supervised methods），即学习其定义性动力学方程的解，而非依赖带标签的 shooting 数据。

Result: 经验与理论证据均表明这些方法具有较高效率。综述还给出在应用中充分发挥其潜力的理论与实践指导，包括处理非马尔可夫效应（non-Markovian effects）与采样（sampling）的策略。

Conclusion: 文章指出了未来研究方向，包括与强化学习（reinforcement learning）和生成建模（generative modeling）的关联。

Abstract: Most molecular transitions occur on timescales far beyond direct molecular dynamics simulations. The committor, the probability that a configuration reaches a product state before a reactant state, is a central kinetic statistic, providing a mechanism-independent reaction coordinate and a foundation for transition path theory and the calculation of rates. This review surveys modern approaches for estimating the committor and related kinetic statistics from molecular simulations, with an emphasis on self-supervised methods that learn solutions of their defining dynamical equations rather than relying on labeled shooting data. We develop a common operator viewpoint connecting generator-based partial differential equations, variational principles, Markov state models, dynamical Galerkin approximation, and neural networks. Empirical and theoretical evidence points to the efficiency of these methods. We provide theoretical and practical guidance for realizing their full potential in applications, including strategies for treating non-Markovian effects and for sampling. We conclude by identifying opportunities for further research, including connections to reinforcement learning and generative modeling.

</details>

### [35] [What does "instant thermalization" in large-$q$ SYK models mean?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17794)
*Alexander Osterkorn,Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究 $q/2$-body 相互作用 SYK 模型中的热化（thermalization）速率，通过淬火（quench）后有效温度的计算发现其仍为普朗克式（Planckian）$\\Gamma \\sim T q^{-1}$，而非此前猜测的发散形式 $\\Gamma \\sim Tq$。作者指出“瞬时热化（instantaneous thermalization）”仅对因果格林函数（causal Green's function）的双时间平面对角块 $t_1,t_2>0$ 成立，非对角块在任何 $q$ 下均非热化，从而给出有限速率的物理来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二体相互作用 SYK 模型中观测到的普朗克热化速率 $\\Gamma \\sim T$ 促使作者考察 $q/2$-body 情形。既有研究在 $1/q$ 领头阶建立了“瞬时热化”概念，并猜测速率可能仍为普朗克式但形式为发散的 $\\Gamma \\sim T q$。本文旨在通过一个可解析且可数值处理的系统检验该猜测。

Method: 针对一个解析与数值均可处理的系统，计算 $t=0$ 时刻淬火（quench）之后的有效温度，并分析因果格林函数 $\\mathcal{G}(t_1,t_2)$ 在双时间平面各区块的行为，同时直接考察非热关联（non-thermal correlations）的时间依赖。

Result: 淬火后有效温度确实呈普朗克式，但速率表现为衰减形式 $\\Gamma \\sim T q^{-1}$，与此前 $\\Gamma \\sim Tq$ 的猜测不同。因果格林函数在 $t_1,t_2>0$ 区块中立即获得热形式，而 $t_1\\cdot t_2<0$ 的非对角区块在任何 $q$ 下本质非热。

Conclusion: 瞬时热化仅对 $t_1,t_2>0$ 区块具有意义。由于有效温度来自跨越所有区块的关联，其速率由非对角区块决定而保持有限，这为观测到的 $\\Gamma \\sim T q^{-1}$ 提供了物理图像。

Abstract: Motivated by the Planckian thermalization rate $Γ\sim T$ observed in the two-body interacting SYK model, we study thermalization in the $q/2$-body case. Previous studies of such systems have established the notion of instantaneous thermalization to leading order in $1/q$. It was conjectured that the thermalization may still be Planckian but with a divergent rate $Γ\sim T q$, explaining the ``instantaneous'' part. For an analytically and numerically tractable system, we calculate the effective temperatures after a quench at time $t = 0$ and indeed find a Planckian rate, albeit with the unexpected decaying behavior $Γ\sim T q^{-1} $. This is contrasted with the behavior of the causal Green's function $\mathcal{G}(t_1, t_2)$, which instantly acquires a thermal form in the two-time plane block $t_1, t_2 > 0$.
  The resulting picture is that instant thermalization is a meaningful concept only for this block, while the off-diagonal blocks $t_1 \cdot t_2 < 0$ are inherently non-thermal at any $q$. Since the effective temperature is obtained from correlations spanning all blocks, it inherits a finite rate set by the off-diagonal. We illustrate this directly by studying the non-thermal correlations' time dependence.

</details>


### [36] [Assessing the Reliability of Anomalous Hall Conductivity Extraction in GdAlSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17872)
*Anil Kumar,Debapratim Pal,Sudhan Koirala,Muhammad Adnan,Youngsang,Ji,Prakash Regmi,Bailey S. Bouley,Ludi Miao,Yun Suk Eo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究以 SrRuO$_3$ 与 GdAlSi 为例，比较提取反常霍尔电导率（anomalous Hall conductivity）的不同方法，指出在普通霍尔背景不明确时，方法选择会导致截然不同的结果，并给出实用警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子材料（quantum materials）中提取超出普通霍尔响应（ordinary Hall）的额外贡献时，缺乏统一、可靠的方法标准。作者希望厘清不同处理方式对反常霍尔电导率结果的影响，并为相关研究提供实践警告。

Method: 对比两类材料体系：SrRuO$_3$ 中普通霍尔背景明确、磁电导（magnetoconductance）较小，分别在电阻率-电导率转换前后扣除普通霍尔贡献；GdAlSi 中横向响应无清晰饱和、低场与高场区域均近似线性，考察将其不同线性区解释为普通霍尔背景，以及纵向电阻处理方式的影响。此外，检验非线性霍尔响应能否在不引入反常贡献的普通霍尔框架内描述。

Result: SrRuO$_3$ 中，转换前后扣除普通霍尔贡献得到的反常霍尔电导率几乎一致；GdAlSi 中，不同线性区被当作普通霍尔背景、以及电阻率到电导率转换中纵向电阻的处理差异，会产生完全不同的反常霍尔电导率估计值。

Conclusion: 在普通霍尔背景定义不清的材料中，反常霍尔电导率的提取结果高度依赖数据处理选择。该分析为量子材料中提取过量霍尔贡献（excess Hall contributions）提供了实际操作的警示。

Abstract: We examine the different methods of extracting the anomalous Hall conductivity using SrRuO$_3$ and GdAlSi. For SrRuO$_3$, where the ordinary Hall background is well defined and the magnetoconductance is small, subtracting the ordinary Hall contribution either before or after conversion from resistivity to conductivity yields nearly identical anomalous Hall conductivities. In GdAlSi, in contrast, the transverse response does not exhibit clear saturation, and both low- and high-field regions can appear approximately linear. We show that different interpretations of these linear regions as the ordinary Hall background, together with the treatment of the measured longitudinal resistance in the resistivity-to-conductivity conversion, can produce completely different estimates of the anomalous Hall conductivity. We also examine whether the nonlinear Hall response can instead be described within an ordinary Hall framework without invoking an anomalous contribution. Our analysis provides practical cautions for extracting excess Hall contributions in quantum materials.

</details>


### [37] [Optical investigation of the electronic structure of a ferromagnetic Weyl semimetal CeAlSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18592)
*Shin-ichi Kimura,Yue Pan,Hiroshi Watanabe,Akimitsu Kirikoshi,Junya Otsuki,Hiroshi Tanida*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过变温光电导谱与 DFT+DMFT 计算，研究了磁性外尔半金属 CeAlSi 铁磁转变过程中的电子态，发现 Ce 4f 态近乎局域、与 Weyl 费米子相互作用极弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究磁性外尔半金属（magnetic Weyl semimetal）CeAlSi 在铁磁转变过程中电子态的行为，尤其是 Ce 4f 态与 Weyl 费米子（Weyl fermions）之间的相互作用。

Method: 测量温度依赖的光电导率（optical conductivity）谱 $σ_1(ω)$，并与密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）能带计算进行对比；同时用扩展 Drude 模型（extended Drude model）评估动态有效质量。

Result: $σ_1(ω)$ 谱在铁磁有序温度（$T_C$）前后无明显变化；近乎局域的 Ce 4f 态的 DFT+DMFT 计算成功再现谱形及跨 $T_C$ 不变的光谱；动态有效质量很小且被计算再现，而更低温度下的散射概率暗示存在铁磁涨落（ferromagnetic fluctuations）。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Weyl 费米子与 Ce 4f 态之间的相互作用强度非常弱，这一结论由 DFT+DMFT 计算所再现，说明 Ce 4f 态近乎局域。

Abstract: To investigate electronic states during the ferromagnetic transition in a magnetic Weyl semimetal CeAlSi, we measured temperature-dependent optical conductivity [$σ_1(ω)$] spectra and compared them with DFT+DMFT band calculations. The $σ_1(ω)$ spectrum did not change significantly across the ferromagnetic ordering temperature ($T_C$), suggesting that the Ce 4f states are almost localized. DFT+DMFT calculations with almost localized Ce 4f states successfully reproduced the spectral shape and the unchanged $σ_1(ω)$ spectra across $T_C$. The dynamic effective mass evaluated from the extended Drude model is very small, which DFT+DMFT calculations also reproduce, but the scattering probability at even lower temperatures suggests ferromagnetic fluctuations. These results suggest that the interaction intensity between the Weyl fermions and Ce 4f states is very weak, as reproduced by DFT+DMFT calculations.

</details>


### [38] [Orbital-Induced Peierls Transitions: How Orbitals Orchestrate Lattice Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18614)
*T. Mizokawa,S. V. Streltsov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该综述讨论由轨道自由度（orbital degrees of freedom）诱导的派尔斯转变（Peierls transition），指出该不稳定性并非一维材料专属，而可在更高维度中出现，并系统梳理其物理机制、相关材料及高温未畸变相特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 派尔斯转变通常被视为一维（1D）材料固有现象，但轨道自由度可在更高维度中引发同类不稳定性，这一现象缺乏系统梳理，故有必要综述其机制与材料表现。

Method: 采用综述（review）形式，从物理机制出发，分析两种主要途径：p、d 轨道的各向异性形状导致电子谱的有效“一维化（1D-zation）”；以及轨道自由度通过局域或能带 Jahn-Teller 效应（local or band Jahn-Teller effect）移动能带、解除能带简并，从而影响费米面嵌套（Fermi surface nesting）。同时考察配体八面体共享边与共享面两种几何构型下的发生频率。

Result: 轨道诱导的派尔斯效应最常见于过渡金属周围配体八面体（ligand octahedra）共享边的情形，在共享面（face-sharing）几何中较少出现。综述还涉及发生该现象的材料、高温未畸变相（high-temperature undistorted phase）的特征，以及分子轨道（molecular orbitals）形成等局域效应。

Conclusion: 轨道自由度是超越一维限制、诱导派尔斯不稳定性的关键因素，其机制涉及能谱有效一维化与能带简并解除对费米面嵌套的调控，相关讨论涵盖材料、高温相特征与局域效应。

Abstract: The Peierls transition is typically regarded as a phenomenon inherent to one-dimensional (1D) materials. However, orbital degrees of freedom can induce this instability even in higher dimensions. Two mechanisms are primarily responsible. First, the anisotropic shape of $p$ and $d$ orbitals can lead to effective "1D-zation" of the electronic spectrum. Second, orbital degrees of freedom can lift band degeneracy by shifting bands relative to each other via the local or band Jahn-Teller effect, thereby affecting the nesting of the Fermi surface. The orbital-induced Peierls effect is most commonly observed when ligand octahedra surrounding transition metals share edges, and less frequently in face-sharing geometries. In this review, we discuss the underlying physical mechanisms, the materials in which this phenomenon occurs, the characteristics of the high-temperature undistorted phase, and the role of local effects such as the formation of molecular orbitals.

</details>


### [39] [Electronic correlations shape the low-energy optical response of the kagome antiferromagnets Mn$_3$Sn and Mn$_3$Ge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18744)
*R. Mathew Roy,Bo Tai,Maxim Wenzel,Achyut Tiwari,Mykhaylo Ozerov,Chandra Shekhar,Claudia Felser,Artem V. Pronin,Xiaolong Feng,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合光谱学与密度泛函理论，本文证明电子-电子相互作用显著影响笼目反铁磁体 Mn₃Sn 的低能光学性质，并给出 Mn₃Sn 与 Mn₃Ge 的强关联图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Mn₃Sn 这一拓扑金属，需澄清其低能光学响应究竟由关联修正的能带还是拓扑（Weyl）特征主导，并解释其与同构 Mn₃Ge 的性质差异。

Method: 采用光学光谱（optical spectroscopy）与密度泛函理论（DFT）相结合，并施加 Lichtenstein 形式的 DFT+U（U = 4 eV，Hund 耦合 J = 0.25 eV）；同时测量磁场（最高 17 T）下的光学响应。

Abstract: We establish a general relation between symmetries of gravitational theories and black hole (BH) quasinormal mode (QNM) spectra. Our results show that these seemingly contradictory conclusions result from differences in the perturbation dynamics or in the boundary conditions.

Result: 结果表明，界限破缺取决于局部轨道不稳定性尺度与表面引力尺度之间的竞争；背景变化可同时影响两个尺度并在表面引力被更强压制时导致破缺，而固定背景下表面引力不变，阈值跨越由粒子参数引起的不稳定轨道动力学变化驱动。粒子参数中总角动量（total angular momentum）主导局部径向不稳定性，自旋（spin）与电荷（charge）进一步修正不稳定性强度并移动破缺阈值。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the chaos bound through the local radial instability of charged spinning test particles on unstable circular orbits in the black-bounce--Kerr--Newman spacetime. Our results show that violation of the bound is governed by the competition between the local orbital-instability scale and the surface gravity scale, with variations of the black hole background and the particle dynamics providing two complementary routes to the same threshold crossing. Background variations affect both scales and can drive the system into the bound-violating regime when the surface gravity is suppressed more strongly than the orbital instability. At fixed background, by contrast, the surface gravity remains unchanged, and the transition across the threshold is driven by changes in the unstable orbit dynamics induced by the particle parameters. The background-controlled and probe-dynamics-controlled routes therefore represent two realizations of a unified horizon--orbit scale competition rather than independent mechanisms. Among the particle parameters considered, the total angular momentum plays the leading role in controlling the local radial instability, while the spin and charge further modify the instability strength and shift the threshold for chaos bound violation.

From the unstable photon region, we construct the exact vacuum critical curve and analyze its area equivalent diameter and displacement, including comparisons with M87$^\ast$ and Sgr~A$^\ast$ within the asymptotically admissible domain. The quadrupolar equations develop an internal regular singular point, which is handled by selecting the regular local solution through a Frobenius analysis. The rotation-induced response of the throat source is incorporated consistently through the junction conditions. Independent shooting and collocation calculations of the quadrupolar sector give mutually consistent solutions. These results provide a self-consistent second-order description of rotating Einstein--scalar--Gauss--Bonnet wormholes.

It is found that the MOG parameter mainly enlarges and brightens higher-order photon rings and broadens the shadow, while the inclination angle breaks axial symmetry and enhances polarized emission inside the shadow. We find that the photon sphere and shadow radii decrease with increasing charge and increase with black hole mass, while NLED effects become significant mainly near extremality. By confronting the dimensionless shadow radius with the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) bounds from Sgr A* and M87*, we derive phenomenological upper limits on the charge, with Sgr A* providing the stronger constraint. We further show that, for fixed impact parameter and charge, the deflection angle is systematically larger than in the corresponding Reissner-Nordström spacetime, and that the critical impact parameter is shifted to larger values. Our results show that NLED corrections produce observable modifications in the optical properties of charged black holes, especially close to extremality.

Result: 分析确定了模型的所有渐近行为与容许的宇宙学时期，给出了各不动点作为自由参数函数的存在性与稳定性条件，并刻画了该对称性驱动的 $k$-essence 模型的完整渐近宇宙历史结构。同时探讨了模型描述宇宙加速（cosmic acceleration）的条件。
