Abstract: We investigate a family of $k$-essence cosmological models selected by the requirement that the field equations admit a nontrivial variational symmetry. For the $k$-essence theory with Lagrangian function $F(R,X,φ)=f_{1}R+f_{2}(φ,X)$, where $f_{2}(φ,X)=αX+β\exp\left((n-1)φ\right)X^{n}$, the existence of a scaling symmetry introduces a conservation law which we use to obtain an exact vacuum solution. We generalize our analysis and investigate the structure of the phase-space of the cosmological field equations by performing a dynamical systems analysis, both in the absence and the presence of a pressureless matter source. We employ Hubble-normalized variables and the Poincaré compactification, so as to determine the asymptotic behavior of the model and identify all admissible cosmological epochs, from early-time to late-time behavior. We discuss in detail the existence and stability conditions of each fixed point as functions of the free parameters, and characterize the full asymptotic structure of the cosmological history as provided by this symmetry-driven $k$-essence model. We explore the conditions under which the model can describe cosmic acceleration. We find that the symmetry driven $k$-essence model can describe the inflationary epoch with a natural exit to the matter dominated era, where the $k$-essence mimics the pressureless matter component.

</details>

Abstract: Antispacetime arises naturally in the vielbein formulation of general relativity yet remains invisible to probes coupling only to the metric. We show that domain walls separating spacetime from antispacetime constitute physically distinct and observable structures. Fermions interacting with such walls exhibit striking signatures: scattering induces particle-hole conversion, reflecting tetrad orientation reversal, while the wall hosts localized zero-energy Majorana and chiral modes. In 1+1 dimensions, these modes acquire a pseudoscalar mass term tied to topology and quantum anomalies, providing a direct link to the observability of $θ$ vacua. These results identify fermionic response as a definitive probe of antispacetime and establish its domain walls as physically detectable.

</details>

### [69] [From Nuclear Many-Body Correlations to Energy Detector Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18754)

TL;DR: 该文提出将相对论核碰撞中的末态多粒子关联与初态核关联表述为一个系统性可改进的匹配问题。作者以渐近能量流（asymptotic energy flow）构造探测器关联子（detector correlators），在领头阶恢复线性流体力学关系，并将角度投影映射到碰撞核内的多极算符关联子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论核碰撞为研究核基态中的多体关联（many-body correlations）提供了实验平台，但初态关联与对撞机测得的末态多粒子观测量之间的联系尚未被形式化为系统性可改进的匹配问题。该工作旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将渐近能量流表达为早期应力张量（stress tensor）的泛函，并按适当模式展开，以在领头阶（leading order）恢复熟悉的线性流体力学（linear hydrodynamic）关系。进而受小-$x$ QCD 启发，把探测器关联子的角度投影映射为在碰撞核中计算的多极算符关联子（multipole-operator correlators）。

Result: 由渐近能量流构建的探测器关联子被证明为实现此类构造的自然框架；模式展开在领头阶重现线性流体力学关系；角度投影与核内多极算符关联子建立对应。

Conclusion: 该文为高能强子碰撞中入射与出射 QCD 态之间长波长关联（long-wavelength correlations）的匹配建立了系统性形式化框架。

Abstract: Relativistic nuclear collisions have opened an experimental arena for studying many-body correlations in nuclear ground states. However, the connection between initial-state correlations and final-state multi-particle observables measured at colliders is not yet formulated as a systematically improvable matching problem. We show that detector correlators built from asymptotic energy flows provide a natural framework for realizing such a construction. In particular, we express the asymptotic energy flow as a functional of the early-time stress tensor, and expand it in suitable modes to recover the familiar linear hydrodynamic relations at leading order. Then, motivated by small-$x$ QCD, we map angular projections of detector correlators to multipole-operator correlators computed in the colliding nuclei. We thus establish a systematic formalism for matching long-wavelength correlations between incoming and outgoing QCD states in high-energy hadronic collisions.

</details>

### [70] [Timelike Ollivier-Ricci curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18664)
*Mathias Braun,Xue-Mei Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文在洛伦兹几何（Lorentzian geometry）中引入一种余维度为一（codimension one）的粗里奇曲率（coarse Ricci curvature）构造，通过比较小类空超曲面（spacelike hypersurface）上的概率测度，在渐近极限下恢复未来指向单位类时方向（timelike direction）的里奇曲率，并进一步推广至带权情形下的类时 Bakry-Émery 张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将度量测度空间中的粗里奇曲率思想推广到洛伦兹（Lorentzian）框架，并建立其与广义相对论中刻画体积膨胀演化的 Raychaudhuri 方程之间的联系。

Method: 采用 $1$-Lorentz-Wasserstein 距离（$\ell_1$）比较支撑于小类空超曲面上的概率测度，借助具有受控位移的传输映射（transport map），并通过 Moser 型流（Moser-type flow）构造该映射；再通过将类空切片涂抹为细类时管并校准时间与空间尺度进行推广。

Result: 在精确的渐近机制下恢复未来指向单位类时方向的周围里奇曲率，并给出反映该余维度为一构造的普适维度前因子；对光滑权重在首阶不敏感；经涂抹与尺度校准后，可恢复与加权参考测度 $\mathfrak{m}=\mathrm{e}^{-V}\,\mathrm{vol}_g$ 相关的类时 Bakry-Émery 张量 $\mathrm{Ric}+\mathrm{Hess}\,V$。

Conclusion: 该构造为洛伦兹几何提供了一个余维度为一的粗里奇曲率框架，与 Raychaudhuri 方程相呼应，并可经由切片涂抹自然延拓至带权（Bakry-Émery）情形。

Abstract: We introduce a codimension one construction of coarse Ricci curvature in Lorentzian geometry. Using the $1$-Lorentz-Wasserstein distance $\ell_1$, we compare probability measures supported on small spacelike hypersurfaces through nearby events and recover, in a precise asymptotic regime, the ambient Ricci curvature in future-directed unit timelike directions, with a universal dimensional prefactor reflecting this codimension one construction.
  The construction echoes the Raychaudhuri equation, which relates the evolution of spatial volume expansion along timelike geodesics to Ricci curvature. Our quantitative estimates rely on transport maps with controlled displacement, constructed through a Moser-type flow.
  At leading order, the slice construction is insensitive to smooth weights. By smearing the spacelike slices into thin timelike tubes and calibrating the temporal and spatial scales, we recover the timelike Bakry-Émery tensor $\mathrm{Ric}+\mathrm{Hess}\,V$ associated with the weighted reference measure $\smash{\mathfrak{m}=\mathrm{e}^{-V}\,\mathrm{vol}_g}$.

</details>

### [71] [Monge's transport problem in synthetic Lorentzian spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17592)
*Afiny Akdemir*

Main category: math.MG

TL;DR: 该论文在满足前向类时测度收缩性质 $TMCP^+(K,N)$ 的合成洛伦兹时空（synthetic Lorentzian spacetimes）上，证明了洛伦兹蒙日问题（Lorentzian Monge problem）解的存在性，其核心工具为针状分解（needle decomposition）技术，并借此建立 $TMCP^+(K,N)$ 与 $TMCP^+_{rLip}(K,N)$ 的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹蒙日问题旨在刻画以时间分离函数（time separation function）为代价的最优传输。作者希望在合成洛伦兹时空框架下证明其解的存在性，从而将黎曼情形的最优传输理论推广至洛伦兹几何设定。

Method: 方法基于针状分解（needle decomposition）技术，将全维问题约化为若干一维对应问题（one-dimensional counterparts）加以处理。代价函数取时间分离函数 $\ell(x,y)$（即洛伦兹距离，Lorentz distance）。

Result: 在满足前向类时测度收缩性质 $TMCP^+(K,N)$ 的合成洛伦兹时空上，证明了洛伦兹蒙日问题解的存在性。作为针状分解的应用，进一步证明了 $TMCP^+(K,N)$ 与 $TMCP^+_{rLip}(K,N)$ 条件相互等价，其中后者定义在反向 1-Lipschitz 函数（reverse 1-Lipschitz functions）的梯度流曲线（gradient flow curves）上。

Conclusion: 研究表明针状分解是解决洛伦兹蒙日问题的有效途径，并揭示两类曲率—维数条件（curvature dimension conditions）的等价性，为合成洛伦兹时空中的最优传输与曲率分析建立了统一框架。

Abstract: We prove the existence of solutions to the Lorentzian Monge problem on synthetic Lorentzian spacetimes that satisfy the forward timelike measure contraction property $TMCP^+(K,N)$. The cost for this problem is the time separation function $\ell(x,y)$ (the Lorentz distance), which represents the maximum amount a particle can age when traveling from $x$ to $y$. The solution is based on the needle decomposition technique, and amounts to a reduction of the full problem to its one-dimensional counterparts. As an application of needle decomposition, we show that the Lorentzian timelike measure contraction property $TMCP^+(K,N)$ is equivalent to the $TMCP^+_{rLip}(K,N)$ condition, where the curvature dimension conditions are defined on gradient flow curves of reverse 1-Lipschitz functions.

</details>

### [72] [On the importance of cosmic-ray background in the Atomki anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18383)
*Hicham Benmansour,Gianluigi Boca,Gianluca Cavoto,Marco Chiappini,Elia G. Grandoni,Luca Galli,Giovanni Gallucci,Angela Papa,Francesco Renga,Antoine Venturini,Cecilia Voena*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 基于Geant4模拟，宇宙线μ子（cosmic-ray muon）在Atomki五臂与六臂e+e-对谱仪几何中产生的符合事件，可在与17 MeV玻色子（boson）信号相似的张角与能量位置形成显著过剩，其宇宙线与本征对转换（IPC）事件比在信号窗内可超过1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Atomki团队曾报告在8Be与4He跃迁中存在约17 MeV玻色子的异常信号，但其宇宙线（cosmic-ray）本底处理是否充分尚不明确。本文旨在检验宇宙线μ子能否在相同几何与选择条件下重现这些异常特征。

Method: 采用Geant4对Debrecen的Atomki五臂与六臂e+e-对谱仪进行完整几何建模，包含位置灵敏探测器（position-sensitive detector）与塑料闪烁体（plastic scintillator），并模拟宇宙线μ子在两种构型下的符合响应。

Result: 六臂谱仪中，闪烁体能量和处于8Be窗（16-20 MeV）时张角分布约140°处出现峰，且对能量不对称对受抑制；4He窗（18-22 MeV）出现约120°的独立峰，而相应本底能量区无类似增强。五臂构型在8Be能量下同样重现140°过剩；按典型Atomki运行条件归一化后，信号窗内宇宙线与IPC之比大于1。

Conclusion: 上述角度、不对称依赖性及本底特征与Atomki报告为17 MeV玻色子证据的行为相似，但该结果并未判定Atomki过剩的起源，而是强调此类测量中稳健宇宙线处理的必要性，并呼吁对束流关闭（beam-off）研究给出详细描述。

Abstract: We report Geant4-based simulations of cosmic-ray muon backgrounds in models reproducing the geometries of the five-arm and six-arm e+e- pair spectrometers operated by the Atomki group at Debrecen. Full detector geometries are implemented for both setups, including position-sensitive detectors and plastic scintillators. In both configurations, cosmic muons generating two-arm coincidences produce statistically significant excesses in the opening-angle distribution whose positions are determined by the discrete azimuthal arrangement of detector arms and the plastic scintillators dimensions: in the six-arm spectrometer a peak appears near 140 degrees when the scintillator energy sum is selected in the 8Be transition window (16-20 MeV) and is suppressed for energy-asymmetric pairs, while a distinct peak near 120 degrees emerges in the 4He window (18-22 MeV), with no comparable enhancement in the respective background energy region. These angles, asymmetry dependences, and background characteristics show behaviours similar to those reported by the Atomki group as evidence for a 17 MeV boson. For the five-arm setup, a 140-degree excess is also reproduced at 8Be energies, and normalizing internal pair conversion events and cosmic coincidences to typical Atomki running conditions yields a cosmic-to-IPC ratio above unity in the signal window, indicating that the cosmic background is a leading contribution to the event rate at the energies and angles of interest. While they do not settle the question of the origin of the Atomki excesses, these results highlight the critical importance of a robust cosmic-ray treatment in this type of measurements, and call for a detailed description of the beam-off studies supporting the search for anomalies in the opening-angle vs. energy-sum distribution around the signal region.

</details>

### [73] [Robust and Efficient AI Frameworks for Scalable Material Design and Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17646)
*Kishalay Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本论文围绕晶体材料发现的两个关键阶段——性质预测与结构生成——构建了统一的 AI 框架，通过图表示学习、预训练、多模态学习与生成建模，缓解密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）计算成本高与标注数据稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料设计流程中，密度泛函理论（Density Functional Theory, DFT）计算成本高昂，且带标签的材料数据有限，制约了可扩展的材料发现。论文因此探索图表示学习（graph representation learning）、预训练（pretraining）、多模态学习（multimodal learning）与生成建模（generative modeling）等方向以降低对标注数据的依赖。

Method: 在性质预测方面，依次提出 CrysXPP（无监督图自编码实现可迁移晶体表示）、CrysGNN（大规模自监督图预训练，融合原子连接性、化学属性与全局结构信息，并通过知识蒸馏迁移至下游预测器）以及 CrysMMNet（联合建模图结构与文本描述的多模态表示）。在结构生成方面，提出 TGDMat，一种文本引导的联合扩散（diffusion）框架，同时建模晶格参数、原子类型与原子坐标，并在去噪过程中引入文本结构知识。

Result: CrysXPP 降低了晶体表示学习对大规模性质标注数据集的依赖；CrysGNN 将多源结构知识迁移至下游性质预测任务；CrysMMNet 通过融合局部化学与全局结构知识丰富晶体表示。TGDMat 能够生成更有效、更稳定的周期性材料，并支持由自然语言描述驱动的条件生成（conditional generation）。

Conclusion: 论文建立了一个统一的 AI 框架，用于数据高效的性质预测与可控晶体生成，表明图学习、多模态表示与生成模型可降低计算成本并提升材料设计的可扩展性。

Abstract: This thesis develops robust and efficient AI frameworks for accelerating crystalline materials discovery by addressing both major stages of the materials-design pipeline: crystal property prediction and crystal structure generation. Motivated by the high computational cost of Density Functional Theory (DFT) and the limited availability of labeled materials data, the thesis explores graph representation learning, pretraining, multimodal learning, and generative modeling for scalable materials design.
  For property prediction, the thesis first introduces CrysXPP, which learns transferable crystal representations through unsupervised graph autoencoding, reducing dependence on large property-labeled datasets. It then proposes CrysGNN, a large-scale self-supervised graph pretraining framework that captures atomic connectivity, chemical attributes, and global structural information and transfers this knowledge to downstream property predictors through knowledge distillation. CrysMMNet further enriches crystal representations by jointly modeling graph structure and textual descriptions, thereby incorporating both local chemical and global structural knowledge.
  For crystal generation, the thesis introduces TGDMat, a text-guided joint diffusion framework that jointly models lattice parameters, atomic types, and atomic coordinates while incorporating textual structural knowledge during denoising. This enables the generation of more valid and stable periodic materials while also supporting conditional generation from natural-language descriptions.
  Overall, the thesis establishes a unified AI-based framework for data-efficient property prediction and controllable crystal generation, demonstrating how graph learning, multimodal representations, and generative models can reduce computational cost and improve the scalability of materials

</details>


### [74] [Conditions for the Emergence of Spontaneous Phonon Frequency Combs from Anharmonic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17707)
*Jayakrishnan SS,Mayanak K Gupta,Dipanshu Bansal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过机器学习增强的分子动力学模拟，探讨了范德华固体中GHz-THz频段声子频率梳（phononic frequency comb）的自发形成，指出CrGeTe₃中先前报道的梳状特征实源于同位素分布，并提出观察此类频率梳的两个必要条件，且在WSe₂的E₂g与A₂u模式中预测了自发频率梳的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子频率梳已在kHz-MHz频段被实验与理论证实，但其在GHz-THz频段的自发形成仍鲜有报道；此外，CrGeTe₃中曾提出的约2 cm⁻¹间距的梳状特征现被归因于同位素分布，需重新审视其物理起源。

Method: 采用机器学习增强的分子动力学模拟（machine-learning-augmented molecular dynamics simulations）计算谱能量密度（spectral energy density），并对CrGeTe₃不同声子模式及其他范德华固体进行系统性分析。

Result: CrGeTe₃中同一模式在50与200 K下出现间距约0.1与0.6 cm⁻¹的梳状特征，但其间距与声子线宽（phonon linewidth）相当，难以精确识别；分析表明可清晰观测频率梳需满足两个条件：(i) 具有较大三阶或更高阶声子自相互作用非谐性的分子内或孤立模式，(ii) 有限的声子散射通道。WSe₂的近平坦分子内E₂g与A₂u模式满足这些条件，计算显示由四声子和五声子本征态相干叠加分别形成间距为1.7与0.67 cm⁻¹的自发频率梳。

Conclusion: 文献中WSe₂的E₂g模式拉曼散射数据为频率梳形成提供了初步（虽非决定性）证据；研究深化了对长寿命声子频率梳的理解，并为其工程化与实际应用开辟了途径。

Abstract: Phononic frequency combs have been demonstrated experimentally and theoretically in the kHz-MHz regime under nonlinear driving; however, their spontaneous formation in the GHz-THz regime remains rare. We investigate the spontaneous formation of frequency combs in van der Waals solid CrGeTe$_3$, where combs of spacing $\sim$2 cm$^{-1}$ were experimentally proposed to occur in a flat phonon mode but are now reported to originate from isotopic distribution. Our spectral energy density calculations using machine-learning-augmented molecular dynamics simulations show comb-like features with reduced spacings of $\sim$0.1 and 0.6 cm$^{-1}$ in the same mode at 50 and 200 K. However, the spacings of the comb-like features are comparable to the phonon linewidth, making precise identification of the frequency comb challenging. From a comprehensive analysis of different modes of CrGeTe$_3$, we identified two conditions to distinctively observe the combs beyond the experimental resolution: (i) an intramolecular or isolated mode with reasonably large third- or higher-order anharmonicity from phonon self-interaction and (ii) limited phonon scattering channels. Our analysis of other van der Waals solids highlighted that these conditions are met for the nearly flat intramolecular $E_{2g}$ and $A_{2u}$ phonon modes of WSe$_2$. The detailed calculations showed the formation of a spontaneous frequency comb with spacings of 1.7 and 0.67 cm$^{-1}$ from coherent superposition of four and five-phonon eigenstates in the $E_{2g}$ and $A_{2u}$ phonon modes, respectively. Available Raman scattering data of the $E_{2g}$ mode in the literature provide preliminary, if not conclusive, evidence of comb formation. Our findings offer a deeper understanding and open avenues for engineering long-lived phononic frequency combs for various practical applications.

</details>


### [75] [A Multipolar Approach to Sliding Ferroelectricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17741)
*Matthew Dykes*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文针对滑移铁电体（sliding ferroelectrics）传统铁电理论难以适用的问题，提出结合群论与Wannier轨道密度畸变的方法，识别驱动极化的对称适配微观参数，并以密度泛函理论验证于六方氮化硼等蜂窝双层体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统铁电性（ferroelectricity）理论无法直接推广到滑移铁电体——这类层状材料的可切换电极化由堆叠层的二维相对运动控制，其极化行为背后的深层过程难以分析。

Method: 提出一种综合性方法，借助群论（group theory）论证与Wannier轨道密度畸变，将宏观对称性与决定铁电性出现的微观电子畸变联系起来；随后利用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）将该策略应用于蜂窝双层体系（honeycomb bilayer systems），包括六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride）。

Result: 研究发现，孤对电子轨道（lone pair electron orbitals）的类偶极（dipole-like）与类四极（quadrupole-like）畸变的组合，控制着此类体系中的电子重排，并进而控制其铁电性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种识别滑移铁电性对称适配微观驱动参数的途径，并揭示孤对电子轨道的偶极—四极畸变组合是其电子重排与铁电性的关键来源。

Abstract: Traditional theoretical treatments of ferroelectricity do not straightforwardly extend to sliding ferroelectrics, which are increasingly-studied layered materials where a switchable electrical polarization is controlled by two-dimensional relative motion of the stacked layers. Therefore, in-depth analyses of the underlying processes which dictate their polarization behavior remain challenging. In this paper, we present a comprehensive approach for identifying the symmetry-adapted microscopic parameters which are responsible for driving the emergence of this polarization. First, we outline our approach, which appeals to group theory arguments and the distortion of Wannier orbital densities to connect macroscopic symmetries to the microscopic electronic distortions which dictate the appearance of ferroelectricity. Then, we illustrate this process by using density functional theory to apply our strategy to honeycomb bilayer systems, including hexagonal boron nitride. In this way, we find that combinations of dipole-like and quadrupole-like distortions of lone pair electron orbitals control electronic reorganization, and by extension, ferroelectricity in such systems.

</details>


### [76] [Surface chemistry investigation of an additively manufactured Al-Mg-Si-Zr alloy: Studies from experiments and first-principles simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17876)
*Zhengqing Wei,Philip Grimm,Inna Plyushchay,Volker Hoffmann,Nebahat Bulut,Lutfi Caglar Ege,Julia Kristin Hufenbach,Sibylle Gemming*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合GDOCS深度剖析与DFT计算，揭示PBF-LB/M制备的Al-Mg-Si-Zr合金近表面Mg富集（约10 μm）与氧浓度升高的机制：Mg表面偏析热力学驱动强、空位迁移势垒低，且吸附氧显著促进Mg向表面扩散并形成Mg-O-Al重构结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造（additive manufacturing）铝合金的表面化学（surface chemistry）直接影响耐蚀性（corrosion resistance）及与外部材料的连接（joining）；因此需阐明近表面元素分布特征及其原子尺度成因，以理解Mg富集与氧化的来源。

Method: 采用辉光放电发射光谱（glow discharge optical emission spectroscopy, GDOES）深度剖析表征激光粉末床熔融（laser powder bed fusion, PBF-LB/M）Al-Mg-Si-Zr合金近表面元素组成；基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）对低指数Al表面开展第一性原理计算，包括Mg表面偏析、空位迁移势垒与吸附表面氧的影响，并用电子局域函数（electron localization function）分析电子转移。

Result: 测量显示近表面区（延伸至约10 μm，与表面粗糙度特征尺度一致）出现显著Mg富集及氧浓度升高，而Al、Si、Zr在更深处趋于稳定的体相水平。计算表明Mg表面偏析具强热力学驱动力（扩散能差约-0.30 eV至-0.41 eV），Zr倾向保留在体相基体；完全结构弛豫使Mg迁移势垒降至低于Si，利于其动力学扩散；吸附表面氧使Mg扩散能差最多降至-3.0 eV，并促进形成局部重构的Mg-O-Al配位前驱体结构及局域电子转移。

Conclusion: GDOES观测到的近表面Mg富集与氧升高可由Mg表面偏析的热力学优势、低空位迁移势垒的动力学优势及吸附氧的促进作用共同解释；Mg-O-Al配位前驱体结构与局域电子转移为理解该合金表面化学与后续腐蚀/连接行为提供了原子尺度依据。

Abstract: The surface chemistry of additively manufactured aluminum alloys plays a critical role in corrosion resistance and joining with external materials. In this work, the near-surface elemental composition of a laser powder bed fusion (PBF-LB/M) processed Al-Mg-Si-Zr alloy was characterized by glow discharge optical emission spectroscopy GDOES depth profiling. The measurements reveal pronounced Mg enrichment within the near-surface region extending to approximately 10 μm, consistent with the characteristic scale of surface roughness, together with an increase in oxygen concentration, whereas Al, Si, and Zr approach stable bulk-like levels at greater depths. To understand this observation on the atomic scale, first-principles calculations based on density functional theory (DFT) were performed on low-index Al surfaces. The calculated results show a strong thermodynamic driving force for Mg surface segregation, with diffusion energy differences ranging from approximately -0.30 eV to -0.41 eV, while Zr exhibits a pronounced preference for remaining in the bulk matrix. Vacancy migration calculations further demonstrate that full structural relaxation substantially reduces the migration barrier for Mg to below that of Si, which makes Mg diffusion kinetically highly favorable. Moreover, the presence of adsorbed surface oxygen dramatically promotes the tendency of Mg to diffuse toward the surface, which lowers the diffusion energy difference of Mg to as much as -3.0 eV. This promotes the formation of a locally reconstructed Mg-O-Al coordinated precursor structure accompanied by localized electron transfer shown by an electron localization function analysis.

</details>


### [77] [Magnetically coupled charge-transport crossover and giant negative magnetoresistance in iodine-incorporated Cr$_2$Se$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17945)
*Bikash Das,Shibnath Mandal,Kapildeb Dolui,Oleksi Laguta,Sambit Choudhury,Suvadip Masanta,Tanima Kundu,Jorge Andres Navarro Giraldo,Alexey Barinov,Shibabrata Nandi,Kai Rossnagel,Sanjoy Kr Mahatha,Rajib Mondal,Petr Neugebauer,Subhadeep Datta*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过在非范德华（non-van der Waals）层状结构的 Cr₂Se₃ 中引入碘（iodine），合成了一种准二维磁性材料；其表现出约 52 K 的反铁磁有序以及在约 150 K 处由短程磁关联、化学无序与导电通道重分布共同驱动的磁耦合输运交叉行为，阴离子掺入被证明是调控此类材料电子与磁性耦合的有效途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cr₂Se₃ 属于非层状（non-layered）过渡金属硫属化合物，其电子与磁性耦合性质的调控及潜在自旋电子学功能有待探索。论文旨在考察阴离子掺入（anion incorporation）能否成为调控该体系耦合电子-磁性的有效手段，并厘清其高温异常现象的物理起源。

Method: 结合结构、磁性、输运、谱学与第一性原理（first-principles）方法，对碘掺入的 Cr₂Se₃ 进行综合表征。具体手段包括磁化强度与电子自旋共振（electron spin resonance）测量、电阻率与霍尔（Hall）测量、三次谐波电信号测量、温度依赖拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）以及角分辨光电子能谱（angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）。

Result: 磁化与电子自旋共振显示 T_N ≈ 52 K 以下存在反铁磁有序态；高温区在 T* ≈ 150 K 附近出现第二个更宽的异常，与电阻率温度曲线中的浅极小值（类金属-绝缘体交叉）相吻合。霍尔测量表明高温（≥150 K）以空穴型导电为主，而 T* 以下的非线性霍尔响应及三次谐波电信号异常提示存在依赖于迁移率的多通道与空间非均匀输运。25 K、12 T 下观察到约 -78% 的不可饱和负磁阻（negative magnetoresistance）。温度依赖拉曼光谱未显示 T* 附近存在改变对称性的结构相变，ARPES 亦未见占据态电子结构发生明显重构。

Conclusion: 该高温异常被识别为一种宽泛的磁耦合输运交叉（magnetically coupled transport crossover），源于短程磁关联（short-range magnetic correlations）、化学无序与竞争性导电通道之间重分布的相互作用。结果表明，阴离子掺入可有效调控非层状 Cr₂Se₃ 的耦合电子与磁性质，使其成为研究过渡金属硫属化合物中关联输运与新兴自旋电子学（spintronic）功能的潜在平台。

Abstract: We report the synthesis and comprehensive investigation of iodine-incorporated $Cr_2Se_3$, a non-van der Waals quasi-two-dimensional magnetic material, using structural, magnetic, transport, spectroscopic, and first-principles methods. Magnetization and electron spin resonance measurements reveal an antiferromagnetically ordered state below $T_N \approx 52$ K. At higher temperatures, a second, broader anomaly emerges near $T^* \approx 150$ K, coinciding with a shallow minimum in the temperature-dependent resistivity that resembles a metal-to-insulator-like crossover. Hall measurements indicate predominantly hole-like conduction at high temperatures ($\geq$ 150 K), while the nonlinear Hall response below T*, together with an anomaly in the third-harmonic electrical signal, suggests the emergence of mobility-dependent multichannel and spatially inhomogeneous transport. A large, non-saturating negative magnetoresistance reaching approximately -78\% at 25 K and 12 T further demonstrates strong coupling between charge transport and the magnetic state. Temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy reveals no symmetry-changing structural transition near T*, whereas angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements show no major reconstruction of the electronic structure occupied across the crossover. Taken together, these results identify the high-temperature anomaly as a broad magnetically coupled transport crossover arising from the interplay of short-range magnetic correlations, chemical disorder, and redistribution among competing conduction channels. These findings demonstrate that anion incorporation provides an effective route to tuning the coupled electronic and magnetic properties of non-layered Cr$_2$Se$_3$, establishing this system as a promising platform for investigating correlated transport and emergent spintronic functionalities in transition-metal chalcogenides.

</details>


### [78] [High-performance orbital-torque magnetic memory on the 300-mm platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18008)
*Dinggui Zeng,Yang Gao,Jinyu Duan,Lei Zhao,Yuhao An,Xing He,Jintao Ke,Yonglong Ga,Shasha Wang,Zhenghui Ji,Muyuan Chen,Hengan Zhou,Xuejie Xie,Enlong Liu,Junlu Gong,Qijun Guo,Yihui Sun,Zejie Zheng,Weiming He,Xiaolei Yang,Fantao Meng,Yaohua Wang,Hongxin Yang,Delin Zhang,Yong Jiang,Wanjun Jiang,Shikun He*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在300-mm晶圆平台上利用Ti/W双层结构产生的轨道力矩（orbital torque, OT），缓解了自旋轨道力矩磁随机存储器（SOT-MRAM）写入/读取效率低、位单元面积大及超薄自旋源通道量产困难等问题，实现了182%的隧道磁阻、纳秒级响应、10^12耐久性与低于0.1 pJ/bit的写入能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代存储技术受限于存储容量、访问延迟与功耗构成的三难困境。SOT-MRAM因快速翻转动态与高耐久性被视为潜在解决方案，但其写入与读取效率较低，导致位单元面积大、感测裕度不足；同时所需的自旋源通道极薄（通常数纳米），给量产带来工艺挑战。

Method: 在300-mm晶圆平台上，利用新兴的轨道自由度（orbital degree of freedom）以及由相对较厚的Ti/W双层结构产生的轨道力矩（orbital torque, OT）来构建OT存储纳米器件。

Result: OT存储纳米器件表现出182%的巨隧道磁阻（tunnel magnetoresistance, TMR）、纳秒级响应速度、10^12耐久性，以及增强的翻转效率（E_b/I_c），从而实现低于0.1 pJ/bit的超低写入能耗。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，轨道角动量（orbital angular momentum）可用于构建能效型MRAM器件，为高性能计算与AI应用所需的低延迟存储器提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Contemporary memory technologies are increasingly constrained by the fundamental trilemma of storage capacity, access latency, and power consumption. Among the emerging technologies, spin-orbit torque magnetic random-access memory (SOT-MRAM) shows promise to circumvent these challenges, owing to its fast switching dynamics and high endurance. However, the application of SOT-MRAM is hindered by the relatively low write and read efficiencies, resulting in a large bitcell area and an insufficient sensing margin. Meanwhile, the involvement of an ultrathin spin-source channel, typically within a few nanometers, imposes technological challenges for mass production. Here, we resolve these issues on a 300-mm wafer platform by exploiting the emerging orbital degree of freedom and the resultant orbital torque (OT) from the relatively thick Ti/W bilayer. In particular, OT memory nanodevices exhibit a giant tunnel magnetoresistance (TMR) of 182%, nanosecond-scale response, 1012 endurance, together with an enhanced switching efficiency (E_b/I_c), which consequently enables an ultra-low write energy of less than 0.1 pJ/bit. Our findings demonstrate that orbital angular momentum can be implemented for building energy-efficient MRAM devices, offering a practical pathway towards low-latency memory that is demanded for high-performance computing and AI applications.

</details>


### [79] [Disentangling Strain and Ti3+ Contributions to the Anomalous Hall Effect in Epitaxial RuO2 Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18059)
*S. G. Jeong,A. Santhosh,S. Lee,S. Sheikh,U. M. Jayathilake,T. -L. Lee,T. Low,A. X. Gray,S. A. Chambers,B. Jalan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过在保持Ti³⁺含量相近的前提下独立调控外延应变（epitaxial strain），该研究证明应变而非Ti³⁺本身是RuO₂体系中反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE）的主导控制参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO₂/TiO₂外延体系中的反常霍尔效应曾被归因于应变稳定的磁性态，但应变与Ru-Ti互混及界面电荷再分布相互纠缠，后者可产生Ti³⁺及潜在局域磁矩，导致机制归属不清。研究旨在解耦这些贡献。

Method: 采用Ti合金化RuO₂异质结构（Ti-alloyed RuO₂ heterostructures），在保留大量Ti³⁺态的同时独立改变外延应变；对比应变膜与弛豫膜的Ti/Ru组分、Ti³⁺比例及霍尔响应，并用椭偏光谱（spectroscopic ellipsometry）表征巡游电子响应。

Result: 应变膜与弛豫膜的Ti/Ru组分几乎相同、Ti³⁺比例相当，但显著的非线性AHE仅出现在共格应变膜中，弛豫异质结构则表现为近乎线性的霍尔响应；AHE活跃的应变态伴随巡游电子响应重构，包括金属性增强与载流子弛豫时间（carrier relaxation time）延长。

Conclusion: 仅Ti³⁺的形成不足以产生反常霍尔态；外延应变通过改变RuO₂的巡游电子结构，被确定为该体系AHE的主导控制参量。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) reported in epitaxial RuO2/TiO2 has been attributed to a strain-stabilized magnetic state, but strain can be entangled with Ru-Ti intermixing and interfacial charge redistribution, which can produce Ti3+ and potentially localized moments. Here, we disentangle these contributions using Ti-alloyed RuO2 heterostructures in which abundant Ti3+ states are retained while epitaxial strain is independently changed. The strained and relaxed films have nearly identical Ti/Ru compositions and comparable Ti3+ fractions, yet a pronounced nonlinear AHE appears only in the coherently strained film, while the relaxed heterostructure exhibits an almost linear Hall response. Spectroscopic ellipsometry further shows that the AHE-active strained state is accompanied by a reconstruction of the itinerant electronic response, including enhanced metallicity and longer carrier relaxation times. These results show that Ti3+ formation alone is insufficient to generate the anomalous Hall state and identify epitaxial strain, through its modification of the itinerant RuO2 electronic structure, as the dominant control parameter.

</details>


### [80] [Nitrogen based electride superconductor Nb5Ir3N under pressure: multifunctional physical properties from DFT based first-principles investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18113)
*M. Abdul Hadi Shah,J. H. Abir,S. H. Naqib*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）的模拟，系统研究了三元氮化物超导体（ternary nitride superconductor）Nb5Ir3N 在 0–20 GPa 压力范围内的结构、力学、电子、光学及超导特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电子化物（superconducting electrides）的发现为凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）开辟了新方向，其多功能特性横跨常压与高压区域，促使对相关三元氮化物超导体开展深入研究。

Method: 采用基于密度泛函理论（DFT）的模拟，在 0–20 GPa 压力范围内计算 Nb5Ir3N 的结构参数、生成焓、弹性常数、声子谱、能带结构与态密度（含与不含自旋轨道耦合，spin orbit coupling）、光学响应，并定性讨论压力诱导的超导特性。

Result: 计算的结构参数与现有数据吻合良好；负生成焓、弹性常数与声子谱共同证实其结构、热力学、力学与动力学稳定性。该化合物表现为延性（ductile）且具弹性各向异性（elastic anisotropy），各向异性程度随压力升高逐渐减弱。光学响应显示低能区显著的带内贡献（intraband contributions）、强烈的紫外吸收与高反射率，并呈现光学各向异性及高压下的谱线展宽。

Conclusion: Nb5Ir3N 在全压力范围内保持稳定，其力学、电子与光学性质均随压力呈现规律性变化，并讨论了压力诱导的超导特征，表明高压调控在该类超导电子化物中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Discovery and study of superconducting electrides have opened new avenues in condensed matter physics, driven by their intriguing multifunctional features spanning ambient and high-pressure regimes. This work investigates the ternary nitride superconductor Nb5Ir3N under pressure ranging from 0 to 20 GPa via density functional theory based simulations. Estimated structural parameters agree well with the available data, confirming their reliability and supporting the validity of this analysis. Negative formation enthalpy, together with evaluated elastic constants and phonon spectra, confirm the structural, thermodynamic, mechanical, and dynamical stability of Nb5Ir3N over the entire pressure range. Pressure dependent elastic constants and polycrystalline elastic moduli are investigated. The compound is categorized as ductile in light of estimated mechanical indices. Elastic anisotropy factors indicate that Nb5Ir3N remains anisotropic, with the degree of anisotropy gradually decreasing as pressure increases. Electronic band structure and density of states are calculated with and without spin orbit coupling to investigate its influence on the electronic structure. Calculated optical response reveals substantial intraband contributions in the low energy region, intense ultraviolet absorption, and strong reflectivity. The spectra exhibit optical anisotropy and a broadening at higher pressures. Pressure induced superconducting features are also discussed qualitatively.

</details>


### [81] [Quantum electrodynamics of equilibrium systems: A rigorous Maxwell-regularized functional-theoretic formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18327)
*Markus Penz,Christian Jöns,Michael Ruggenthaler,Angel Rubio*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出一个平衡系统量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics, QED）框架，以麦克斯韦方程（Maxwell equations）为基础，并将其与量子系统的密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）结构相联系，最终得到一个完全重整化（renormalized）且非微扰（non-perturbative）的光-物质描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密度泛函理论主要基于纯内部可观测量（如单粒子密度），难以自然纳入外部源及其能量，且存在可表示性（representability）问题。作者希望建立一个以麦克斯韦方程为出发点的理论框架，从而统一处理光与物质并避免上述困难。

Method: 通过从纯内部可观测量转向内部与外部量相结合，将外部源及其能量纳入理论；进而构建完全正则化的泛函理论，并借助 Kohn-Sham 构造（Kohn-Sham construction）链接到辅助非耦合系统，使紫外截断（ultra-violet cutoff）得以移除。

Result: 得到完全重整化且非微扰的光-物质描述；含磁场与不含磁场的正则化密度泛函理论形式作为边界情形出现，且所涌现的物理图像可重现宏观麦克斯韦方程。

Conclusion: 该框架以麦克斯韦方程为基础，将量子电动力学与密度泛函理论结构统一，既规避了可表示性问题，又通过移除紫外截断实现了正则化与非微扰的光-物质处理，并自然恢复宏观麦克斯韦方程。

Abstract: A framework for quantum electrodynamics of equilibrium systems is proposed that takes the Maxwell equations as foundational and links them to the structure of density-functional theory for the quantum system. By switching from purely internal observables like the one-particle density to combined internal and external quantities, the external sources and their energy are taken into account. A fully regularized functional theory emerges that avoids the usual representability problems. With the Kohn-Sham construction that links to an auxiliary uncoupled system, the ultra-violet cutoff can be removed and one arrives at a fully renormalized and non-perturbative light-matter description. Regularized forms of density-functional theory with and without magnetic fields appear as boundary cases, while the emergent physical picture reproduces the macroscopic Maxwell equations.

</details>


### [82] [Direct observation of phonon mode with an antisymmetric Raman tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18332)
*Shangfei Wu,Shang Ren,Kai Du,Xianghan Xu,Sang-Wook Cheong,David Vanderbilt,Girsh Blumberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文在反铁磁手性相 Cr$_2$O$_3$ 中，利用偏振分辨拉曼光谱（polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy）探测到位于 266 cm$^{-1}$、属于 $A_2$ 赝矢量（pseudovector）对称通道的声子模式，其拉曼活性源于 Cr 空位对局部对称性的破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子角动量（phonon angular momentum）在声子霍尔效应、声子磁矩、Einstein-de Haas 效应、Weyl 声子及驱动手性声子等现象中起决定性作用。然而，具有反对称拉曼张量（antisymmetric Raman tensor）、在赝矢量对称通道中携带内禀声子角动量的声子模式，过去在手性声子研究中被忽视。

Method: 采用偏振分辨拉曼光谱对 Cr$_2$O$_3$ 反铁磁手性相进行探测；并结合第一性原理声子计算（first-principles phonon calculations），将晶格与自旋同等视为“慢”自由度进行统一处理。

Result: 在 Cr$_2$O$_3$ 反铁磁手性相的 $A_2$ 赝矢量对称通道中观测到 266 cm$^{-1}$ 的声子；第一性原理计算复现了该 $A_2$ 模式的频率与椭圆运动。该模式因 Cr$_2$O$_3$ 中 Cr 空位局部破坏垂直于三重轴的二重旋转对称性而获得拉曼活性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一种探测赝矢量对称通道中具有反对称拉曼张量的新型对称禁戒 $A_2$ 声子模式的新途径。

Abstract: Phonon angular momentum, characterized by circular or elliptical motion of atoms, plays a decisive role in diverse phenomena, ranging from the phonon Hall effect, phonon magnetic moment, Einstein-de Haas effect, Weyl phonons, and driven chiral phonons. The phonon modes with an antisymmetric Raman tensor, which carry an intrinsic phonon angular momentum in a pseudovector symmetry channel, have been overlooked in the past in the chiral phonon community. Here, we report the detection of this type of phonon mode using polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy. We observe a phonon at 266cm$^{-1}$ in the antiferromagnetic chiral phase of Cr$_2$O$_3$ in the $A_2$-pseudovector symmetry channel. This $A_2$ mode's frequency and the elliptical motion are captured by first-principles phonon calculations, which treat both lattice and spins on an equal footing as 'slow' degrees of freedom. This mode gets Raman activity due to the presence of Cr vacancies in Cr$_2$O$_3$ that locally break the twofold rotational symmetry perpendicular to the threefold axis. Our results provide a new way to detect the novel symmetry-forbidden $A_2$ phonon modes with an antisymmetric Raman tensor in a pseudovector symmetry channel.

</details>


### [83] [Complex magnetic properties of EuAgAs single crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18354)
*Karolina Kowalczyk,Kamila Komędera,Janusz Przewoźnik,Łukasz Gondek,Czesław Kapusta,Wojciech Tabiś,Michał Babij,Lan Maria Tran,Damian Rybicki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: EuAgAs 作为一种反铁磁拓扑材料（antiferromagnetic topological material），其结构、磁性与局域电子性质通过多种实验手段被系统研究，并据此构建了磁相图（magnetic phase diagram），揭示低场下的非共线磁结构及可能的竞争磁相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EuAgAs 表现出有趣的磁性行为，但其磁结构、相图及磁性相互作用机制尚待明确。研究旨在通过多手段实验刻画其磁性与局域电子性质，并探讨其磁场诱导磁态的可能成因。

Method: 采用 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）、穆斯堡尔谱（Mössbauer spectroscopy）、直流磁化（dc magnetization）、交流磁化率（ac susceptibility）及热容（heat capacity）测量，对 EuAgAs 的结构、磁性和局域电子性质进行联合表征。

Result: 结果证实 EuAgAs 在 $T_\text{N}$ 以下存在反铁磁有序（antiferromagnetic ordering），并显示显著磁各向异性（magnetic anisotropy）以及多处磁场诱导的变磁转变（metamagnetic transitions）；据此构建的磁相图（magnetic phase diagram）包含多个不同磁区。

Conclusion: 场与温度依赖行为提示低场区存在非共线磁结构（noncollinear magnetic structure）。其磁场诱导转变序列与具有斯格明子相（skyrmion phases）的中心对称稀土化合物相似，暗示竞争磁相互作用（competing magnetic interactions）可能在稳定所观测磁态中起重要作用。

Abstract: EuAgAs is an antiferromagnetic topological material exhibiting intriguing magnetic behavior. We investigate its structural, magnetic, and local electronic properties using X ray diffraction, Mössbauer spectroscopy, dc magnetization, ac susceptibility, and heat capacity measurements. The results confirm antiferromagnetic ordering below $T_\text{N}$ and reveal pronounced magnetic anisotropy and several field induced metamagnetic transitions. We construct the magnetic phase diagram of EuAgAs, identifying several distinct magnetic regions. The field and temperature dependent behavior observed suggests a noncollinear magnetic structure in the low field regime. The sequence of field induced transitions resembles that observed in centrosymmetric rare earth compounds hosting skyrmion phases, suggesting that competing magnetic interactions may play an important role in stabilizing the observed magnetic states.

</details>


### [84] [Electronic Origins of Elastic Behavior in Rocksalt, Zinc-Blende and Wurtzite 3d Transition-Metal Nitrides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18365)
*Jesus Cañas,Oliver Ambacher*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过第一性原理（ab-initio）计算系统研究第四周期过渡金属氮化物（IV-period transition metal nitrides）的弹性性质，揭示其力学行为与电子结构之间的关联，并比较岩盐（rocksalt）、闪锌矿（zincblende）与纤锌矿（wurtzite）三种晶相下的变化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解第四周期过渡金属氮化物弹性性质的演化规律，并建立其与电子结构之间的关联，同时考察不同晶体结构对弹性行为的影响。

Method: 采用第一性原理（ab-initio）计算方法，通过分析电子态密度（electronic density of states），考察成键与反成键轨道（bonding/antibonding orbitals）的占据情况，并对岩盐、闪锌矿和纤锌矿三种晶相进行对比。

Result: 分析表明，成键或反成键轨道的占据决定了弹性性质沿该周期的演化趋势；不同晶相间晶体对称性（crystal symmetry）与原子配位（atomic coordination）的差异会影响其弹性性质。

Conclusion: 第四周期过渡金属氮化物的弹性行为可由电子结构给出解释，且晶体对称性与原子配位的变化是调控其弹性性质的重要因素。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive ab-initio study of the elastic properties of the IV-period transition metal nitrides, highlighting the correlation between their mechanical behavior and electronic structure. By analyzing the electronic density of states, we reveal how the occupation of bonding or antibonding orbitals determines the evolution of elastic properties along the period. Furthermore, we examine trends across different crystal structures, specifically comparing rocksalt, zincblende and wurtzite phases, to uncover how variations in crystal symmetry and atomic coordination influence their elastic properties.

</details>


### [85] [Rapid Parameter Estimation from Photoluminescence Decays of Halide Perovskite Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18438)
*Robin Heumann,Toby Rudolph,Gaosheng Huang,Thomas Kirchartz,Chris Dreessen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于人工神经网络加速的参数估计工作流，用于从瞬态光致发光（transient photoluminescence）测量中提取铅卤钙钛矿薄膜的材料参数，并可通过纳入稳态数据降低缺陷密度等参数的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当实验数据与目标材料参数之间无法用可逆解析式关联，而仅由一组非线性微分方程描述时，传统拟合需反复求解方程，导致优化耗时且难以获得参数不确定性信息。因此需要一种更高效、能快速扫描参数空间并揭示参数相关性的估计方法。

Method: 提出一个面向铅卤钙钛矿薄膜瞬态光致发光测量的参数估计工作流，利用人工神经网络（artificial neural networks）实现实验与模拟之间的快速比较，从而快速扫描多维材料参数空间并识别参数间的相关性。此外，将稳态光致发光（steady-state photoluminescence）数据纳入估计流程与瞬态数据进行比较。

Result: 该工作流能够快速扫描多维材料参数空间并识别参数之间的相关性，为理解卤化物钙钛矿中非辐射复合（non-radiative recombination）的物理机制提供线索。通过纳入稳态数据，可降低缺陷密度（defect density）等参数的不确定性。

Conclusion: 基于神经网络的加速参数估计工作流可有效用于瞬态光致发光数据分析，兼顾快速参数空间扫描与物理洞察，并表明联合稳态与瞬态测量有助于减小关键材料参数的不确定性。

Abstract: Extracting material parameters from experimental data is often challenging if no invertible analytical equation can be used to link the data with the quantities of interest. If the link between experiment and material parameters is given mathematically by a set of non-linear differential equations, these must be solved repeatedly during the traditional fitting procedure, resulting in long optimization times and limited insight into parameter uncertainty. Here, we present a parameter estimation workflow specifically aimed at transient photoluminescence measurements performed on lead-halide perovskite films. This workflow is accelerated using artificial neural networks for rapid comparison between experiment and simulation. An advantage of the method is the ability to rapidly scan multidimensional material parameter spaces and identify correlations between parameters that provide insights into the physics of non-radiative recombination in halide perovskites. Finally, we compare steady-state and transient photoluminescence and show how uncertainty in parameters such as the defect density can be reduced by including steady-state data in the parameter estimation workflow.

</details>


### [86] [The role of disconnections in redox-induced phase transformation of metal oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18446)
*Martina Ruffino,Baptiste Bienvenu,Xuyang Zhou,Dierk Raabe,Yan Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出以具有位错特征的界面台阶（disconnections）作为磁铁矿（magnetite, Fe₃O₄）向赤铁矿（haematite, Fe₂O₃）相变的基本传播缺陷，并结合拓扑模型、扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）与原子模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管金属氧化物体系氧化还原反应（redox reactions）广受关注，但磁铁矿与赤铁矿之间相变的原子尺度机制仍缺乏深入理解。相变涉及氧的加入与净扩散通量，而赤铁矿沿特定惯习面生长，其界面传播机制尚待阐明。

Method: 采用界面缺陷拓扑模型（topological model of interfacial defects）预测该体系的三种disconnection模式；利用扫描透射电子显微镜（scanning transmission electron microscopy, STEM）在部分氧化的磁铁矿粉末的赤铁矿/磁铁矿界面中确认其存在；并通过原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）确定其平衡结构、运动方式及相变实现过程。

Result: 拓扑模型预测出三种disconnection模式；STEM观测确认这些缺陷存在于赤铁矿/磁铁矿界面中；原子模拟得到的disconnection平衡结构及其运动与相变过程，与原子分辨率实验观察结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 界面disconnection可作为传播磁铁矿向赤铁矿相变的基本缺陷，理论预测、实验观测与原子模拟三者相互印证，为理解该氧化物相变的原子机制提供了统一图景。

Abstract: Detailed atomic-level understanding of the phase transformation between magnetite (${\rm Fe_3 O_4}$) and haematite (${\rm Fe_2 O_3}$) is lacking, despite widespread interest in redox reactions of metal oxide systems. While the magnetite-to-haematite transformation entails the addition of oxygen to the system and thus a net diffusive flux, haematite formations have been reported to grow along well-defined habits on the close-packed $\{0001\}_{\rm Fe_2 O_3}//\{111\}_{\rm Fe_3 O_4}$ planes. The propagation of the phase interface in the bulk is thus thought to maintain the oxygen sublattice fixed up to a shear, and to require long-range diffusion of iron ions. In this letter we propose interfacial steps with dislocation character, i.e. disconnections, as the elementary defects propagating the transformation. We use the topological model of interfacial defects to predict three disconnection modes for the system, and we employ scanning transmission electron microscopy to ascertain their presence in a haematite/magnetite interface of a partially oxidised magnetite powder. Using atomistic simulations, we determine the equilibrium structures of the disconnections, and study their motion and how they accomplish the transformation, obtaining excellent agreement with atomic-resolution experimental observations.

</details>


### [87] [A Pseudoscalar Representation Mapping from Parent-Group Vibrational Normal Modes to Symmetry-Adapted Magnetic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18449)
*Yachao Liu,Haibo Niu,Vei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种对所有 32 个晶体学点群通用的“行列式诱导赝标量扭曲（determinant-induced pseudoscalar twist）”，建立母格振动与磁序之间精确的表示—几何对应关系，并以单层 Cd₂N₃ 验证该“模板原理（Template Principle）”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅对对称性允许的磁构型进行分类，但本身无法建立与母格振动之间直接的、按模式分辨（mode-resolved）的对应。该研究旨在填补这一空缺，给出振动模式与磁几何之间的严格映射。

Method: 针对全部 32 个晶体学点群构造行列式诱导赝标量扭曲，在顺磁灰色群 G×Θ_T 中区分空间扭曲（决定磁几何）与时间反演宇称（决定磁性质）；通过投影恒等式 P_mag,mn^(Γ⊗Γ_ps)=P_ph,mn^(Γ) 建立母声子不可约表示 Γ 到 Γ_mag=Γ⊗Γ_ps 的映射。

Result: 映射保持重数不变，并在共同笛卡尔实现下给出上述投影恒等式，从而确立“模板原理”：母振动模式提供实空间模板，其对称性强制的节点流形（nodal manifolds）被精确继承。应用于单层 Cd₂N₃ 时，框架从母 A_2u 扇区识别出亚铁磁基态，并经第一性原理计算证实，同时给出团簇磁八极（magnetic octupoles）与反铁磁流形。

Conclusion: 该框架为筛选对称性允许的线性磁响应提供了先验的母群判据（a priori parent-group criterion），将磁序分析与母格振动统一于同一表示论结构中。

Abstract: Conventional approaches classify symmetry-allowed magnetic configurations but do not by themselves establish a direct, mode-resolved correspondence with parent-lattice vibrations. Here, we formulate a universal determinant-induced pseudoscalar twist for all 32 crystallographic point groups, establishing an exact representation-to-geometry correspondence between parent vibrations and magnetic order. Within the paramagnetic gray group $G\timesΘ_{\mathcal{T}}$, the spatial twist determines symmetry-defined magnetic geometry, while time-reversal parity independently specifies magnetic character. Each parent phonon irrep $Γ$ maps to $Γ_{\mathrm{mag}}=Γ\otimesΓ_{\mathrm{ps}}$, preserving multiplicities and yielding the projection identity $P_{\mathrm{mag},mn}^{(Γ\otimesΓ_{\mathrm{ps}})}=P_{\mathrm{ph},mn}^{(Γ)}$ under the common Cartesian realization. This establishes the \textit{Template Principle}: parent vibrational modes furnish real-space templates whose symmetry-enforced nodal manifolds are inherited exactly. Applied to monolayer $\mathrm{Cd}_2\mathrm{N}_3$, the framework identifies the ferrimagnetic ground state from the parent $A_{2u}$ sector, confirmed by first-principles calculations, alongside cluster magnetic octupoles and antiferromagnetic manifolds. It further provides an \textit{a priori} parent-group criterion for screening symmetry-allowed linear magnetic responses.

</details>


### [88] [Thermally Desorbable InN Capping Layers for Nitride Surface Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18573)
*Mellie Lemon,Amitayush Thakur,Anthony Rice,Glenn Teeter,Michelle Smeaton,Renae Gannon,Jessica L. McChesney,M. Brooks Tellekamp*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出以牺牲性 InN 层（sacrificial InN layer）作为 III 族氮化物（III-nitride）薄膜的临时保护帽层，利用 InN 与金属 In 较低的分解和脱附温度实现原位热脱附去除，从而在空气暴露后恢复 AlGaN 表面并支持角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: III 族氮化物薄膜在光电子与功率器件中至关重要，其表面与界面质量直接决定器件性能。薄膜在加工或表征过程中常需经大气转移，会引入显著表面污染，因此需要一种可在大气暴露期间保护表面、又不损伤薄膜本体的手段。

Method: 采用分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy, MBE）生长的 AlGaN 薄膜作为代表性被保护材料，在其表面覆盖牺牲性 InN 帽层；借助 InN 与金属 In 较低的分解与脱附温度，通过原位热脱附（in situ thermal desorption）去除帽层。随后以角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）测量 Al0.4Ga0.6N 的价带色散（valence band dispersion）。

Result: ARPES 测量的 Al0.4Ga0.6N 价带色散表明，InN 去帽（InN decapping）后薄膜表面得到恢复，且底层 AlGaN 薄膜未受损伤。

Conclusion: 该 InN 牺牲帽层结合热脱附去帽的工艺，可使曾暴露于空气的薄膜重新获得洁净表面，从而支持后续表面敏感表征，显示出该方法的通用性。

Abstract: Group III-nitride thin films are essential for optoelectronic and power devices, where surface and interface quality critically influence performance. It is often necessary to transfer these films through atmosphere for processing or characterization steps, which can introduce significant surface contamination. Here we demonstrate a technique to protect the surface of III-N films during atmospheric exposure by capping with sacrificial InN layers. Using molecular beam epitaxy grown AlGaN films as our representative protected material, we take advantage of the lower decomposition and desorption temperatures of InN and metallic In to remove the protective cap layer in situ via thermal desorption without damaging the AlGaN film beneath. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements of the valence band dispersion in Al0.4Ga0.6N demonstrate that the film surface is recovered after InN decapping, highlighting the versatility of this process in allowing further surface-sensitive characterization of films that had been exposed to air.

</details>


### [89] [ERAF4XRD: A multimodal agentic framework for constructing validated experimental X-ray diffraction databases from scientific literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18583)
*Afnan Mostafa,William Ratcliff,Simon J. L. Billinge,Niaz Abdolrahim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ERAF4XRD，一个全自动多模态、多智能体框架，用于从科学文献中重建经校验的 X 射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）记录，实现从信息抽取向实验记录重建与验证的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数十年的实验测量数据分散在图表、图注、正文与表格中，难以作为结构化数据被现代 AI 与数据驱动研究直接利用；需先识别、关联并验证实验数据及其上下文。此瓶颈促使研究构建自动化框架以将已发表科学信息转化为可机读的实验数据集。

Method: ERAF4XRD 为全自动多模态（图像与文本）、多智能体（multi-agent）框架：下载并筛选文献、识别 XRD 图、抽取元数据并与对应实验数据链接，最后通过独立验证智能体（validation agent）依据原文证据校验输出。评价基于人工整理基准，含 273 篇文献、3,150 个候选图。

Result: 在基准上，XRD 图识别准确率最高达 98.7%，并在 22 个字段上生成 1,400 个元数据值。对最终校验记录的独立人工评估显示精确率（precision）98.5%、召回率（recall）90.7%，在 443 个受评字段中未发现缺乏依据的元数据。

Conclusion: 通过超越单纯信息抽取，实现对关联实验记录的重建与验证，ERAF4XRD 提供了将已发表科学信息自动转化为机器可读实验数据集的途径，服务于 AI 与数据驱动科学。

Abstract: The scientific literature contains decades of experimental measurements that remain difficult to access as structured data for modern AI and data-driven research. Much of this information is distributed across figures, captions, text, and tables, requiring experimental data and their context to be identified, connected, and verified before they can be reused. Here we introduce ERAF4XRD (Experiment Reader Agentic Framework for X-Ray Diffraction), a fully automated multimodal (i.e., image and text), multi-agent framework that reconstructs validated X-ray diffraction (XRD) records from scientific publications. ERAF4XRD downloads and screens documents, identifies XRD figures, extracts and links metadata to the corresponding experimental data, and validates outputs against source evidence using an independent validation agent. On a manually curated benchmark of 273 scientific publications containing 3,150 candidate figures, ERAF4XRD achieved up to 98.7% accuracy for XRD figure identification and generated 1,400 metadata values across 22 fields. Independent manual assessment of the final validated records yielded 98.5% precision and 90.7% recall, with no unsupported metadata observed among 443 evaluated fields. By moving beyond information extraction to the reconstruction and validation of linked experimental records, ERAF4XRD establishes an automated approach for transforming published scientific information into machine-readable experimental datasets for AI and data-driven science.

</details>


### [90] [Nonempirical Time-Dependent Density Functional Theory Framework for Nonlocal Exchange--Correlation Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18584)
*Zhandos A. Moldabekov,Michele Pavanello,Thomas D. Gawne,Jan Vorberger,Tobias Dornheim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 针对轨道依赖交换关联泛函（XC functional）在含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）线性响应框架下谱性质变差的问题，本文指出根源在于动态密度响应处理中非局域性未在完整非对角密度矩阵层面被一致考虑，并提出对动态交换关联核（XC kernel）的非经验可加修正 Δf_xc(q,ω) 以重新满足非局域 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿量的精确 f 求和规则（f-sum rule），在多种体系上显著改善谱性质且无额外计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进轨道依赖交换关联泛函（exchange–correlation functional）能显著改善电子结构性质，却会在标准线性响应含时密度泛函理论（linear-response TDDFT）框架下严重恶化谱性质。作者强调这并非 Kohn-Sham 态本身的失效，而是当交换关联势的非局域性（non-locality）未在完整非对角密度矩阵（off-diagonal density matrix）层面被一致处理时，动态密度响应处理中存在形式上的不一致。

Method: 为规避上述复杂性，提出对动态交换关联核（dynamic XC kernel）的非经验可加修正 Δf_xc(q,ω)，在 TDDFT 内重新强制满足非局域 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿量的精确 f 求和规则（f-sum rule）。相应实现扩展至开源 GPAW 代码并在线免费提供；同时考察非局域赝势（non-local pseudopotential）的影响。

Result: 将新结果与一组代表性精确实验测量（室温铝、硅、碳，以及加热与压缩铝）对比，所有情形均出现显著改善，且未增加额外计算成本。对非局域赝势的分析表明，其非局域性具有重要的物理动机效应，在缺少全电子（all-electron）模拟时对正确捕捉等离激元色散（plasmon dispersion）不可或缺。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅对大量动态与谱性质（dynamic and spectral properties）的估计具有重要意义，也是迈向可高精度估计各类可观测量之通用交换关联泛函（universal XC functional）的重要一步。作者进一步概述该框架在发展先进非局域交换关联泛函方面的潜在价值，并提出一种通过与集体激发（collective excitation）实验测量对比来严格验证非局域赝势的新途径。

Abstract: Advanced, orbital-dependent exchange--correlation (XC) functionals can significantly improve the description of electronic structural properties, but they substantially worsen spectral properties that are computed within standard linear-response time-dependent density functional theory frameworks. This is not a failure of the underlying Kohn-Sham states, but due to a formal inconsistency in the treatment of the dynamic density response when the non-locality of the XC potential is not taken into account consistently on the level of the full off-diagonal density matrix. To avoid these complexities, we present a non-empirical additive correction $Δf_\textnormal{xc}(\mathbf{q},ω)$ to the dynamic XC kernel that re-enforces the exact f-sum rule of the non-local KS Hamiltonian within TDDFT. Comparing our new results against a representative set of accurate experimental measurements (ambient aluminum, silicon and carbon, as well as heated and compressed aluminum) reveals a dramatic improvement in all cases without any additional computational cost. The corresponding extension to the open-source GPAW code is made freely available online. We further investigate the implications of non-local pseudopotentials and show that the non-locality has an important, physically motivated effect that is indispensable to capture the correct plasmon dispersion in lieu of full all-electron simulations. In addition to being important for the estimation of a plethora of dynamic and spectral properties, our work constitutes an important step towards a universal XC functional that can be used to estimate all kinds of observables with high accuracy. Finally, we outline the potential utility of our framework for the development of advanced non-local XC functionals, and suggest a new way to rigorously verify non-local pseudopotentials against experimental measurements of collective excitations.

</details>


### [91] [Hypothesis-Driven Autonomous Materials Synthesis with Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18598)
*Izumi Takahara,Kazunori Nishio,Akira Aiba,Shigeru Kobayashi,Takao Nakajima,Taro Hitosugi,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SynAgent 将大语言模型智能体（large language model agents）作为自主实验系统的决策层，其核心产出不是优化样本，而是对合成过程显式且可修正的理解。在 LiCoO2 (001) 薄膜沉积的 18 次自主实验中，该框架合成高结晶度薄膜并揭示衬底温度对结晶的突变阈值与 650-690 °C 的最优生长窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主实验室（self-driving laboratories）的决策层通常为黑箱优化器（black-box optimizer），测量被简化为预定义标量目标，输出仅为一组优化样本，成功原因未被阐明。该工作旨在将自主实验的产出从"优化样本"拓展为可检验、人类可读的理解。

Method: SynAgent 由大语言模型智能体（large language model agents）操作自动化实验系统，并在无预设分析流程的条件下，针对新获取数据自适应生成分析技能（analysis skills）。其理解通过多模态推理（multimodal reasoning）在 X 射线衍射图谱（X-ray diffraction patterns）与电子显微图像（electron micrographs）等数据上迭代演化，并由验证—证伪机制（verify-falsify scheme）引导，主动测试被预测为失败及成功的条件。

Result: 在 LiCoO2 (001) 薄膜沉积这一测试平台上开展的 18 次自主实验（autonomous experiments）中，SynAgent 合成了高结晶度薄膜，并演化出对衬底温度调控结晶机制的理解，发现存在突变阈值以及 650-690 °C 的狭窄最优生长窗口。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该框架将自主实验从单纯获得优化样本推进到产生可检验且人类可读的理解（testable, human-readable understanding），使合成过程的显式、可修正认识成为实验活动的主要输出。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories can explore synthesis conditions autonomously, but their decision-making layer is typically a black-box optimizer, and the output is a set of optimized samples, with the measurements reduced to predefined scalar objectives and the reasons behind success left unarticulated. Here we present SynAgent, a framework in which large language model agents operate an automated experimental system and maintain an explicit, revisable understanding of the synthesis process as the campaign's primary output. Starting with no predefined analysis pipeline, SynAgent adaptively generates analysis skills for newly acquired data and evolves this understanding through multimodal reasoning over experimental data such as X-ray diffraction patterns and electron micrographs. The evolution is guided by a verify-falsify scheme, in which the agent deliberately challenges its own hypotheses by testing conditions predicted to fail as well as those predicted to succeed. In a single campaign of 18 autonomous experiments using LiCoO2 (001) thin-film deposition as a testbed, SynAgent synthesized highly crystalline films and evolved an understanding of how the substrate temperature governs crystallization, discovering an abrupt threshold and a narrow optimal growth window at 650-690 °C. These results extend autonomous experimentation beyond optimized samples to testable, human-readable understanding.

</details>


### [92] [Low Temperature Halide Assisted HVPE Growth of Single Crystal AlN Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18647)
*K. Udwary,M. Smeaton,J. S. Mangum,R. Gannon,G. Dodson,B. Tellekamp,K. L. Schulte,J. H. Leach,J. Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过向生长表面直接注入无水氯化氢（HCl）气体，抑制低温生长时形成的反极性晶粒，从而在1000°C的石英反应腔中实现高质量单极性氮化铝（AlN）薄膜的卤化物气相外延（HVPE）生长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HVPE法生长低缺陷、单极性AlN需要远超1100°C（最佳结果约1400°C）的生长温度，以获得具有光滑形貌和高结晶质量的Al极性薄膜；但高温使石英反应腔难以使用（石英在1200°C开始软化），需专门设计反应器，故有必要探索降低生长温度的途径。

Method: 在生长过程中将无水氯化氢（anhydrous hydrogen chloride, HCl）气体直接注入生长表面，以抑制低温生长时形成的反极性（inverted polarity）晶粒；在石英反应腔中以1000°C的温度、20 µm/h的生长速率，在c面(0001)蓝宝石（sapphire）衬底上生长5 µm厚的AlN层，并采用X射线摇摆曲线（rocking curve）与电子背散射衍射（electron backscatter diffraction, EBSD）进行表征。

Result: 所得单晶AlN层的对称(0002)摇摆曲线反射半高全宽（full-width at half-maximum, FWHM）低于400 arcsec，非对称(10-12)反射低于1000 arcsec；EBSD测量显示，随自由HCl增加，反极性畴（inversion domains）被抑制，薄膜更加光滑且质量更高。

Conclusion: 直接向生长表面注入无水HCl可有效抑制低温下的反极性晶粒，使高质量单晶AlN层可在1000°C的石英反应腔中生长，从而规避传统高温生长对专用反应器的需求。

Abstract: Low defect, single polarity aluminum nitride layers grown by halide vapor phase epitaxy (HVPE) methods have classically needed growth temperatures well exceeding 1100°C with many of the best results being grown in the range of 1400°C. These high temperatures have typically been required to obtain Al-polar AlN films with smooth morphologies and high crystalline quality. However, these elevated temperatures make the use of quartz reactor chambers difficult, as quartz begins to soften at 1200°C, and requires specially designed reactors. This study examines the use of anhydrous hydrogen chloride (HCl) gas, injected directly to the growth surface, to suppress inverted polarity grains that form at low growth temperatures. This method allows for high quality, single crystal AlN layers with a growth temperature of just 1000°C in a quartz reactor chamber. With this method, AlN layers of 5 um thickness were grown on c-plane (0 0 0 1) sapphire, with a growth rate of 20 um/hour, and a resulting full-width at half-maximum (FWHM) of symmetric (0 0 0 2) rocking curve reflection under 400 arcsec and asymmetric (1 0 -1 2) reflection under 1000 arcsec. Electron backscatter diffraction measurements showed the suppression of inversion domains with increasing free HCl, resulting in smoother and higher quality films.

</details>


### [93] [Adaptive Substrate Support Based on Thin-Film Piezoelectric Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18653)
*Ertuğ Şimşek,Bas Jansen,Marcelo Ackermann,Muharrem Bayraktar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作首次演示了基于薄膜压电致动器（thin-film piezoelectric actuators）的主动式基底支撑（active substrate support）概念，用于纳米级基底平整度校正，原型器件位移超过9 nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进光刻扫描机（lithography scanners）需将基底平整度控制在纳米量级以避免焦点误差（focus errors）；静态支撑难以达到并维持该平整度，而体压电或线性致动器因布线（wiring）与体积限制，在需数千根支撑柱的场景下不可行。

Method: 提出基于薄膜压电致动器的主动式基底支撑概念，并详细给出原型器件（proof-of-principle device）的工作原理、制造步骤与表征，同时以有限元建模（finite element modeling, FEM）作为支撑。

Result: 所制器件实现超过9 nm的位移，足以校正在光刻系统中的部分焦点误差。

Conclusion: 该新概念有望为更小芯片尺寸（chip dimensions）与更高良率（yield）开辟路径。

Abstract: Advanced lithography scanners require extreme substrate flatness in the range of nanometers to prevent focus errors. Such a flatness is challenging to reach and maintain using static substrate supports. Active correction concepts using bulk piezoelectric or linear actuators become prohibitive due to wiring and volume limitations, considering the thousands of pillars required. In this work, we present to our knowledge the first ever demonstration of an active substrate support concept based on thin-film piezoelectric actuators. The working principle, fabrication steps, and characterization of a proof-of-principle device, supported by finite element modeling (FEM), are presented in detail. The fabricated device shows a displacement above 9 nm, which is sufficient to correct some of the focus errors in lithography systems. We foresee that this new concept may open the way towards smaller chip dimensions and improved yield.

</details>


### [94] [Photoresponse properties of single-crystalline thick film based on high-entropy topological insulator (Bi3/4Sb1/4)2(Te2/5Se2/5S1/5)3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18771)
*Alexei Vasilev,Marina Zhezhu,Oleg Ivanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次通过自蔓延高温合成与熔融法制备高熵拓扑绝缘体（high-entropy topological insulator）(Bi3/4Sb1/4)2(Te2/5Se2/5S1/5)3，并基于约0.1 mm厚单晶厚膜样品制成光电探测器，在可见光下表现出强光伏响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅指出该高熵拓扑绝缘体为首次制备，并将其应用于光电响应性质考察与光电探测器构建。

Method: 采用自蔓延高温合成（self-propagating high-temperature synthesis）与熔融法（melting methods）制备材料，并使用室温斩波光技术（room-temperature chopped-light technique）对约0.1 mm厚单晶厚膜样品进行光电响应测试。

Result: 光电探测器在白光LED照射（420-730 nm范围）下呈现强光伏响应；光电流随光强由100增至600 W/m2而从约20增至约55 mkA，源于光生非平衡载流子（non-equilibrium carriers）数量增加；室温响应度（responsivity）约8.7 mA/W，探测率（detectivity）约109 Jones。

Conclusion: 该高熵拓扑绝缘体可成功制备并用于光电探测，在可见光波段具备强光伏响应及一定的响应度与探测率。

Abstract: High-entropy topological insulator (Bi3/4Sb1/4)2(Te2/5Se2/5S1/5)3 has been for the first time prepared by self-propagating high-temperature synthesis and melting methods. Single-crystalline thick-filmed sample with thickness of ~0.1 mm was applied to examine the photoresponse properties by using room-temperature chopped-light technique. Photodetector based on (Bi3/4Sb1/4)2(Te2/5Se2/5S1/5)3 demonstrated strong photovoltaic response under irradiation of a white LED light, covering 420-730 nm range. The photocurrent increases from ~20 to ~55 mkA with the increase in intensity from 100 to 600 W/m2 that is originated from the increase in the number of non-equilibrium carriers generated by light. Room-temperature responsivity and detectivity of the photodetector are ~8.7 mA/W and ~109 Jones, respectively.

</details>


### [95] [Gauge-including neural-network quantum Monte Carlo for molecules in magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18826)
*Chengye Lü,Weizhong Fu,Xin-gao Gong,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出在神经网络量子蒙特卡洛（neural network quantum Monte Carlo, NNQMC）中引入规范相位因子（gauge-including phase factor），直接作用于多电子波函数，以处理磁场下的分子体系。方法改善了平移一致性与尺寸一致性，复现了 \ce{H2} 的弱场磁化率与强场键收缩，并用于白矮星磁场下 \ce{CN} 与 \ce{C2} 跃迁分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外磁场通过轨道与自旋耦合使关联电子态复杂化，并对波函数施加坐标依赖的相位，导致电子结构计算难度大幅上升。无核体系已可由 NNQMC 高精度处理，但分子体系在远离规范原点区域出现快速变化的相位，构成额外挑战。

Method: 引入直接作用于完整多电子波函数的规范包含相位因子（gauge-including phase factor），用于承载规定的外磁场相位，使神经网络只需学习更平滑的关联残差。该设计旨在提升分子的平移一致性（translation consistency）与尺寸一致性（size consistency）。

Result: 基于该方法复现了 \ce{H2} 的弱场磁化率（magnetizabilities）与强场键收缩（bond contraction）。进一步应用于与白矮星磁场相关的 \ce{CN} 红带系（red system）与 \ce{C2} 天鹅带系（Swan system）的选定跃迁；\ce{CN} 跃迁的场致位移显著大于 \ce{C2} 对应跃迁。

Conclusion: 该相位因子方法为在磁场下用 NNQMC 研究分子体系提供了可行路径。\ce{CN} 跃迁对磁场的高敏感性提示其可作为白矮星磁场的潜在探针。

Abstract: External magnetic fields, through their coupling to orbital and spin motion, complicate the correlated electronic states and impose coordinate-dependent phases on the wavefunction, thereby making accurate electronic structure calculations substantially more demanding. Recently, neural network-based quantum Monte Carlo (NNQMC) has emerged as a highly accurate approach to study nucleus-free systems in magnetic fields. For molecular systems, however, things get more complicated as the magnetic field would introduce a rapidly varying phase in the region far from the gauge origin. Here we introduce a gauge-including phase factor that acts directly on the full many-electron wavefunction and accounts for the prescribed magnetic phase, leaving a smoother correlated residual for the network to learn. This factor greatly improves molecular translation consistency and size consistency, providing a route for studying systems in magnetic fields with NNQMC. Upon this approach, we reproduce weak-field magnetizabilities and strong-field bond contraction in \ce{H2}. We further apply the method to selected transitions in the \ce{CN} red and \ce{C2} Swan systems at magnetic fields relevant to white dwarfs. The \ce{CN} transition exhibits a much larger field-induced shift than its \ce{C2} counterpart, suggesting its potential as a probe of white-dwarf magnetic fields.

</details>


### [96] [Epitaxial inversion of spontaneous polarization in ε-Ga2O3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18843)
*Yan Wang,Zhigao Xie,Weihua Tang,Chee Keong Tan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究解决了正交晶系 ε-Ga2O3 绝对自发极化（spontaneous polarization, Psp）矢量方向的长期歧义，并通过衬底工程实现其宏观极性的可控生长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性宽带隙半导体（polar wide bandgap semiconductors）可调控内建电场并诱导二维电子气（two-dimensional electron gas, 2DEG），而 ε-Ga2O3 因大自发极化而具有潜力；但其绝对 Psp 矢量方向不明且外延取向难以控制，制约了材料应用。

Method: 将干涉型压电响应（interferometric piezoresponse）与原子构型相关联，建立以不对称四原子序列中的几何高度为判据的极性识别标准；据此在 Al 极性 AlN 与蓝宝石（sapphire）上进行外延以调控宏观极性。

Result: 在 Al 极性 AlN 上外延得到均匀的 Ga 极性（Psp 向上）结构，在蓝宝石上得到 O 极性（Psp 向下）结构；当结晶质量恶化时该对应关系被破坏，出现混合极性（mixed-polarity）。

Conclusion: 该原子尺度到宏观尺度的关联消除了非中心对称氧化物（non-centrosymmetric oxides）绝对极性取向的长期歧义，为在异质界面设计宏观极化不连续性、推动 ε-Ga2O3 用于先进极化电子学（polarization electronics）提供了基础平台。

Abstract: Polar wide bandgap semiconductors offer the unique capability to manipulate internal electric fields and induce two-dimensional electron gases (2DEG). The emerging orthorhombic ε-Ga2O3 is a promising candidate owing to its large spontaneous polarization (Psp). However, exploiting this material is hindered by fundamental ambiguities surrounding its absolute Psp vectors and the inability to govern its epitaxial direction. Here we show the resolution of the absolute Psp vectors in ε-Ga2O3 and control of its macroscopic polarity via substrate engineering. By correlating interferometric piezoresponse with atomic configurations, we establish an identification criterion where opposing polarities are assigned through the geometric elevation within an asymmetric four-atom sequence. Guided by this signature, we demonstrate that epitaxy on Al-polar AlN and sapphire yields uniformly Ga-polar (upward Psp) and O-polar (downward Psp) architectures, respectively, whereas deteriorated crystallinity disrupts this registry and triggers mixed-polarity. This atomic-to-macroscopic correlation eliminates long-standing ambiguities regarding absolute polar orientations in non-centrosymmetric oxides. Analogous to mature III-Nitride architectures, this blueprint provides the foundational platform for designing macroscopic polarization discontinuities at heterointerfaces, unlocking ε-Ga2O3 for advanced polarization electronics.

</details>


### [97] [Effect of independent parameters on nanoparticle sizes in magnetron-sputtering inert-gas condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19043)
*Yizhou Wang,Evropi Toulkeridouc,Abisegapriyan K. S,Yair Ein-Eli,Panagiotis Grammatikopoulos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 磁控溅射惰性气体凝聚（MS-IGC）可规模化制备定制纳米颗粒（NPs），但工艺参数影响机制复杂、输出难预测。本文剖析典型 MS-IGC 系统机制，借助多元线性回归（multiple linear regression）分离各沉积参数效应，量化其对 NP 尺寸及尺寸分布的影响。结果显示，延长初生 NPs 在凝聚腔（condensation chamber）内停留时间的参数（尤以出口喷嘴直径最为显著）可正向影响最终 NP 尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MS-IGC 具备可规模化、环境友好的气相合成（vapour-phase synthesis）优势，可通过调节沉积参数制备具有定制性能的纳米颗粒。然而该高水平调控存在代价：合成机制受沉积参数复杂影响，输出常难以预测，故需系统厘清参数调控规律以指导优化。

Method: 报道典型 MS-IGC 系统的工作机制，依靠凝聚腔、筛选腔、沉积腔三个真空腔室的协同运行，实现纳米团簇（nanoclusters）的原位合成、尺寸筛选与定向沉积。系统考察惰性气体流量（inert-gas flows）、聚集长度（aggregation length）等关键工艺参数对纳米团簇形成、尺寸分布与沉积行为的调控规律，并采用多元线性回归分析分离各沉积参数的独立效应。

Result: 多元线性回归实现了对每个沉积参数效应的分离，并量化其对 NP 尺寸及尺寸分布的影响。结果表明，可延长初生 NPs 在凝聚腔内停留时间的参数对最终 NP 尺寸具有正向作用，其中出口喷嘴直径（exit nozzle diameter）的影响最为显著。

Conclusion: 研究深化了对 NP 形成机理的理解，为改进实验控制与工艺优化（process optimisation）提供了依据。参数调控规律的量化有助于合理选择工艺参数取值，从而获得更优化的输出。

Abstract: Magnetron-sputtering inert-gas condensation (MS-IGC) provides a scalable, environmentally friendly vapour-phase synthesis approach for preparing customised nanoparticles (NPs) with bespoke properties via fine-tuning several deposition parameters. However, this high-level control comes with a caveat: the synthesis mechanisms are affected by deposition parameters in complicated ways, often yielding unpredictable outputs. This report details the working mechanism of a typical MS-IGC system, achieving in situ synthesis, size screening, and directional deposition of nanoclusters through the synergistic operation of the three vacuum chambers (condensation, screening, and deposition). The study systematically explores the regulation laws of multiple key process parameters (e.g., inert-gas flows, aggregation length, etc.) on the formation, size distribution, and deposition behaviour of nanoclusters, to rationalise their chosen values toward optimised output. To this end, multiple linear regression analysis was performed to isolate the effect of each deposition parameter and thus quantify its effect on the NP size and size distribution. Our results indicate that parameters that may prolong the nascent NPs' residence inside the condensation chamber (most prominently, the exit nozzle diameter) can positively affect the final NP size. This study expands the understanding of NP formation, enabling improved experimental control and process optimisation.

</details>


### [98] [An Atlas and Design Rules for Single- and Dual-Atom Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19087)
*Fabian Berger,Yicheng Wang,E. Charles H. Sykes,Angelos Michaelides*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）高通量筛选，构建了Cu与Ag表面过渡金属单原子及双原子合金（single- and dual-atom alloys）的稳定性图谱，揭示掺杂原子配对稳定性遵循d电子计数规律，并提出“反应器锚定（reactor-anchor）”概念以实现表面掺杂结构的设计调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异相催化（heterogeneous catalysis）、材料科学与凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）中，按预设局域原子排列设计合金是长期目标；稀浓度三金属合金（dilute trimetallic alloys）虽可解锁双金属体系无法实现的化学性质，但需在巨大组分空间中确定何种掺杂结构能够形成。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论筛选，构建覆盖Cu和Ag表面上过渡金属单原子与双原子合金的图谱（atlas），并通过扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）进行验证。

Result: 稳定性遵循电子计数规律：当掺杂原子的d电子计数之和接近十时配对最强；宿主金属（host metal）与表面晶面（surface facet）可调控活性位点构型（active site motifs），尺寸失配（size mismatch）与自旋（spin）解释该趋势周围的偏差。此外提出反应器锚定概念，即一个掺杂原子可将另一个原本体相偏析（bulk-segregating）的掺杂原子锚定于表面。

Conclusion: 上述结果确立了面向目标活性位点结构进行合金工程设计的设计原则（design principles）。

Abstract: A long-standing goal across heterogeneous catalysis, materials science, and condensed matter physics is to design alloys with prescribed local atomic arrangements. Recent experiments show that dilute trimetallic alloys unlock chemistries inaccessible to bimetallics, but realizing this potential requires knowing which dopant structures form across an enormous compositional space. Using density functional theory screening, we construct an atlas spanning transition metal single- and dual-atom alloys in Cu and Ag surfaces, which we validate by scanning tunneling microscopy. The stability follows an electron count: dopants pair most strongly when their combined d-electron count approaches ten. Host metal and surface facet can be used to tune the resulting active site motifs, while size mismatch and spin explain variations around this trend. We further introduce a reactor-anchor concept, in which one dopant anchors a second, otherwise bulk-segregating dopant at the surface. Together, these results establish design principles for engineering alloys with targeted active site structures.

</details>


### [99] [Divergence between long- and short-wavelength magnon damping in spinel ferrites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19112)
*Christopher T. Parzyck,Octave Duros,Hari Paudyal,Noah M. Edmiston,Katya Mikhailova,Lerato Takana,Daisy O'Mahoney,Sauviz P. Alaei,Daniel J. Foster,Hiroki Suga,Naoya Kurahashi,Jun Miyawaki,Michael E. Flatte,Georgi Dakovski,Yuri Suzuki,Durga Paudyal,Wei-Sheng Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合铁磁共振（ferromagnetic resonance, FMR）与共振非弹性X射线散射（resonant inelastic x-ray scattering, RIXS），在尖晶石铁氧体 Li$_{0.5}$Al$_x$Fe$_{2.5-x}$O$_4$ 中同时探测短波长与长波长极限的磁振子（magnon），揭示铝替代对两种极限下阻尼影响的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现实用高速磁振子（magnonic）器件需在宽频范围内制备低耗散磁性材料，但基于光学与微波探针推断的 $q\sim0$ 低能/长波长模式阻尼，能否推广至更高能量、有限动量模式，仍是未解问题。

Method: 采用铁磁共振（FMR）与共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）联用技术，对尖晶石铁氧体 Li$_{0.5}$Al$_x$Fe$_{2.5-x}$O$_4$ 进行表征，从而在同一材料体系中探测短波长与长波长极限下的磁振子行为。

Result: 铝替代既显著降低磁振子带宽，又大幅缩短高 $q$ 磁振子寿命，这与由 FMR 推断出的极低磁振子阻尼形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 非磁性替代元素对磁振子阻尼的影响在长波长与短波长极限间存在不一致性，为磁振子器件候选材料的评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: The realization of practical, high-speed magnonic devices requires engineering magnetic materials with low dissipation over wide frequency ranges. While optical and microwave probes are used to infer the damping of low energy/long wavelength modes, the degree to which these $q\sim0$ properties translate into higher-energy, finite-momentum modes remains an important open question. Here, we utilize a combination of ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) and resonant inelastic x-ray scattering on spinel ferrites Li$_{0.5}$Al$_x$Fe$_{2.5-x}$O$_4$ to probe magnons in both the short- and long-wavelength limits. We observe that aluminum substitution both markedly reduces the magnon bandwidth and drastically shortens the high-$q$ magnon lifetimes, in sharp contrast to the ultralow magnon damping inferred from FMR. These findings demonstrate a disparity between how non-magnetic substituents impact magnon damping in the long- and short-wavelength limits, providing a new perspective for assessing candidate materials for magnonic devices.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [The Golden Junction of Gaussian Covariance Geometries: Sector Anisotropy, Relative-Entropy Response, and Local Penalties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17574)
*Christian Kerskens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在统一的迹正交归一（trace-orthonormal）约定下，比较中心化单模玻色高斯协方差（centered one-mode bosonic Gaussian covariances）的四种局部几何，指出其行列式（determinant）在特定点交汇，并给出黄金比（φ）标度惩罚结构及操作含义的区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心化单模玻色高斯协方差可承载多种几何（Bures、Fisher--Rao、Bures--Wasserstein、Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori），但这些几何的操作意义常被混淆。该文旨在统一归一化下厘清它们的行列式关系与结构差异。

Method: 采用共同迹正交归一化比较四种局部几何的行列式；引入常数作用量匹配参数（action-matching parameter）η后考察 Bures--Wasserstein 行列式的交点；分析 Bures 余度量（cometric）与 Fisher--Rao 的相对结构，并用归一化 BKM 度量的形状余度量本征值作为诊断；辅以显式 sp(4,R) 测试检验未加权右不变 Frobenius 门范数。

Result: 在共同归一化下，Bures 与协方差 Fisher--Rao 余度量行列式在 x²=φ 处内在交叉；引入 η 后 Bures--Wasserstein 行列式仅在 (x,η)=(√φ,√φ) 与之汇合。交点处相对 Fisher--Rao 的 Bures 余度量为 Q_gold=φ⁻²Π₀+φΠ₂，det Q_gold=1，其逆给出标量到权重二的惩罚比恰为 φ³。归一化 BKM 度量与 Bures 径向一致，但形状余度量本征值为 4ħ²x/log[(x+1)/(x-1)]，故不构成第四个行列式交点，并在纯态边界出现切向刚性。

Conclusion: 操作上，权重二运动由仅系统的高斯幺正（system-only Gaussian unitary）生成，径向运动则需高斯信道或辅助膨胀（ancillary dilation）。已知的 Uhlmann--Ruan 纯化商实现局部 Bures 态度量，但显式 sp(4,R) 测试表明未加权右不变 Frobenius 门范数无法复现黄金惩罚。条件分支选择与有限速率 Bures--Wasserstein 代价分离被列为次级陈述，而非动力学相变。

Abstract: We compare four local geometries of centered one-mode bosonic Gaussian covariances while keeping their operational meanings distinct. In a common trace-orthonormal normalization, the Bures and covariance Fisher--Rao cometric determinants cross intrinsically at \(x^2=\varphi\). After introducing a constant action-matching parameter \(η\), the Bures--Wasserstein determinant joins them only at \((x,η)=(\sqrt\varphi,\sqrt\varphi)\). Equal determinant density does not mean tensor equality: relative to Fisher--Rao, the Bures cometric at the junction is \[
  Q_{\rm gold}=\varphi^{-2}Π_0+\varphiΠ_2,
  \qquad \det Q_{\rm gold}=1, \] so its inverse has the exact scalar-to-weight-two quadratic penalty ratio \(\varphi^3\). The normalized Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori metric supplies an independent relative-entropy diagnostic. It agrees radially with Bures but has shape cometric eigenvalue \(4\hbar^2x/\log[(x+1)/(x-1)]\); it therefore does not form a fourth determinant junction and becomes tangentially stiff at the pure-state boundary. Operationally, weight-two motion is generated by a system-only Gaussian unitary, whereas radial motion requires a Gaussian channel or ancillary dilation. The known Uhlmann--Ruan purification quotient realizes the local Bures state metric, but an explicit \(\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb R)\) test shows that an unweighted right-invariant Frobenius gate norm does not reproduce the golden penalty. Conditional branch selection and finite-rate Bures--Wasserstein cost separation are presented as secondary statements, not as dynamical phase transitions.

</details>


### [101] [Depth analysis of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with a Grover mixer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17835)
*Bojko N. Bakalov,Dimitar Grantcharov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究采用 Grover 混频器（Grover mixer）且代价角与混频角独立采样的量子近似优化算法（Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm, QAOA），证明在初始态所携带代价值的格点条件（lattice condition）下，损失方差及其关于末次混频角导数的深度无关下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA 的优化景观（landscape）是否存在贫瘠高原（barren plateau）等问题关乎其可训练性；本文旨在为带 Grover 混频器与独立采样角度的变体给出与深度无关的方差下界，从而刻画其优化行为的结构性限制。

Method: 在初始态携带的代价值满足格点条件的前提下，对独立采样的代价角与混频角进行解析分析，推导损失方差的下界及对最后一个混频角偏导数的同类下界，并建立 Grover 型可达性（reachability）界。

Result: 得到与深度无关的损失方差下界，且对末次混频角的导数具有相同下界；该估计依赖具体实例，在固定局域性（locality）下，对局部项一致有界的整数值局部目标函数（尤其 MaxCut）表现为量子比特数的逆多项式；同时给出可达性下界，表明近似指定携带本征子空间（carried eigenspace）所需深度不低于其初始概率平方根倒数的常数倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明该 QAOA 变体在格点条件下不存在随深度衰减至零的方差（即无深度诱导的贫瘠高原），但其可训练性受逆多项式量级与可达性下界共同约束。

Abstract: We study the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with a Grover mixer and independently sampled cost and mixing angles. Under a lattice condition on the cost values carried by the initial state, we prove a depth-independent lower bound for the variance of the loss at every depth, together with the same bound for the derivative with respect to the final mixing angle. The estimate is instance-dependent and, for fixed locality, is inverse polynomial in the number of qubits for integer-valued local objective functions with uniformly bounded local terms, in particular for MaxCut. We also establish Grover-type reachability bounds showing that the depth required to approximate a prescribed carried eigenspace is bounded below by a constant multiple of the inverse square root of its initial probability.

</details>


### [102] [Rényi and Tsallis information entropies for a harmonic position-dependent mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17941)
*Dorcas A. Addo,Daniel Sabi-Takou,Eugene Adjei,Latévi M. Lawson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究计算了在谐振子势（harmonic oscillator potential）中具有位置依赖质量（position-dependent mass）的哈密顿系统的 Renyi 与 Tsallis 信息熵（information entropies），并检验了相关的熵不确定关系（entropic uncertainty relations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置依赖质量系统在凝聚态与量子阱等物理情境中具有意义，而 Renyi 与 Tsallis 信息熵作为 Shannon 熵的推广可刻画其信息性质；但位置表象下这些熵的积分泛函形式难以直接求解。故研究旨在评估该系统在两表象下的信息熵与不确定性。

Method: 采用 Gegenbauer 多项式（Gegenbauer polynomials）获取位置本征函数，利用第二类修正 Bessel 函数（modified Bessel function of the second kind）确定等价动量本征函数；基于两个表象的概率密度解析与数值计算类海森堡不确定性（Heisenberg-like uncertainty）。对位置表象下的 Renyi 与 Tsallis 熵，在系统渐近极限（对应未变形谐振子行为）下求值，而动量表象下则未使用近似方法。

Result: 两个表象下，当熵参数 α→1 时，Renyi 与 Tsallis 信息熵趋近 Shannon 熵，且结果与文献中类似模型接近；数值计算的熵不确定关系验证了上述观测。

Conclusion: Renyi 与 Tsallis 信息熵在该位置依赖质量谐振子系统中表现出与 Shannon 熵的一致性及与既有模型的可比性，且所导出的熵不确定关系得到数值支持。
