Abstract: In this paper, we study Renyi and Tsallis information entropies for a Hamiltonian system with position-dependent mass confined in harmonic oscillator potential. Gegenbauer polynomials are used to obtain the position eigenfunction of such a system, and the modified Bessel function of the second kind is used to determine the equivalent momentum eigenfunction. By means of probability densities of both representations, we analytically and numerically evaluate the Heisenberg-like uncertainty of this system. Because the Renyi and Tsallis information entropies in position representation are described by integral functionals of the Gegenbauer polynomials, these quantities are much more difficult to calculate. To get around this problem, we evaluate these information entropies at the system s asymptotical limit, which corresponds to the behavior of an undeformed harmonic oscillator. Nevertheless, no approximation method is used to obtain the Tsallis and Renyi information entropies in momentum representation. In both representations, we find that these information entropies approach the Shannon entropy when the entropic parameter alpha-> 1 and are closed to the results of similar models of the literature. Finally, we evaluate the related entropic uncertainty relations numerically to validate the latter obersevations.

</details>


### [103] [Unbounded Holevo additivity gaps in finite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18222)
*Jinzhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在有限维情形下构造了无界的两用 Holevo 可加性间隙（two-use Holevo additivity gaps），间隙随输出量子比特数线性增长，代价为输入维度按 Θ_K(n^2) 二次增长，并给出明确的 n 阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典容量（classical capacity）与 Holevo 量（Holevo quantity）在多用情形下的可加性问题是量子信息论的核心问题之一。该工作旨在有限维中给出两用可加性的定量违反证据。

Method: 构造输出维度为 K^n、输入维度为 exp(Θ_K(n^2)) 的信道 T_n，来源为 Collins 自由概率（free probability）证明中所用互补混合酉信道（complementary mixed-unitary channels）的结构化张量积；并借助 Collins–Youn 的积群 Haagerup 不等式与 Bordenave–Collins 的定量强收敛估计建立最小输出熵间隙。

Result: 对足够大的固定整数 K 及所有足够大的 n，得到 χ(T_n^{⊗2}) - 2χ(T_n) ≥ n[log_2 K / K - 2 log_2(1+9/K)] - O(1/n)；间隙在输出量子比特上线性，输入量子比特代价为二次且显式。此外还存在单用 Holevo 量趋于零、而两用 Holevo 信息（进而经典容量）发散的信道。

Conclusion: 结果表明有限维信道的 Holevo 可加性可在两用层面被无界地违反，且该违反由结构化张量积构造与自由概率工具组合所实现。

Abstract: We establish unbounded two-use Holevo additivity gaps in finite dimensions. For each sufficiently large fixed integer $K$ and all sufficiently large $n$, we construct channels $T_n$ with output dimension $K^n$, input dimension $\exp(Θ_K(n^2))$, and \[
  χ(T_n^{\otimes2})-2χ(T_n)
  \ge n\left[\frac{\log_2K}{K}-2\log_2(1+9/K)\right]-O(1/n). \] The gap is linear in output qubits, with an explicit quadratic input-qubit cost and an explicit threshold on $n$. We also obtain channels whose single-use Holevo quantity tends to zero while their two-use Holevo information, and hence classical capacity, diverges. The channels arise from structured tensor products of the complementary mixed-unitary channels used in Collins's free-probabilistic proof. We establish minimum-output-entropy gaps by combining Collins--Youn's product-group Haagerup inequality with Bordenave--Collins's quantitative strong-convergence estimates

</details>


### [104] [Information capacity of quantum statistics: Fock-state tests of a discrete binary-sequence model on cloud photonic quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10216)
*Chiran Wijesundara,Octavia T. Volpe,Dejan Stojkovic,Herbert Fotso,Tim Thomay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将量子力学（quantum mechanics）视为某种更基本离散理论的统计极限（statistical limit），并利用商用云光子量子处理器（cloud photonic quantum processors）对系统的信息容量（information capacity）给出下限约束。实验在可编程干涉仪（programmable interferometers）上测量光子 Fock 态，排除了 $n \\le 100$，表明双光子态寄存器若容量有限亦超过 $10^2$，并将 $10^{-3}$（$n \\sim 10^3$）下限向现有硬件开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心前提是量子力学可能为更基本离散理论的统计极限，此类理论赋予物理系统有限信息容量，其对量子统计的偏离由系统使用容量的比例控制。因此需要实验判定该容量是否有限，而商用云光子处理器被认为已达到所需精度。

Method: 以 Powers 等人的二元序列模型（binary-sequence model）作为具体检验理论：结果概率来自长度为 $n$ 的离散序列计数，量子力学在 $n \\to \\infty$ 时恢复，$n$ 度量寄存器的信息容量。在可编程干涉仪上测量光子 Fock 态 $|1\\rangle$、$|1,1\\rangle$、预报 $|2\\rangle$ 及级联分束器对（cascaded beam-splitter pairs），并原位确定主导系统误差；采用要求旋转可复合的复合一致参数化。

Result: 复合一致参数化以 $1.24/n$ 的偏差恢复量子力学；经参数自助法（parametric bootstrap）校准的随机效应似然分析排除了所有 $n \\le 100$，即承载双光子态寄存器的信息容量若有限则超过 $10^2$。级联分束器直接检验复合律：数据满足分裂不变性（split-invariance），以 $8\\sigma$ 排除朴素计数复合，并确证干涉符号规则。与模型无关地，曲线平均偏离量子配分律（quantum partition law）大于 $2.3\\times10^{-2}$ 者在 95% 置信水平（CL）下被排除，原发表的线性参数化被直接排除。

Conclusion: 由于编译偏移（compilation offset）按电路冻结而可校准，$10^{-3}$ 下限（$n \\sim 10^3$）对当前硬件开放，云光子处理器可作为量子基础（quantum foundations）研究的定量仪器，信息容量亦可成为可由实验约束的量。

Abstract: Our central premise is that quantum mechanics may be the statistical limit of a more fundamental discrete theory: any such theory equips a physical system with a finite information capacity, and its departure from quantum statistics is controlled by how much of that capacity the system uses. We show that commercial cloud photonic quantum processors have reached the precision required to bound this capacity from below, using the binary-sequence model of Powers et al. as the concrete test theory: outcome probabilities arise from counting discrete sequences of length $n$, quantum mechanics is recovered as $n \to \infty$, and $n$ measures the information capacity of the register behind a prepared state. Photon Fock states $|1\rangle$, $|1,1\rangle$, heralded $|2\rangle$, and cascaded beam-splitter pairs are measured on programmable interferometers with dominant systematics determined in situ. The model's composition-consistent parametrization, singled out by requiring that rotations compose, recovers quantum mechanics with deviations $1.24/n$; a random-effects likelihood analysis calibrated by parametric bootstrap excludes all $n \le 100$: the information capacity of the register carrying the two-photon state, if finite, exceeds $10^2$. Cascaded beam splitters test the composition law directly: the data are split-invariant, excluding naive count composition at $8σ$ and confirming the interference-sign rule. Model-independently, curve-averaged deviations from the quantum partition law larger than $2.3\times10^{-2}$ are excluded at 95% CL, and the originally published linear parametrization is excluded outright. Because the compilation offset is frozen per circuit it is calibratable, opening the $10^{-3}$ floor ($n \sim 10^3$) to current hardware: cloud photonic processors are quantitative instruments for quantum foundations, and information capacity an experimentally boundable quantity.

</details>


### [105] [Spectral Optimization for Absolutely PPT States: Purity, Entropy, and Volume Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18568)
*Anh T. Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究绝对正部分转置（absolutely positive partial transpose, APPT）态的最大纯度与最小冯·诺依曼熵（von Neumann entropy），并给出其谱集的相对体积。通过显式外谱多胞体得到 APPT 态纯度上界 4/(3mn)，改进了绝对可分离态的 2/(mn) 界，并否证了 Dũng–Khôi 猜想，同时给出熵下界与相对体积的指数衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于绝对可分离态（absolutely separable states）的纯度上界为 2/(mn)，而 APPT 态是包含绝对可分离态的更大类，其纯度、熵与谱集体积的量化尚不完整。Dũng–Khôi 关于 qutrit–qudit 的猜想亦有待检验。

Method: 由 Hildebrand 判据（Hildebrand's criterion）导出显式外谱多胞体（outer spectral polytope）并对其顶点进行分类；在该多胞体上优化纯度，结合 Song–Chen 关于 2⊗3 系统的结果进行推广；同时构造内多胞体（inner polytope）以估计相对谱体积。

Result: 对任意 mn≥6，APPT 态纯度上界渐近为 4/(3mn)，且在 2⊗n（n≥3）系统中是紧的；在 3⊗n（n≥3）系统中唯一外多胞体极点非 APPT，故界严格并否证 Dũng–Khôi 猜想。多胞体给出任意两体维度下的显式熵下界与 2⊗n 系统精确最小熵；内外多胞体体积公式给出 a_n 的两侧界（比值小于 4 且趋于 3），故 a_n=Θ(√n(4/27)^n)，相对体积的精确指数衰减率为 ln(27/4)。

Conclusion: APPT 态的纯度与熵可由外谱多胞体有效刻画，所得上界优于绝对可分离态且对 2⊗n 系统紧致，同时否证 Dũng–Khôi 猜想。APPT 谱集（等价于绝对可分离谱集）的相对体积具有精确指数衰减率 ln(27/4)。

Abstract: We study maximum purity and minimum von Neumann entropy of absolutely positive partial transpose (APPT) states, together with the relative volume of their spectral sets. A consequence of Hildebrand's criterion gives an explicit outer spectral polytope, whose vertices we classify. Optimizing purity over this polytope, together with the Song--Chen result for the $2\otimes3$ system, yields, for every $mn\ge6$, an explicit upper bound on the purity of APPT states that is asymptotic to $4/(3mn)$. This improves the previous $2/(mn)$ upper bound for absolutely separable states. The new bound applies to every APPT state, a class containing all absolutely separable states, and is sharp for every $2\otimes n$ system with $n\ge3$. For every $3\otimes n$ system with $n\ge3$, however, the unique outer-polytope maximizer is not APPT, so the bound is strict and disproves the Dũng--Khôi qutrit--qudit conjecture. The polytope also gives an explicit entropy lower bound in arbitrary bipartite dimensions and, together with the Song--Chen extreme-point classification, the exact minimum entropy for every $2\otimes n$ system. Finally, exact formulas for the relative volumes of an inner polytope and the outer spectral polytope give explicit two-sided bounds on the qubit--qudit relative spectral volume $a_n$ whose ratio is less than $4$ and tends to $3$. Consequently, $a_n=Θ\!\left(\sqrt n(4/27)^n\right)$, and the relative volume of the qubit--qudit APPT spectral set (equivalently, the absolutely separable spectral set) has exact exponential decay rate $\ln(27/4)$.

</details>


### [106] [Feasibility Ordering of Entanglement-Source Placement for Qubit Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18803)
*Samuel Marquez Gonzalez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明：对任意量子比特信道（qubit channel），若串行复合（sequential composition）仍能保持纠缠，则对应的并行作用（parallel action）不可能完全湮灭纠缠。由此确认了近期关于“中点放置（midpoint placement）在可行性意义下最优”的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究沿两条噪声量子比特信道构成的通信线路中纠缠源（entanglement source）的最优放置问题。近期工作从解析与数值角度主张中点放置至少不劣于端点放置（endpoint placement），本文旨在给出严格证明。

Method: 证明基于近期工作提出的转置因子化判据（transpose-factorization criterion）。利用量子Sinkhorn缩放（quantum Sinkhorn scaling）将严格正量子比特信道化为单位（unital）代表元，再借助单位量子比特信道的特殊标准形（normal form）实现转置映射通过原信道的因子化；并通过去极化正则化（depolarizing regularization）与纠缠破坏信道集合的闭性（closedness of the set of entanglement-breaking channels）推广至任意信道。

Result: 对任意量子比特信道得到：若 Λ1 ⊗ Λ2 ∈ EA，则 Λ2 ∘ Λ1 ∈ EB；交换两信道次序后，相反复合顺序亦成立。

Conclusion: 结果证明了近期猜想：就原定义下的可行性（feasibility）意义而言，中点放置对所有量子比特信道均为最优。

Abstract: This work studies the placement of an entanglement source along a communication line formed by two noisy qubit channels. Recent work argued, on analytical and numerical grounds, that midpoint placement should be at least as favorable as endpoint placement. Here it is shown that, for arbitrary qubit channels, if a sequential composition can preserve entanglement, then the corresponding parallel action cannot annihilate all entanglement. The proof uses the transpose-factorization criterion introduced in that recent work. Quantum Sinkhorn scaling converts every strictly positive qubit channel into a unital representative, and the special normal form of unital qubit channels then yields the required factorization of the transposed map through the original channel. Depolarizing regularization and the closedness of the set of entanglement-breaking channels extend the result to arbitrary channels. Consequently, $Λ_1 \otimes Λ_2 \in \mathrm{EA}$ implies $Λ_2 \circ Λ_1 \in \mathrm{EB}$, and, by exchanging the two channels, the same holds for the opposite composition order. This proves the recent conjecture that midpoint placement is optimal for all qubit channels, in the feasibility sense in which that optimality was originally defined.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum Behaviors Are Not Semialgebraic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18865)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Chenghua Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文否定了 Tsirelson 于 1993 年提出的问题：在贝尔场景（Bell scenario）中，双方各作四个二值测量时，有限维量子行为集合、其闭包以及交换算子集合均不是半代数集（semialgebraic set），甚至在某经典行为局部也不存在有限的实解析描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsirelson 提出的量子行为集合是否可由有限个多项式等式与不等式的布尔组合刻画（即是否半代数）这一问题自 1993 年以来一直悬而未决，该研究旨在给出明确回答。

Method: 通过构造性论证：在每方四个二值测量（four binary measurements per party）的贝尔场景下，考察有限维量子行为集合、其闭包与交换算子（commuting-operator）集合的可描述性，并针对某特定经典行为分析其局部是否具有有限实解析（real-analytic）描述。

Result: 上述三类集合均为非半代数集；更强地，其中任何一个在特定经典行为附近局部都不具备有限的实解析描述。

Conclusion: 该结果排除了精确的有限半定表示（semidefinite representation），并表明 Navascués-Pironio-Acín 层级（hierarchy）的任何有限层级都无法精确刻画这些集合。

Abstract: The conditional probabilities achievable by local measurements on a shared quantum state in a Bell scenario form the set of quantum behaviors, whose structure was studied by Tsirelson. In 1993, Tsirelson asked whether this set is semialgebraic, that is, describable by finite Boolean combinations of polynomial equations and inequalities. This question has remained open. We answer it in the negative: with four binary measurements per party, the set of finite-dimensional quantum behaviors, its closure, and the commuting-operator set are all nonsemialgebraic. More strongly, none admits a finite real-analytic description even locally near a particular classical behavior. These results rule out exact finite semidefinite representations and show that no finite level of the Navascués-Pironio-Acín hierarchy characterizes these sets exactly.

</details>


### [108] [Optimal Temporal Hiding in Correlated Quantum Reference-Frame Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18948)
*Maxim V. Churilov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将有限群值时间参考系（temporal reference frame）刻画为关联随机酉过程，证明在载波包含群的全部不可约表示时，时间隐藏（temporal hiding）等价于 $q$ 元编码问题，并给出嵌套码的载荷-深度-距离界与相干隐藏的量子擦除纠错界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间参考系通常被视为一组独立信道的列表，但有限群值情形下其轨迹定律具有内在关联性，需重新刻画其可操作性。该文旨在将时间隐藏问题转化为可量化的编码问题，并区分经典轨迹隐私与相干时间隐私。

Method: 将有限群值时间参考系建模为关联随机酉过程，识别其完整轨迹定律成为精确操作坐标的区间。在载波含 $G$ 全部不可约表示时，用单次辅助输入分辨所有群元素；对 $G^n$ 上的任意定律 $\mu,\nu$，证明策略半距离等于 $d_{\mathrm{TV}}(\mu,\nu)$，且最优输出侧因果后处理归结为 $G^n$ 上的经典卷积。随后将问题映射为 $[n,k]_q$ 码的陪集定律分析及嵌套码 $C\subset D$ 的界，并推导投影秩泄漏剖面与相干叠加隐藏的量子擦除纠错条件。

Result: $[n,k]_q$ 码的均匀陪集定律在任意少于 $d(C^\perp)$ 个槽位上完全相同、全局可完美区分。对 $M$ 个扇区、泄漏至多 $\eta$、全局解码误差 $\epsilon$，得 $(1-\epsilon)\log M \leq (n-t)\log q+\eta+h_2(\epsilon)$。嵌套码满足 $R+t+d_{\mathrm{rel}}(D,C)-1\leq n$ 与 $R+t+2e+f\leq n$，其中 $R=\dim D-\dim C$、$t=d_{\mathrm{rel}}(C^\perp,D^\perp)-1$；嵌套广义 Reed-Solomon 码（generalized Reed-Solomon codes）在其存在范围内达到这些界。相干叠加隐藏满足 $\log_q K+2t\leq n$。此外给出精确的投影秩泄漏剖面。

Conclusion: 时间隐藏可精确转化为编码问题：经典轨迹隐私由嵌套码的载荷-深度-距离权衡刻画，相干时间隐私则等价于量子擦除纠错，两者可被明确分离。该框架量化了在约化过程层析（reduced process tomography）下稳健隐藏的有效载荷。

Abstract: A finite group-valued temporal reference frame defines a correlated random-unitary process rather than a list of independent channels. We identify a regime in which its complete trajectory law is an exact operational coordinate. If the carrier contains every irreducible representation of the finite group $G$, one ancilla-assisted input resolves all group elements, and for arbitrary laws $μ,ν$ on $G^n$ the strategy half-distance equals $d_{\mathrm{TV}}(μ,ν)$. Optimal output-side causal post-processing likewise reduces to classical convolution on $G^n$. This converts temporal hiding into a $q$-ary coding problem. Uniform coset laws of an $[n,k]_q$ code are identical on every set of fewer than $d(C^\perp)$ slots and perfectly distinguishable globally. For $M$ sectors with leakage at most $η$ from $t$ selected slots and global decoding error $ε$, we obtain $(1-ε)\log M \leq (n-t)\log q+η+h_2(ε)$. For nested codes $C\subset D$, the payload $R=\dim D-\dim C$, invisible depth $t=d_{\mathrm{rel}}(C^\perp,D^\perp)-1$, and sector distance $d_{\mathrm{rel}}(D,C)$ obey $R+t+d_{\mathrm{rel}}(D,C)-1\leq n$ and $R+t+2e+f\leq n$, where $e$ and $f$ are adversarial errors and known erasures. Nested generalized Reed-Solomon codes attain the bounds in their existence range. We also give the exact projection-rank leakage profile and show that hiding arbitrary coherent superpositions from $t$ slots is precisely quantum erasure correction, yielding $\log_q K+2t\leq n$. The results separate classical trajectory privacy from coherent temporal privacy and quantify the robust payload hidden from reduced process tomography.

</details>


### [109] [A Gauge Sign Rule for Quantum Rotor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18969)
*Swagata Acharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续致密变量（量子转子网络）中的符号问题（sign problem），证明了一条类似 Marshall 符号规则的判据：电荷基哈密顿量无符号当且仅当所有回路磁通量模 $2\pi$ 为零，并揭示符号代价是磁通的规范不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号问题（sign problem）指路径积分权重非正，导致量子系统难以经典模拟，其广泛出现于费米子反对称性、几何阻挫、实时演化、拓扑角及有限密度等看似无关的问题中。现有理解多针对离散或特定模型，作者希望为连续致密变量（以量子转子网络建模）建立统一的符号判据。

Method: 将连续致密变量建模为量子转子网络（quantum rotors），哈密顿量为 $\hat H=\sum_i 4E_C(\hat n_i-n_{g,i})^2-\sum_{\langle ij\rangle} E_{ij}\cos(\hatφ_i-\hatφ_j-θ_{ij})$。识别出单一规范不变量——相互作用图中独立回路的阻挫磁通量（frustration flux），并据此证明一条转子版 Marshall 符号规则，辅以精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization）验证。

Result: 证明电荷基哈密顿量无符号当且仅当每个回路磁通量模 $2\pi$ 为零。数值结果显示符号代价是磁通的规范不变函数：单回路情形下，零磁通时为零、在 $\pi$ 处达到峰值、且随回路周长指数衰减；多回路求和后符号代价为广延量，随系统尺寸增长。

Conclusion: 同一回路磁通量统一了三个通常分开处理的问题的符号：莫特转变（Mott transition，经由 slave-rotor 映射）、致密 $U(1)$ 格点规范理论（compact $U(1)$ lattice gauge theory）以及阻挫连续优化（frustrated continuous optimization）。此外，超导转子阵列可原生实现该模型及其符号。

Abstract: The sign problem is the non-positive path-integral weight that makes a quantum system hard to simulate classically. It shows up in problems that look unrelated, from fermion antisymmetry and geometric frustration to real-time evolution, topological angles, and finite density. We study it here for compact continuous variables, modeled as networks of quantum rotors, $\hat H=\sum_i 4E_C(\hat n_i-n_{g,i})^2-\sum_{\langle ij\rangle} E_{ij}\cos(\hatφ_i-\hatφ_j-θ_{ij})$. For these, a single gauge-invariant quantity controls the obstruction, the frustration flux through the independent loops of the interaction graph. We prove a sign rule, the rotor analogue of Marshall's. The charge-basis Hamiltonian is sign-free if and only if every loop flux vanishes modulo $2π$. Exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization then show that the sign cost is a gauge-invariant function of the flux. For a single loop it vanishes at zero flux, peaks at $π$, and is exponentially small in the loop's perimeter. Summed over many loops it is extensive, growing with system size. The same loop flux generates the sign of three problems usually treated apart, namely the Mott transition (through the slave-rotor mapping), compact $U(1)$ lattice gauge theory, and frustrated continuous optimization. A superconducting rotor array realizes both the model and its sign natively.

</details>


### [110] [Code-Visible Liouvillian Modes and Coherent Logical Errors in Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18978)
*Marina Gordeychuk,Oleg Kiselev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将线性系统理论中的可观测性（observability）与最小实现（minimal realization）思想应用于量子纠错（quantum error correction）的逻辑信道，定义码可见空间（code-visible space），揭示相干噪声（coherent noise）的谱特征如何依赖解码器（decoder）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干哈密顿漂移（coherent Hamiltonian drifts）与系统性误差经过量子纠错的方式不同于随机泡利噪声（stochastic Pauli noise）：它们会产生逻辑信道的相位分量，而这些分量在完全泡利旋转（full Pauli twirling）下会丢失。因此需要一个能刻画这些相位分量的方法论框架。

Method: 将线性系统理论中的标准可观测性与最小实现概念应用于逻辑信道 ω ↦ Φ_t(ω) = 𝓓 e^{t𝓛} 𝓔(ω)，其中 𝓔 为编码映射、𝓓 为所选恢复映射与最终解码。定义最小码可见空间 𝓚_code 为可解码逻辑可观测量关于 𝓛† 的 Krylov 闭包，并通过可观测因子化给出逻辑信道的最小实现。

Result: 在该框架下，Knill–Laflamme 条件（Knill–Laflamme conditions）表现为短时一致性检验；稳定子码（stabilizer codes）上的泡利噪声约化为症状-逻辑类（syndrome-logical classes）上的链；恢复映射的选择对可见模式起到谱滤波（spectral filter）作用。对量子存储器（quantum memory），该语言给出以校正周期逻辑信道主导可见特征值表示的存储时间估计。研究特别关注相干噪声的谱特征及其对所选解码器的依赖性。

Conclusion: 线性系统理论的最小实现视角为量子纠错逻辑信道提供了统一的方法论框架，可将 Knill–Laflamme 条件、泡利噪声约化与恢复映射谱滤波纳入同一体系，并刻画相干噪声的谱特征及其对解码器的依赖。

Abstract: Coherent Hamiltonian drifts and systematic errors can pass through quantum error correction differently than stochastic Pauli noise: they generate phase components of the logical channel that are lost under full Pauli twirling. We consider the Lindbladian dynamics of \(n\) physical qubits propagated through an effective logical channel of a fixed encoding, recovery, and decoding scheme. The work is methodological in character: standard ideas of observability and minimal realization from linear systems theory are applied to the channel \[
  ω
  \mapsto
  Φ_t(ω)
  =
  \cD e^{t\cL}\cE(ω), \] where \(\cE\) encodes the logical state and \(\cD\) is the chosen recovery map with final decoding. The minimal code-visible space \(\cK_{\rm code}\) is defined as the Krylov closure of the decodable logical observables with respect to \(\cL^\dagger\); its observable factorization yields a minimal realization of the logical channel. In this form, the Knill--Laflamme conditions serve as a short-time consistency check, Pauli noise on stabilizer codes reduces to a chain on syndrome-logical classes, and the choice of recovery map acts as a spectral filter on the visible modes. For quantum memory, the same language yields an estimate of the storage time in terms of the dominant visible eigenvalues of the logical channel of the correction cycle. Particular attention is paid to the spectral signatures of coherent noise and to how these signatures depend on the chosen decoder.

</details>


### [111] [Measuring correlations in quantum and statistical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18412)
*V. I. Yukalov,E. P. Yukalova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦复合量子与统计系统中的关联（correlation）度量问题，重点介绍关联指数（correlation indices）方法，该方法可统一量化量子与统计、静态与动态过程中的关联强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合物理系统中关联强度的度量需要一个足够通用的框架。既有标准关联描述方法各有局限，作者希望强调一种能同时处理量子与统计系统、并能统一衡量各类关联的度量手段。

Method: 在简要回顾标准关联描述方法后，文章以关联指数方法为核心，阐述其可针对关联算符（correlation operators）、约化密度矩阵（reduced density matrices）及其他含关联信息的算符进行定义。

Result: 关联指数能够度量包括量子纠缠（entanglement）在内的量子关联以及经典关联，并适用于静态与动态过程；文中给出了若干量子态以及自旋（spin）与赝自旋（pseudospin）系统中的关联指数示例。

Conclusion: 通过计算受交变场作用下的囚禁玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate）的关联指数，展示了非平衡系统（nonequilibrium system）中关联量化的可行性，说明该方法的通用性。

Abstract: The survey discusses the problem of measuring correlations in composite quantum and statistical systems. After briefly recalling the standard methods of describing correlations, the emphasis is on the method of correlation indices. The latter method for quantifying the strength of correlations in composite physical systems is sufficiently general allowing for measuring in a unique way the correlation strength in both quantum and statistical systems, for static as well as for dynamic processes. The correlation indices, can be defined for correlation operators, reduced density matrices, and other operators containing information on correlations in the studied composite systems. The correlation indices measure all types of correlations, quantum correlations, such as entanglement, as well as classical correlations. They are applicable for characterizing static as well as dynamical processes. Examples are given of correlation indices for several quantum states and for spin and pseudospin systems. The quantification of correlations in a nonequilibrium system is exemplified by calculating the correlation index for a trapped Bose-Einstein condensate subject to the action of an alternating field.

</details>


### [112] [Lindbladian quantum chaos beyond classical strange attractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18464)
*Ángel L. Corps,Armando Relaño*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究两个耦合集体自旋（coupled collective spins）耗散系统中经典混沌与量子混沌的联系，发现即使经典动力学仅有稳定不动点吸引子，其弛豫前瞬态仍表现出显著混沌特征，并在量子层面伴随Liouvillian谱的随机矩阵统计转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有观点通常将量子混沌与混沌吸引子（chaotic attractor）等混沌渐近行为相联系；本文旨在探讨耗散系统中是否可在无混沌渐近吸引子的情形下建立经典混沌与量子混沌的对应关系。

Method: 采用两个耦合集体自旋的耗散模型，对比分析经典动力学（包括吸引子与瞬态行为）与量子Liouvillian谱统计；考察Liouvillian谱相关部分的随机矩阵统计（random-matrix statistics）转变，以及贡献于可观测期望值的Liouvillian模式数目变化。

Result: 经典动力学具有稳定不动点吸引子，但在弛豫前的瞬态中出现明显混沌迹象；量子模型中该瞬态混沌伴随Liouvillian谱相关部分向随机矩阵统计的转变，以及参与期望值的Liouvillian模式数目的定性增加；该转变发生在稳定不动点消失并出现混沌吸引子的分岔（bifurcation）之前。

Conclusion: 结果表明Liouvillian量子混沌可与瞬态经典混沌相关联，而无需依赖混沌渐近吸引子；这一发现对Grobe-Haake-Sommers猜想（Grobe-Haake-Sommers conjecture）给出了改进与细化。

Abstract: We investigate the connection between classical and quantum chaos in a dissipative system of two coupled collective spins. Although the classical dynamics possesses a stable fixed-point attractor, we find pronounced signatures of chaos in the transient dynamics preceding relaxation. In the quantum model, such transient chaos is accompanied by a transition to random-matrix statistics in the relevant part of the Liouvillian spectrum and by a qualitative increase in the number of Liouvillian modes contributing to expectation values of physical observables. The transition occurs before the bifurcation at which the stable fixed point disappears and a chaotic attractor emerges. Our results show that Liouvillian quantum chaos can be associated with transient classical chaos, rather than requiring a chaotic asymptotic attractor, and provide a refinement of the Grobe-Haake-Sommers conjecture.

</details>


### [113] [Universal entanglement embezzlement and divergent nonlocal magic from generic local chaotic quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18691)
*Matias Karjula,Teemu Ojanen,Kim Pöyhönen,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Moein N. Ivaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 混沌局域幺正演化在达到热化前的中间阶段，会自然生成具有多尺度纠缠谱结构的态，可作为通用纠缠窃取（entanglement embezzlement）的催化储库，并伴随随系统尺寸发散的非局域非稳定化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用纠缠窃取通常需要特殊设计的催化态，而本文旨在探究此类所需的中间态结构是否能在无须精细调节的条件下，由常规量子演化自然产生。

Method: 从直积态（product state）出发，施加局域幺正量子演化（local unitary quantum evolutions），考察其中间态所呈现的纠缠谱（entanglement-spectrum）结构及其催化性质。

Result: 中间态普遍表现出多尺度纠缠谱结构，可在使多体催化态渐近不变的前提下提取纠缠；该结构在达到最大熵、热化抹平谱层级之前出现，并伴随随系统尺寸发散的非局域非稳定化性（nonlocal nonstabilizerness）。

Conclusion: 混沌量子演化无需精细调节即可生成构成通用催化储库（catalytic reservoirs）的中间态家族，且其行为与“通用窃取等价于发散非局域 magic”的既有结论一致。

Abstract: We show that, starting from a product state, local unitary quantum evolutions generate intermediate states which exhibit a multiscale entanglement-spectrum structure required for universal entanglement embezzlement. This facilitates entanglement extraction from a catalyst many-body state while leaving it asymptotically unchanged. Remarkably, these atypical structures emerge generically at intermediate stages, well before reaching maximum entropy where thermalization has flattened out the spectral hierarchy. The resulting state is accompanied by nonlocal nonstabilizerness that diverges with the system size, consistent with a recently established equivalence between universal embezzlement and divergent nonlocal magic. Thus, without any fine tuning, a chaotic quantum evolution generates intermediate states which form a universal family of catalytic reservoirs.

</details>


### [114] [Thermal entanglement on a frustrated tetrahedron: Probing quantum resources through concurrence and stabilizer structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18721)
*Reza Pourkhodabakhshi,Francis Dominie,Deep Gajera,Stephanie H. Curnoe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在四面体（tetrahedron）上的四个自旋-1/2 体系中，以一般四参数交换哈密顿量（exchange Hamiltonian）研究高纠缠本征态如何构成可分离热混合态，并通过线性优化（linear optimization）给出多体 concurrence 的上界与显式可分离分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在阻挫自旋系统（frustrated spin system）中，高度纠缠的本征态可能形成可分离的热混合态，这种区分对理解有限温度量子资源具有重要意义。论文旨在超越基态极限，刻画热纠缠（thermal entanglement）与对称多重态（symmetry multiplet）劈裂及占据之间的关系。

Method: 研究对象为烧绿石晶格（pyrochlore lattice）的基本单元——四面体上的四个自旋-1/2 矩，采用一般四参数交换哈密顿量。利用四面体对称性将热态分解的搜索限制为线性优化问题，从而得到多体 concurrence 的上界及该上界消失时的显式可分离分解；并辅以 negativity 与混合态 stabilizer Rényi 诊断（quantum magic）。

Result: 低温下 concurrence 图谱显示，在 all-in–all-out 极限附近存在扩展的可分离区域，而自旋冰点（spin-ice point）附近的抑制则局域狭窄。升温会因邻近多重态被重新占据而拓宽后者区域。即使本征态平均 concurrence 仍然很大，优化仍可识别出完全可分离的热态。Negativity 独立证实了所选耦合区域存在有限温度纠缠；stabilizer Rényi 诊断偏好不同耦合，并可在温度升高时局部增强。

Conclusion: 结果将热纠缠与对称多重态的劈裂和占据联系起来，并为识别可用于基态极限之外量子资源研究的阻挫自旋构型提供了实用基础。

Abstract: In a frustrated spin system, highly entangled eigenstates can form a separable thermal mixture. We study this distinction for four spin-$1/2$ moments on a tetrahedron, the elementary unit of the pyrochlore lattice, with the general four-parameter exchange Hamiltonian. Tetrahedral symmetry allows a restricted search over thermal-state decompositions to be carried out by linear optimization, yielding an upper bound on multipartite concurrence and explicit separable decompositions where this bound vanishes. At low temperature, the concurrence maps show extended separable regions near the all-in--all-out limit, whereas suppression near the spin-ice point is narrowly localized. Heating broadens the latter region as nearby multiplets are repopulated. The optimization identifies fully separable thermal states even when the eigenstate-averaged concurrence remains large. Negativity independently confirms finite-temperature entanglement in selected coupling regions. A mixed-state stabilizer Rényi diagnostic, used to explore quantum magic, favours different couplings and can increase locally with temperature. These results connect thermal entanglement to the splitting and population of symmetry multiplets, and provide a practical basis for identifying frustrated spin configurations for studies of quantum resources beyond the ground-state limit.

</details>


### [115] [Liouvillian exceptional points in the emergence of quantum synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18867)
*Carlos Ortega Taberner,Rosalind E. Cotton,Paul R. Eastham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在量子理论框架下考察耦合激光器或极化激元凝聚体（polariton condensates）的同步（synchronization）转变，揭示经典极限中各稳态由量子 Liouvillian 慢模的本征谱与本征矩阵形式区分，并证明同步转变由受广义 PT 对称（generalized PT symmetry）保护的例外点（exceptional points）级联所控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自持振荡器（self-sustained oscillators）的耦合在不同耦合强度下呈现独立振荡或同步等不同稳态，经典与半经典理论将此类转变归因于相空间吸引子（phase space attractors）拓扑变化及广义例外点。作者旨在探究量子理论中对应的稳态变化机制，即量子效应主导的少粒子（few-particle）区域是否仍存在可定义的同步转变特征。

Method: 采用耦合激光器或凝聚体的量子理论，分析经典极限下不同稳态 regime 所对应的 Liouvillian 慢模（slow modes）的谱（spectra）与本征矩阵（eigenmatrices）形式，并追踪不同 regime 之间的谱流（spectral flow）。

Result: 不同经典稳态 regime 对应慢模谱与本征矩阵的不同形式；同步转变由慢模中例外点的级联所控制，且这些例外点受广义 PT 对称保护。该机制给出同步转变的实验特征，且在量子效应主导的少粒子区域仍然良定义。

Conclusion: 量子 Liouvillian 的奇点（singularities）控制着不同的动力学 regime，由此建立的同步转变判据在少粒子量子区域仍成立，为量子层面观测同步转变提供依据。

Abstract: The coupling between self-sustained oscillators, such as lasers or polariton condensates, leads to different steady-states in different regimes. Weak coupling allows the oscillators to behave independently, with different frequencies and uncorrelated phases, while stronger coupling can produce synchronization. In classical or semiclassical theories transitions between these steady-states correspond to changes in the topology of the phase space attractors and occur at generalized exceptional points. We investigate the corresponding changes in a quantum theory of coupled lasers or condensates. We show how the different steady-state regimes of the classical limit give rise to distinct forms for the spectra and eigenmatrices of the slow modes of the Liouvillian. By following the spectral flow between the different regimes we show that the synchronization transition is controlled by cascades of exceptional points in these slow modes, protected by a generalized PT symmetry. Our results show how singularities of the quantum Liouvillian control the different dynamical regimes, and give rise to experimental signatures of the synchronization transition which remain well-defined in the few-particle regime dominated by quantum effects.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Flag Game: A Toy Model for Mechanistic Swarm Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19124)
*Elizabeth Pavlova,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Flag Game 这一玩具模型（toy model），用于机制性地研究 AI 智能体（AI agents）集体信念（collective belief）的形成与传播，揭示小群体信念崩溃向大群体信念极化的转变，并提出社会回路归因（social circuit attribution）与统计力学理论（statistical mechanical theory）两种互补分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体涌现出的协同行为（emergent coordinated behaviors）已开始带来关键安全风险，而驱动此类行为的关键现象是世界信念的快速形成与扩散，因此需要机制性理解以支撑集体对齐（collective alignment）。

Method: 构建 Flag Game：隐藏国旗定义真实答案（ground truth），每个有限理性智能体（bounded agent）仅直接观察私有局部图像（private crop），但可交换信念并权衡同伴的社会证据（social evidence）。分析手段包括社会回路归因，用于预测对集体动态最关键的智能体及其观点，并以对智能体的因果干预（causal interventions）验证；针对大规模群体，另建立统计力学理论。

Result: Flag Game 复现了丰富的集体现象：性能随群体规模呈非单调缩放（non-monotonic scaling），社会意识提示（social-awareness prompting）与团队多样性带来准确率提升，组织结构影响显著。小规模下出现集体信念崩溃（collective belief collapse），随群体增大转为集体信念极化（collective belief polarization），后者导致大规模性能下降，同时产生信念多样性。因果干预对智能体的有效性随群体规模增长而下降，但统计力学理论与经验相图（empirical phase diagram）相符。

Conclusion: 该工作面向“机制性群体可解释性（mechanistic swarm interpretability）”，即理解个体智能体属性及其通信如何产生涌现的集体行为，是迈向该方向的第一步。

Abstract: Emergent coordinated behaviors of AI agents are starting to present critical safety risks. A key phenomenon driving these behaviors is the rapid formation and spread of beliefs about the world, and mechanistic understanding is crucial for collective alignment. To this end, we introduce the Flag Game, a toy model for studying the mechanisms of collective belief formation. Concretely, a hidden country flag defines the ground truth, and each bounded agent directly observes only a private crop but can exchange beliefs and weigh social evidence from peers. Despite its simplicity, the Flag Game reproduces rich collective phenomenology: non-monotonic scaling of performance with population size, accuracy gains from social-awareness prompting and team diversity, and strong effects of organizational structure. In particular, we identify that collective belief collapse at small population sizes turns into collective belief polarization as the population grows. This polarization causes the performance decline at large population sizes, but creates diversity in collective beliefs. Finally, we dissect the mechanisms underlying collective belief collapse and polarization with two complementary approaches. We first introduce social circuit attribution, a technique to predict which agent, and what view, matters most to collective dynamics, and verify its predictions by causal interventions on agents, tracing how agent patching changes collective outcomes. However, the efficacy of causal interventions on agents decreases as the population grows. We therefore develop a statistical mechanical theory for larger populations and verify that it matches the empirical phase diagram. Together, these results take a first step toward mechanistic swarm interpretability, a science of how the properties of individual agents and their communication give rise to emergent collective behavior.

</details>
