# nucl-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Constraining the $f_0(980)$ Structure and Formation Dynamics via Anisotropy-Response Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17765)
*Roy A. Lacey*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 该研究采用物种分辨的各向异性响应标度框架（species-resolved anisotropy-response scaling framework），结合响应构造与有效末态响应参数ζ_{f₀}，对相对论核碰撞中f₀(980)的结构与形成动力学进行判别，提出各向异性响应标度作为新的实验探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: f₀(980)的结构性质（单介子态与分子态之争）缺乏唯一的结构判别手段；仅依靠单介子标度不足以区分不同结构假设，需要结合更多可观测量加以约束。

Method: 使用物种分辨的各向异性响应标度框架，比较单介子响应构造与对称K K̄组分响应构造，并将显式分子态K K̄计算结果纳入对照；分析高多重度p+Pb碰撞（√s_NN=8.16 TeV）的已发表测量数据。

Result: 单介子响应构造呈现较宽的标度闭合，有效末态响应很小（ζ_{f₀}≈0.04）；对称K K̄组分响应构造的闭合明显更差；显式分子态K K̄计算也表现出类似单介子的标度，但有效末态响应显著更大（ζ_{f₀}≈0.16）。

Conclusion: 单介子标度本身并非唯一的结构判别依据；结合响应构造与ζ_{f₀}约束，测量到的f₀(980)表现为类单介子的各向异性响应且末态敏感性弱，与分子态基准明显不同。该工作将各向异性响应标度确立为研究f₀(980)形成动力学的新实验探针，并推动在不同碰撞系统与束流能量下开展系统检验。

Abstract: A species-resolved anisotropy-response scaling framework is used to investigate the structure and formation dynamics of the $f_0(980)$ in relativistic nuclear collisions. Published measurements in high-multiplicity p+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=8.16$~TeV exhibit broad scaling closure under a single-meson response construction, with a small effective final-state response, $ζ_{f_0}\simeq0.04$, whereas a symmetric ${\rm K\bar K}$ constituent-response construction gives substantially poorer closure. An explicitly molecular ${\rm K\bar K}$ calculation also exhibits single-meson-like scaling but gives a substantially larger effective final-state response, $ζ_{f_0}\simeq0.16$. Thus, single-meson scaling alone is not a unique structural discriminator. The combined response construction and $ζ_{f_0}$ constraints characterize the measured $f_0(980)$ by a single-meson-like anisotropy response with weak final-state sensitivity, distinctly different from the molecular benchmark. These results establish anisotropy-response scaling as a new experimental probe of $f_0(980)$ formation dynamics and motivate systematic tests across collision systems and beam energies.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [The Kerr/CFT energy gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17656)
*Robert Penna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用 Kinnersley–Kelley 给出的单参数 NHEK 推广解（在 NHEK 与解析延拓的 Minkowski 时空之间插值），计算 NHEK 相对 Minkowski 参考度规的共形能量，并讨论 Minkowski 真空共形能量中的极限次序问题及若干修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力中的 Noether 荷（Noether charges）总需相对于某个参考度规（reference metric）定义，但近极端 Kerr（near-horizon extremal Kerr, NHEK）解缺乏显然的参考度规选择，例如它并非渐近平坦（asymptotically flat）。因此需要构造合适的参考背景以定义 NHEK 的共形能量（conformal energy）。

Method: 采用 Kinnersley 与 Kelley 提出的 NHEK 单参数推广解，该解族在 NHEK 与解析延拓版本的 Minkowski 时空（analytically continued Minkowski spacetime）之间插值；以此插值解族为工具，计算 NHEK 在 Minkowski 参考度规之上的共形能量。

Result: 在计算 Minkowski 真空的共形能量时发现存在一个值得关注的极限次序（order of limits）问题；文中讨论了若干自然的修正处方（prescriptions），其中包括一个受 Kerr/CFT 对应（Kerr/CFT correspondence）启发的方案。

Conclusion: NHEK 共形能量的定义依赖于参考度规的选取及极限次序的处理；Kinnersley–Kelley 插值解族提供了可行框架，而 Kerr/CFT 对应可为消除极限次序歧义提供动机性方案。

Abstract: Noether charges in gravity are always defined with respect to a reference metric, but the near-horizon extremal Kerr (NHEK) solution does not come with an obvious choice of reference metric (it is not asymptotically flat, for example). We discuss a one parameter generalization of the NHEK solution due to Kinnersley and Kelley which interpolates between NHEK and an analytically continued version of Minkowski spacetime. We use this interpolating family of solutions to compute the conformal energy of NHEK above the Minkowski reference metric. We find an interesting order of limits issue when computing the conformal energy of the Minkowski vacuum and we discuss a few natural prescriptions for fixing it, including one motivated by the Kerr/CFT correspondence.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum Dynamics of Probe Particles in Thermal Fields and the Emergence of Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17657)
*Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从一般哈密顿量出发，推导探针粒子（probe particle）约化密度矩阵的量子主方程（quantum master equation）、热环境下的平衡条件（KMS 性质）及朗之万（Langevin）描述，且不依赖环境与耦合的具体形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有相关推导通常依赖对环境自耦合、耦合算符、关联函数统计或粒子运动学（是否相对论性）的具体假设，缺乏一般性；本文旨在建立不依赖这些特定假设的第一性原理描述。

Method: 从描述探针粒子与环境自由度相互作用的一般哈密顿量出发，推导约化密度矩阵演化所满足的量子主方程、环境处于热态时的普遍平衡条件（以实时延展线算符的 KMS 性质体现）以及由此出现的朗之万描述。

Result: 得到具有普遍性的主方程、KMS 型平衡条件与朗之万描述；仅需假设时空平移、宇称（parity）与时间反演（time reversal）为哈密顿量对称性，且探针粒子色散关系（dispersion relation）与环境特征尺度之间存在尺度分离（separation of scales）。另讨论了高斯布朗运动（Gaussian Brownian motion）出现的极限情形。

Conclusion: 结果构成对底层量子场论（quantum field theory）中支配探针粒子能量损失与动量涨落性质的首次原理性推导，且不依赖于环境自耦合、耦合算符、关联函数统计（高斯与否）及粒子是否相对论性等具体假设。

Abstract: Starting from a general Hamiltonian describing the quantum dynamics of probe particles interacting with a set of environment degrees of freedom, we derive the quantum master equation with which the reduced density matrix of a probe particle evolves, the general equilibration condition that they satisfy when the environment is prepared in a thermal state -- encoded in a KMS property for line operators extended in real time -- and the Langevin description that emerges. The novelty of our results resides in their generality: We do not assume a specific form of the environment self-coupling, of the environment operator that couples the particle to it, of the statistics of its correlation functions, whether the statistics of the momentum transfer is Gaussian or not, or whether the probe particles move relativistically. Our results only require that spacetime translations, parity and time reversal be symmetries of the Hamiltonian, and that there exists a separation of scales between those characterizing the dispersion relation of the probe particle and those of the environment. We also discuss the limiting case in which Gaussian Brownian motion emerges. Overall, our results constitute a first-principles derivation of what properties of the underlying quantum field theory govern the energy loss and momentum fluctuations of probe particles.

</details>


### [120] [Bosonic String Amplitudes From Ribbon Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17660)
*Sam Christian,Ben Mazel,Yuchen Wang,Xi Yin,Yutai Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于 Kontsevich 带状图（ribbon graph）参数化带孔黎曼面模空间（moduli space）$\mathcal{M}_{g,n}$ 的数值方法，用于计算高亏格共形场论（CFT）关联函数与弦振幅。作者据此计算了至亏格 6 的紧致玻色子配分函数，以及至亏格 3 的玻色弦真空振幅模空间被积函数，并通过多项非平凡数值检验，其中亏格 2 与 3 的结果重现了 Igusa 尖点形式（Igusa cusp forms）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亏格情形下共形场论关联函数与弦振幅的解析计算极为困难，缺乏实用的数值手段。该工作旨在借助模空间的显式参数化，为高亏格计算提供可行方法并对其结果进行验证。

Method: 方法核心为 Kontsevich 对带孔黎曼面模空间 $\mathcal{M}_{g,n}$ 的带状图参数化，并据此进行数值积分与求和，以计算高亏格 CFT 关联函数与弦振幅。

Result: 计算得到至亏格 6 的紧致玻色子配分函数，以及至亏格 3 的玻色弦真空振幅模空间被积函数。结果通过多项非平凡数值检验；关键的是，玻色弦振幅的模空间被积函数在亏格 2 与亏格 3 下重现了 Igusa 尖点形式。

Conclusion: 基于带状图参数化的数值方法可有效处理高亏格 CFT 与弦振幅计算，其结果与已知解析结构（Igusa 尖点形式）一致，表明该方法具有可靠性。

Abstract: We develop a method for numerically computing higher genus CFT correlators and string amplitudes based on Kontsevich's ribbon graph parametrization of the moduli space $\mathcal{M}_{g,n}$ of punctured Riemann surfaces. Using this method, we compute the compact boson partition function up to genus 6 as well as the moduli space integrand for bosonic string vacuum amplitudes up to genus 3. Our results pass a number of nontrivial numerical checks. In particular, the moduli space integrand of bosonic string amplitudes reproduce the Igusa cusp forms at genera 2 and 3.

</details>


### [121] [Full Eigenstate Thermalization in Quantum Field Theory and Gravitational Scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17663)
*Ricardo Espíndola,Viktor Jahnke*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用热解析性（thermal analyticity），在连续量子场论（continuum quantum field theory）中为完全本征态热化（full eigenstate thermalization）所涉及的平滑函数导出方向性大频率界，并揭示其非对称多频结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将此前在格点系统（lattice systems）中标定的热化尺度推广至连续量子场论框架，并通过解析性域刻画更完整的频率结构。其动机源于对完全本征态热化在高频区行为进行定量约束的需求。

Method: 方法基于热解析性，推导平滑函数在方向性大频率下的界，并分析完整解析性域所呈现的非对称多频（multifrequency）结构；同时考察热共形关联函数（thermal conformal correlators）与引力 Regge 贡献的饱和行为。

Result: 最强方向上的界重现了先前为格点系统标定的尺度；完整解析性域显示出非对称多频结构。热共形关联函数饱和二阶与三阶界，而主导的引力 Regge 贡献（leading gravitational Regge contribution）饱和最强的四阶方向。

Conclusion: 热解析性可用于约束连续量子场论中的本征态热化尺度，其最强方向与格点结果一致，且二阶至四阶界分别由热共形关联函数与引力 Regge 贡献饱和，表明该界具有可达到性。

Abstract: Using thermal analyticity, we derive directional large-frequency bounds on the smooth functions entering full eigenstate thermalization in continuum quantum field theory. The strongest directions reproduce the scales previously identified for lattice systems, while the complete analyticity domain reveals an asymmetric multifrequency structure. Thermal conformal correlators saturate the second- and third-order bounds, and the leading gravitational Regge contribution saturates the strongest fourth-order direction.

</details>


### [122] [Representation Theory of Three-dimensional Corner Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17666)
*Giulio Neri,Ludovic Varrin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维圆形角（circular corner）对应的扩展角对称群（extended corner symmetry group）的表示论，借助诱导表示（induced representation）技术构造无穷维对称群的表示族，分析其酉性（unitarity）与不可约性（irreducibility），并确定最大中心扩展（maximal central extension）及相应射影表示（projective representations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角对称性（corner symmetries）在量子引力（quantum gravity）中扮演重要角色，被角变换作用的边界自由度（boundary degrees of freedom）被认为可编码时空量子结构的某些方面。三维情形下出现的表示论结构与此前引力研究中居核心地位的对称群与量子理论密切相关，故需系统刻画量子引力角自由度。

Method: 采用诱导表示技术，构造无穷维角对称群的若干表示族，并对其酉性与不可约性进行分析；同时确定该对称群的最大中心扩展，并构造对应的射影表示。

Result: 得到无穷维角对称群的表示族，并明确其酉性与不可约性；给出最大中心扩展的确定结果及与之对应的射影表示。三维情形下涌现的表示论结构与已作为引力研究核心的对称群和量子理论密切相关。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维量子引力角自由度的系统性描述提供了第一步，即建立起角对称群表示论的基本框架。

Abstract: We study the representation theory of the three-dimensional extended corner symmetry group associated with a circular corner. Using induced representation techniques, we construct families of representations of the infinite-dimensional corner symmetry group and analyze their unitarity and irreducibility. We also determine the maximal central extension of the symmetry group and construct the corresponding projective representations. The analysis is motivated by the role that corner symmetries play in quantum gravity, where boundary degrees of freedom --- acted upon by the corner transformations --- are expected to encode aspects of the quantum structure of spacetime. In three dimensions, the representation-theoretic structures that emerge are closely related to symmetry groups and quantum theories that already appeared as central to the study of gravity. This work provides a first step toward a systematic description of quantum gravitational corner degrees of freedom in three dimensions.

</details>


### [123] [Full Eigenstate Thermalization in Quantum Field Theory: Free Cumulants and Gravitational Scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17669)
*Ricardo Espíndola,Viktor Jahnke*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用热解析性（thermal analyticity）推导连续量子场论中全本征态热化（full eigenstate thermalization, full ETH）所涉光滑函数的方向性大频率界，并在二维共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）与全息 CFT 中验证其饱和性，从而建立 full ETH、自由累积量（free cumulants）与引力扰动（gravitational scrambling）之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: full ETH 此前主要在格点系统（lattice systems）中通过标度（scales）刻画，其在连续量子场论中的大频率行为尚缺乏系统界。作者希望借助热解析性给出连续情形下的方向性界，并厘清 full ETH 与非交换概率、引力物理之间的深层关系。

Method: 采用热解析性（thermal analyticity）方法，推导 full ETH 中光滑函数在方向上的大频率界，并给出完整的解析性区域以刻画非对称多频（multifrequency）结构；同时在一维、二维热 CFT 中用精确的两点与三点函数进行对照检验。

Result: 最强的方向性界复现了此前在格点系统中识别的标度，而完整解析域揭示出更广的非对称多频结构。在热二维 CFT 中，精确的两点与三点函数达到相应界；在四阶（genuine dynamical information 首次出现的阶数），所研究的全息 CFT 达到最强的 full-ETH 界。

Conclusion: 结果表明 full ETH、自由累积量与引力扰动之间存在直接联系，并与引力扰动代数（gravitational scrambling algebra）所实现的渐近自由性（asymptotic freeness）的出现相一致。

Abstract: Using thermal analyticity, we derive directional large-frequency bounds on the smooth functions entering full eigenstate thermalization in continuum quantum field theory. The strongest directions reproduce the scales previously identified for lattice systems, while the complete analyticity domain reveals a broader asymmetric multifrequency structure. In thermal two-dimensional conformal field theory, exact two- and three-point functions saturate the corresponding bounds. At fourth order, where genuine dynamical information first appears, we show that the holographic CFTs studied here saturate the strongest full-ETH bounds. These results establish a direct connection between full ETH, free cumulants, and gravitational scrambling, and are consistent with the emergence of asymptotic freeness realized by the gravitational scrambling algebra.

</details>


### [124] [Fixed Points, Floquet Entanglement Asymmetry, and Quantum Mpemba Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17724)
*Jayashish Das,Filiberto Ares,Arnab Kundu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文利用基于迭代共形映射（iterated conformal maps）的动力学系统描述，研究周期驱动二维共形场论（2D CFT）中纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）的演化，并指出局部对称恢复、量子姆潘巴效应（quantum Mpemba effect）及其逆效应可由共形映射的不变量数据给出几何化描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解周期驱动量子多体系统中纠缠不对称性的非平衡动力学，并寻求对局部对称恢复、量子姆潘巴效应及其逆效应等现象的统一描述框架。

Method: 方法上采用带全局 U(1) 对称性的周期驱动二维共形场论，借助迭代共形映射尤其是莫比乌斯映射（Möbius maps）构建动力学系统描述。以局域算符插入准备的破缺对称激发态为初态，并利用共形映射的不变量数据刻画其演化。

Result: 结果显示，莫比乌斯映射的不变量结构由其共轭类（conjugacy classes）描述，纠缠不对称性的动力学由态制备算符插入位置、子系统及不动点的相对位置决定，从而呈现增长、衰减、振荡和饱和等不同模式。作者进一步论证该几何图像可推广至一般全纯映射（holomorphic maps）。

Conclusion: 结论是共形映射动力学为组织周期驱动二维共形场论中纠缠不对称性现象学提供了自然框架。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of entanglement asymmetry in periodically driven two-dimensional conformal field theories with a global U$(1)$ symmetry, using a dynamical-system description based on iterated conformal maps, in particular Möbius maps. Starting from a symmetry-breaking excited state prepared by a local operator insertion, we show that a broad range of nonequilibrium phenomena, including local symmetry restoration, the quantum Mpemba effect, and its inverse, admit a simple geometric description in terms of the invariant data of the conformal map. We explicitly demonstrate this using Möbius maps, for which the invariant structure is described by the conjugacy classes. In particular, the dynamics of entanglement asymmetry is determined by the relative positions of the state-preparing operator insertion, the subsystem, and the fixed points, yielding distinct patterns of growth, decay, oscillation, and saturation. We further argue that this geometric picture extends to general holomorphic maps, establishing the conformal-map dynamics as a natural framework for organizing the phenomenology of entanglement asymmetry in periodically driven two-dimensional conformal field theories.

</details>


### [125] [Matter Swapping between Two Branes: A Local Type IIB D3-D7-D3 Realization of Charged-Fermion Mixing via E3 Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17858)
*Michaël Sarrazin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文探讨膜世界间费米子混合（fermion mixing）的双层（two-sheeted）有效描述是否可由D膜机制产生，并在Type IIB中构建D3₊-D7-D3₋/E3局部实现，用E3瞬子（instanton）非微扰生成直接混合。结果重现了双层费米子算符的赝标量结构及其标量伴子，并给出紧凑化模型构建路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效红外双层费米子混合描述在多种实现中反复出现，并导致可检验的物质交换（matter-swapping）效应，推动低能搜寻与宇宙学应用。该算符结构的反复出现促使作者追问：这一结构是否也能由D膜机制产生。

Method: 构建Type IIB中的局部D3₊-D7-D3₋/E3实现，其中直接的膜间混合（interbrane mixing）在微扰层面被禁止，而由适当的E3瞬子扇区非微扰生成；D7 Wilson输运（Wilson transport）提供所需的规范不变相对联络（gauge-invariant relative connection）。

Result: 所得低能相互作用重现了双层费米子算符的特征性赝标量（pseudoscalar）结构及其标量伴子；该构造在所述的瞬子与零模（zero-mode）条件下，提供了同一算符结构的局部弦论实现。

Conclusion: 分析进一步给出通往紧凑现象学实现的具体模型构建路线图：紧凑化（compactification）须实现两个费米子扇区的电磁隔离（electromagnetic sequestering），并支持足够轻的非均匀D7规范模以传递长程相对相互作用。在此完整紧凑化中，混合参数g原则上可由微观弦数据计算，并与低能约束直接比对检验。

Abstract: Effective infrared two-sheeted descriptions of fermion mixing between braneworlds have emerged in several distinct realizations and lead to testable matter-swapping effects, motivating low-energy searches and cosmological applications. The recurrence of the same low-energy fermion operator motivates asking whether this structure can also arise from a D-brane mechanism. We construct a local Type IIB D3$_+$-D7-D3$_-$/E3 realization in which direct interbrane mixing is perturbatively forbidden but generated non-perturbatively by a suitable E3 instanton sector. The resulting low-energy interaction reproduces the characteristic pseudoscalar structure of the two-sheeted fermion operator, together with its scalar companion, while D7 Wilson transport provides the required gauge-invariant relative connection. The construction therefore provides a local string realization of the same operator structure, subject to the stated instanton and zero-mode conditions. The analysis further provides a concrete model-building roadmap toward a compact phenomenological realization: the compactification must achieve electromagnetic sequestering of the two fermion sectors and support a sufficiently light non-uniform D7 gauge mode capable of mediating a long-range relative interaction. In such a complete compactification, the mixing parameter $g$ would in principle become computable from microscopic string data and directly testable against low-energy constraints.

</details>


### [126] [Six Particles, Infinite Optimism: Towards Positivity Bounds for Six-Point Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17861)
*Stefan Kremminger,Andrew J. Tolley*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文尝试将 $2\\to2$ 散射的 $S$-矩阵正性与自举界推广到更高点振幅，通过计算单标量 EFT 的树级 UV 完备（UV-completion）中四、五、六点振幅的低能展开系数，寻找正性关系。结果表明，符号不定三次耦合（cubic coupling）所对应的一类费曼图通常破坏正性界；但隔离出不受其影响的交叉对称低能振幅扇区后，部分四、五、六点 Wilson 系数可组织为半正定矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 $S$-矩阵正性（positivity）与自举界（bootstrap bounds）已在 $2\\to2$ 散射中确立，但向更高点振幅的系统推广长期未获解决。该文旨在填补这一空白，通过考察一大类单标量有效场论（EFT）的树级 UV 完备来探索高点多点振幅是否存在类似正性约束。

Method: 考虑 $D=4$ 中最一般的可重整 $N$-标量理论作为单标量 EFT 的树级 UV 完备类。显式计算四点、五点与六点散射的树级振幅并在低能下展开，进而搜索展开系数之间的正性关系。

Result: 由于一类线性依赖于符号不定三次耦合（sign-indefinite cubic couplings）的费曼图污染，正性界一般不可得。然而，若隔离出全交叉对称低能振幅中不受这些符号不定三次耦合影响的一个扇区（sector），则所选六点、五点与四点 Wilson 系数（Wilson coefficients）可组织为半正定矩阵（positive semi-definite matrices）。

Conclusion: 对这些正性陈述的违反仅意味着给定 EFT 与该类 UV 完备不相容，而非普遍排除。该结果为更高点相互作用（higher-point interactions）的更一般正性界应当与不应当呈现何种形式提供了指导。

Abstract: The systematic generalisation of the well-established $S$-matrix positivity and bootstrap bounds from $2 \to 2$ scattering processes to higher-point amplitudes has, to date, remained elusive. To address this, we consider a large class of tree-level UV-completions of a single-scalar EFT. Specifically, we consider the most general renormalisable $N$-scalar theory in $D=4$. By explicitly computing tree-level amplitudes for four-, five- and six-point scattering and expanding at low energies, we can search for positivity relations among the expansion coefficients. We find that quite generally positivity bounds are unavailable due to contamination from a specific class of Feynman diagrams that are linear in sign-indefinite cubic couplings. However, by isolating a sector of the full crossing-symmetric low-energy amplitude that is unaffected by these sign-indefinite cubic couplings, we find that selected six-, five- and four-point Wilson coefficients organise into positive semi-definite matrices. While a violation of these positivity statements only implies that a given EFT is inconsistent with our class of UV completions, the result helps to guide towards what more general positivity bounds for higher-point interactions could, and could not, look like.

</details>


### [127] [Smooth cutoffs and analytic continuation in Casimir physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17917)
*Lucía N. Helou,Francisco D. Mazzitelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于 Tao 的光滑和（smoothed sums）理论，为 Casimir 能量计算中发散级数的两种处理方式（物理正则化减除与形式解析延拓）建立了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Casimir 能量计算中出现的发散级数存在两种看似不同的处理方式：物理动机的正则化（regularization）与减除（subtraction）程序，以及直接赋予发散表达式有限值的形式解析延拓（analytic continuation）方法。二者对平行板间 Casimir 能量给出相同结果虽为已知，但其原因往往未被明确说明。

Method: 借助 Tao 的光滑和理论，引入光滑截断（smooth cutoff），得到渐进展开（asymptotic expansion），其有限且与正则化子无关的部分由相应 Dirichlet 级数（Dirichlet series）的解析延拓普遍决定；该对应关系来自 Mellin 变换（Mellin transform）表示，其中发散与有限贡献被编码于被积函数的极点结构（pole structure）中，并将方法推广至与 Casimir 问题相关的一类级数。

Result: 在所推广的设定中，对数发散（logarithmic divergences）源于极点重合（pole coincidences）；由此得到结果向普适项与截断依赖项的分解，其结构与量子场论中的重整化（renormalization）相对应。

Conclusion: 该统一框架使物理正则化减除与形式解析延拓之间的等价性变得透明，并为相关级数提供了类似于重整化的普适/截断依赖项分解。

Abstract: Divergent series arising in the computation of Casimir energies admit two seemingly different treatments: a physically motivated regularization and subtraction procedure, and formal analytic continuation methods that assign finite values directly to divergent expressions. The agreement between these approaches for the Casimir energy between parallel plates is well known, but its origin is often left implicit. In this work, we provide a unified framework that makes this equivalence transparent. Building on Tao's theory of smoothed sums, we show that the introduction of a smooth cutoff leads to an asymptotic expansion whose finite, regulator-independent part is universally determined by the analytic continuation of the associated Dirichlet series. This identification follows from a Mellin-transform representation, in which divergences and finite contributions are encoded in the pole structure of the integrand. We extend this approach to a class of series relevant to Casimir problems. In this setting, we show that logarithmic divergences arise from pole coincidences, and we obtain a natural decomposition of the result into universal and cutoff-dependent terms, mirroring the structure of renormalization in quantum field theory.

</details>


### [128] [Central charge and black hole entropy for regular extremal black-bounce spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18028)
*Xu Ye,Shan-Ping Wu,Yu-Kun Zhang,Shao-Wen Wei*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文采用 Kerr/CFT 对应（Kerr/CFT correspondence）方法，研究无曲率奇点的正则极值 black-bounce 时空（regular extremal black-bounce spacetimes）的中心荷与黑洞熵，发现 Cardy 公式给出的微观熵与 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bekenstein-Hawking 熵与视界面积成正比，是黑洞热力学的基础，并可借助 AdS/CFT 对应（AdS/CFT correspondence）理解。本文旨在检验 Kerr/CFT 方法在无曲率奇点（curvature singularity）的正则时空中的适用性。

Method: 针对 Kerr、Kerr-Newman 与 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞的 black-bounce 对应体，推导其近视界几何（near horizon geometry），发现 SL(2,R)×U(1) 或 SL(2,R)×SO(3) 的增强对称性；施加合适边界条件后分析渐近对称群（含微分同胚与电磁场诱导的 U(1)_gauge 对称性），从荷代数提取中心荷，并计算 Frolov-Thorne 真空的左行温度。对 black-bounce Reissner-Nordström 情形，通过引入 U(1) 规范纤维将 4D 几何提升至 5D。

Result: 在 black-bounce Kerr-Newman 情形中，来自电磁贡献的中心荷为零。由 Cardy 公式计算得到的微观熵与 Bekenstein-Hawking 熵一致。

Conclusion: 该一致性表明 Kerr/CFT 方法对某些无曲率奇点的正则时空仍然有效，从而为黑洞熵提供了微观统计层面的理解。

Abstract: The Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, proportional to one quarter of the horizon area, is fundamental in black hole thermodynamics and can also be understood via the AdS/CFT correspondence, such as the 3D BTZ black hole and 2D CFT. In this work, we adopt the Kerr/CFT approach to analyze the central charge and black hole entropy for regular extremal black-bounce spacetimes, including the counterparts of the Kerr, Kerr-Newman, and Reissner-Nordström black holes. These spacetimes are free of curvature singularities at $r=0$. We derive the near horizon geometries of these spacetimes and find that they exhibit enhanced symmetry, namely SL$(2,\mathbb{R})\times \mathrm{U}(1)$ or SL$(2,\mathbb{R}) \times \mathrm{SO}(3)$. By imposing appropriate boundary conditions, we analyze their asymptotic symmetry groups, which contain diffeomorphisms as well as the $\mathrm{U}(1)_{\rm gauge}$ symmetry arising from the electromagnetic field. We then extract the central charge from the charge algebra and evaluate the left-moving temperature of the Frolov-Thorne vacuum. It is worth emphasizing that in the black-bounce Kerr-Newman case, the central charge from the electromagnetic contribution vanishes. Furthermore, in the black-bounce Reissner-Nordström case, we uplift the 4D geometry to a 5D configuration by incorporating a $\mathrm{U}(1)$ gauge fiber. Our results show that the microscopic entropy calculated from the Cardy formula is consistent with the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. This agreement suggests that the Kerr/CFT approach remains valid for certain regular spacetimes without curvature singularities, thereby providing a microscopic statistical understanding of black hole entropy.

</details>


### [129] [Vacuum Structure of Maximally Supersymmetric Two-Dimensional Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18074)
*Richard Eager*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维最大超对称杨–米尔斯理论（maximally supersymmetric Yang–Mills theory）的真空结构，指出低能下理论与轨道折叠共形场论（orbifold conformal field theory）及有质量真空构成的超选择扇区相对应，并据此提出广义 Springer 对应（generalized Springer correspondence）的丛式数据应向全纯因子化代数（holomorphic factorization algebra）增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究源于对最大超对称杨–米尔斯理论低能真空结构与数学对应关系之间联系的关注，试图以 Lusztig 广义 Springer 对应中的尖点数据（cuspidal data）刻画理论的扇区分解。

Method: 采用对二维最大超对称杨–米尔斯理论低能有效结构的分析，将超选择扇区（superselection sector）识别为轨道折叠共形场论与有质量真空的组合，并借助 Lusztig 广义 Springer 对应的尖点数据对这些扇区进行标号。

Result: 理论在低能下分解为若干超选择扇区，各扇区由轨道折叠共形场论和有质量真空组成，其标号可由 Lusztig 广义 Springer 对应中出现的尖点数据给出。

Conclusion: 该对应关系提示将广义 Springer 对应增强为全纯因子化代数，从而为真空扇区结构提供更精细的代数框架。

Abstract: We study the vacuum structure of maximally supersymmetric Yang--Mills theory in two dimensions. At low energies, the theory decomposes into superselection sectors consisting of orbifold conformal field theories and massive vacua. The sectors are indexed by the cuspidal data appearing in Lusztig's generalized Springer correspondence. This suggests an enhancement of the correspondence to holomorphic factorization algebras.

</details>


### [130] [Growing Einstein-Rosen Bridge with Multi-partite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18225)
*Takanori Anegawa,Akihiro Miyata,Shota Suzuki,Kotaro Tamaoka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文探讨子系统纠缠熵饱和后纠缠是否仍可增长，利用真多体熵（genuine multi-entropy）刻画双分纠缠之外的多体纠缠结构，发现双侧BTZ黑洞中该量在三分区各子系统熵饱和后仍线性增长，从而扩展了纠缠探测爱因斯坦-罗森桥（Einstein-Rosen bridge）生长的时间窗口，并细化了ER=EPR关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：当子系统的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）已饱和后，量子纠缠是否还能继续增长。既有基于双分纠缠（bipartite entanglement）的熵量无法刻画更精细的多体纠缠结构，因此需要引入超越双分熵的探测量。

Method: 采用真多体熵（genuine multi-entropy）作为探测多体纠缠结构（multi-partite entanglement structure）的工具，并在双侧BTZ黑洞（two-sided BTZ black hole）背景下考察固定三分区（tripartition）的情形，同时对比若干自旋链模型（spin-chain models）与空间分割的Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev（SYK）模型的行为。

Result: 在双侧BTZ黑洞中，固定三分区的每个子系统熵均饱和之后，真多体熵仍随时间线性增长，延长了纠缠可探测爱因斯坦-罗森桥生长的时间区间。若干自旋链模型与空间分割的SYK模型表现出不同的瞬态（transients）；而全息（holography）、SYK模型与Haar随机态（Haar-random states）的晚期平台值（late-time plateau values）呈现普适行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多体纠缠结构可揭示普通纠缠熵所不可见的信息，从而对ER=EPR关系作出细化；同时全息与随机态在晚期平台值上的普适性提示该量具有更一般的意义。

Abstract: Can quantum entanglement continue to grow even after the entanglement entropies of subsystems have saturated? We address this question using genuine multi-entropy, which probes multi-partite entanglement structure beyond bipartite entanglement. In a two-sided BTZ black hole, this quantity grows linearly with time after every subsystem entropy of a fixed tripartition has saturated, extending the interval in which entanglement detects Einstein-Rosen bridge growth. The result refines the ER=EPR relation by identifying multi-partite structure invisible to ordinary entanglement entropies. Some spin-chain models and the spatially partitioned Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model exhibit distinct transients, while the late-time plateau values in holography, the SYK model, and Haar-random states share a universal behaviour.

</details>


### [131] [Reduction of the six-dimensional $q$-form fields to the four-dimensional fields by coupling with gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18277)
*Yong-Tao Lu,Heng Guo,Qun Wei,Bing Wei*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究余两维厚膜（thick brane）上多种 q-形式场（q-form fields）的局域化（localization），通过在六维作用量中引入依赖体标量曲率的耦合函数 F(R) 实现。结果显示，无质量模在耦合参数 t1、t2 为正值时可被局域化；有质量模的局域化行为由 t2 决定，并随 t2 取值不同呈现三种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨不同阶数的 q-形式场在包含引力耦合的膜世界（braneworld）场景中能否被局域化，并考察广义引力耦合函数 F(R) 对局域化性质的影响。

Method: 在余两维厚膜背景下，对 0-形式标量场、1-形式 U(1) 规范矢量场和 2-形式 Kalb-Ramond 场构建含引力耦合的六维作用量，其中引入依赖体标量曲率的耦合函数 F(R)，随后分析各类 q-形式场的有质量与无质量模局域化及相应 Kaluza-Klein 模的有效势。

Result: 当耦合参数 t1 与 t2 为正值时，不同 q-形式场的无质量模可在厚膜上局域化。有质量模的局域化性质相似且由 t2 决定：0<t2<v²/24 时无法局域化，但可能作为共振模（resonant modes）存在；t2=v²/24 时有限个有质量模可局域化，且数量随 t1 增大而增加；t2>v²/24 时相应有效势形成无限深势阱（infinitely deep potential wells），所有有质量模均可局域化。此外，各类 q-形式场的快子有质量模（tachyonic massive modes）均被排除。

Conclusion: 在含 F(R) 引力耦合的余两维厚膜框架中，q-形式场的局域化由耦合参数 t1 与 t2 调控；通过选取 t2 的不同取值，可实现无质量模局域化、共振模存在、有限或全部有质量模局域化等不同结果，同时快子不稳定模可被始终排除。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the localization of various $q$-form fields on a codimension-two brane. In particular, the $0$-form scalar field, the $1$-form $U(1)$ gauge vector field, and the $2$-form Kalb-Ramond field are considered with gravitational coupling, where a coupling function $F(R)$ is introduced into the six-dimensional actions of these fields. The function $F(R)$ depends on the scalar curvature of the bulk. Within this framework, we find that the massless modes of different $q$-form fields can be localized on the thick brane for positive values of the coupling parameters $t_1$ and $t_2$. For the massive modes, the different $q$-form fields exhibit similar localization properties determined by the coupling parameter $t_2$. When $0<t_2<v^2/24$, the massive modes of these fields cannot be localized on the brane, while they may exist as the resonant modes. In the case $t_2=v^2/24$, a finite number of massive modes can be localized on the thick brane, and the number of localized modes increases with the coupling parameter $t_1$. Finally, when $t_2>v^2/24$, the effective potentials associated with the Kaluza-Klein modes of these $q$-form fields form infinitely deep potential wells, so that all massive modes can be localized on the brane. Moreover, the tachyonic massive modes can always be excluded for different $q$-form fields.

</details>


### [132] [Turbulent gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18295)
*Giulio Taiocchi,Christiana Pantelidou,Miguel Zilhão*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次在四维渐近反德西特（asymptotically AdS$_4$）时空中构建湍流引力尾迹（turbulent gravitational wakes），通过求解完整非线性爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）揭示其自相似标度与从边界延伸至视界的相干结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索引力系统中湍流尾迹的构造与特征，填补四维渐近反德西特时空中该类研究的空白。摘要明确指出这是对四维渐近AdS时空湍流引力尾迹的首次研究。

Method: 采用特征形式（characteristic formulation）求解完整非线性爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equations）；通过作用于助推黑膜（boosted black brane）的局域边界形变（localized boundary deformation）生成尾迹，并分析沿x方向传播的尾迹的标度行为及其在视界面积密度（horizon area density）、无迹视界外曲率（traceless horizon extrinsic curvature）与体度规微扰（bulk metric perturbations）上的几何印记。

Result: 尾迹的边界动力学呈现出相干涡旋结构（coherent vortical structures），并被背景流（background flow）向下游平流；沿x方向传播的尾迹表现出经典湍流尾迹（classical turbulent wakes）特有的自相似标度（self-similar scaling）；在视界面积密度、无迹视界外曲率及体度规微扰中识别出从渐近边界延伸至视界的相干结构。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了四维渐近反德西特时空中湍流引力尾迹的首个研究，表明引力尾迹兼具经典湍流尾迹的自相似特征与贯穿体内部的相干几何结构。

Abstract: We construct turbulent gravitational wakes in asymptotically AdS$_4$ spacetimes by solving the full non-linear Einstein equations in the characteristic formulation. Specifically, we show that a localized boundary deformation acting on a boosted black brane generates a wake whose boundary dynamics exhibit the formation of coherent vortical structures that are advected downstream by the background flow. Analysing a wake propagating along the x direction, we show that it displays the characteristic self-similar scaling of classical turbulent wakes. We then identify the geometric imprint of the wake on the horizon area density, the traceless horizon extrinsic curvature, and bulk metric perturbations, finding coherent structures that extend from the asymptotic boundary to the horizon. These results provide the first study of turbulent gravitational wakes in four-dimensional asymptotically AdS spacetimes.

</details>


### [133] [Differential equations for Dotsenko--Fateev integrals: the case of degenerate fields $Φ_{n,1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18410)
*Aleksandra Ivanova*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究对应于 Dotsenko--Fateev 型积分、并与含退化场（degenerate field）$\\Phi_{n,1}$ 的四点关联函数（four-point correlation function）相关的高阶微分方程（higher-order differential equation），给出方程的一般结构与系数表达，并建立其与超几何算子（hypergeometric operator）的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 Dotsenko--Fateev 型积分所满足的高阶微分方程，并借助共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）中的特殊情形进行检验，同时厘清此类方程与超几何算子之间的联系。

Method: 以积分参数 $\\{A,B,C,g\\}$ 为基本变量，确定高阶微分方程的一般结构及其系数；并以 CFT 中的特殊情形作为验证手段，进一步建立高阶微分方程与超几何算子之间的关系。

Result: 得到了以积分参数 $\\{A,B,C,g\\}$ 表示的高阶微分方程的一般结构与系数；CFT 特殊情形被用于验证，且高阶微分方程与超几何算子之间的关系得以建立。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 Dotsenko--Fateev 型积分对应高阶微分方程的系统描述，并为 CFT 中退化场四点关联函数的方程结构及其与超几何算子的联系提供了依据。

Abstract: We study higher-order differential equations corresponding to integrals of the Dotsenko--Fateev type and associated with four-point correlation functions with degenerate fields $Φ_{n,1}$. We determine the general structure and coefficients of the equations in terms of the integral parameters $ \{ A,B,C,g \} $. In particular, special cases of these equations in CFT are used for verification. In addition, the relationship between higher-order differential equations and hypergeometric operators is established.

</details>


### [134] [Schwinger stability of T-duality-inspired extremal black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18528)
*Chiang-Mei Chen,Kimet Jusufi,Douglas Singleton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究零点长度 T-对偶（T-duality）方案下带电正则黑洞几何中与 Schwinger 对产生相关的近视界带电标量不稳定性，并给出极值分支、AdS₂×S² 近视界喉区及通用形状因子（form factor）下的不稳定性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究带电正则黑洞（regular black hole）近视界区域中 Schwinger 对产生所引发的带电标量不稳定性，并检验该不稳定性阈值是否为正则性（regularity）的普遍结果，而非特定几何的产物。

Method: 采用与零点长度 T-对偶方案相同的有效几何（effective geometry）与正则化规范势（regularized gauge potential），构造极值分支、其 AdS₂×S² 近视界喉区，并针对通用形状因子推导带电标量不稳定性阈值；在具体 T-对偶形状因子下求解端点，并与 Ayón-Beato-García（ABG）Einstein-非线性电动力学解进行对比。

Result: 对 T-对偶形状因子，极值分支终止于 r_ext = √2 l₀，此处极值电荷与近视界电场消失，而 AdS₂ 半径在 √3 l₀ 处保持有限；该端点处精确近视界不稳定性参数为负，半经典（semiclassical）区间的电荷-质量阈值在趋近端点时发散。相比之下，ABG 解的极值近视界电场非零，附加最小耦合带电标量探针的局部 Schwinger 阈值保持有限。

Conclusion: 对每个固定的有质量粒子种类与有限 q/m，极值分支中足够靠近端点的部分不存在局部带电标量不稳定性；T-对偶分支中出现的近视界 Schwinger 势垒发散并非正则性的普遍结果。

Abstract: We study the near-horizon charged-scalar instability associated with Schwinger pair production in the charged regular black hole geometry of the zero-point-length T-duality prescription. Using the same effective geometry and regularized gauge potential, we construct the extremal branch, its ${\rm AdS}_2 \times S^2$ near-horizon throat, and the charged-scalar instability threshold for a general form factor. For the explicit T-duality form factor the extremal branch terminates at $r_\mathrm{ext} = \sqrt2 \, l_0$, where the extremal charge and the near-horizon electric field vanish while the ${\rm AdS}_2$ radius remains finite at $\sqrt3 \, l_0$. At this endpoint the exact near-horizon instability parameter is negative. In the semiclassical regime the corresponding charge-to-mass threshold diverges as the endpoint is approached. Thus, for each fixed massive species with finite $q/m$, a sufficiently near-endpoint portion of the extremal branch is free of the local charged-scalar instability. For comparison, the Ayón-Beato-García (ABG) Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics solution has a nonvanishing extremal near-horizon electric field. For an additional minimally coupled charged-scalar probe, the corresponding local Schwinger threshold remains finite. Thus the divergent near-endpoint Schwinger barrier found in the T-duality branch is not a generic consequence of regularity.

</details>


### [135] [Deep learning emergent spacetime from fermionic spectral functions in holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18566)
*Koji Hashimoto,Hyun-Sik Jeong,Keun-Young Kim,Daichi Takeda,Kwan Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于神经常微分方程（Neural Ordinary Differential Equations）的物理信息机器学习（physics-informed machine learning）框架，从边界费米子谱函数（fermionic spectral functions）反演带电 AdS 黑洞的体时空与规范场，并可同时推断探针电荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息对偶（holographic duality）中由边界数据重构体时空属逆问题，通常不适定；本文旨在以物理约束引导的神经网络可靠求解该逆问题，并检验其解的唯一性边界。

Method: 采用神经常微分方程架构，将 UV 渐近行为（UV asymptotics）、视界正则性（horizon regularity）与零温极端性（extremality）作为硬约束（hard constraints）编入网络结构，从边界费米子谱函数重建极端 Reissner-Nordström AdS 几何与 U(1) 规范场。

Result: 框架在由 U(1) 探针电荷设定的三种量子临界区间——非费米液体（non-Fermi liquid）、边缘费米液体/奇异金属（marginal Fermi liquid, strange metal）与类费米液体（Fermi-liquid-like）态——中均能重建几何，并以亚百分比精度联合推断探针电荷；放松近 AdS 边界约束后出现几何简并。

Conclusion: 不同训练运行中自发出现的等谱非唯一性（isospectral non-uniqueness）与零温下一般全息论证预期的体简并一致，表明网络捕捉的是红外共形场论（IR CFT）普适性，而非对单一 UV 完备化的过拟合。

Abstract: We present a physics-informed machine learning framework based on Neural Ordinary Differential Equations that solves the holographic inverse problem: reconstructing the bulk spacetime and gauge field of a charged AdS black hole directly from boundary fermionic spectral functions. Encoding the UV asymptotics, horizon regularity, and zero temperature extremality as hard constraints in the neural network architecture, our framework reliably reconstructs the extremal Reissner-Nordström AdS geometry across three quantum critical regimes set by the $U(1)$ probe charge---non-Fermi liquid, marginal Fermi liquid (strange metal), and Fermi-liquid-like states---and can jointly infer the probe charge itself to sub-percent accuracy. Relaxing the near-AdS boundary constraint uncovers a geometrical degeneracy: bulk profiles that differ throughout the radial direction but share the same near-horizon $AdS_2 \times \mathbb{R}^2$ data reproduce identical spectral functions near the Fermi surface. This isospectral non-uniqueness is precisely the bulk degeneracy expected on general holographic grounds at zero temperature, and its spontaneous emergence across independent training runs shows that the network isolates the IR CFT universality rather than overfitting a single UV completion.

</details>


### [136] [The geometry of multiloop Feynman Integrals from the Scattering Facet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18668)
*José Ríos-Sánchez,Germán Rodrigo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将宇宙学多胞体（cosmological polytope）的散射面（Scattering Facet, SF）重新表述为一种底-纤维几何（base-fiber geometry），其纤维为一类变形图解环带体（deformed graphical zonotopes）。该分解自然引出因果表示（causal representation），并提供了刻画全部正则三角剖分（regular triangulations）的新数学工具，对应生成树表示（spanning tree representation），从而无需积分即可获得因果与生成树表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射面（SF）是由费曼图（Feynman diagram）定义的正当几何（positive geometry），其典范微分形式（canonical differential form）可重现标量费曼积分中对圈动量能量分量的积分结果，因而与圈-树对偶（Loop-Tree Duality, LTD）密切相关。本文旨在为 SF 提供新的几何描述，以更深入地理解其组合结构与散射振幅信息。

Method: 将 SF 描述为底-纤维几何（base-fiber geometry），其纤维为一族称为变形图解环带体（deformed graphical zonotopes）的多胞体，后者可用底层费曼图的因果演化（causal evolution）自然解释。基于该分解推导典范形式（canonical form），得到因果表示；并利用该几何框架给出刻画全部正则三角剖分（regular triangulations）的数学工具。

Result: SF 的分解诱导出典范形式的描述，进而导出所谓的因果表示（causal representation）；由这些三角剖分得到的典范形式对应生成树表示（spanning tree representation），与以生成树求和形式表达的原始 LTD 振幅相匹配。

Conclusion: 该几何视角提供了获取因果表示与生成树表示的新方法，且无需执行任何积分（without the need of performing any integration）。

Abstract: The Scattering Facet (SF) of the Cosmological Polytope is a positive geometry defined from a Feynman diagram and encoding its combinatorics. Its canonical differential form reproduces the result of the integration over the energy components of loop momenta in scalar Feynman integrals, and is therefore closely related to the Loop-Tree Duality (LTD). In this article, we present a novel description of the SF as a base-fiber geometry. The fibers are a family of polytopes called deformed graphical zonotopes, which have a natural interpretation in terms of the causal evolution of the underlying Feynman diagram, and capture interesting information on scattering amplitudes. This decomposition of the SF induces a description of the canonical form leading to the so-called causal representation. Moreover, it provides new mathematical tools that allow us to characterize all its regular triangulations. Canonical forms obtained from these triangulations correspond to spanning tree representations, matching the original LTD amplitudes expressed as a sum over spanning trees. This geometrical viewpoint provides us with new methods to obtain the causal and spanning tree representations, without the need of performing any integration.

</details>


### [137] [Carrollian axion electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18791)
*Hemant Rathi,Ashish Shukla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了与轴子场耦合的卡罗尔电磁学理论（Carrollian electromagnetism），通过对轴子电动力学（axion electrodynamics）作 $c \to 0$ 展开，得到电型与磁型两类理论，并给出其拉格朗日量与一般经典解。磁型理论具有非平凡轴子耦合，呈现 Witten 效应的卡罗尔类比，但线性磁电效应完全消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将轴子电动力学推广至卡罗尔极限（Carrollian limit，即 $c \to 0$ 展开），以探索非洛伦兹（non-Lorentzian）框架下轴子与电磁场耦合的新现象学。

Method: 方法为对轴子电动力学执行 $c \to 0$ 展开，构造电型与磁型两类理论的拉格朗日量并求解一般经典解；对磁型理论的约束结构进行完整的 Dirac-Bergmann 分析以识别其物理相空间。

Result: 结果显示：电型理论中轴子与电磁场有效解耦；磁型理论具有非平凡轴子耦合，出现 Witten 效应的卡罗尔类比——在恒定非零轴子背景下磁单极子获得电荷而成为双荷子（dyon）；同时线性磁电效应（linear magnetoelectric effect）完全消失。

Conclusion: 结论为卡罗尔轴子电动力学存在电型与磁型两种形式，其中磁型理论兼具非平凡轴子耦合与显著现象学，而线性磁电效应的消失经由 Dirac-Bergmann 约束分析与物理相空间的正确识别得以确立。

Abstract: We construct theories of Carrollian electromagnetism coupled to an axion field. Obtained by performing a $c \to 0$ expansion of axion electrodynamics, these are of two types: electric and magnetic. We derive the Lagrangians and work out the general classical solutions for both. While the electric theory has the axion effectively decoupled from the electromagnetic fields, the magnetic theory is endowed with a nontrivial axion coupling that leads to interesting phenomenology. In particular, a Carrollian analogue of the Witten effect, wherein a magnetic monopole in the presence of a constant non-vanishing axion acquires an electric charge and becomes a dyon, is present in the magnetic theory. At the same time, the linear magnetoelectric effect vanishes entirely, a conclusion we establish by performing a thorough Dirac-Bergmann analysis of the constraint structure of the magnetic theory and a proper identification of its physical phase space.

</details>


### [138] [Auxiliary Field Deformations of the Lambda Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18831)
*Christian Ferko,Cian Luke Martin,Pranat Sharma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对 λ 模型（lambda model）引入一种辅助场形变（auxiliary field deformation），构造出平坦性等价于运动方程的 Lax 联络（Lax connection），并通过哈密顿分析证明该形变理论保持 Maillet 结构与经典 Yangian（classical Yangian），即改变经典动力学但不改变可积结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可积 σ 模型（integrable sigma models）普遍存在通过耦合辅助场（auxiliary fields）并令其满足代数运动方程而得到的可积形变族。作者将这一范式推广到种子理论（seed theory）为 λ 模型的情形，该模型在 Wess-Zumino-Witten 模型与非阿贝尔 T 对偶（non-Abelian T-dual）的主手征模型（principal chiral model）之间插值。

Method: 构造 λ 模型的辅助场形变，并给出一个 Lax 联络（Lax connection），其在施加代数场方程后平坦性等价于剩余运动方程；随后对形变理论进行哈密顿分析（Hamiltonian analysis）。

Result: 所得 Lax 联络的平坦性在代数场方程约束下等价于剩余运动方程；哈密顿分析验证形变理论满足 arXiv:2605.18213 中定理的假设，从而保证 (i) 模型具有 Maillet 结构（Maillet structure）以确保守恒荷的对合性（involution），以及 (ii) 理论具有经典 Yangian。

Conclusion: 与其它情形一致，λ 模型的辅助场形变改变其经典动力学，但在本质上一仍保持可积结构不变。

Abstract: Many examples of integrable sigma models admit families of integrable deformations that involve coupling the undeformed ``seed'' theory to auxiliary fields with algebraic equations of motion. In this work, we apply this paradigm to the case where the seed theory is the lambda model, which interpolates between the Wess-Zumino-Witten model and the non-Abelian T-dual of the principal chiral model. We present an auxiliary field deformation of the lambda model and exhibit a Lax connection whose flatness is equivalent to the remaining equations of motion after imposing the algebraic field equations. We also carry out a Hamiltonian analysis of the deformed theory, verifying that it obeys the assumptions of theorems in arXiv:2605.18213 which guarantee both (i) that the model enjoys the Maillet structure that ensures involution of its conserved charges, and (ii) that the theory possesses a classical Yangian. Thus, as in other cases, the auxiliary field deformation of the lambda model changes its classical dynamics while leaving the integrable structure essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [139] [The Geometry of the CKM matrix, the Standard Model and RG fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18884)
*Brian P. Dolan,Charles Nash*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从几何视角研究标准模型中费米子混合矩阵，将CKM矩阵视为双陪集空间 M = T\\SU(3)/T 的元素并赋予黎曼度量，其奇点结构与重整化群不动点及Weyl群 S₃ 的六边形顶点相对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于以几何方法刻画标准模型中费米子混合矩阵（fermion mixing matrix）的结构，特别是CKM矩阵所蕴含的对称性与奇异性特征。通过对陪集空间的几何分析，试图为已知的重整化群不动点等物理性质提供统一描述。

Method: 方法上，将CKM矩阵 V 视为双陪集空间（double coset space）M = T\\SU(3)/T 的元素，其中 T = U(1)×U(1)。在该空间 M 上引入黎曼度量（Riemannian metric），并借助 SU(3) 的Weyl群 W（即对称群 S₃）分析其离散结构，后者以六边形顶点的形式出现在 V 中。

Result: 结果表明，空间 M 同时具有点奇异（point singularities）与连接这些奇点的线奇异（line singularities）；其中点奇异被证明对应于已知的重整化群不动点（renormalization group fixed points）。Weyl群 W ≅ S₃ 是诱导上述诸多性质的关键离散对象。

Conclusion: 结论指出，CKM矩阵的几何结构可由双陪集空间 M 的黎曼几何与其奇点（对应于重整化群不动点）统一描述，而 SU(3) 的Weyl群 S₃ 作为核心离散结构，主导了该空间中奇点分布及六边形顶点等几何特征。

Abstract: We investigate the geometry of the Fermion mixing matrix in the Standard Model. The principal structure studied is the CKM matrix, $V$, viewed as an element of the double coset space $M$, where $M=T\backslash SU(3)/T$, and $T=U(1)\times U(1)$. The space $M$ is given a Riemannian metric, and has both point singularities, which it is shown correspond to the known renormalization group fixed points, and line singularities connecting the fixed points. A key discrete object inducing many of these properties is the Weyl group, $W$, of $SU(3)$, $W$ being the symmetric group $S_3$, which appears in the guise of the vertices of a hexagon in $V$.

</details>


### [140] [Reading Topological Hair from Black-Hole Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18944)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将黑洞毛发（black-hole hair）对边界混合态纠缠的影响分离为局部应力-能量（local stress-energy）通道与经典度规不可见（invisible to the classical metric）的拓扑信息通道，并以非旋转 BTZ 黑洞上的 Nielsen–Olesen 涡旋（vortex）为对象加以刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞毛发可通过局域应力-能量与拓扑信息两条途径影响边界混合态纠缠，但二者此前未被清晰区分。作者旨在将这两条通道分离，以厘清拓扑毛发（topological hair）与引力修饰（gravitational dressing）的区别。

Method: 首先证明超选择定理（superselection theorem），表明 Shannon 部门熵（Shannon sector entropy）从 Markov 间隙（Markov gap）中相消；继而指出热态 $U(1)_k$ CFT 中标准 $U(1)$ 对称分辨反射熵（symmetry-resolved reflected entropy）在领头阶为等分（equipartitioned），排除了不平衡分辨间隙中普适的 $\log|n|$ 项。据此在探针 $U(1)_k$ Chern–Simons 完备化中定义 Wilson 穿线反射矩（Wilson-threaded reflected moment），并与紧致涡旋-通量类（compact vortex-flux class）耦合；同时由锚定视界的 Einstein-Abelian-Higgs 解计算带电矩模量与普通 Markov 间隙。

Result: 在具有环绕数（linking number）$\nu$ 的反射副本部门中，归一化相位为 $2\pi\kappa p n\nu/k$（$\kappa\in\mathbb Z$ 为混合拓扑耦合）；当 $\gcd(\kappa\nu,k)=1$ 时，离散傅里叶变换（discrete Fourier transform）可重构 $n\bmod k$。RT 连通性相变（RT connectivity transition）开启或关闭指定的连接轮廓，而独立标度 $\ell_\star r_+/L^2=1.128378\ldots$ 决定涡旋诱导的相变位移方向。带电矩模量与普通 Markov 间隙仍为几何可观测量。

Conclusion: 相位与模量的分离为区分拓扑毛发与引力修饰提供了判据，且无需引入缺乏支持、依赖绕数（winding-dependent）的熵。

Abstract: Black-hole hair can affect boundary mixed-state entanglement through local stress-energy and through topological information invisible to the classical metric. We separate these channels for a Nielsen--Olesen vortex on a nonrotating BTZ black hole. A superselection theorem shows that Shannon sector entropy cancels from the Markov gap, while standard $U(1)$ symmetry-resolved reflected entropy in a thermal $U(1)_k$ CFT is equipartitioned at leading order, excluding a universal $\log|n|$ term in the imbalance-resolved gap. We therefore define a Wilson-threaded reflected moment in a probe $U(1)_k$ Chern--Simons completion coupled to the compact vortex-flux class. In a reflected-replica sector with linking number $ν$, its normalised phase is $2πκpnν/k$, where $κ\in\mathbb Z$ is the mixed topological coupling; for $\gcd(κν,k)=1$, a discrete Fourier transform reconstructs $n\bmod k$. The RT connectivity transition switches the specified linked contour on or off, whereas the independent scale $\ell_\star r_+/L^2=1.128378\ldots$ determines the direction of the vortex-induced shift of that transition. The charged-moment modulus and the ordinary Markov gap remain geometric observables and are computed from a horizon-anchored Einstein-Abelian-Higgs solution with a complete first-variation kernel including the motion of the entanglement-wedge cross-section endpoints. This phase and modulus separation distinguishes topological hair from gravitational dressing without assigning an unsupported winding-dependent entropy.

</details>


### [141] [Majoron dark matter detection via hybrid magnon transmon qubit system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19056)
*Le Bin Ho,Do Thi Huong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出一种混合磁振子-量子比特（magnon-qubit）的haloscope方案，利用Majoron与电子自旋耦合产生的等效赝磁场，通过铁磁钇铁石榴石（YIG）球中的Kittel模共振响应、经腔介导色散相互作用传递至超导transmon量子比特，并用量子非破坏Ramsey干涉测量进行探测，从而评估对Majoron暗物质的预期灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻Majoron是可良好动机的暗物质候选者，可与电子自旋耦合，产生振荡赝磁场（pseudo-magnetic field）。该耦合为利用自旋体系进行探测提供了可能，因此需要设计专门的量子传感方案来搜寻此类暗物质。

Method: 采用混合磁振子-量子比特haloscope：Majoron场共振驱动铁磁YIG球的Kittel模，产生由大自旋系综增强的集体磁振子响应；磁振子布居通过腔介导的色散相互作用（dispersive interaction）转导至超导transmon量子比特，并用量子非破坏Ramsey干涉测量（quantum-nondemolition Ramsey interferometry）探测。基于已有实验参数推导信号、评估灵敏度，并分析借助磁场调谐的质量扫描策略。

Result: 基于已实验验证的磁振子-腔-量子比特系统参数，推导出Majoron诱导信号，给出预期灵敏度，并分析由磁场调谐实现的质量扫描策略；将预期探测范围与现有的轴子/Majoron-电子耦合的实验室及天体物理限制进行基准比较。

Conclusion: 结果表明，混合磁振子-量子比特架构是搜寻Majoron暗物质的有前景的量子传感平台，并可推广至更普遍的与电子自旋耦合的超轻玻色子场（ultralight bosonic fields）。

Abstract: Ultralight Majorons are well-motivated dark-matter candidates that can couple to electron spins, generating an oscillating pseudo-magnetic field. We propose a hybrid magnon-qubit haloscope that exploits this interaction to search for Majoron dark matter. In our scheme, the Majoron field resonantly drives the Kittel mode of a ferrimagnetic yttrium iron garnet (YIG) sphere, producing a collective magnon response enhanced by the large spin ensemble. The resulting magnon population is transduced to a superconducting transmon qubit through a cavity-mediated dispersive interaction and detected using quantum-nondemolition Ramsey interferometry. Using experimentally demonstrated parameters for magnon-cavity-qubit systems, we derive the Majoron-induced signal, evaluate the projected sensitivity, and analyze the corresponding mass-scan strategy enabled by magnetic-field tuning, benchmarking the projected reach against existing laboratory and astrophysical constraints on the axion/Majoron-electron coupling. Our results establish hybrid magnon-qubit architectures as a promising quantum-sensing platform for searches for Majoron dark matter and, more generally, ultralight bosonic fields coupled to electronic spins.

</details>


### [142] [When duality changes the poles: $SL(2,\mathbb{Z})$ transformations of linear response EFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19075)
*Andrea Amoretti,Daniel K. Brattan,Jonas Rongen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ 变换在 $(2+1)$ 维极点截断线性响应有效理论（effective field theory, EFT）中的作用，推导了静态磁化率、爱因斯坦关系与交流输运系数的显式映射，并在 D3/D5 探针膜系统中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于在有限低频域内亚纯的关联函数，低能有效描述不仅包含导数展开的输运系数，还保留了非流体力学极点。由于 $S$ 变换会反转光学电导率并交换其极点与零点，它未必与红外截断对易，因此需要明确该变换如何作用于截断后的 EFT 数据。

Method: 在 $(2+1)$ 维中分析 $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ 变换对极点截断线性响应 EFT 的作用，推导静态磁化率、爱因斯坦关系（Einstein relation）与交流输运系数的显式映射，并在替代量子化的 D3/D5 探针膜（probe-brane）系统中进行全息检验。

Result: 在纯流体力学（hydrodynamic）扇区，映射在原始输运数据上闭合；在准流体力学（quasihydrodynamic）区域，变换后的极点位置乃至留数构成额外的红外数据。全息结果验证了解析映射，并在控制参数较大时呈现逆德鲁德（inverse-Drude）电导率。

Conclusion: $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ 变换与红外截断一般不对易，导致准流体力学情形下需要额外的红外数据来描述变换后的理论；该构造在 D3/D5 探针膜系统中得到验证，其中保留一个原始有隙极点的 EFT 可对应保留零个、一个或两个极点的变换后 EFT。

Abstract: We study how $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ transformations act on pole-truncated linear-response effective theories in $(2+1)$ dimensions. For correlators meromorphic within a finite low-frequency domain, the EFT data include both derivative-expanded transport coefficients and the retained non-hydrodynamic poles. Since the $S$-trans\-for\-mation inverts the optical conductivity and exchanges its poles and zeros, it does not necessarily commute with the infrared truncation. We derive explicit maps for static susceptibilities, the Einstein relation and AC transport coefficients. In the purely hydrodynamic sector the map closes on the original transport data, while in quasihydrodynamic regimes the transformed pole positions and, in general, residues constitute additional infrared data. We test the construction in the alternatively quantised D3/D5 probe-brane system, where an EFT retaining one original gapped pole can correspond to transformed EFTs retaining zero, one or two. The holographic results verify the analytic maps and exhibit an inverse-Drude conductivity at large values of the control parameter.

</details>
