### [143] [Entropy spectroscopy of a tunable two-site Hubbard molecule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17818)
*Uhjin Kim,Seokyeong Lee,Gibum Yun,Dongsung T. Park,Soobeom Choi,Sangwoo Jeong,Donghoon Kim,V. Umansky,Yunchul Chung,Hyoungsoon Choi,H. -S. Sim,Hyung Kook Choi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究建立双量子点熵测量协议，测量GaAs双点构成的二格点Hubbard分子总熵，揭示隧穿与相互作用如何决定热活性微观态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Hubbard物理中，当隧穿与相互作用竞争时，哪些微观态保持热活性是核心问题；单点已有的基于电荷的熵探测方法需扩展至双点体系。

Method: 将单点电荷基熵测量方案扩展至双量子点，在固定失谐（detuning）下同步平移两个点能级，仅用单个电荷传感器（charge sensor）正确探测体系熵。

Result: 随点间隧穿增强，体系从两个原子式量子点经杂化分子态演化为合并单点；熵随隧穿诱导能级劈裂超过热能而下降，可分辨反键态（antibonding states）贡献减弱及杂化单态与三重态热贡献变化，与二格点Hubbard模型定量吻合。

Conclusion: 通过区分电荷构型相同但轨道与自旋内容不同的态，熵揭示了隧穿与相互作用如何决定最小Hubbard体系的热活性态。

Abstract: Determining which microscopic states remain thermally active when tunneling and interactions compete is a central question in Hubbard physics. Here we develop an entropy measurement protocol for a double quantum dot, extending the charge-based approach established for a single dot, and measure the total entropy of a tunable two-site Hubbard molecule realized in a GaAs double dot. By shifting both dot levels together at fixed detuning, the entropy is correctly probed with only a single charge sensor. As interdot tunneling increases, the system evolves from two atomic-like dots through hybridized molecular states to a merged single dot. Across this evolution, the entropy decreases as tunnel-induced energy splitting exceeds the thermal energy and suppresses the occupation of higher-energy states. The measurements resolve the diminishing contribution of antibonding states and the changing thermal contributions of hybridized singlet and triplet states, in quantitative agreement with a two-site Hubbard model. By distinguishing states with the same charge configuration but different orbital and spin content, entropy reveals how tunneling and interactions determine the thermally active states of the minimal Hubbard system.

</details>


### [144] [A semiconductor photon-pair source based on a polariton cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17900)
*Karol Kawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于永久激子偶极（permanent exciton dipole）所允许的辐射级联（radiative cascade）的半导体光子对源（photon-pair source），由置于金属纳米颗粒与反射镜之间的 GaAs 量子点（quantum dot）构成，激子与局域电磁模耦合形成上下极化激元（polariton）。永久偶极打破奇偶激发数态间的对称性禁戒，使上极化激元可向下极化激元衰变并发射闲频光子（idler photon），再返回基态发射信号光子（signal photon）。作者用有效模型（effective model）计算双光子经金属天线发射的概率，并分析光子计数涨落与两通道相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在半导体平台上实现可控的光子对（photon-pair）发射源。既有极化激元体系中，偶/奇激发数态间的对称性会禁戒极化激元间的光子发射，因此需要引入机制打破该对称性以开启级联发射通道。

Method: 构建理论模型：GaAs 量子点位于金属纳米颗粒与反射镜之间，激子与局域电磁模混合形成上、下极化激元。利用电子与空穴平均位置分离产生的永久激子偶极打破对称性，并采用有效模型计算制备于上极化激元态的系统经金属天线发射双光子的概率，以及发射光子计数的涨落与两发射通道之间的关联。

Result: 分离的平均电子与空穴位置赋予激子永久偶极并破坏禁戒对称性；上极化激元衰变至下极化激元时发射闲频光子，下极化激元返回基态时发射信号光子。计算给出了在假定发射体性质与光学损耗率下双光子经金属天线发射的概率，并给出光子计数涨落与两通道相关性结果。

Conclusion: 该计算为给定发射体性质与光学损耗率下的器件运行原理（operating principle）提供了依据。器件能否实际制备及其光子相关性（photon correlations）能否被测量仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of a semiconductor photon-pair source based on a radiative cascade enabled by a permanent exciton dipole. The source consists of a GaAs quantum dot placed between a metal nanoparticle and a mirror. The dot exciton and the localized electromagnetic mode mix to form upper and lower polaritons. Without a permanent dipole, a symmetry separating states with even and odd excitation numbers forbids photon emission between the polaritons. Separation of the mean electron and hole positions gives the exciton a permanent dipole and breaks this symmetry. The upper polariton can then emit an idler photon as it decays to the lower polariton. The lower polariton can emit a signal photon as the system returns to its ground state. Using an effective model, we calculate the probability that a prepared upper polariton emits both photons through the metal antenna. We also analyse fluctuations in the emitted photon counts and correlations between the two emission channels. The calculation establishes an operating principle for assumed emitter properties and optical loss rates. Whether the device can be built and its photon correlations measured remains open.

</details>


### [145] [Experimental assessment of the Wiedemann-Franz law in thin metal films using thermoreflectance and electrical measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17990)
*Zhiwei Deng,Jinlong Ma,Puqing Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究结合方波脉冲源（SPS）热反射法与范德堡（van der Pauw）电学测量，表征 Al、Ti、Ta 薄膜在 80–300 K 的热电输运性质，揭示以体相 Sommerfeld-Lorenz 数 L_0 为基础的 Wiedemann-Franz（WF）定律在沉积金属薄膜中的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属薄膜热物性对微电子热建模及对薄膜热输运敏感的热反射（thermoreflectance）测量解析至关重要，而实践中常用 WF 定律与 L_0 由电阻率推断热导率，忽略了声子贡献与微结构相关散射。

Method: 采用方波脉冲源（square-pulsed source, SPS）热反射法结合范德堡（van der Pauw）测量，对热蒸发、电子束蒸发与磁控溅射制备的 Al、Ti、Ta 薄膜进行 80–300 K 的热电表征，并将测得 k_m 与基于 L_0 及模型化电阻率 ρ_model(T)=ρ_bulk(T)+ρ_0 的 WF 估算值对比。

Result: SPS 给出薄膜热导率 k_m 与体积热容 C_m，C_m 与体相值偏差在 ±8% 内；与 WF 估算相比，Al 和 Ti 的 k_m 偏差为 5–20%，300 K 下溅射 Ta 偏差可达 40%；150–300 K 的表观 Lorenz 数 L_app=k_m ρ_meas/T 显示沉积致无序、晶界散射及可能相相关效应会改变热与电荷输运的关联。

Conclusion: 较小偏差与实验不确定度相当，而 Ta 的显著失配表明将体相 L_0 用于高阻金属薄膜存在局限；结果澄清了 WF 定律对沉积金属薄膜的适用性，并为热建模与热反射分析提供实用指导。

Abstract: Accurate thermal properties of metal thin films are essential for microelectronic thermal modeling and for interpreting thermoreflectance measurements when signals are sensitive to film thermal transport. In practice, film thermal conductivity is commonly inferred from electrical resistivity via the Wiedemann-Franz (WF) law with the Sommerfeld-Lorenz number L_0, neglecting phonon contributions and microstructure-dependent scattering. Here, we combine square-pulsed source (SPS) thermoreflectance with van der Pauw measurements to characterize thermal and electrical transport in Al, Ti, and Ta thin films prepared by thermal evaporation, e-beam evaporation, and magnetron sputtering. SPS measurements from 80 to 300 K yield the film thermal conductivity k_m and volumetric heat capacity C_m, with C_m agreeing with bulk values within +/-8%. Compared with WF-based estimates using L_0 and a modeled resistivity, \r{ho}_model (T)=\r{ho}_bulk (T)+\r{ho}_0, k_m deviates by 5-20% for Al and Ti and up to 40% for sputtered Ta at 300 K. While the smaller deviations are comparable to experimental uncertainty, the large mismatch for Ta demonstrates the limitation of applying the bulk L_0 to highly resistive metal films. Apparent Lorenz numbers L_app=k_m \r{ho}_meas/T from 150 to 300 K further reveal that deposition-induced disorder, grain-boundary scattering, and possible phase-related effects can modify the correlation between heat and charge transport in thin metal films. These results clarify the applicability of the WF law to deposited metal thin films and offer practical guidance for thermal modeling and thermoreflectance analysis.

</details>


### [146] [Unconventional linear transverse exciton transport in valley-layer coupling two-dimensional materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18035)
*Ci Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将“谷—层耦合（valley-layer coupling, VLC）”概念从电子体系拓展至激子（exciton）体系，以 TiSiCo 为原型材料，证明垂直电场 $E_{\perp}$ 可调控激子色散各向异性，从而在单层与扭角双层中产生非常规的线性横向激子输运，并可与 $\mathcal{T}$-even 层霍尔/能斯特激子逆流竞争与解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLC 二维材料（two-dimensional, 2D）中谷间由晶体对称性而非时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry, $\mathcal{T}$）联系，可实现栅控的谷对比层极化；作者希望把这一机制由电子推广到激子，探索电场对激子输运的调控能力。

Method: 以 TiSiCo 作为 VLC 原型材料，考察垂直电场 $E_{\perp}$ 对激子色散各向异性的调控，并在单层与扭角双层（twisted bilayer）结构中分析激子横向输运响应。单层中关注电导张量对角元反对称组合导致的横向电导；双层中利用对称性允许的电导张量对称部分响应，并考虑层间激子由 Förster 耦合（Förster coupling）联系。

Result: 单层中横向响应由各向异性诱导的横向电导刻画，来源于电导张量对角元的反对称组合，并可由 $E_{\perp}$ 强烈调控；扭角双层中对称性额外容许电导张量对称部分产生横向响应。由于不同层的层内激子由 Förster 耦合相连，对称与反对称响应共存，并与近期提出的 $\mathcal{T}$-even 层霍尔（layer Hall）和能斯特（Nernst）激子逆流相互竞争。

Conclusion: 该相互作用可由扭角、温度与面内驱动力方向高度调控，为实验上区分不同横向激子输运信号提供途径；结果确立 $E_{\perp}$ 作为借助 VLC 调控激子输运的有力手段，并将 VLC 二维材料定位为工程化激子现象（excitonic phenomena）的候选平台。

Abstract: Valley-layer coupling (VLC) two-dimensional (2D) materials define a distinct class of quantum systems in which valleys are related by crystal, rather than time-reversal ($\mathcal{T}$) symmetry, enabling gate-controlled valley-contrasted layer polarization. Here we extend this concept to excitons. Using TiSiCo as a prototype VLC material, we show that a perpendicular electric field $E_{\perp}$ controls exciton- dispersion anisotropy and thereby generates unconventional linear transverse exciton transport in both monolayer and twisted bilayer structures. In the monolayer, this response is characterized by anisotropy-induced transverse conductivities, arising from the antisymmetric combination of diagonal elements in the conductivity tensor and strongly tunable by $E_{\perp}$. In the twisted bilayer, symmetry additionally permits transverse responses from the symmetric part of the conductivity tensor. Since intralayer excitons in different layers are connected by the Förster coupling, these symmetric and antisymmetric responses coexist and compete with the recently proposed $\mathcal{T}$-even layer Hall and Nernst exciton counterflow. This interplay is highly tunable by twisted angle, temperature, and the direction of the in-plane driving force, providing a route to disentangle distinct transverse exciton transport signals experimentally. Our results establish $E_{\perp}$ as a powerful knob for controlling exciton transport via VLC and identify VLC 2D materials as a promising platform for engineered excitonic phenomena.

</details>


### [147] [Microscopic theory of spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance in nonmagnetic-metal/ferromagnetic-metal heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18090)
*Takumi Funato,Takeo Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立非磁金属/铁磁金属异质结中自旋力矩铁磁共振（ST-FMR）的微观理论，将ST-FMR谱与邻近电子体系的自旋响应直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有ST-FMR研究多依赖唯象描述，缺乏从微观层面统一刻画界面对称性与电子自由度贡献的框架，因此需要建立将界面交换耦合与电子自旋响应联系起来的理论。

Method: 通过积掉（trace out）铁磁体中传导电子自由度，推导局域自旋与相邻电子体系传导电子自旋之间的有效界面交换耦合；据此计算电流诱导驱动力矩、共振频移、阻尼调制及产生的直流电压；并以无序Rashba二维电子气（two-dimensional electron gas）为例进行具体求解。

Result: 在无序Rashba二维电子气情形下，ST-FMR谱被证明反映了相邻电子体系的动力学自旋响应（dynamical spin responses）。

Conclusion: 该理论适用于广泛的异质结体系，并确立了将ST-FMR谱直接关联至相邻体系电子自旋响应的统一微观框架。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic theory of spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance (ST-FMR) in nonmagnetic-metal/ferromagnetic-metal heterostructures. Tracing out the conduction-electron degrees of freedom in the FM, we derive an effective interfacial exchange coupling between the localized spins and the conduction electron spins in the adjacent electron system. Based on this interaction, we calculate the current-induced driving torque, resonance-frequency shift, damping modulation, and resulting dc voltage. As a concrete example, we apply the formulation to a disordered Rashba two-dimensional electron gas and demonstrate that the ST-FMR spectrum reflects the dynamical spin responses of the adjacent electron system. Our formulation applies to a broad class of heterostructures and establishes a unified microscopic framework connecting ST-FMR spectra directly to the electronic spin responses of adjacent systems.

</details>


### [148] [Landscape geometry of Majorana zero modes in inhomogeneous superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18138)
*Guo-Jian Qiao,Zhi-Lei Zhang,Kang Xu,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种实空间“景观（landscape）”方法，将零能方程改写为一阶动力系统，用稳定轨迹判定非均匀超导器件中马约拉纳零能模的存在与数目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间非均匀性破坏平移对称（translational symmetry），使传统布洛赫能带拓扑不变量（Bloch-band topological invariants）无法直接回答非均匀超导器件中马约拉纳零能模（Majorana zero modes）是否存活这一实际问题。

Method: 不将零能方程作为边值问题求解，而是把空间坐标视为演化参数，将方程改写为一阶动力系统（first-order dynamical system）；马约拉纳零能模对应满足物理边界条件且在大距离处趋于原点的稳定轨迹，并由景观几何确定稳定与不稳定子空间（stable and unstable subspaces）的维度。

Result: 景观几何固定稳定与不稳定子空间的维度，稳定子空间与物理边界子空间的交集决定马约拉纳零能模的数目；在均匀极限下，拓扑相变（topological phase transition）表现为景观的几何转变；应用于一维无自旋 p 波超导体（spinless p-wave superconductors）与纳米线—超导体系统，得到非均匀性幅度与空间梯度的充分界限。

Conclusion: 该实空间景观方法为马约拉纳器件设计提供定量判据（quantitative criteria），可给出非均匀性幅度与梯度的充分界限。

Abstract: Spatial inhomogeneity breaks translational symmetry and prevents conventional Bloch-band topological invariants from directly answering a practical question: do Majorana zero modes survive in a given inhomogeneous superconducting device? In this Letter, we develop a real-space landscape approach to address this question. Rather than solving the zero-energy equation as a boundary-value problem, we treat the spatial coordinate as an evolution parameter and recast the equation as a first-order dynamical system. A Majorana zero mode is then identified with a stable trajectory that satisfies the physical boundary condition and approaches the origin at large distance. We show that the landscape geometry fixes the dimensions of the stable and unstable subspaces, while the intersection of the stable subspace with the physical boundary subspace determines the number of Majorana zero modes. In the homogeneous limit, the topological phase transition is manifested as a geometric transition of the landscape. Applied to one-dimensional spinless $p$-wave superconductors and nanowire--superconductor systems, the approach yields sufficient bounds on both the amplitude and spatial gradient of the inhomogeneity, providing quantitative criteria for the design of Majorana devices.

</details>


### [149] [Tuneable terahertz transitions in zigzag graphene nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18340)
*R. R. Hartmann,M. E. Portnoi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 横向电场可在锯齿型石墨烯纳米带中打开可调至太赫兹频段的带隙，并实现频率依赖的偏振选择与谷选择性光激发，从而产生圆光电流响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在利用横向电场调控锯齿型石墨烯纳米带的电子结构，探索其在太赫兹（THz）频段的光学与光电响应机制，为紧凑可调太赫兹发射器提供物理基础。

Method: 通过分析横向电场对锯齿型石墨烯纳米带反射对称性的破缺效应，考察其诱导的带隙、允许的光学跃迁，以及平行与横向偏振跃迁强度随激发频率的变化关系。

Result: 电场诱导的带隙能量可在太赫兹范围内调谐；平行与横向偏振跃迁相对强度随频率变化并在特定频率相等；在该频率下，每种螺旋性（helicity）选择性激发两个场诱导边缘态谷（valley）之一，相反螺旋性对应谷反转；由于激发远离谷极小值，光激发载流子具有有限群速度，产生圆光电流（circular photogalvanic）响应；Van Hove奇异性（Van Hove singularities）增强带边辐射复合。

Conclusion: 该机制展示了锯齿型石墨烯纳米带中电场调控的谷选择性光激发与圆光电流效应，并结合带边增强的辐射复合，为紧凑、可调谐的太赫兹发射器提供了可能路径。

Abstract: We show that a transverse electric field applied to a zigzag graphene nanoribbon opens a band gap whose energy can be tuned through the terahertz (THz) range. The field breaks the reflection symmetry of the ribbon, allowing optical transitions that are forbidden in its absence. The relative strengths of transitions polarized parallel and transverse to the ribbon depend strongly on excitation frequency and become equal at a particular frequency. At this frequency, each helicity selectively excites carriers in only one of the two field-induced edge-state valleys, with the selected valley reversed for the opposite helicity. Since this excitation occurs away from the valley minima, the photoexcited carriers have finite group velocity, giving rise to a circular photogalvanic response. Van Hove singularities enhance radiative recombination at the band edge, providing a route towards compact, tunable THz emitters.

</details>


### [150] [Altermagnetic Magnons in Dipolar Nanomagnet Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18398)
*Rhea Hoyer,Ephraim Spindler,Lukas Körber,Tobias Wagner,Mathias Weiler,Alexander Mook*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出在仅由偶极相互作用耦合的纳米磁体阵列中实现交错磁（altermagnetism）特征，突破传统自旋守恒（spin-conserving）框架，通过宏自旋理论与微磁模拟证明交错磁对称性阵列具有自旋劈裂磁振子（spin-split magnon）能带及d波自旋期望值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对交错磁的理解基于自旋守恒框架，其中自旋劈裂源于共线磁体在净磁化消失条件下的对称性约束。本文动机在于检验该定义性特征能否在自旋守恒被内禀破缺的体系中存续。

Method: 采用偶极耦合铁磁纳米岛（ferromagnetic nanoislands）的宏自旋理论（macrospin theory），并以微磁模拟（micromagnetic simulations）进行验证，构建具有交错磁对称性的纳米磁体阵列。

Result: 具有交错磁对称性的阵列呈现自旋劈裂磁振子能带，其本征态可部分保持强自旋极化；自旋期望值在整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）呈现特征性d波图案，实现超越传统自旋守恒范式的介观交错磁磁振子。

Conclusion: 自旋波传播表现出强烈的方向依赖性，为各向异性磁振子输运（anisotropic magnon transport）与合成交错磁功能（synthetic altermagnetic functionality）提供了高度可调的平台。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is conventionally understood in a spin-conserving framework, where symmetry-enforced momentum-dependent spin splitting emerges in collinear magnets with vanishing net magnetization. Here, we show that its defining signatures persist in nanomagnet arrays coupled exclusively by dipolar interactions, despite the intrinsic breaking of spin conservation. Using a macrospin theory of dipolar-coupled ferromagnetic nanoislands, corroborated by micromagnetic simulations, we demonstrate that arrays engineered with altermagnetic symmetries exhibit spin-split magnon bands whose eigenstates can partially remain strongly spin polarized. The resulting spin expectation value displays the characteristic $d$-wave pattern throughout the Brillouin zone, establishing a mesoscopic realization of altermagnetic magnons beyond the conventional spin-conserving paradigm. As a consequence, spin-wave propagation becomes strongly direction dependent, providing a highly tunable platform for anisotropic magnon transport and synthetic altermagnetic functionality.

</details>


### [151] [Fröhlich Bipolarons in Two-Dimensional Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18402)
*A. Kudlis,V. Shahnazaryan,I. Iorsh,I. A. Shelykh,I. V. Tokatly*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究极性单层（polar monolayers）中双极化子（bipolaron）的形成，基于二维 Fröhlich 模型的推广，采用 Feynman 路径积分变分法（Feynman path-integral variational approach），发现其稳定区域与无色散声子的准二维模型存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受二维材料物理进展与 Fröhlich 模型近期二维推广的推动，研究极性单层中的双极化子形成。由于二维中非常特殊的非局域介电屏蔽（nonlocal dielectric screening），该体系与常规 Fröhlich 模型存在定性差异。

Method: 采用 Feynman 路径积分变分法（Feynman path-integral variational approach）。模型包含三项二维特征：(i) 长波长 LO 声子获得非平庸色散；(ii) Fröhlich 电子-声子顶点（electron-phonon vertex）变得依赖动量并在小动量处正则；(iii) 载流子间直接排斥取 Keldysh-Rytova 形式。

Result: 双极化子稳定存在的参数空间区域相较无色散声子的常规准二维模型被强烈改变。在最有利极限下，即静态极化率与高频极化率之比 σ₀ 趋于无穷时，形成双极化子所需的下临界耦合（lower critical coupling）可任意小。更意外的是，在强耦合极限下不存在稳定双极化子：孤立单层中稳定区域始终局限于有限的耦合常数范围。总体而言，稳定区域被强烈推向较大的极化率比 σ₀，远超代表性晶体的参数范围。

Conclusion: 极性单层不利于双极化子的形成（polar monolayers do not favor bipolarons）。稳定区域强烈移向大 σ₀ 值，超出代表性晶体的参数区间，表明在这些材料中难以实现稳定双极化子。

Abstract: Motivated by the progress in the physics of two-dimensional materials and the recent two-dimensional generalization of the Fröhlich model, we study the formation of bipolarons in polar monolayers. Because of very special nonlocal dielectric screening in two dimensions, this setting differs qualitatively from the conventional Fröhlich model. In monolayers, (i) long wavelength LO phonons acquire a nontrivial dispersion; (ii) the Fröhlich electron-phonon vertex becomes momentum-dependent and regular at small momenta; and (iii) direct repulsion between charge carriers takes the Keldysh-Rytova form. Using the Feynman path-integral variational approach, we show that the region in the parameter space where stable bipolarons exist is strongly modified compared to the usual quasi-two-dimensional model with dispersionless phonons. Specifically, in the most favorable limit, when the ratio $σ_0$ of the static polarizability to the high-frequency polarizability tends to infinity, the lower critical coupling, sufficient for the formation of bipolarons, can be made arbitrarily small. More surprisingly, we demonstrate that no stable bipolarons can exist in the strong coupling limit: in the isolated monolayer the stability region is always confined to a finite range of coupling constants. In general, the stability region is strongly shifted toward large values of the polarizability ratio $σ_0$, well beyond the parameter regimes in representative crystals, which indicates that polar monolayers do not favor bipolarons.

</details>


### [152] [Chiral classical and quantum acoustics with hole-spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18485)
*Zhanning Wang,Yongtao Li,Nelson E. Rivas,Gonzalo García,Irene Castro,Rubén Seoane Souto,Daniel Ramos,José C. Abadillo-Uriel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出并刻画了空穴自旋量子比特中的“自旋-声学手性（spin-acoustic chirality）”，即对向传播表面声波在耦合强度上的横向不对称性，并表明其可通过栅控量子点约束与磁场方向调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 栅控空穴自旋兼具强自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与优异的应变敏感性，使表面声波（surface acoustic waves, SAW）成为实现远程、相位相干控制的自然途径；但不同传播方向声波与自旋耦合的差异及其可调性此前缺乏系统描述。

Method: 采用多带 Luttinger-Kohn 哈密顿量，结合应变的 Bir-Pikus 描述与三维压电有限元模拟，得到控制相干 SAW 驱动的声学 g-矩阵（acoustic g-matrix）调制；随后对同一应变介导相互作用进行量子化，导出单声子耦合及含共转、反转与纵向分量的广义各向异性 Rabi 相互作用。

Result: 发现对向传播 SAW 在耦合强度及驱动类型（自旋旋转或量子比特频率调制）上存在差异，此横向跃迁强度不平衡即自旋-声学手性；其可通过栅控重塑量子点约束进行调节。磁场取向与点形状可调控声学手性及算符选择性，并将经典手性与量子各向异性、方向性单声子发射联系起来。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了以声学手性与算符选择性为自由度的控制策略，并通过双色相干保护（bichromatic coherence protection）与声子介导的预告式贝尔态初始化（heralded Bell-state initialization）加以例证。

Abstract: Gate-defined hole spins combine strong spin-orbit coupling with exceptional strain sensitivity, making surface acoustic waves a natural route to remote, phase-coherent control. We show that counterpropagating surface acoustic waves can differ in coupling strength and in whether they predominantly drive spin rotations or modulate the qubit frequency. We call the transverse transition-strength imbalance spin-acoustic chirality and show that it is tunable through gate-controlled reshaping of the dot confinement. Combining a multiband Luttinger-Kohn Hamiltonian with the Bir-Pikus description of strain and 3D piezoelectric finite-element simulations, we obtain the acoustic g-matrix modulation governing coherent SAW driving. Quantizing the same strain-mediated interaction yields the corresponding single-phonon coupling, and a generalized anisotropic Rabi interaction with co-rotating, counter-rotating, and longitudinal components, linking classical chirality to quantum anisotropy and directional one-phonon emission. Magnetic field orientation and dot shape tune the acoustic chirality and operator selectivity. We illustrate this framework through bichromatic coherence protection and phonon-mediated heralded Bell-state initialization.

</details>


### [153] [Layer-Dependent Vibrational and Optical Properties of $\mathrm{Mo}{0.58}\mathrm{W}{0.42}\mathrm{Se}_2$ Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18572)
*Szymon Socha,Tomasz Wozniak,Elena Blundo,Malgorzata Brzoska,Grzegorz Krasucki,Piotr Wrobel,Antonio Polimeni,Adam Babinski,Maciej R. Molas,Katarzyna Olkowska-Pucko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作结合拉曼散射、光致发光、反射对比光谱与第一性原理声子计算，系统研究了机械剥离的 Mo₀.₅₈W₀.₄₂Se₂ 合金从单层（1L）到九层的光学与电子结构随层数的演化，建立了层数、层间耦合与能带结构的完整光谱指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体 MoₓW₁₋ₓSe₂ 合金可通过组分与层厚调控二维材料光学性质，但缺乏对不同层厚下光谱特征、层间耦合与电子结构演化的系统认识。

Method: 对机械剥离（mechanical exfoliation）的 Mo₀.₅₈W₀.₄₂Se₂ 薄片（1L 至九层）联合使用拉曼散射（Raman scattering, RS）、光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）、反射对比（reflectance contrast, RC）光谱与第一性原理声子计算（first-principles phonon calculations）。

Result: 识别出 13 个 RS 峰，其中低频层间剪切模（interlayer shear mode）的厚度依赖可由线性链模型（linear-chain model）良好描述，得到层间力常数 K_s=(2.996±0.015)×10¹⁹ N m⁻³；PL 显示从直接带隙（direct bandgap）1L 到间接带隙（indirect bandgap）多层的转变，其厚度演化由量子限域模型（quantum-confinement model）定量重现，给出面外约化有效质量 μ_⊥=0.75 m₀；RC 揭示四个激子共振（excitonic resonances），K± 谷相关的 A、B 激子几乎不随层厚变化，而来自能带嵌套区（band-nesting regions）的高能 C、D 共振显著红移。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了 MoₓW₁₋ₓSe₂ 合金中层厚、层间耦合与电子结构的光谱指纹，并提供可靠的非破坏性光学表征框架。

Abstract: Semiconducting Mo$_x$W$_{1-x}$Se$_2$ alloys provide a versatile platform for tailoring the optical properties of two-dimensional materials through both composition and layer thickness. Here, we systematically investigate mechanically exfoliated Mo$_{0.58}$W$_{0.42}$Se$_2$ flakes ranging from monolayer (1L) to nine layers by combining Raman scattering (RS), photoluminescence (PL), reflectance contrast (RC) spectroscopy, and first-principles phonon calculations. Thirteen RS peaks are identified, including the low-frequency interlayer shear mode, whose thickness dependence is well described by a linear-chain model, yielding an interlayer force constant of $K_s=(2.996\pm0.015)\times10^{19}$ N m$^{-3}$. PL measurements reveal a crossover from the direct-bandgap 1L to indirect-bandgap multilayers. The thickness evolution of the indirect optical transition is quantitatively reproduced using a quantum-confinement model, yielding an out-of-plane reduced effective mass of $μ_\perp=0.75 m_0$. RC spectroscopy reveals four excitonic resonances. While the A and B excitons associated with the $K^\pm$ valleys remain nearly independent of layer thickness, the higher-energy C and D resonances originating from the band-nesting regions exhibit pronounced redshifts, reflecting substantial thickness-induced modifications of the electronic band structure. These results establish comprehensive spectroscopic fingerprints of flake thickness, interlayer coupling, and electronic structure in Mo$_x$W$_{1-x}$Se$_2$ alloys and provide a reliable, non-destructive framework for their optical characterization.

</details>


### [154] [Synthetic twist geometry in mesoscopic quantum circuits: geometric capacitance and chiral mode splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18940)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出在介观量子电路（mesoscopic quantum circuits）中以有限可量子化电路图实现螺旋扭曲的合成几何（synthetic geometry），其静电电容核（capacitance kernel）的手性耦合项经由对角桥接电容实现，并预测反向旋转合成模式（counter-rotating synthetic modes）的分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是为螺旋扭曲（helicoidally twisted）的合成几何提供可在介观量子电路层面编码的可行路径，将含扭曲参数 $\Om$ 的无挠空间度规（torsionless spatial metric）对应到可实现的电路结构，从而在电路量子电动力学（circuit-QED）平台中产生可观测的手性效应。

Method: 以含扭曲参数 $\Om$ 的无挠度规导出电容核，其角向—轴向扇区含手性耦合 $-2\Om mk$；用仅由正性两节点电容（positive two-node capacitors）构成的有限可量子化电路图实现该核，其中对角桥（diagonal bridges）产生交叉项，无量纲扭曲量等于对角电容与总角向电容之比。电容矩阵经 $C^{-1}(\etaT)$ 进入哈密顿量，并采用有限图精确对角化、模拟光谱（simulated spectroscopy）及无序模拟进行检验。

Result: 精确有限图对角化证实了解析谱，并显示需要闭合的纵向环流（closed longitudinal circulation）：在开边界下桥相位可被规范消除，双峰（doublets）保持简并。模拟光谱分辨出手性扇（chiral fan）；无序模拟表明在所考察参数范围内，标定至百分量级的反应性无序（reactive disorder）不会遮蔽确定性分裂。扭曲织构（twist textures）产生界面模式，约瑟夫森非线性（Josephson nonlinearities）使克尔扇区（Kerr sector）呈现手性依赖，同一双峰还可作为非厄米奇异点（non-Hermitian exceptional-point）控制的失谐旋钮。对代表性电路量子电动力学参数，分裂处于数十兆赫（tens-of-MHz）量级，远高于典型谐振腔线宽。

Conclusion: 研究表明螺旋扭曲合成几何可由正电容构成的有限量化电路图直接实现，其手性分裂在闭合环流条件下稳健且可通过光谱观测，并可扩展至界面模式、手性克尔效应及非厄米奇异点调控等应用。

Abstract: We propose a route for encoding a helicoidally twisted synthetic geometry in mesoscopic quantum circuits. A torsionless spatial metric with twist parameter $\Om$ defines an electrostatic capacitance kernel whose angular--axial sector contains the chiral coupling $-2\Om mk$. We show that this kernel can be implemented by a finite, quantizable circuit graph built only from positive two-node capacitors: diagonal bridges generate the cross term, and the dimensionless twist is the ratio of diagonal to total angular capacitance. The resulting capacitance matrix enters the Hamiltonian through $C^{-1}(\etaT)$ and splits counter-rotating synthetic modes. Exact finite-graph diagonalization confirms the analytic spectrum and shows that closed longitudinal circulation is required; with open boundaries the bridge phase is gauge removable and the doublets remain degenerate. Simulated spectroscopy resolves the chiral fan, while disorder simulations indicate that calibrated percent-level reactive disorder does not obscure the deterministic splitting in the parameter range considered. Twist textures produce interface modes, Josephson nonlinearities make the Kerr sector chirality dependent, and the same doublet provides a detuning knob for non-Hermitian exceptional-point control. For representative circuit-QED parameters the splitting is in the tens-of-MHz range, well above typical resonator linewidths.

</details>

### [155] [The number of communities in real networks grows as a power law of their size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18962)
*Alexei Vazquez*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文测量了近一百个真实网络中社区（community）数量与网络规模的关系，发现社区数目随规模呈幂律增长（power law），指数介于二分之一到三分之二之间，高于分形网络（fractal network）的平方根值而低于趋近于线性增长的界限，表明这是一种稳定的网络组织特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络普遍会分裂为社区结构，但“一个网络应有多少个社区、该数目如何随规模增长”这一基本问题仍未解决。已有简单模型仅给出可能性边界：由稠密模块构成的洞穴图（caveman graph）中社区数与规模成正比（n），而自相似分形网络（self-similar, fractal network）中增长慢得多，为平方根，真实网络可能位于两者之间。

Method: 研究测量了近一百个真实网络的社区数量，规模跨越四个数量级（从34个成员的俱乐部到数百万节点），并追踪若干单一系统在数十年生长过程中的变化；同时采用基于局部规则（local rules）的简单网络生长模型进行分析。

Result: 真实网络中社区数量随规模呈幂律增长，指数介于二分之一与三分之二之间，高于分形网络的指数值、低于线性极限；基于局部规则的简单生长模型能够复现相同的“超分形（super-fractal）”指数。

Conclusion: 网络演化会将系统组分聚集成簇，其簇（社区）数目随规模以幂律方式增长，该指数是网络组织中一种稳定且可测量的性质。

Abstract: Most networks, from friendships to the internet, break up into communities: groups of nodes more densely connected to one another than to the rest. A basic question has remained open: how many communities should a network have, and how does that number grow with size? Simple models bracket the possibilities. In a small-world network built from dense modules -- a caveman graph of cliques -- each module is a community, so the number grows in proportion to size, as $n$. In self-similar, fractal networks it grows far more slowly, as the square root. Real networks could lie anywhere between. Here we measure the number of communities across close to a hundred real networks spanning four orders of magnitude, from a $34$-member club to millions of nodes, and within individual systems followed over decades as they grow. We find a power law with an exponent between one half and two thirds -- above the fractal value and below the linear limit. Simple models of network growth by local rules reproduce the same super-fractal exponent, a stable and measurable property of network organization. In short, network evolution herds a system's components into clusters, whose number grows as a power law of size.

</details>

### [156] [Endpoint eigenfunction restriction estimates in codimension two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18290)
*Xing Wang,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该研究确定光滑闭黎曼流形中余维 2 子流形上 Laplace 特征函数的端点 L² 限制估计最优速率，证得比经典 O(λ^{1/2}√logλ) 略优的 o(λ^{1/2}√logλ)，并构造反例证明该改进在一般情况下是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典估计（Burq–Gérard–Tzvetkov 与 Hu）已给出 O(λ^{1/2}√logλ) 的端点限制界，但能否进一步去掉对数因子（log-removal question）尚不明确。作者希望刻画该端点估计的真正最优速率，并回答对数项能否移除的问题。

Method: 对每个固定的光滑余维 2 子流形 Σ，利用高斯变换（Gaussian transform）与泰勒逼近（Taylor approximation）将问题归约为可由 Stein–Street 奇异 Radon 变换（singular Radon transform）估计处理的多项式模型；三维情形则直接使用 Ricci–Stein 估计。同时构造显式例子验证最优性。

Result: 对每个固定光滑余维 2 子流形 Σ，证明端点限制估计取得 little-oh 改进 o(λ^{1/2}√logλ)；并通过显式反例表明该改进在一般情况下最优，从而论证无对数估计 O(λ^{1/2}) 一般不成立。

Conclusion: 结论给出了端点特征函数限制估计的对数移除问题（log-removal question）的否定回答，与 Montgomery–Smith 及 Carbery–Hofmann 关于端点 Strichartz 估计（endpoint Strichartz estimates）的反例密切相关。

Abstract: We investigate the optimal endpoint $L^2$ restriction estimates of Laplace eigenfunctions on submanifolds of codimension 2 in a smooth closed Riemannian manifold $M$. For every fixed smooth codimension-two submanifold $Σ$, we prove a little-oh improvement $o(λ^{1/2}\sqrt{\logλ})$ on the classical estimate $O(λ^{1/2}\sqrt{\logλ})$ of Burq--Gérard--Tzvetkov and Hu. Our proof uses the Gaussian transform and Taylor approximation to reduce the problem to polynomial models that can be handled by Stein--Street's estimate on singular Radon transforms. The three-dimensional case can be handled directly by Ricci--Stein's estimate. Moreover, we construct explicit examples to show that this improvement is optimal in general. These are closely related to earlier counterexamples for endpoint Strichartz estimates by Montgomery--Smith and Carbery--Hofmann. In particular, we give a negative answer to the log-removal question on the endpoint eigenfunction restriction estimates, as the log-free estimate $O(λ^{1/2})$ cannot hold in general.

</details>


### [157] [A very short proof of a new energy identity for strong solutions to the Navier-Stokes system in 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18808)
*Thomas Ruf*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明三维不可压缩齐次 Navier-Stokes 系统（Navier-Stokes system）在 $H^1_\sigma(\mathbb{R}^3)$ 初值下强解（strong solutions）全局存在，核心是一个排除有限时间爆破（finite-time blow-up）的新能量估计（energy estimate）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 Navier-Stokes 系统的全局正则性（global regularity）与有限时间爆破问题是流体方程领域的核心公开难题，本文旨在通过新的能量估计方法排除爆破并由此推出强解的全局性与弱解的唯一性结论。

Method: 证明的关键是一个新的能量估计，用于排除 $L^r(\mathbb{R}^3)$（$r = 2 + 2/\sqrt{3}$）中的有限时间爆破；该估计依赖物质导数（material derivative）的性质与无散度约束（divergence-free constraint）的结合，并通过精细估计处理新能量泛函的粘性强制性（viscous coercivity）与扰动压力项（pressure term）控制之间的微妙相互作用。

Result: 该估计不适用于仅共享标准能量结构的系统（如 T. Tao 的平均 Navier-Stokes 系统）；作为推论，当 $u(0,\cdot) \in L^2_\sigma(\mathbb{R}^3) \cap L^3(\mathbb{R}^3)$ 时每个 Leray-Hopf 弱解（weak solution）全局唯一；当 $u(0,\cdot) \in L^2_\sigma(\mathbb{R}^3)$ 时，Leray-Hopf 弱解类中唯一可能的非唯一性来源是初始分叉（initial branching），且每个解在 $(0,\infty) \times \mathbb{R}^3$ 上 $C^\infty$-光滑。

Conclusion: 在 $H^1_\sigma(\mathbb{R}^3)$ 初值下强解全局存在；若初值光滑且各阶导数在无穷远处快速衰减，则解在 $[0,\infty) \times \mathbb{R}^3$ 上 $C^\infty$-光滑。

Abstract: We prove that strong solutions to the incompressible, homogeneous Navier-Stokes system on $\mathbb{R}^3$ are global in time for initial data in the Sobolev space $H^1_σ(\mathbb{R}^3)$. The central ingredient of the proof is a new energy estimate that rules out finite-time blow-up in $L^r(\mathbb{R}^3)$ for $r = 2 + 2 / \sqrt{3}$. This estimate is based crucially on properties of the material derivative in combination with the divergence-free constraint, together with an intricate estimate that harnesses the delicate interplay between the viscous coercivity of the new energy functional and the control of a perturbing pressure term. Notably, the estimate does not carry over to systems that merely share the standard energy structure, such as T. Tao's averaged Navier-Stokes system. As a consequence of the new energy estimate, every Leray-Hopf weak solution $u$ to the homogeneous Navier-Stokes system is globally unique whenever the initial value $u(0, \cdot)$ belongs to $L^2_σ(\mathbb{R}^3) \cap L^3(\mathbb{R}^3)$. Even for $u(0, \cdot) \in L^2_σ(\mathbb{R}^3)$, the only possible non-uniqueness in the class of Leray-Hopf weak solutions is initial branching, and every such solution is $C^\infty$-smooth on $\left( 0, \infty \right) \times \mathbb{R}^3$. Moreover, if $u(0, \cdot)$ is smooth with derivatives of all orders decaying rapidly at infinity, we show that $u$ is in fact $C^\infty$-smooth on all of $\left[ 0, \infty \right) \times \mathbb{R}^3$.

</details>

### [158] [From Yang-Baxter to Robinson-Schensted-Knuth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18502)
*Leonid Petrov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本摘要阐述如何从杨-巴克斯特方程（Yang-Baxter equation）推导代数组合学（algebraic combinatorics）中的基本双射 RSK 对应（Robinson-Schensted-Knuth correspondence），并进一步在一般参数下以概率化方式推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSK 对应是代数组合学的核心双射，杨-巴克斯特方程则源于量子多体系统（quantum many-body systems）并成为可解格模型（solvable lattice models）理论的基石。将二者联系，可揭示组合双射的深层物理与代数结构，并为其变形提供推广路径。

Method: 在顶点模型（vertex model）中，箭头占据网格边，每个顶点依据四条相邻边上的箭头赋予玻尔兹曼权重（Boltzmann weight），配分函数（partition function）为给定边界条件下所有构型权重之和。对配分函数为舒尔多项式（Schur polynomials）的顶点模型，杨-巴克斯特方程两端的求和项之间存在唯一保权的匹配，沿网格传递即得到 Fomin 生长图（growth diagrams）形式的经典 RSK 对应。在自然坐标下，该局部匹配成为组合三维 R（combinatorial three-dimensional R），即扎莫洛奇科夫四面体方程（Zamolodchikov tetrahedron equation）的集合论解，其周期闭包给出单行晶体（one-row crystals）的组合 R-矩阵（combinatorial R-matrices）。

Result: 该强制匹配为舒尔权重所特有。对于 Hall-Littlewood 与 q-Whittaker 变形及其自旋版本（spin versions），在一般参数值下不存在对所有边界数据均有效的确定性匹配。将杨-巴克斯特方程的各实例按概率方式读取，以两端耦合（coupling）取代匹配——即双射化（bijectivization），或称概率双射（probabilistic bijection）——可获得输运顶点模型概率测度的马尔可夫算子（Markov operators）。

Conclusion: 这些马尔可夫算子在网格上迭代，产生随机化的 RSK 型动力学与相互作用粒子系统（interacting particle systems），包括 q-PushTASEP 与随机六顶点模型（stochastic six-vertex model）。由此，从杨-巴克斯特方程出发可统一理解经典 RSK 对应及其概率化推广。

Abstract: We explain how to derive the Robinson-Schensted-Knuth (RSK) correspondence, a fundamental bijection in algebraic combinatorics, from the Yang-Baxter equation. The Yang-Baxter equation arose in the study of quantum many-body systems and later became a cornerstone of the theory of solvable lattice models, particularly vertex models. In a vertex model, arrows occupy the edges of a grid, and each vertex carries a Boltzmann weight determined by the arrows on the four edges meeting at it. The weight of a configuration is the product of these local weights, and a partition function is the sum of the weights of all configurations with prescribed boundary conditions. For a vertex model whose partition functions are the Schur polynomials, the two sides of each instance of the Yang-Baxter equation admit exactly one weight-preserving matching of their summands. Carried across a grid, this forced matching is the classical RSK correspondence in the form of Fomin's growth diagrams. In natural coordinates the local matching becomes the combinatorial three-dimensional R, a set-theoretic solution of the Zamolodchikov tetrahedron equation. Its periodic closure returns the combinatorial R-matrices of one-row crystals.
  The forced matching is special to the Schur weights. For the Hall-Littlewood and q-Whittaker deformations and their spin versions, at generic parameter values no deterministic matching works for all boundary data. Reading each instance of the Yang-Baxter equation probabilistically, we replace the matching by a coupling of the two sides - a bijectivization, or probabilistic bijection - and obtain Markov operators that transport probability measures attached to vertex models. Iterated over the grid, these operators produce randomized RSK-type dynamics and interacting particle systems, including q-PushTASEP and the stochastic six-vertex model.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Graph lattice sums and graph zeta functions for long-range interacting quantum lattice models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18918)
*Andreas Alexander Buchheit,Andreas Rupp*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该工作解决了高阶关联簇展开（high-order linked-cluster expansions）中高维振荡格点和（lattice sums）难以计算的问题，使涉及幂律核（power-law kernels）的图 zeta 函数（graph zeta functions）均可计算，将最先进级数展开的求值时间从数万核心小时降至分钟量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有隙量子晶格模型（gapped quantum lattice models）模拟中希尔伯特空间维度随系统尺寸指数增长，是理解与设计非常规量子材料（尤其含非局域相互作用者）的核心障碍；高阶关联簇展开可求解无限系统本征值问题，但其依赖的高维振荡格点和此前只能用蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）方法处理。

Method: 先将格点和按块（block）分解；再按各块树宽（treewidth, tw）选取最廉价策略：基本块（basic blocks）以广义 zeta 函数（generalized zeta functions）的解析形式表示；树宽 tw≤2 的串并联块（series-parallel blocks）基于 Epstein zeta 函数与快速衰减傅里叶级数的半解析代数，以节点数与动量网格尺寸的线性代价计算；tw>2 时结合张量网络桶消元（tensor-network bucket elimination），使数值工作量与内存对动量网格尺寸呈多项式标度，指数仅随 tw 增长而非随顶点数增长；并借助快速傅里叶变换（FFT），以单次动量求值的代价恢复整个动量网格。

Result: 方法将最先进级数展开的求值时间从数万核心小时降至分钟；文中给出针对解析与数值基准（benchmarks）的精度与运行时间详细分析；并复现了横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）在一维、二维、三维不同晶格上的已发表蒙特卡洛数据，结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 通过按树宽分层的块分解策略，原本只能依赖蒙特卡洛的图 zeta 函数格点和变得可精确高效计算，显著降低高阶关联簇展开的计算成本，并在横场伊辛模型多维度晶格上验证了与已有蒙特卡洛结果的一致性。

Abstract: Taming the exponential increase of the Hilbert space dimension with system size in the simulation of gapped quantum lattice models is of the highest relevance for understanding and designing exotic quantum materials, where nonlocal interactions are of particular interest. High-order linked-cluster expansions provide access the solution of the eigenvalue problem for the infinite system, yet rely on the computation of high-dimensional oscillatory lattice sums with a graph structure, only approachable with Monte Carlo methods so far. This work resolves this issue, rendering all required graph lattice sums, referred to as graph zeta functions for kernels involving power-laws, computable. The resulting method reduces the evaluation time for state-of-the art series expansions from tenthousands of core-hours to minutes. After factorizing the lattice sum over blocks, each block is evaluated by the cheapest available strategy depending on its treewidth $\mathrm{tw}$. Basic blocks admit analytic forms in terms of generalized zeta functions. Series-parallel blocks with $\mathrm{tw}\le 2$ can be computed at linear cost in the number of graph nodes and in the size of the momentum grid using a semi-analytical algebra based on Epstein zeta functions and rapidly decaying Fourier series. Finally, for $\mathrm{tw}>2$, the method is combined with tensor-network bucket elimination yielding polynomial scaling of numerical work and memory in momentum grid size with exponents only growing with $\mathrm{tw}$ rather than with the number of vertices. Through use of FFT, the full momentum grid is recovered at the cost of a single momentum evaluation. We provide a detailed analysis of the precision and runtime of our method against analytic and numerical benchmarks. We further reproduce published Monte Carlo data for the transverse-field Ising model on different 1D, 2D, and 3D lattices, obtaining full agreement.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Two-Parameter Family of Nonlinear Dirac Equations With Scalar-Scalar plus Vector-Vector Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18170)
*Avinash Khare,Fred Cooper,John F. Dawson,Avadh Saxena*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 该论文在1+1维中求解了标量-标量（scalar-scalar, SS）与矢量-矢量（vector-vector, VV）混合相互作用下非线性狄拉克方程（nonlinear Dirac equation）的精确孤波解，并分析其电荷、能量及稳定性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机为探究含SS与VV两类相互作用、且具有普遍参数p与κ的非线性狄拉克模型中，是否存在稳定的孤波束缚态，并厘清其能量-电荷关系与参数空间依赖。

Method: 作者采用定态形式ψ(x,t)=e^{-iωt}ψ(x)，在0<ω<m范围求解精确解，计算各孤波解的电荷Q与能量E，并推导该双参数族在非相对论极限下的修正非线性薛定谔方程（nonlinear Schrödinger equation, NLSE）。

Result: 求得该ω区间内全部解；发现E与Q虽依赖耦合常数g，但比值E/Q与g无关；孤波束缚态（E/Q<m）存在的(p,κ)区域被确定；pκ≤1时电荷密度为单峰，pκ>1时出现双峰到单峰的转变（由ω/m决定）；所有p下E/Q随ω的行为在κ=2处发生转变，推测与κ=2时解的不稳定性起始相关。

Conclusion: 该模型给出与g无关的E/Q关系及由p、κ控制的孤波结构转变；κ=2处E/Q行为的转变可能标志解不稳定性的出现，且在此处得到修正NLSE并讨论了单峰孤波在其有效域内的稳定性。

Abstract: We obtain exact solutions of the nonlinear Dirac equation in $1+1$ dimensions of the form $ψ(x,t) = e^{-iωt} ψ(x)$ for scalar-scalar (SS) plus vector-vector (VV) interaction with interaction Lagrangian given by $$L_{I} = \frac{g^2}{κ+1}[(\barψ ψ)^{κ+1} +\frac{1}{p} (\barψ γ_μψ\barψ γ^μ ψ)^{κ+1}]$$ where $p > 0$ but arbitrary otherwise. We look for solutions with $0 < ω< m$ where $ω, m$ are frequency and mass, respectively. We find solutions for all values of $ω$ in this range. We compute the charge $Q$ and the energy $E$ for each of the solitary wave solutions and explore the region in the ($p, κ$) parameter space in which solitary wave bound states exist (i.e., for which $E/Q < m$). We show that for all the cases while both $E$ and $Q$ depend on the coupling constant $g$, their ratio $E/Q$ is independent of $g$. We further find that in case $pκ\le 1$, the charge density for all the solitary waves have only single hump while for $p κ> 1$ there is a transition from double to single hump and we determine it as a function of $ω/m$. We notice that for all $p$ there is a transition at $κ=2$ in the behavior of $E/Q$ as a function of $ω$ which we speculate is related to the onset of instability of the solutions at $κ=2$. We obtain the nonrelativistic reduction of the two-parameter family to a non-relativistic modified nonlinear Schrödinger equation (NLSE) and discuss the stability of the single hump olitary waves in the domain of validity of the modified NLSE.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Atomic-scale structure of static screening in noble-metal nanoparticles from clusters to the conductor limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18781)
*Pulkit Joshi,Marek Sierka*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 金属纳米颗粒的屏蔽电荷具有原子尺度的结构化特征，可在约一个原子宽的表层内保持，且无法由偶极矩等积分量或连续介质描述确定。作者以第一性原理诱导密度校准了 Ag 与 Au 的原子级电荷—偶极模型，误差降低约 2.5 倍，并经连续快速多极子实现扩展至数百万原子体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极矩、极化率（polarizability）等积分可观测量无法唯一确定诱导电荷密度（induced charge density），因为不同屏蔽剖面可给出相同积分值。研究动机在于揭示并定量解析贵金属纳米颗粒中该密度的原子级结构，这是连续介质（continuum）描述所缺失的。

Method: 将 Ag、Au 纳米颗粒的原子级电荷—偶极模型（charge-dipole model）直接校准到第一性原理（first-principles）诱导密度剖面，校准中纳入纯静电模型忽略的硬度核（hardness kernel）短程动能与交换关联（exchange-correlation）贡献。模型采用连续快速多极子（continuous fast multipole）实现。

Result: 纳入短程贡献使诱导密度剖面误差降低约 2.5 倍。每种元素单一平均参数集可预测未参与拟合的团簇，并可无调整地迁移至 Ag/Au 核壳（core-shell）、合金化与拉长颗粒。计算量随原子数近似线性增长，可达数百万原子颗粒，远超第一性原理方法的能力范围。

Conclusion: 贵金属纳米颗粒的屏蔽电荷在面、边、顶点位点呈现差异化响应，该结构在积分响应已达导体极限时仍存在于约一个原子宽的表层。所提原子级模型兼顾精度与可迁移性，并将可处理体系规模扩展至数百万原子。

Abstract: The static screening response of a metallic nanoparticle is encoded in its induced charge density. Integrated observables such as the dipole moment or the polarizability do not determine this density, because many different screening profiles yield the same integrals. We show that in noble-metal nanoparticles the screening charge is atomically structured, with facet, edge, and vertex sites responding differently from a smooth classical conductor. This structure survives in a surface layer of about one atomic width even when the integrated response has reached the conductor limit, and it is absent from continuum descriptions. To resolve it across size, morphology, and composition, we calibrate an atomistic charge-dipole model for Ag and Au nanoparticles directly to first-principles induced-density profiles. The calibration includes the short-range kinetic and exchange-correlation contribution to the hardness kernel that a purely electrostatic model omits, reducing the induced-density-profile error by about a factor of 2.5. A single averaged parameter set per element predicts clusters withheld from the fit and transfers without adjustment to Ag/Au core-shell, alloyed, and elongated particles. A continuous fast multipole implementation of the model scales almost linearly with the number of atoms and reaches several-million-atom particles, far beyond the reach of first-principles methods.

</details>


### [162] [Floquet-Plasmon Enhanced Charge Transfer at Catalytic Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19117)
*Annabelle Canestraight,Phillip Christopher,Vojtech Vlcek*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究等离激元（plasmon）瞬态电场如何动态扰动分子电子结构并影响催化界面电荷注入。以 CO2/Au(111) 为模型，在含关联前沿轨道活性空间下实时计算分子格林函数，发现驱动场产生瞬态 Floquet 型复制能带，结合双温度 Sommerfeld 热电子分布预测注入相关的准粒子谱重叠显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于等离激元驱动电荷转移的理论多将电荷转移视为由分子-衬底平衡电子结构与等离激元衰减产生的时变载流子分布共同决定，忽略了局域表面等离激元伴随的强瞬态电场在超快时间尺度上对分子电子结构本身的动态扰动。该文旨在探究此类时变场如何改变分子谱函数并影响催化界面处的电荷注入。

Method: 采用 CO2 吸附于 Au(111) 的模型体系，在含关联的前沿轨道活性空间（correlated frontier-orbital active space）内、类等离激元驱动条件下实时计算分子格林函数（Green's function）；并将演化中的分子谱与双温度 Sommerfeld 框架描述的时间依赖热电子分布相耦合。

Result: 驱动场会在分子态密度中快速产生瞬态 Floquet 型复制能带（Floquet-type replica bands），从而开启在无外场驱动或时间局域（time-local）描述中不存在的额外热载流子注入共振通道。将演化谱与热电子分布耦合后，预测支配等离激元退相位（dephasing）期间注入的准粒子谱重叠出现大幅增强。

Conclusion: 结果表明，动态产生的非平衡谱结构可能在等离激元辅助催化（plasmon-assisted catalysis）中发挥重要作用，并为超越静态电子结构描述、研究驱动催化界面提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Plasmonic excitation of metallic nanostructures can generate energetic carriers capable of transferring charge into nearby adsorbed molecules, providing a possible pathway for driving chemical transformations. In many theoretical descriptions of this process, charge transfer is determined by the equilibrium electronic structure of the molecule and substrate together with the time-dependent carrier distribution produced during plasmon decay. However, the intense transient electric fields associated with localized surface plasmons can also dynamically perturb the molecular electronic structure itself on ultrafast timescales. In this work, we investigate how these time-dependent fields modify the molecular spectral function and influence charge injection at catalytic interfaces. Using a model of CO2 adsorbed on Au(111), we compute the real-time molecular Green's function within a correlated frontier-orbital active space under plasmon-like driving. We find that the driving field rapidly produces transient Floquet-type replica bands in the molecular density of states, opening additional resonant pathways for hot-carrier injection that are absent without external driving or in time-local descriptions. Coupling the evolving molecular spectrum to a time-dependent hot-electron distribution described within a two-temperature Sommerfeld framework, we predict large enhancements in quasiparticle spectral overlap that governs injection during plasmon dephasing. These results suggest that dynamically generated non-equilibrium spectral structure may play an important role in plasmon-assisted catalysis and provide a framework for studying driven catalytic interfaces beyond static electronic structure descriptions.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Structure of Measurement-Induced Entanglement in Infinite-Randomness Critical States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17670)
*Oliver Breach*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了针对由无限随机性不动点（infinite-randomness fixed points, IRFPs）支配的一维临界态的投影测量（projective measurements）理论，以随机 XXZ 链与随机横场 Ising 模型为例。结果显示测量诱导纠缠（measurement-induced entanglement）以普适指数 (3-√5)/2 幂律衰减，且所需最小测量数服从普适标度形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影测量对多体量子态的影响与其纠缠结构紧密相关，临界态因长程纠缠而对局域测量尤为敏感；现有研究多集中于可由共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）描述的临界态，而对具有淬火无序（quenched disorder）、无 CFT 描述的临界态知之甚少。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对两种密切相关的模型——随机 XXZ 链与随机横场 Ising 模型——发展了一套描述由 IRFP 支配的一维临界态上测量的理论，并分析测量诱导纠缠的衰减行为与建立该纠缠所需的最小测量数。此外，考察仅施加有限密度测量时态临界性质的稳健性。

Result: 在两种模型中，测量诱导纠缠均以带普适指数 (3-√5)/2 的幂律衰减，建立该纠缠所需的最小测量数服从普适标度形式。在有限密度测量下，态的临界性质表现出显著的稳健性：不存在“测量改变的临界性（measurement-altered criticality）”，测量要么保持临界指数不变，要么完全破坏临界性。

Conclusion: 结果表明 IRFP 为研究临界态上的测量提供了一个可解析处理的平台，与 CFT 情形互为补充。在无限随机性下，洁净系统中需要复制方法（replica methods）处理的结果随机性变得平凡，其普适响应由淬火无序所诱导的统计规律决定。

Abstract: The impact of projective measurements on a many-body quantum state is tightly linked to its underlying entanglement structure. Critical states are particularly sensitive, as long-range entanglement allows local measurements to have global consequences. This has been extensively studied in the context of critical states described by a conformal field theory (CFT), where measurements can alter critical properties in post-measurement quantities ('measurement-altered criticality') and induce long-range entanglement with universal features ('measurement-induced entanglement'). By contrast, little is known about the role of measurements on critical states with quenched disorder, which admit no CFT description. Here, we develop a theory of measurements on one-dimensional critical states governed by infinite-randomness fixed points (IRFPs), focusing on two closely related examples: the random XXZ chain and the random transverse-field Ising model. We show that the measurement-induced entanglement decays as a power-law with universal exponent $(3-\sqrt{5})/2$ in both models, and that the minimum number of measurements required to establish this entanglement obeys a universal scaling form. When only a finite density of measurements is made, we show that the critical properties of the state are remarkably robust: `measurement-altered criticality' is absent, with measurements either preserving the critical exponents, or destroying the criticality entirely. These results establish IRFPs as an analytically tractable arena for measurements on critical states, complementary to the CFT case. The outcome randomness that necessitates replica methods in clean systems becomes trivial at infinite randomness, with the universal response governed by the statistics induced by the quenched disorder.

</details>


### [164] [Hydrodynamic memory in overdamped colloidal dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17677)
*Benjamin Sorkin,Günther Turk,Howard A. Stone*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文证明流体动力学记忆（hydrodynamic memory）在过阻尼（overdamped）极限下依然存在，并给出了含记忆效应的过阻尼朗之万方程（overdamped Langevin equation）的闭式形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微米级胶体粒子在流体中扩散时存在水动力惯性与记忆效应，导致速度自相关呈幂律衰减；但多数理论为方便起见采用过阻尼朗之万动力学（overdamped Langevin dynamics），消去速度、忽略惯性与幂律记忆，可能遗漏关键物理。

Method: 通过识别控制早期与晚期动力学交叉的无量纲参数，在胶体无惯性（colloid-inertialess）极限下推导出含流体动力学记忆的过阻尼朗之万方程的闭式表达。

Result: 理论预测水动力记忆在更小粒子与更强外驱动力下更为显著，并为从常规指数弛豫（exponential relaxation）向记忆主导的幂律动力学（power-law dynamics）的交叉提供实验探测方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为真实描述流体中胶体动力学提供理论框架，并为高分辨率实验中观测到的位置记忆（positional memory）建立严格基础。

Abstract: Micron-sized colloid particles diffusing through a fluid experience hydrodynamic inertial and memory effects, the latter causing velocity autocorrelation to decay as a power law. At the same time, many theoretical descriptions treat colloidal diffusion in a fluid using overdamped Langevin dynamics for its convenience, eliminating velocity, omitting inertia, but also ignoring the power-law memory. In this Letter, we show that hydrodynamic memory survives in the overdamped (colloid-inertialess) limit. By identifying the dimensionless parameter controlling the crossover between early- and late-time dynamics, we derive in closed form the overdamped Langevin equation in the presence of hydrodynamic memory. This provides a theoretical framework for realistically describing colloidal dynamics in a fluid, and establishes a rigorous basis for the positional memory observed in high-resolution experiments. Our theory predicts that hydrodynamic memory becomes increasingly pronounced for smaller particles and under stronger external forcing, and offers experimental probes for the crossover from conventional exponential relaxation to memory-dominated power-law dynamics.

</details>


### [165] [Brownian motion with geometry-dependent hydrodynamic memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17678)
*Benjamin Sorkin,Günther Turk,Howard A. Stone*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个理论框架，可将任意流体流动问题的解转化为单个胶体（colloid）的欠阻尼与过阻尼运动方程，其中包含斯托克斯阻力（Stokes drag）与类巴塞特（Basset-like）流体动力学记忆效应，并给出过阻尼极限下的额外漂移项，适用于复杂受限几何中的被动与活性粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微米尺度粒子在流体中运动时，除稳态斯托克斯摩擦（Stokes friction）外，还因流体守恒律表现出流体动力学记忆效应（hydrodynamic memory effects）。然而，复杂几何与受限情形下的流动问题虽有数值或近似解，却难以转化为单个胶体的闭式运动方程，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: (i) 利用洛伦兹互易定理（Lorentz reciprocal theorem），将胶体运动方程表示为几何格林函数（Green's function）与流体流动剖面的形式，从而同时得到斯托克斯阻力与类巴塞特记忆贡献；(ii) 针对胶体惯性通常可忽略的情形，推导相应的过阻尼（overdamped）极限，该极限继承斯托克斯与类巴塞特阻力，并额外产生一个虚假漂移（spurious drift）。

Result: 该框架能够从任意流动问题的解中导出胶体的欠阻尼（underdamped）与过阻尼动力学方程，涵盖斯托克斯阻力、类巴塞特记忆项以及过阻尼极限下的虚假漂移。

Conclusion: 作者提出将该框架应用于受复杂受限条件与问题几何影响的被动与活性粒子（passive and active particles），为复杂环境中胶体动力学的解析描述提供途径。

Abstract: Micron-sized particles moving through a fluid are subject to viscous resistance and thermal fluctuations. Beyond steady Stokes friction, colloids also exhibit hydrodynamic memory effects arising from conservation laws of the surrounding fluid. Although fluid-flow problems in complex geometries and confinements are often highly involved, numerical or approximate solutions can be obtained; translating these solutions into closed-form equations of motion for individual colloids, however, is rarely possible. Here, we develop a theoretical framework that provides both underdamped and overdamped colloidal dynamics from the solution of an arbitrary flow problem: (i) Using the Lorentz reciprocal theorem, we express the colloidal equation of motion in terms of the geometry's Green's function and the fluid-flow profile. This formulation yields both the corresponding Stokes drag as well as a Basset-like hydrodynamic memory contribution. (ii) As colloid inertia is often negligible, we furthermore derive the corresponding overdamped (colloid-inertia-less) limit, which inherits the Stokes and Basset-like resistances and additionally gives rise to a spurious drift. We propose applications of this framework to passive and active particles subject to involved confinements and problem geometries.

</details>


### [166] [Activity-induced emergent flatness, instabilities and pattern formation in fluid membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17716)
*Debayan Jana,Astik Haldar,Abhik Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究证明微观上反演不对称的可渗透活性流体膜在大尺度上统计平坦且有效反演对称，其涨落由渐近精确的线性流体力学方程支配，并在中间尺度呈现含空间长程噪声的KPZ行为，同时揭示活性应力与渗透流可导致膜失稳与图案形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注活性流体膜在微观尺度反演不对称（inversion asymmetry）条件下的宏观统计行为，旨在厘清活性应力与渗透流如何影响膜的平坦性、对称性与有序性，并探索活性驱动下膜失稳的机制。

Method: 采用理论流体力学与统计场论框架：推导渐近精确的线性流体力学方程描述大尺度涨落，并在中间尺度借助含空间长程噪声的Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）方程刻画动力学，结合已知精确标度指数分析有序性。

Result: 大尺度上膜统计平坦且有效反演对称，具有取向长程有序（orientational long-range order）与位置准长程有序（positional quasi-long-range order）；中间尺度表现为粗糙膜，呈短程平移有序与长程取向有序，标度指数可精确给出。活性应力可在长波长处使膜失稳，足够强的活性渗透流可驱动有限波矢不稳定性与图案形成（pattern formation）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一条由活性驱动导致平坦流体膜被破坏的新路径，表明活性应力与渗透流是控制膜稳定性与有序性的关键因素，并统一了不同尺度下的膜涨落与有序行为。

Abstract: We show that microscopically inversion-asymmetric, permeable active fluid membranes are statistically flat and effectively inversion-symmetric at large scales. Their fluctuations are governed by an asymptotically exact linear hydrodynamic equation giving orientational long-range order and positional quasi-long-range order. At intermediate scales, their dynamics is described by a Kardar-Parisi-Zhang equation with spatially long-range noise, producing rough membranes with short-range translational and long-range orientational order described by exactly known scaling exponents. Active stresses can destabilize the membrane at long wavelengths, while sufficiently strong active permeation flow can drive finite-wavevector instabilities and pattern formation. These results reveal a novel activity-driven route to the destruction of flat fluid membranes.

</details>


### [167] [Macroscopic Response Diagnoses the Noise Sensitivity of Terminal Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17982)
*Bo Li,Chaoqian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究高斯型微观输入下多体系统终端宏观结果的噪声敏感性（noise sensitivity），提出一个可测量的充要判据：协同扰动的终端结果与原结果的关联渐近消失，当且仅当平衡阈值处结果概率对控制场的斜率增长慢于系统体积的平方根。三维驱动随机场Ising模型的模拟结果与该噪声敏感区一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文不满足于构造从微观初始数据直接推断宏观终态的"捷径"，而追问一个更基本的问题：对于高斯型微观输入，任意固定的Wiener-Hermite阶数（Wiener-Hermite degree）能否在系统增大时仍保留终端结果方差的非零比例。这涉及系统宏观结果是否对微观噪声高度敏感这一基本性质。

Method: 研究对象为具有独立高斯无序（Gaussian disorder）的均匀系统，其所有微观坐标在对称性上等价，终端事件对每个无序变量单调，且均匀无序平移精确等价于控制场平移（转换因子与系统尺寸无关）。方法上结合正向与反向高斯影响界（forward and inverse Gaussian influence bounds）以及高斯Russo公式，推导噪声敏感性的判据；并采用事件驱动模拟（event-driven simulation）验证。

Result: 推导出直接可测的充要判据：当且仅当平衡阈值处结果概率对控制场的斜率增长慢于系统体积的平方根时，原始终端结果与坐标扰动终端结果之间的相关性在任意固定非零坐标噪声水平下渐近消失。三维驱动随机场Ising模型（driven random-field Ising model）在线性尺寸至192的事件驱动模拟显示，归一化响应与扰动样本间相关性整体下降，与有限尺寸下的噪声敏感区一致。对带预设种子的空间Stag-Hunt动力学，判据通过有效影响坐标数得到推广。路径依赖最优反应博弈（best-response game）的模拟表明，在所研究尺寸范围内终端均衡可依赖于更新时序，同时仍携带大量有限阶预测信息。

Conclusion: 高斯微观输入下多体系统的终端宏观结果可以是噪声敏感的，其敏感性由控制场斜率相对系统体积平方根的增长速率决定；该判据在空间Stag-Hunt动力学与路径依赖博弈等场景中得到推广或补充，表明固定阶数模型无法保留终端结果方差的非零比例。

Abstract: Can the terminal macroscopic outcome of a many-body system be inferred from its microscopic initial data without simulating the full trajectory? Rather than construct such a shortcut, we address a more fundamental question for Gaussian microscopic inputs: can any fixed Wiener-Hermite degree retain a nonvanishing fraction of the variance of the terminal outcome as the system grows? We consider homogeneous systems with independent Gaussian disorder in which all microscopic coordinates are symmetry-equivalent, the terminal event is monotone in each disorder variable, and a uniform disorder shift is exactly equivalent to a control-field shift with a size-independent conversion factor. Using forward and inverse Gaussian influence bounds together with a Gaussian Russo formula, we derive a directly measurable criterion that is both necessary and sufficient for noise sensitivity. Specifically, the correlation between the original and coordinate-perturbed terminal outcomes vanishes asymptotically for every fixed nonzero level of coordinatewise noise if and only if the slope of the outcome probability with respect to the control field at the balanced threshold grows more slowly than the square root of the system volume. Event-driven simulations of the three-dimensional driven random-field Ising model up to linear size 192 find that both the normalized response and the correlations between perturbed samples decrease overall, consistent with the noise-sensitive regime at finite size. For spatial Stag-Hunt dynamics with prescribed seeds, the criterion generalizes through an effective number of influential coordinates. Separately, simulations of a path-dependent best-response game show, over the sizes studied, that the terminal equilibrium can depend on the update schedule while still carrying substantial finite-order predictive information.

</details>


### [168] [The quantum Mpemba effect in symmetric random Clifford circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17988)
*Shion Yamashika,Filiberto Ares,Pasquale Calabrese*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文在具有U(1)守恒荷的随机Clifford电路中证实了量子姆潘巴效应（quantum Mpemba effect），并借助纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）刻画动力学对称性恢复过程，最终通过映射到电荷守恒量子自动机给出解析解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应是一种反直觉现象：初始离平衡更远的量子态反而比初始更近的态弛豫更快，但其微观机制尚不清晰。本文旨在在可精确模拟的随机Clifford电路中验证该效应并揭示其机制。

Method: 采用纠缠不对称性来表征动力学的对称性恢复；对具有U(1)守恒荷的随机Clifford电路进行精确数值模拟；并将动力学映射为电荷守恒的量子自动机（quantum automaton），其纠缠不对称性的衰减由两类粒子在对称简单排他过程（symmetric simple exclusion process）中的相遇统计所控制。

Result: 数值模拟显示，初始更不对称的态可以变得比初始较不对称的态在局域上更对称。该映射进一步给出了纠缠不对称性的解析表达式，并表明更强的初始对称性破缺对应更密的粒子构型，从而提高粒子相遇频率并加速对称性恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子姆潘巴效应提供了简单的微观机制：初始对称性破缺的强度通过粒子密度调控相遇频率，进而决定对称性恢复的速率，并给出了纠缠不对称性的解析描述。

Abstract: The quantum Mpemba effect is the counterintuitive phenomenon whereby a quantum state initially farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to it. We demonstrate this effect in random Clifford circuits with a conserved $\mathrm{U}(1)$ charge, using the entanglement asymmetry to characterize relaxation through dynamical symmetry restoration. Exact numerical simulations show that an initially more asymmetric state can become locally more symmetric than an initially less asymmetric one. We explain this behavior by mapping the dynamics onto a charge-conserving quantum automaton, in which the decay of the entanglement asymmetry is controlled by the statistics of encounters between two particle species evolving according to a symmetric simple exclusion process. This mapping yields an analytic expression for the entanglement asymmetry and provides a simple microscopic mechanism for the quantum Mpemba effect: stronger initial symmetry breaking corresponds to a denser particle configuration, which enhances the frequency of particle encounters and thereby accelerates symmetry restoration.

</details>


### [169] [Entropy Estimates from Stochastic Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18093)
*Phillip M. Rauscher*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）的通用方法，用于估计任意概率分布之间的熵差，仅依赖Gibbs-Shannon熵定义，并在多个复杂体系中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有熵差估计通常需要昂贵的散度计算或直接学习score field，且受限于特定统计力学系综。该工作旨在提供一种计算简便、适用范围更广的熵差估计方法。

Method: 利用随机插值构造连接基分布与目标分布的边缘分布，其满足连续性方程，从而将熵差表达为概率流速度（probability flow velocity）与score field的内积。当选取可处理的基分布（如高斯链、理想气体）时即可恢复统计热力学熵。

Result: 方法在四个复杂度递增的体系上得到验证：(i) 40维高斯混合模型，(ii) 一维N自旋经典XY模型，(iii) 13原子Lennard-Jones团簇，(iv) 活性布朗聚合物。

Conclusion: 该框架无需对两个场进行高成本散度计算，若无需模型可迁移性甚至无需直接学习score field，且训练过程中可近乎免费地在线估计熵。由于仅基于Gibbs-Shannon熵定义而非特定统计力学系综，可计算广义非平衡熵。

Abstract: We present a general method for estimating entropy differences between arbitrary probability distributions using stochastic interpolants. The marginal distributions which bridge the base and target obey a continuity equation, which allows a straightforward calculation of the entropy difference in terms of an inner product of the probability flow velocity and score fields. This formulation has several advantages: (i) no computationally expensive divergence calculations of either field are required, (ii) the score field need not even be learned directly if model transferability is not required, and (iii) on-the-fly estimates are produced nearly for free during training. When tractable base distributions are chosen (e.g. Gaussian chain, ideal gas, etc.), statistical thermodynamic entropies are then immediately recovered. Notably, the analysis relies only on the definition of the Gibbs-Shannon entropy, rather than any particular statistical mechanical ensemble, so that generalized non-equilibrium entropies may be computed. The method is demonstrated on several systems of increasing complexity: (i) a 40-dimensional Gaussian mixture model, (ii) the classical XY model of N spins arranged in one dimension, (iii) the 13-atom Lennard-Jones cluster, and (iv) an active Brownian polymer.

</details>


### [170] [Logarithmic singularity in a dynamical quantum phase transition for free fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18208)
*Yasser Bezzaz,Dimitri M. Gangardt,Pavel L. Krapivsky,Jean-Marc Luck,Kirone Mallick,Sylvain Prolhac*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究从双畴壁（double-domain-wall）初态释放的 N 个无相互作用无自旋格点费米子（spinless lattice fermions）体系中的 Loschmidt echo（洛施密特回声），在大 N 极限下以动态自由能（dynamical free energy）这一大偏差率函数描述，并揭示动态量子相变（dynamical quantum phase transition, DQPT）与主导瞬子（instanton）构型的拓扑变化之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画双畴壁初态下格点费米子体系的 Loschmidt echo 与动态量子相变，并借助瞬子构型理解相变的物理与拓扑本质。

Method: 方法为：在 N 个无相互作用无自旋格点费米子体系中，于大 N 极限下用大偏差率函数（即动态自由能）刻画返回概率，通过复瞬子构型分析 Loschmidt echo，并进行短时与长时解析求解并辅以数值计算。

Result: 结果显示：Loschmidt echo 由复瞬子构型主导，动态自由能在 DQPT 处呈现对数奇异性；相变可理解为主导瞬子构型的拓扑变化，类似于随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）中割缝（cut）的出现；后选择（post-selection）可驱动体系经历两次连续 DQPT，对应于复时间中主导瞬子构型的连续拓扑变化。

Conclusion: 结论为：DQPT 可由主导瞬子构型的拓扑变化统一理解，且通过后选择可实现连续两次 DQPT，体现瞬子构型在复时间中的连续拓扑转变。

Abstract: We study the Loschmidt echo in a system of N non-interacting spinless lattice fermions released from a double-domain-wall initial state. In the large-N limit, the return probability is characterized by a large-deviation rate function known as the dynamical free energy. The Loschmidt echo is dominated by a complex instanton configuration, and the dynamical free energy develops a logarithmic singularity at the dynamical quantum phase transition. We obtain analytical results in the short-time and long-time regimes and support them with numerical computations. We show that the transition can be understood as a topological change of the dominant instanton configuration, analogous to the emergence of a cut in random matrix theory. We further show that post-selection can drive the system through two successive DQPTs, associated with successive topological changes of the dominant instanton configuration in complex time.

</details>


### [171] [Exact Nonlinear Active Microrheology in Diffusive Single-File Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18733)
*Aurélien Grabsch,Olivier Bénichou*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过结合流体力学输运系数、局部驱动的压力平衡表述与单列对偶性，论文为一般扩散型单列体系（single-file）建立了封闭的精确边值问题，获得了任意恒定力下的非线性响应与完整密度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动微流变学（active microrheology）以恒定力驱动示踪粒子来探测拥挤介质，但单列输运中任意力下的精确非线性响应长期仅限于少数可解模型。

Method: 将近期流体力学输运系数进展、局部驱动的压力平衡表述（pressure-balance formulation）与单列对偶性（single-file duality）三者结合，消除运动边界，得到封闭的精确边值问题。

Result: 对具有一般相互作用的过阻尼布朗粒子（overdamped Brownian particles），微观相互作用细节仅通过平衡态物态方程进入，从而将含有限宽度准一维通道的真实相互作用体系纳入精确可解范围；并揭示普适全局律，其中浴密度偶极子由外加力固定而与相互作用势无关。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了单列主动微流变学中任意力精确解的限制，为一般扩散单列提供了确定 ξ(F) 与完整密度分布的封闭精确框架，并给出与相互作用无关的普适规律。

Abstract: Active microrheology probes a crowded medium by forcing a tracer and measuring its response. In single-file transport, where particles cannot overtake, a constant force $F$ produces a subballistic displacement $\langle X_t\rangle\simeq \sqrt{t}\,ξ(F)$ together with a persistent bath deformation. Despite decades of work, the exact nonlinear response at arbitrary force has remained confined to a few special solvable models. Here we remove this restriction by combining recent advances in hydrodynamic transport coefficients, a pressure-balance formulation of the local drive, and single-file duality, which eliminates the resulting moving boundary. This yields a closed exact boundary-value problem for general diffusive single files, determining both $ξ(F)$ and the full density profile. For overdamped Brownian particles with general interactions, all microscopic interaction details enter only through the equilibrium equation of state, bringing realistic interacting systems, including finite-width quasi-one-dimensional channels, within exact reach. The solution also reveals universal global laws; in particular, the bath-density dipole is fixed by the applied force independently of the interaction potential.

</details>


### [172] [Recent progress on thermal transport in one-dimensional long-range interacting Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18765)
*Daxing Xiong,Nianbei Li,Jie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该综述统一梳理了一维长程（long-range, LR）FPUT型晶格中，由以幂律衰减的四次非谐耦合所主导的热输运行为，阐明LR非谐性如何改变微观能量交换、集体动力学与宏观热传导标度律，并区分已确立结论与仍存争议的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程相互作用已不再停留于理论构想，可在多种低维平台中被工程化实现，并提供调控热输运的新途径；同时它突破了主要针对短程、动量守恒晶格建立的标准图像。因此有必要系统厘清LR非谐性对热传导的影响。

Method: 以综述（review）形式，考察一维Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou（FPUT）型晶格，其中LR效应主要通过衰减为幂律的四次非谐耦合（quartic anharmonic coupling）引入，并以指数σ表征其衰减特征。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或定量结果，仅说明该综述以统一方式阐释LR非谐性对微观能量交换、集体动力学（collective dynamics）以及热传导宏观标度律（scaling laws）的作用。

Conclusion: 综述旨在统一说明LR非谐性如何改变从微观到宏观的热输运图像，并明确区分其中已被充分确立的结论与尚存争议的问题。

Abstract: Long-range (LR) interactions are no longer just a theoretical idea: they can be engineered in several low-dimensional platforms and offer a new way to control heat transport. At the same time, they push beyond the standard picture developed mainly for short-range, momentum-conserving lattices. In this review, we consider one-dimensional Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou (FPUT)-type lattices where LR effects enter mainly through a quartic anharmonic coupling that decays as a power law, characterized by an exponent σ. Our goal is to explain, in a unified way, how LR anharmonicity changes microscopic energy exchange, collective dynamics, and the macroscopic scaling laws of heat conduction-while also clarifying what is well established and what is still debated (For more details of the abstract, please see the main text and paper).

</details>


### [173] [A first introduction to Matrix Product State algorithms for the integration of Lindblad equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18841)
*Christophe Chatelain*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文综述并比较了一维量子晶格系统 Lindblad 方程数值积分的四种算法，均基于矩阵乘积态（Matrix Product State）表示，可视为时间演化块抽减（TEBD）在开放量子系统中的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一维量子晶格系统的开放量子动力学（open quantum dynamics）数值模拟需求，需梳理并对比现有基于矩阵乘积态的 Lindblad 方程求解方法，以明确各方法的原理与适用性。

Method: 方法分为两类：两种直接积分矢量化（vectorized）Lindblad 方程，其中一种显式强制密度矩阵的正定性（positivity）；另两种基于随机解缠（stochastic unraveling），即量子轨迹（quantum trajectory）与量子态扩散（quantum state diffusion）方法。

Result: 讨论并比较了各方法的原理、数值实现、精度与计算效率，并以一个可精确求解的自由费米子（free fermion）模型作为基准进行测试。

Conclusion: 四种方法均构成 TEBD 向开放量子系统的扩展，其性能与精度通过可精确求解模型的基准测试加以评估，为方法选择提供依据。

Abstract: In this introductory review, we present and compare four algorithms for the numerical integration of the Lindblad equation for one-dimensional quantum lattice systems. All four methods are based on Matrix Product State representations and can be viewed as extensions of the Time-Evolving Block Decimation (TEBD) algorithm to open quantum systems. Two approaches directly integrate the vectorized Lindblad equation, one of them explicitly enforcing the positivity of the density matrix. The other two rely on stochastic unravelings of the Lindblad equation, namely the quantum trajectory and quantum state diffusion approaches. We discuss the principles, numerical implementation, accuracy, and computational efficiency of the different methods, and benchmark them against an exactly solvable free fermion model.

</details>


### [174] [Current fluctuations of diffusive systems with a battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18851)
*Thibaut Jonckheere,Bernard Derrida*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用宏观涨落理论（macroscopic fluctuation theory, MFT）研究环形扩散系统中置于原点的"电池"所驱动的电流大偏差函数，并与微观精确计算及数值模拟进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带电池的环形扩散系统中电流涨落的大偏差行为，并检验宏观涨落理论（MFT）在此类非平衡稳态下的适用性与精确性。

Method: 采用宏观涨落理论（MFT）推导环形系统电流的大偏差函数（large deviation function）；对无相互作用粒子情形与直接微观精确计算进行对比；对带电池的对称简单排他过程（Symmetric Simple Exclusion Process, SSEP）与长时间蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟结果进行对比。

Result: 无相互作用粒子情形下，MFT预言与直接微观精确计算结果一致；且带电池的无相互作用粒子大偏差函数与开边界条件下SSEP的形式相同。对环形带电池SSEP，MFT预言与长时间蒙特卡洛模拟结果似呈一致，但需更长时间模拟以更好验证。

Conclusion: MFT在无相互作用粒子情形下得到精确验证，并揭示其大偏差函数与开边界SSEP的普适形式相同；对SSEP情形，MFT与模拟的一致性尚需更长时间模拟进一步检验。

Abstract: For a diffusive system on a ring, with a battery at the origin, we use the macroscopic fluctuation theory (MFT) to obtain the large deviation function of the current. In the case of non-interacting particles, the predictions of the MFT agree with the result of a direct exact microscopic calculation. To our surprise, the large deviation function of non-interacting particles with a battery has the same form as for the Symmetric Simple Exclusion Process (SSEP) with open boundary conditions. For the SSEP on a ring with a battery, the predictions of the MFT seem to agree with the results of long Monte Carlo simulations, although much longer simulations would be needed to better test the validity of these predictions.

</details>


### [175] [Hierarchy of time scales in kinetically constrained models via stochastic-generator expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18882)
*Vanja Marić,Juan P. Garrahan,Lenart Zadnik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将原本为量子动力学约束模型（quantum kinetically constrained models）发展的展开方法迁移至经典随机KCM，通过对随机生成元按低温激发平衡浓度展开，揭示了嵌套式亚稳态层级及对应的时间尺度层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典动力学约束模型（kinetically constrained models, KCM）中弛豫时间尺度层级（hierarchy of relaxation time scales）已有描述，但缺乏系统性框架；作者希望检验近期为量子KCM开发的展开方法能否同样用于经典情形，以统一刻画缓慢弛豫。

Method: 在满足细致平衡（detailed balance）的经典随机KCM中，将随机生成元（stochastic generator）按激发的低温平衡浓度（low-temperature equilibrium concentration of excitations）作微扰展开；对展开逐级截断，考察各阶截断所对应的亚稳态构型。

Result: 将该方法应用于经典一维East模型与Fredrickson-Andersen模型的高温到低温淬火（high-to-low temperature quench），复现了已知的亚稳态平台层级及其微扰时间尺度；并发现这些层级时间尺度与最小畴长度（smallest domain lengths）相关。

Conclusion: 结果表明，为量子KCM发展的展开方法同样可为经典对应模型中的慢弛豫提供系统描述，说明该方法在经典与量子动力学约束系统中具有通用性。

Abstract: We revisit the hierarchy of relaxation time scales in stochastic kinetically constrained models (KCMs) obeying detailed balance, using an expansion method developed recently for their quantum counterparts. In the classical setting, we expand the stochastic generator in the low-temperature equilibrium concentration of excitations. As in quantum KCMs, successive truncations of the expansion reveal a nested hierarchy of metastable configurations that remain frozen on progressively longer time scales. Applying the method to the high-to-low temperature quench in the classical one-dimensional East and Fredrickson-Andersen models, we recover the known hierarchy of metastable plateaus and the associated perturbative time scales. We find that the hierarchical time scales are related to the smallest domain lengths. Our results show that the method developed for quantum kinetically constrained models can be used to provide a systematic description of slow relaxation in their classical counterparts as well.

</details>


### [176] [A microscopic heat engine with many hidden variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18941)
*Mesfin Asfaw Taye*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究分析了过阻尼布朗马达（overdamped Brownian motor）中一个可观测坐标与多个隐藏内部相位通过单一共享势耦合的情形，证明在机械失速（mechanical stall）时仍存在非零熵产生（entropy production）。由于耦合具有秩一（rank-one）互易结构，观测与隐藏概率流（probability current）之间存在精确的点对点恒等关系，从而给出失速时熵产生的精确表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的微观马达（microscopic motor）监测通常只依赖单一机械坐标，而维持运动的化学、构象或旋转循环仍未被解析。机械失速以平均观测速度为零来定义，但这一标量条件通常并不意味着热力学可逆性（thermodynamic reversibility），因此需要厘清失速状态下的真实能量耗散。

Method: 构建并分析一个过阻尼布朗马达模型，其中单一观测坐标通过一个共享的相互作用势与若干内部相位耦合。利用传递力与反作用力矩源自同一能量这一事实，推导出秩一互易结构所导致的观测与隐藏概率流之间的精确点对点恒等关系，并据此对任意周期性相互作用给出失速时熵产生的精确表达式。

Result: 失速时的熵产生由机械通道中的正电流平方耗散（current-square dissipation）与失速负载共同决定，前者基于完整定态并通过耦合坐标分辨的局部速度定义，可确定投影到耦合方向上的集体隐藏电流。定态的单一坐标边际电流（marginal current）在失速时恒为零，因此定态边际密度与平均位移电流无法确定该耗散。

Conclusion: 与耦合方向正交的隐藏电流在不影响观测运动的情况下产生耗散，并贡献一个不可约项，该项无法从秩一机械通道中推断。结果表明机械失速不等同于热力学平衡，单坐标观测在原理上不足以刻画马达的全部耗散行为。

Abstract: Microscopic motors are commonly monitored through a single mechanical coordinate, whereas the chemical, conformational, or rotational cycles that sustain their motion remain unresolved. Mechanical stall is defined by the vanishing of the mean observed velocity; this scalar condition, however, does not generally imply thermodynamic reversibility. We analyze an overdamped Brownian motor in which one observed coordinate is coupled to several internal phases through a single shared interaction potential. Because the transmitted force and the reaction torques derive from the same energy, the resulting rank-one reciprocal structure yields an exact pointwise identity between the observed and hidden probability currents. This identity provides an exact expression for the entropy production at stall for an arbitrary periodic interaction. In particular, a positive current-square dissipation in the mechanical channel, defined from the full stationary state through the coupling-coordinate-resolved local velocity, determines together with the stall load the collective hidden current projected onto the coupling direction. The stationary one-coordinate marginal current nevertheless vanishes identically at stall; consequently, the stationary marginal density and mean displacement current do not determine this dissipation. Hidden currents orthogonal to the coupling direction dissipate without affecting the observed motion and generate an irreducible contribution that cannot be inferred from the rank-one mechanical channel.

</details>


### [177] [Traveling Waves as Renormalization-Group Fixed Points without Universality Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18984)
*Ko Okumura*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将近期提出的非线性偏微分方程统一重整化群（renormalization-group, RG）框架推广至行波（traveling waves）解，以 Burgers 方程与 KdV 方程为范例。通过对数变换将行波映射为渐近自相似解，发现尺度不变性强制场的标度维数（scaling dimension）为零，导致 RG 变换仅重标度时空而保持场幅值不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行波是非线性物理系统的基本渐近结构，其与自相似性（self-similarity）的关联已被认识，但其重整化群地位仍不明确。作者旨在将既有统一 RG 框架扩展至行波解，以厘清其 RG 结构与普适类（universality class）形成机制。

Method: 以 Burgers 方程与 KdV 方程为典型例子，采用对数变换（logarithmic transformation）将行波解映射为渐近自相似解，从而进行系统的 RG 处理，并分析尺度不变性对场标度维数的约束。

Result: 分析显示行波情形显著偏离标准 RG 范式：尺度不变性唯一地迫使场的标度维数消失（Δ=0），RG 变换仅重标度空间与时间而场幅值不变。因此所有解析微扰均变为尺度不变，传统上关于相关（relevant）与无关（irrelevant）结构的分类随之消失。经典激波（shock-wave）与孤子（soliton）解作为静止 RG 不动点（fixed points）出现，但普适类形成的机制——无关结构的逐步消除——根本上缺失。

Conclusion: 行波代表一类独特的、不具有普适类的 RG 不动点，这一发现确立了 RG 不动点与普适类两个概念之间的关键区别，并为行波为何表现出对初始条件与系统参数的强记忆提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Traveling waves are fundamental asymptotic structures in nonlinear physical systems. While their connection to self-similarity is recognized, their renormalization-group (RG) status remains elusive. We extend a recently developed unified RG framework for nonlinear PDEs to traveling-wave solutions, using Burgers' and KdV equations as paradigmatic examples. By employing a logarithmic transformation, we map traveling waves onto asymptotically self-similar solutions, allowing for a systematic RG treatment. Our analysis reveals a striking departure from the standard RG paradigm: for traveling waves, scale invariance uniquely forces the field's scaling dimension to vanish (A=0). This vanishing dimension implies that the RG transformation rescales space and time while leaving the field magnitude unchanged. Consequently, all analytic perturbations become scale-invariant, eliminating the conventional classification into relevant and irrelevant structures. While classical shock-wave and soliton solutions emerge as stationary RG fixed points, the mechanism for universality class formation - the progressive elimination of irrelevant structures - is fundamentally absent. Traveling waves thus represent a unique class of RG fixed points without universality classes.
