This finding establishes a crucial distinction between these two concepts and provides a rigorous theoretical basis for why traveling waves exhibit strong memory of initial conditions and system parameters.

</details>


### [178] [Field-driven quantum phase transitions in a spin-1/2 Heisenberg antiferromagnet on an extended Lieb lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19051)
*David Sivy,Jozef Strecka*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）与量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）方法，研究扩展Lieb格子上自旋-1/2海森堡反铁磁体在外磁场下的磁行为，给出零温磁化曲线与基态相图，并揭示有限温度下的相变特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Lieb格子上的自旋-1/2海森堡反铁磁体在外磁场中的磁性尚需系统刻画，特别是相互作用比对磁场驱动量子相变、磁化平台与无能隙相的影响有待明确。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）计算零温磁化曲线，并在磁场-相互作用比平面构建基态相图；同时运用量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo, QMC）模拟考察有限温度行为。

Result: 零温磁化曲线随相互作用比呈现磁场驱动的量子相变：在饱和磁化的1/5与3/5处存在有能隙的中间平台相，以及磁化随外场连续变化的无能隙自旋倾斜（spin-canted）相。QMC模拟给出这些场诱导量子相变与中间磁化平台的有限温度特征。

Conclusion: QMC未发现连续或非连续热相变的证据，表明该体系中场诱导量子相变与磁化平台在有限温度下可被观测，但不存在热致相变。

Abstract: The magnetic behavior of the spin-1/2 Heisenberg antiferromagnet on the extended Lieb lattice is examined in the presence of an external magnetic field. Using density matrix renormalization group calculations, we determine the zero-temperature magnetization curves and construct the ground-state phase diagram in the magnetic field vs. the interaction ratio plane. Depending on the interaction ratio, the zero-temperature magnetization curves exhibit field-driven quantum phase transitions between the gapped phases manifested as the intermediate plateaus at $1/5$ and $3/5$ of the saturation magnetization and a gapless spin-canted phase, in which the magnetization varies continuously with the applied magnetic field. Quantum Monte Carlo simulations reveal clear finite-temperatures signatures of the field-induced quantum phase transitions and the intermediate magnetization plateaus, while they provide no evidence for either continuous or discontinuous thermal phase transitions.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Joint Continuity and Selberg--ODE Equivalence for the $\mathrm{Sine}_β$ Pair Correlation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17596)
*Weiyang Fang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文回应 Qu 与 Valkó 关于 $\mathrm{Sine}_\beta$ 过程对关联函数（pair correlation function）的两个问题：去掉了联合连续性（joint continuity）结果中 $\beta>2$ 的限制，并在 $\beta=2n$ 时直接证明 Qu–Valkó 矩阵递推幂级数与 Forrester 记录的 Selberg 积分表示一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对 Qu 与 Valkó 研究 $\mathrm{Sine}_\beta$ 过程对关联函数时提出的两个未决问题：其一，其联合连续性结论受 $\beta>2$ 限制；其二，Qu–Valkó 矩阵递推幂级数与 Forrester 记录的 Selberg 积分表示之间的关系尚缺直接证明。

Method: 第一问基于一般性观察：一族概率律（probability laws）的分别弱连续（separate weak continuity）加上关于一个参数的随机单调性（stochastic monotonicity）可推出联合弱连续，并将其应用于 Qu–Valkó 扩散的终值，配合其 Palm 密度公式（Palm-density formula），无需对傅里叶展开（Fourier expansion）求导。第二问对 $\beta=2n$ 使用 Krawtchouk 变换（Krawtchouk transform）将 Qu–Valkó 系统化为参数为 $-(n+1)$ 的 $(2n+1)$ 维微分系统，并与参数为 $n+1$ 的中心化 Aomoto–Selberg 迹系统（trace system）对比，构造显式三角微分交织子（triangular differential intertwiner）以反映参数映射 $\eta\mapsto-\eta$。

Result: 证明了对关联函数在 $(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}$ 上关于逆温度与空间参数存在联合连续版本，从而去掉了 $\beta>2$ 的限制；并在 $\beta=2n$ 时，通过匹配指数为 $2$ 的唯一 Frobenius 分支（Frobenius branch）得到恰为 Selberg 归一化（normalization），由此将 Qu–Valkó 递推的每个系数与对应 Jacobi–Selberg 迹统计量的偶数中心矩（even centered moment）相对应。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明 Qu–Valkó 的联合连续性结论可在更广的参数范围内成立，且其矩阵递推幂级数与 Selberg 积分表示在 $\beta=2n$ 情形下一致，并给出递推系数与 Jacobi–Selberg 迹统计量偶数中心矩的对应关系。

Abstract: We address two questions posed by Qu and Valkó in their study of the pair correlation function of the $\mathrm{Sine}_β$ process. First, we prove that the pair correlation function admits a jointly continuous version in the inverse-temperature and spatial parameters on $(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}$, removing the restriction $β>2$ in their joint-continuity result. The argument uses a general observation: separate weak continuity of a family of probability laws, together with stochastic monotonicity in one parameter, implies joint weak continuity. Applied to the terminal value of the Qu--Valkó diffusion, their Palm-density formula then gives the result without differentiating the Fourier expansion.
  Second, for $β=2n$ we give a direct proof that the Qu--Valkó matrix-recursion power series agrees with the Selberg-integral representation recorded by Forrester. A Krawtchouk transform converts the Qu--Valkó system into a $(2n+1)$-dimensional differential system with parameter $-(n+1)$, while a centered Aomoto--Selberg trace system has parameter $n+1$. An explicit triangular differential intertwiner reflects the parameter $η\mapsto-η$. Matching the unique Frobenius branch of exponent $2$ gives exactly the Selberg normalization. As a consequence, every coefficient of the Qu--Valkó recursion is identified with an even centered moment of the corresponding Jacobi--Selberg trace statistic.

</details>


### [180] [String equation for conformal loop ensemble on Liouville quantum gravity sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17826)
*Xin Sun,Baojun Wu,Shengjing Xu*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文在刘维尔量子引力（Liouville quantum gravity, LQG）中为矩阵模型（matrix models）的弦方程（string equation）结构提供概率实现：对 γ²∈(8/3,4)，由量子圆盘与独立共形环系综（conformal loop ensemble, CLE）构造量子 n 孔球面，其面积拉普拉斯变换由超几何弦方程确定，并在 γ↓0 时收敛到 Weil–Petersson 体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵模型（matrix models）通过弦方程（string equation）表达具指定边界长度的随机曲面配分函数，但这一代数结构缺乏概率层面的实现。文中旨在 LQG 框架下给出该结构的概率对应，并探究其与模空间体积的联系。

Method: 对 γ²∈(8/3,4)，以量子圆盘（quantum disk）装饰独立共形环系综（CLE）构造量子 n 孔球面，计算固定边界长度下的面积拉普拉斯变换（area Laplace transform）；借助 LQG/CLE 耦合，从稳定 Lévy 游弋（stable Lévy excursions）推导超几何弦方程，并由共形焊接方程（conformal welding equation）得到对称多项式。

Result: 面积拉普拉斯变换除一个简单前因子外仅依赖于总边界长度，并由一个超几何弦方程（hypergeometric string equation）确定；共形焊接方程给出的对称多项式表示积分化的 LQG/CLE 耦合可观测量。

Conclusion: 所得对称多项式在 γ↓0 时收敛到具 n 条测地边界的球面的 Weil–Petersson 体积，从而在 LQG 与模空间体积之间建立了联系。

Abstract: Matrix models express partition functions of random surfaces with prescribed boundary lengths through string equations. We give a probabilistic realization of this structure in Liouville quantum gravity (LQG). For $γ^2\in(8/3,4)$, we construct the quantum $n$-hole sphere from a quantum disk decorated by an independent conformal loop ensemble (CLE) and compute its area Laplace transform at fixed boundary lengths. The transform depends only on the total boundary length apart from a simple prefactor and is determined by a hypergeometric string equation. We derive this equation from stable Lévy excursions using LQG/CLE coupling. The conformal welding equation yields symmetric polynomials that represent integrated LQG/CLE coupling observables. These polynomials converge to Weil--Petersson volumes of spheres with $n$ geodesic boundaries as $γ\downarrow0$.

</details>


### [181] [Relaxation and Steady-State Entropy Production for Langevin SPDEs: A Dirichlet-Form Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18201)
*Shuyuan Fan,Yuanke Chen,Lifei Wang,Jinqiao Duan*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文为预条件 Langevin 随机偏微分方程（stochastic PDE）的弛豫与稳态熵产生建立了 Dirichlet 型（Dirichlet-form）框架，在无穷维设定下回避了对环境 Lebesgue 密度的依赖，给出精确的 de Bruijn 熵-耗散公式、相对熵衰减率，以及非细致平衡情形下熵产生率的路径空间刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无穷维中，基于环境 Lebesgue 密度与概率流密度的常规 Fokker–Planck 计算通常不可用，故需为预条件 Langevin SPDE 的弛豫与稳态熵产生寻找更适切的分析工具。

Method: 针对细致平衡类，在 Gibbs 态空间上构造准正则对称 Dirichlet 型，并结合自伴性、坐标鞅判据与形式-SPDE 对应；对一维 $Φ^4_1$ 与凸 Allen–Cahn 型 Gibbs 动力学，通过直接验证对数导数、形式封闭性与准正则性获得衰减界；对非细致平衡情形，考察有界斜自伴线性外力及柱可观测量上的反对称作用与 Cameron–Martin 流。

Result: 细致平衡类得到正则密度的精确 de Bruijn 熵-耗散公式及任意有限熵初值的积分不等式；$Φ^4_1$ 与凸 Allen–Cahn 型动力学的相对熵衰减指数分别为 $2$ 与 $2(1-λ/m)$（$m>λ$）。非细致平衡下，有界斜自伴线性外力在无需外力与协方差交换条件时仍保持高斯不变律；电流能量平方等于由前向-反向路径空间相对熵定义的稳态熵产生率，并等于 Galerkin 速率的单调极限；在交换条件下还给出质量淬火后的显式瞬态 Onsager 分解。排除过程与高斯转子提供有限态与高斯基准。

Conclusion: Dirichlet 型框架可在无穷维中统一处理预条件 Langevin SPDE 的细致平衡与非细致平衡熵产生问题，并将稳态熵产生率与路径空间相对熵、Galerkin 极限相衔接。

Abstract: We develop a Dirichlet-form framework for relaxation and steady-state entropy production in preconditioned Langevin stochastic partial differential equations. In infinite dimensions, the usual Fokker--Planck calculations based on ambient Lebesgue densities and the corresponding probability-current densities are generally unavailable. For the detailed-balance class, a quasi-regular symmetric Dirichlet form on the Gibbs state space yields an exact de Bruijn entropy-dissipation formula for regular densities and an integrated inequality for arbitrary finite-entropy initial laws. Self-adjointness gives detailed balance and stationary path reversal, while a coordinate-martingale criterion identifies the associated process with the prescribed SPDE. For the one-dimensional $Φ^4_1$ and convex Allen--Cahn-type Gibbs dynamics, we combine the established strong well-posedness theory with direct verification of the logarithmic derivatives, form closure, quasi-regularity and form--SPDE correspondence, and obtain relative-entropy decay bounds with exponents $2$ and $2(1-λ/m)$, respectively, with $m>λ$ in the latter case. Away from detailed balance, bounded skew-adjoint linear forcing preserves a Gaussian invariant law without requiring commutation between the forcing and covariance. We identify the antisymmetric action on cylinder observables and its Cameron--Martin current and prove that the squared current energy equals the steady-state entropy-production rate defined by forward--reverse path-space relative entropy per unit time, as well as the monotone limit of the Galerkin rates. Under commutation, we additionally obtain an explicit transient Onsager decomposition after a mass quench. Exclusion processes and Gaussian rotors provide finite-state and Gaussian benchmarks.

</details>


### [182] [A Stein Proof of the Burke Property for the Stationary O'Connell-Yor Polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18214)
*Ruixuan Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文利用 Stein 方法（Stein's method）直接证明平稳 O'Connell-Yor 聚合物（O'Connell-Yor polymer）的 Burke 性质（Burke property），核心工具为多层联合高斯-伽马 Stein 恒等式。零温极限下可得到平稳点到线布朗尼最后通过渗流（Brownian last-passage percolation）的对应结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burke 性质是平稳聚合物与渗流模型中的关键结构性质，已有证明通常依赖较间接的途径。本文旨在借助 Stein 方法提供一条直接证明路径，并推广相关自由能恒等式（free energy identity）。

Method: 方法建立在与 Firas Rassoul-Agha、Xiao Shen、Guangqu Zheng 合作工作中发展的自由能恒等式之上，将其推广为多层（multilevel）形式，从而得到联合高斯-伽马 Stein 恒等式（joint Gaussian-Gamma Stein identity）。证明依托该工作中建立的高斯分部积分框架（Gaussian integration-by-parts framework）。

Result: 由联合高斯-伽马 Stein 恒等式可推出完整的 Burke 性质。对该结果取零温极限（zero temperature），即得到平稳点到线布朗尼最后通过渗流的相应结论。

Conclusion: 该工作给出平稳 O'Connell-Yor 聚合物 Burke 性质的直接 Stein 方法证明，并通过零温极限将结论扩展至平稳点到线布朗尼最后通过渗流。

Abstract: We give a direct proof of the Burke property for the stationary O'Connell-Yor polymer using Stein's method. Building on a free energy identity developed in joint work with Firas Rassoul-Agha, Xiao Shen, and Guangqu Zheng, we establish a multilevel extension that yields a joint Gaussian-Gamma Stein identity, from which the full Burke property follows. The proof is based on a Gaussian integration-by-parts framework developed in that work. Passing to zero temperature gives the corresponding result for stationary point-to-line Brownian last-passage percolation.

</details>


### [183] [Factor of i.i.d. with polynomial tails for the long-range Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18758)
*Corentin Faipeur*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 证明了 Z^d 上极高温长程 Ising 模型（long-range Ising model）是独立同分布过程（i.i.d. process）的一个因子（factor），并给出编码半径（coding radius）的显式多项式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有构造虽已知该模型可表示为 i.i.d. 过程的因子，但缺乏对编码半径尾部行为的定量刻画；本文旨在回答 Chazottes、Gallo 与 Takahashi 近期预印本（arXiv:2602.14618）提出的问题。

Method: 在极高温条件下，不假设模型为铁磁性（ferromagnetic），仅要求相互作用衰减快于 1/n^{2d}；通过构造性方法获得因子映射（factor map）。

Result: 得到编码半径尾部的显式多项式上界，并由此构造出具有有限期望编码体积（finite expected coding volume）的因子映射。

Conclusion: 将原有因子性结果强化为带定量编码半径控制的版本，回答了近期预印本提出的问题。

Abstract: We prove that the very-high temperature long-range Ising model on $\mathbf{Z}^d$ is a factor of an i.i.d. process, and provide quantitative information on the coding radius. We do not assume that the model is ferromagnetic, but require the interactions to decay faster than $1/n^{2d}$. While previous constructions were known, the novelty of our approach is that we obtain an explicit polynomial upper bound on the tail of the coding radius. In particular, our construction yields a factor map with finite expected coding volume. This answers a question of a recent preprint by Chazottes, Gallo and Takahashi, see arXiv:2602.14618.

</details>


### [184] [Delocalisation and scaling limit for the disordered long-range Discrete Gaussian Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18786)
*Christopher Chalhoub,Paul Dario,Corentin Faipeur,Arnaud Le Ny*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究外部随机场对长程离散高斯链（Discrete Gaussian Chain, DGC）局域化/离域化的影响，证明当多项式衰减指数 α>3/2 时，模型在任意逆温度下均离域，并刻画了离域相的生长指数与原点高度的标度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 Garban（arXiv:2312.04536）与 Coquille 等（arXiv:2412.15782）研究了长程离散高斯链；本文旨在考察外加随机场（random field）如何改变其局域化/离域化行为，并与无无序情形的已知结果进行对比。

Method: 采用严格的数学证明方法，分析在多项式衰减 α>3/2 条件下受随机场扰动的长程离散高斯链的离域化性质，并识别离域相的生长指数（growth exponent）与原点处高度的标度极限（scaling limit）。

Result: 证明模型在 α>3/2 时对任意逆温度均离域；该行为与无无序长程 DGC 存在定性差异——后者仅在 α>2 时才在每个温度下离域，类似 Aizenman 与 Wehr 已证明的长程 Ising 模型情形。此外，给出 α>3/2 时离域相的详细描述。

Conclusion: 随机场的引入使长程 DGC 在更宽的衰减范围（α>3/2）内始终离域，从而定性改变其相图；并进一步刻画出该离域相的生长指数与原点高度标度极限。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the effect of an external random field on the localisation / delocalisation of the long-range Discrete Gaussian Chain (DGC), recently studied by Garban arXiv:2312.04536 and Coquille \emph{et al.} arXiv:2412.15782. We prove that the model is delocalised at every inverse temperature for any polynomial decay $α> 3/2$. This behaviour differs qualitatively from the one of the disorder-free long-range DGC, for which delocalisation at every temperature occurs only for $α> 2$, similarly to what happens for long-range Ising models as already proved by Aizenman and Wehr. Additionally, we provide a more detailed description of the delocalised phase by identifying the growth exponent and scaling limit of the height at the origin for any decay exponent $α> 3/2$.

</details>


### [185] [Facilitated Exclusion Process in Higher Dimensions: Recurrent Structure and Transient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18885)
*Seonwoo Kim,Sanha Lee,Insuk Seo*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究定义在 $d$ 维离散环面（discrete torus）上的受促排开过程（facilitated exclusion process, FEP），刻画其活性递归类（recurrent class）数目与吸收时间随密度 $ρ$ 的变化，给出双相变（double phase transition）的严格证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受促排开过程是吸收态相变（absorbing-state phase transition）的典型模型，与接触过程（contact process）、激活随机游走（activated random walks）和随机沙堆（stochastic sandpiles）存在类比；但二维及以上维度的吸收与暂态时间此前缺乏严格估计。

Method: 在边长 $N$、维度 $d\ge2$ 的离散环面上，对固定密度 $ρ\in(0,1)$ 的伯努利初值（Bernoulli initial data）进行分析，考察初值所选定粒子数扇区（particle-number sector）内递归类（recurrent class）的数目与到达递归集所需时间。

Result: 以高概率成立：当 $ρ>1-2^{-d}$ 时存在唯一活性递归类，当 $ρ<1-2^{-d}$ 时存在多个递归类。对足够小的 $ρ$，过程在 $N$ 的多对数时间（poly-logarithmic time）内以高概率到达吸收态（absorbing state，即单点递归类）；而对 $ρ\in(1/2,3/4)$ 且 $N$ 为偶数，到达递归集所需时间具有多项式下界（polynomial lower bound）。

Conclusion: 结果构成 FEP 在二维及以上维度上关于吸收时间与暂态时间的首批严格估计，并为与接触过程等模型类比的双相变提供了有力证据。

Abstract: In this article, we study the facilitated exclusion process (FEP) on the $d$-dimensional discrete torus of side length $N$, with $d\ge2$. For Bernoulli initial data with a fixed density $ρ\in(0,1)$, we first prove that, with high probability, the particle-number sector selected by the initial configuration has a unique active recurrent class if $ρ>1-2^{-d}$ and multiple recurrent classes if $ρ<1-2^{-d}$. We then show that, for sufficiently small $ρ$, the process reaches an absorbing state (a singleton recurrent class) within a poly-logarithmic time in $N$ with high probability. In contrast, for $ρ\in(1/2,3/4)$ and even $N$, we establish a polynomial lower bound on the time required to reach the recurrent set. These results provide strong evidence for a double phase transition, analogous to absorbing-state phase transitions in the contact process, activated random walks, and stochastic sandpiles. To our knowledge, these are the first rigorous estimates for absorption and transient times for the FEP in dimensions two and higher.

</details>


### [186] [RDT based upper bounds on the largest average submatrix values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18312)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文针对经典最大平均子矩阵问题（largest average submatrix problem）的统计变体，在子矩阵维度与原矩阵呈线性比例的“线性区域”内取得理论进展。作者提出通用随机对偶理论（Random Duality Theory, RDT）框架，得到闭式上界，并证明提升版 RDT 在线性区间内严格优于朴素版，且在小子矩阵极限下与已有复制（replica）与次线性结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小尺寸子矩阵情形已有较好理解，被认为存在统计-计算间隙（statistical-computational gap, SCG）；但线性区域的信息论与算法分析仍具挑战性，尚无严格结果能证明或否定该情形下 SCG 的存在。本文旨在推进线性区域的理论刻画。

Method: 构建通用的随机对偶理论（Random Duality Theory, RDT）框架以刻画最大平均子矩阵取值；采用朴素 RDT 变体推导上界，并引入提升版 RDT 变体进行改进分析。该方法给出以维度比例参数为自变量的闭式上界表达式。

Result: 朴素 RDT 变体给出以维度比例参数为显式函数的闭式上界；在一定的子矩阵维度线性范围内，提升版 RDT 严格优于朴素 RDT。当子矩阵维度的比例常数趋于零时，所得结果与文献[34]经由一步复制对称破缺（one-step replica symmetry breaking）得到的结果以及文献[18,45]的次线性（sublinear）结果相匹配。

Conclusion: 在线性区域内，RDT 框架为最大平均子矩阵问题提供了统一且具深度的分析工具：既给出闭式上界，又通过提升版本获得改进，并在小子矩阵极限下与既有复制与次线性结果自洽，为探讨该情形下统计-计算间隙（SCG）问题奠定基础。

Abstract: We study statistical variants of the classical largest average submatrix problem. For small submatrices (where the dimension is less than linearly proportional to the original matrix), the problem is typically well understood and believed to exhibit the statistical-computational gap (SCG). However, analytical treatment of both the information-theoretic and algorithmic aspects of the linear regime remains challenging, and no mathematically rigorous results have yet arrived anywhere close to proving or disproving SCG existence in this setting.
  Focusing on the linear regime, we make strong progress in several key directions: 1) We develop a generic Random Duality Theory (RDT) framework to characterize largest average submatrix values. 2) Using the plain RDT variant, we obtain closed-form upper bounds as explicit functions of dimensionality proportionality parameters. 3) We demonstrate that a lifted RDT variant strictly improves upon the plain RDT within a certain linear range of submatrix dimensions. 4) For small submatrices where the dimensional proportionality constants approach zero, we prove that our results match both the replica results from [34] (obtained via one-step replica symmetry breaking) and the sublinear results from [18,45].

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Dark Matter Inelastic Scattering with Nuclei for Direct Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16529)
*Shao-Feng Ge,Oleg Titov,Yakun Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究暗物质（dark matter）直接探测中 WIMP-原子核散射的核响应（nuclear response），重点关注 $^{129}$Xe 与 $^{131}$Xe 同位素的非弹性（inelastic）通道。作者采用相对论组态相互作用密度泛函理论（relativistic configuration-interaction density functional theory, RCI-DFT）计算核响应函数，发现非弹性贡献在某些算符下可与弹性相当、甚至高出三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质直接探测通常以弹性（elastic）通道为主进行分析，非弹性通道在重核中可能被忽视。氙（xenon）同位素是当前实验的重要靶核，需评估其非弹性散射对信号解释的影响。

Method: 将弹性与非弹性散射通道统一推广处理，对有效算符（effective operators）做多极展开（multipole expansion），以角动量、宇称与时间反演选择定则（selection rules）确定允许的跃迁。核响应函数采用相对论组态相互作用密度泛函理论计算，并与核壳模型（nuclear shell model）结果对比。

Result: 对某些相互作用算符，非弹性贡献可与弹性贡献相当，部分情形甚至主导，最高达三个数量级；更高的激发核态信号率（signal rate）可与第一激发态相当。与核壳模型计算结果存在差异。

Conclusion: 非弹性通道及高激发态在重核直接探测中不可忽略；不同核结构模型给出的差异会显著影响暗物质直接探测搜索的数据解释。

Abstract: We investigate the nuclear responses for the WIMP-nucleus scattering in the dark matter direct detection with particular emphasis on the inelastic channel for the $^{129}$Xe and $^{131}$Xe isotopes. Our generalization incorporates both the elastic and inelastic scattering channels. With multipole expansion of the effective operators, the angular momentum, parity and time-reversal selection rules can effectively determine the allowed transitions. Instead of the nuclear shell model, we use the state-of-the-art relativistic configuration-interaction density functional theory, which is more suitable for heavy nuclei such as xenon isotopes, to calculate the nuclear response functions. For certain interaction operators, the inelastic contribution can be comparable as its elastic counterpart and some can even dominate by up to three orders of magnitude. Additionally, the higher excited nuclear states can have comparable signal rate as the first excited states. We compare our results with the nuclear shell model calculation. The differences would have significant effects for interpreting the dark matter direct detection searches.

</details>


### [188] [Domain Adaptation against Background Sculpting in Anomaly Detection at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17659)
*Vincent Benne,Marie Hein,Michael Krämer,Humberto Reyes-Gonzalez,Philipp Soldin,Christopher Wiebusch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对弱监督（weakly supervised）异常检测在新物理共振搜索中的背景塑形（background sculpting）问题，提出基于域自适应（domain adaptation）的异常分数与共振质量解耦方法，并在 LHC Olympics 数据集上验证其可在保持检测性能的同时显著抑制背景塑形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督异常检测已成为模型无关（model-agnostic）新物理搜索的有效工具，尤其适用于共振搜索。但异常分数（anomaly score）与共振质量之间的相关性会在按异常分数筛选后扭曲背景分布，使基于边带（sidebands）的背景估计变得困难。

Method: 提出一种基于域自适应的解耦方法，用以削弱异常分数与共振质量之间的相关性。研究使用 LHC Olympics R&D 数据集，并结合多种弱监督异常检测方法对该方法进行评估。

Result: 域自适应能够显著减少背景塑形，同时基本保持原有的异常检测性能。当输入特征与共振质量之间的相关性损害原方法性能时，域自适应还可恢复其灵敏度（sensitivity）。

Conclusion: 域自适应解耦为弱监督异常检测中的背景估计提供了一种可行方案，可在缓解背景塑形与维持（乃至恢复）检测灵敏度之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Weakly supervised anomaly detection has been shown to be an effective tool for model-agnostic searches for new physics, especially in the context of resonance searches. However, correlations between the anomaly score and the resonant mass can distort the background distribution after selecting on the anomaly score, complicating background estimation from the sidebands. To mitigate this background sculpting, we propose a domain-adaptation-based decorrelation of the anomaly score from the resonant mass. We study this approach using the LHC Olympics R&D data set and several weakly supervised anomaly detection methods. We find that domain adaptation can substantially reduce background sculpting while largely preserving the anomaly detection performance. When correlations between the input features and the resonant mass degrade the original method's performance, domain adaptation can also recover sensitivity.

</details>


### [189] [WIMPs with Enhanced Annihilation from a Feebly Interacting Unstable Partner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17672)
*Chance Hoskinson,Pearl Sandick,Barmak Shams Es Haghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMPs）与一个仅与其耦合、并可衰变为 WIMPs 的较重伴随粒子所构成的扩展框架。伴随粒子的衰变时机会改变 WIMP 的热历史与遗迹丰度，从而要求更大的湮灭截面，并可通过间接探测实验加以检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WIMPs 是最有动力的暗物质（dark matter, DM）候选者之一，但标准 WIMP 范式面临诸多限制，因此需要探讨对 WIMP 范式的简单扩展。该扩展引人之处在于其可能改变暗物质丰度与晚期湮灭信号，从而影响间接探测的可检验性。

Method: 构建 WIMPs 与一个仅与其耦合、可衰变为 WIMPs 的较重粒子的扩展模型。在早期宇宙中，处于热平衡的 WIMPs 通过逆衰变（inverse decays）填充伴随粒子；随后通过半解析与数值方法求解玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equations），研究不同伴随粒子寿命下的热历史与冻结（freeze-out）行为。

Result: 若伴随粒子在 WIMP 冻结之前衰变，标准热历史不变；若衰变发生在冻结之后，则注入的非热 WIMP 群会导致暗物质过丰，因而需要相对标准情形更大的湮灭截面，增强可达三个数量级。冻结可能因再湮灭（re-annihilation）而延迟或随后进入冻结注入（freeze-in）阶段，并带来晚期宇宙中更强的湮灭信号。

Conclusion: 该增强的湮灭信号使当前与未来的间接探测实验（indirect detection experiments）能够探测伴随粒子的寿命。文中简要探讨了该情形与宇宙重子不对称（baryon asymmetry）的可能联系，并指出次最小超对称标准模型（NMSSM）可作为该情形的实现，或可容纳具有正确遗迹丰度的轻 higgsino 暗物质。

Abstract: Weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs) constitute one of the best-motivated dark matter (DM) candidates. In this study, we consider a simple extension of the WIMP paradigm in which WIMPs are coupled to a heavier particle that interacts only with them and can decay into WIMPs. In the early Universe, when WIMPs are in thermal equilibrium with the Standard Model (SM) bath, they serve as a portal to populate the heavy partner through inverse decays. Depending on the lifetime of the partner particle, its eventual decay into WIMPs can significantly alter the WIMP abundance. If the partner decays into WIMPs before their freeze-out from equilibrium, the standard thermal history remains unchanged. When the decay occurs after WIMP freeze-out, however, the injected non-thermal population of WIMPs can result in an overproduction of DM, therefore requiring a larger WIMP annihilation cross section relative to the standard scenario without the partner. For a given partner lifetime and WIMP annihilation cross section, freeze-out may be delayed due to re-annihilation or followed by a freeze-in phase. By solving the Boltzmann equations both semi-analytically and numerically, we explore these thermal histories and study the required enhancement in the WIMP annihilation cross section compared to the canonical value. This enhancement, which can reach up to three orders of magnitude, leads to stronger annihilation signals in the late Universe. As a result, current and upcoming indirect detection experiments are able to probe the lifetime of the partner particle. We briefly explore possible connections between our scenario and the baryon asymmetry of the Universe. As an example, we show that the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (NMSSM) can provide a realization of our scenario, with the possibility of accommodating light higgsino DM with the correct relic abundance.

</details>


### [190] [The production of bottom-flavoured jets at the LHC through bottom-quark pair production at NNLO+PS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17812)
*Rhorry Gauld,Marius Wiesemann,Giulia Zanderighi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了LHC上底夸克对产生导致的底味喷流（bottom-flavoured jets）的主产生机制，给出保留完整底夸克质量效应的次次领头阶（NNLO）QCD修正并与部分子簇射匹配（NNLO+PS），预测与ATLAS、CMS及LHCb数据总体一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 底味喷流是LHC重要末态，其精确描述需同时包含高阶QCD修正、底夸克质量效应与部分子簇射。不同喷流算法与底味定义之间存在数值差异，且此前对单/双底味喷流的理论与实验对比尚不充分，故需系统研究。

Method: 在底夸克对产生过程中计算NNLO QCD修正，保留完整底夸克质量效应，并与部分子簇射匹配（NNLO+PS）；比较不同喷流味（jet-flavour）定义，并采用实验底味标记方案与ATLAS、CMS、LHCb在7和13 TeV的数据对比。

Result: 预测在部分子与强子层次上描述了含一个及两个底味喷流的事件。不同喷流算法的主要数值差异源于含两个来自$g\to b\bar b$劈裂的底夸克的处理方式。与ATLAS、CMS分布吻合良好，并较好描述LHCb数据的形状。

Conclusion: 该NNLO+PS框架可真实描述单/双底味喷流事件，并可与实验底味标记方案直接对比。文中进一步讨论了底夸克质量幂修正（power corrections）、对称/非对称喷流选择截断以及多部分子相互作用（multi-parton interactions）等物理效应对分布的影响。

Abstract: We consider the dominant production mechanism of bottom-flavoured jets at the LHC, which proceeds through bottom-quark pair production. We compute next-to-next-to- leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections retaining full bottom-quark mass effects and match them with a parton shower (NNLO+PS). Our predictions provide a realistic description of events with one and two bottom-flavoured jets at both the partonic and hadronic level. We compare different jet-flavour definitions and confirm that the dominant numerical differences between the jet algorithms result from the treatment of jets that contain two bottom quarks originating from $g\to b\bar b$ splittings. We compare our predictions, using the experimental bottom-flavour tagging prescription, with measurements by ATLAS, CMS, and LHCb at 7 and 13 TeV, finding good agreement for the distributions presented by ATLAS and CMS and a good description of the shapes of the LHCb data. We also discuss the impact of various physics effects on the considered distributions: power corrections in the bottom-quark mass, the implications of symmetric/asymmetric jet-selection cuts, as well as contributions from multi-parton interactions.

</details>


### [191] [$D^{(*)}Δ$ interactions and the molecular interpretation of $Σ_c(3200)^0$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17952)
*Rui Chen,Yu-Yue Cui,Fu-Lai Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于LHCb在Λ_c^+π^-不变质量谱中观测到的Σ_c(3200)^0，本文在单玻色子交换框架下系统研究D^{(*)}Δ相互作用，提出Σ_c(3200)的分子态解释并预言多个伴态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb合作组近期在Λ_c^+π^-不变质量谱中观测到Σ_c(3200)^0重子，其内部结构（常规三夸克态还是分子态）尚不明确，需要一个统一的理论解释。

Method: 在单玻色子交换（one-boson-exchange）框架下，全面计算D^{(*)}Δ相互作用，并进一步引入DΔ与D^*Δ之间的耦合道效应（coupled channel effects）。

Result: I(J^P)=1(1/2^-)的D^*Δ分子态被视为Σ_c(3200)最自然的候选者；同时预言存在I(J^P)=1(3/2^-, 5/2^-)和2(1/2^{±}, 3/2^{±}, 5/2^-)的多个D^*Δ分子伴态。考虑耦合道效应后，还出现若干浅束缚、以DΔ为主的分子候选态，特别是I(J^P)=1,2(3/2^-)与2(1/2^+,3/2^+)系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为新观测到的Σ_c(3200)^0及未来奇异粲重子态的实验搜寻提供了统一的分子态解释与理论指引，并预期LHCb Upgrade与Belle II的高统计量数据可直接检验这些预言。

Abstract: Inspired by the recent observation of the $Σ_c(3200)^0$ baryon in the $Λ_c^+π^-$ invariant mass spectrum by the LHCb Collaboration, we perform a comprehensive investigation of the $D^{(*)}Δ$ interactions within the one-boson-exchange framework. Our results show that the $D^*Δ$ molecule with $I(J^P)=1(1/2^-)$ can be regarded as the most natural candidate for the $Σ_c(3200)$, and predict the existence of several $D^*Δ$ molecular partners with $I(J^P)=1(3/2^-, 5/2^-)$ and $2(1/2^{\pm}, 3/2^{\pm}, 5/2^-)$. After including the coupled channel effects between $DΔ$ and $D^*Δ$, several shallowly bound $DΔ$-dominated molecular candidates also emerge, especially for the $I(J^P)=1,2(3/2^-)$ and the $2(1/2^+,3/2^+)$ systems. These results provide a unified molecular interpretation of the newly observed $Σ_c(3200)^0$ and useful guidance for future experimental searches for exotic charmed-baryon states. We expect the high-statistics data from the LHCb Upgrade and Belle~II runs will directly test these predictions.

</details>


### [192] [Nuclear modifications on longitudinal-transverse structure-function ratio $R$ in the deuteron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17955)
*S. Kumano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本工作指出，轻子-原子核散射中纵向-横向结构函数比（longitudinal-transverse structure-function ratio）$R$ 并不像以往默认的那样不存在核修正（nuclear modification）；在卷积（convolution）描述下，核子的费米运动（Fermi motion）会导致纵、横结构函数混合，并产生 $R$ 的核效应，文中给出氘核的数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 $F_2$ 的核效应已被长期研究，但人们默认 $R$ 不存在核修正，并在此假设下分析轻子-原子核散射实验数据。作者认为该假设并不恰当，理论上 $R$ 中确实存在核修正，因此需要重新审视。

Method: 采用卷积描述（convolution description）处理原子核结构函数，重点分析氘核，用以解释核子费米运动（Fermi motion）的效应。纵向与横向结构函数以虚光子动量方向沿 $z$ 轴定义，而核内核子可在任意方向运动，造成纵、横结构函数在核介质中相互混合；混合效应量级约为 $\vec p_\perp^{\,2}/Q^2$，其中 $\vec p_\perp$ 为核子横向动量，$Q^2=-q^2$（$q$ 为虚光子动量），横向费米运动是混合的来源。

Result: 在卷积模型中，由于纵向与横向结构函数的 $x$ 依赖函数形式不同，核效应对二者并不相同，导致二者的卷积积分结果存在差异，从而在 $R$ 中产生核修正。文中给出了氘核中 $R$ 的核效应的数值结果。

Conclusion: 由于 Thomas Jefferson National Accelerator Facility 正在测量氘核的 $R$，作者期望该核效应能被未来实验（包括重核靶）所证实。

Abstract: In lepton scattering from nuclei, it has been taken for granted that nuclear modifications do not exist for the longitudinal-transverse structure-function ratio $R$, although nuclear effects in $F_2$ have been investigated for a long time. In fact, experimental data of lepton-nucleus scattering have been analyzed with this assumption. It is obviously not appropriate because nuclear modifications exist in the function $R$ theoretically as shown in this work. In this work, we explain nucleon's Fermi motion's effects by using a convolution description for nuclear structure functions, especially on the deuteron. The longitudinal and transverse structure functions are defined by taking the virtual photon momentum direction along the $z$ axis. However, nucleons in a nucleus could move in any direction, so that nucleon's longitudinal and transverse structure functions could mix with each other in the nuclear medium. Such mixing effects could be of the order of $\vec p_\perp^{\,2}/Q^2$, where $\vec p_\perp$ is the nucleon's transverse momentum and $Q^2$ is given by $Q^2=-q^2$ with the virtual-photon momentum $q$, because the transverse Fermi-motion is the source of such a mixture. In addition, nuclear effects are different between the longitudinal and transverse structure functions in the convolution model because their $x$-dependent functional forms are different. Convolution integrals have different results between the longitudinal and transverse structure functions for nuclei. Because the experiment is in progress at the Thomas Jefferson National Accelerator Facility to measure the function $R$ for the deuteron, the numerical results are shown on the nuclear effects of $R$ in the deuteron. Hopefully, such nuclear effects are found experimentally by future experiments including heavy nuclear targets.

</details>


### [193] [Theoretical and phenomenological aspects of the Electroweak physics WG studies for the 2026 ESPPU Physics Briefing Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18607)
*Jorge de Blas,Monica Dunford,Emanuele Bagnaschi,Ayres Freitas,Pier Paolo Giardino,Christian Grefe,Michele Selvaggi,Angela Taliercio,Falk Bartels*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文档是欧洲粒子物理战略（European Strategy for Particle Physics）2025-2026更新背景下，物理筹备组电弱工作组（PPG EW WG）工作的技术性补充说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025-2026版欧洲粒子物理战略强调对未来对撞机项目（collider projects）进行定量稳健的比较。为此，PPG EW WG对不同设施在希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）、电弱（electroweak）与顶夸克（top-quark）观测量上的预期灵敏度开展了综合评估，尤其关注不同理论不确定性（theoretical uncertainties）情景，其成果汇总于《Physics Briefing Book》。本文献旨在补充说明该评估中理论不确定性的处理方式，并提升结果的可复现性（reproducibility）与可扩展性（extensibility）。

Method: 文档提供两部分内容：(a) 对PPG EW WG内部所开展的理论不确定性评估给出更详细的说明；(b) 记录用于电弱与标准模型有效场论（Standard Model effective field theory, SMEFT）拟合的各对撞机专属输入（collider-specific inputs），并讨论为协调（harmonize）不同项目间假设所需的调整。

Result: 通过呈现详细的理论不确定性处理方式以及明确的对撞机输入与假设，本文献建立了与《Physics Briefing Book》所载结果的对应关系，并使其计算过程更为透明、可追溯。

Conclusion: 本文献并非提出新的物理结果，而是作为方法学与输入数据的说明性文档，旨在提升《Physics Briefing Book》中结果的复现性与未来可扩展性，服务于未来对撞机项目之间的定量比较。

Abstract: The 2025-2026 update of the European Strategy for Particle Physics placed renewed emphasis on quantitatively robust comparisons between future collider projects. Within this context, the Electroweak physics working group of the Physics Preparatory Group (PPG EW WG) carried out a comprehensive assessment of the projected sensitivities of different facilities to Higgs boson, electroweak and top-quark observables, with particular attention on different scenarios of theoretical uncertainties. These studies are summarized in the Physics Briefing Book.
  The aim of this document is to (a) provide a more detailed account of the evaluation of theoretical uncertainties, which was undertaken within the PPG EW WG, and (b) document the collider-specific inputs used in the electroweak and Standard Model effective field theory (SMEFT) fits and discuss any needed modifications in order to harmonize assumptions between projects. In presenting the detailed theoretical uncertainty treatment and the explicit collider inputs and assumptions, this work aims to improve the reproducibility and future extensibility of the results presented in the Physics Briefing Book.

</details>


### [194] [Transverse Tau Spin Correlations and the Weak Electric Dipole Moment at Future $Z$ Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18892)
*Xin-Yu Du,Zi-Yue Zou,Xiao-Gang He,Keping Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 $Z$ 极点利用 $\tau$ 自旋关联（spin correlations）探测 CP 破缺（CP violation），横向矩（transverse moments）可规避手性压低并显著增强灵敏度，FCC-ee/CEPC 预期精度较 ALEPH 提升约三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\tau$ 自旋关联对 CP 破缺的灵敏度强烈依赖自旋投影，纵向与三乘积（triple-product）观测量受手性压低（chirality suppression）限制，因此需寻找可规避该压低的观测量以提升固定 $Z$ 样本下的响应。

Method: 采用横向矩分析，其中两个横向矩选取轴矢 $\tau$ 荷（axial tau charge），并考察符号加权法向矩（sign-weighted normal moment）的响应；同时与单 $\pi$ 独立测量下的 Cramér-Rao 下界（Cramer-Rao bound）比较单参数不确定性，并进行五矩联合拟合（five-moment fit）配合 $5\%$ 离峰亮度（off-peak luminosity）。

Result: 横向矩响应增强因子为 $m_Z/2m_\tau=26$，轴矢荷选择带来 $|a_\tau/v_\tau|=13.5$，符号加权法向矩响应为三乘积的 $521$ 倍；两个横向矩的单参数不确定性不超过 Cramér-Rao 下界的 $14\%$。五矩拟合给出 $\operatorname{Re}\widetilde{d}_\tau^W$ 与 $\operatorname{Im}\widetilde{d}_\tau^W$ 的统计不确定性分别为 FCC-ee 的 $4.5\times10^{-7}$、$2.7\times10^{-6}$ 与 CEPC 的 $5.5\times10^{-7}$、$3.2\times10^{-6}$，较 ALEPH 改善三个数量级；电磁偶极（electromagnetic-dipole）不确定性为 $\operatorname{Re}\widetilde{d}_\tau^\gamma$ 的 $9.6\times10^{-6}$（FCC-ee）与 $12\times10^{-6}$（CEPC），较 Belle 提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: 横向矩方法在 $Z$ 极点可显著提高 $\tau$ CP 破缺观测量的灵敏度，使 FCC-ee 与 CEPC 对弱偶极与电磁偶极的精度大幅超越既有实验；上述预期基于理想角重建（ideal angular reconstruction）与扫描范围内形状因子（form factors）恒定的假设。

Abstract: Tau spin correlations at the $Z$ pole probe CP violation with a sensitivity that depends strongly on the spin projection. For a fixed $Z$ sample, transverse moments avoid the chirality suppression of longitudinal and triple-product observables, enhancing the response by a factor $m_Z/2m_τ=26$. Two transverse moments also select the axial tau charge, gaining $|a_τ/v_τ|=13.5$; the sign-weighted normal moment has a response $521$ times that of the triple product. These two transverse moments give single-parameter uncertainties no more than $14\%$ above our calculated Cramer-Rao bound for independent single-pion measurements, approaching the best precision within this analysis. A five-moment fit with $5\%$ off-peak luminosity yields statistical uncertainties of $4.5 (5.5)\times10^{-7}$ and $2.7 (3.2)\times10^{-6}$ on $\operatorname{Re}\widetilde{d}_τ^W$ and $\operatorname{Im}\widetilde{d}_τ^W$, respectively, at FCC-ee (CEPC), improving on ALEPH by three orders of magnitude. The electromagnetic-dipole uncertainty is $9.6 (12)\times10^{-6}$ on $\operatorname{Re}\widetilde{d}_τ^γ$, two orders beyond Belle. These projections assume ideal angular reconstruction and constant form factors across the scan.

</details>


### [195] [New Dynamics (?) of $J/ψJ/ψ$ and $ΥΥ$ families from QCD Laplace sum rules at NLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18906)
*S. Narison,Andry Rabemananjara,D. Rabetiarivony*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于 CMS 关于 \(J/\psi J/\psi\) 质量谱中 \(2^{++}\) 态族的新数据，首次采用两条最低的 QCD 拉普拉斯求和规则（Laplace Sum Rules, LSR）矩比来确定 \(2^{++}\) 的 \(J/\psi J/\psi\) 分子态与 \(cc\bar c\bar c\) 态质量，并进一步扩展至 \(0^{++}\) 态与 \(\Upsilon\Upsilon\) 族，对 X(6600)、X(6900)、X(7100) 等态给出解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS 近期在 \(J/\psi J/\psi\) 质量谱中观测到一族 \(2^{++}\) 态，促使作者重新审视这些态的本质（分子态或 \(cc\bar c\bar c\) 四夸克态）及其质量谱。研究动机在于借助 QCD 求和规则框架，为这些新数据提供理论质量与耦合的定量预言，并判断其是否暗示超出普通 \(\bar cc\) 态的新动力学。

Method: 使用两条最低的 QCD 拉普拉斯求和规则（Laplace Sum Rules, LSR）矩比，在标准优化准则下对 LSR 变量 \(\tau\) 与 QCD 连续谱阈值 \(t_c\) 进行优化，并施加 \(R_{P/C}\) 条件（即最低基态 Pole 贡献大于 QCD 连续谱贡献）。算符乘积展开（OPE）中计入因子化的 NLO 微扰项与 LO \(G^4\) 胶子凝聚。分析还扩展至 \(0^{++}\) 态与 \(\Upsilon\Upsilon\) 族，并采用双分量质量混合方案处理 X(6900) 与 X(7100)。

Result: 最低基态质量为 \(M^{2^{++}}_{J/\psi J/\psi}=6521(69)\) MeV 与 \(M^{2^{++}}_{cc\bar c\bar c}=6347(99)\) MeV，对应耦合 \(f^{2^{++}}_{J/\psi J/\psi}=96(14)\) keV 与 \(f^{2^{++}}_{cc\bar c\bar c}=151(29)\) keV（以 \(f_\pi=93\) MeV 归一）。第一径向激发态质量为 \(M^{2^{++}\mathrm{rad}}_{J/\psi J/\psi}=7987(251)\) MeV 与 \(M^{2^{++}\mathrm{rad}}_{cc\bar c\bar c}=7739(221)\) MeV，质量劈裂约为 \(2.5(M_{\psi(2S)}-M_{J/\psi})\)。径向激发态衰变常数为 \(f^{2^{++}\mathrm{rad}}_{J/\psi J/\psi}=248(70)\) keV 与 \(f^{2^{++}\mathrm{rad}}_{cc\bar c\bar c}=453(96)\) keV，意外地大于最低基态值。作者还批判性评述了其他 QCD 谱求和规则（QCD Spectral Sum Rules, QSSR）结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明这些态可能暗示与普通 \(\bar cc\) 态不同的新动力学。作者建议 X(6600) 可能为分子态基态；借助双分量质量混合方案，X(6900) 与 X(7100) 可被解释为 \(cc\bar c\bar c\) 最低基态与其第一径向激发态以角 \(\theta=5^\circ\) 混合的结果。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent CMS data on a family of $2^{++}$ states from $J/ψJ/ψ$ mass spectrum, we determine the masses of the $2^{++}$ $J/ψJ/ψ$ molecule and $cc\bar c\bar c$ states, by using (for the first time) the two lowest QCD Laplace Sum Rules (LSR) moment-ratios within the standard optimization criteria with respect to the LSR variable $τ$ and to the QCD continuum threshold $t_c$ implemented by the $R_{P/C}$ requirement that the lowest ground state (Pole) contribution to the LSR is larger than the QCD continuum one. Including factorized NLO perturbative and LO $G^4$ gluon condensates in the OPE, we obtain the the lowest ground state masses: $M^{2^{++}}_{J/ψJ/ψ}=6521(69)$ MeV and $M^{2^{++}}_{cc\bar c \bar c}=6347(99)$ MeV with the couplings\,: $f^{2^{++}}_{J/ψJ/ψ}=96(14)$ keV and $f^{2^{++}}_{cc\bar c \bar c}= 151(29)$ keV normalized as $f_π=93$ MeV. The masses of the first radial excitations are :$M^{2^{++}{rad}}_{J/ψJ/ψ}=7987(251 )$ MeV and $M^{2^{++}{rad}}_{cc\bar c \bar c}=7739(221)$ MeV indicating a mass-splitting about $2.5 (M_{ψ(2S)}-M_{J/ψ})$. The decay constants are $f^{2^{++}{rad}}_{J/ψJ/ψ}=248(70)$ keV and: $f^{2^{++}{\rm rad}}_{cc\bar c\bar c}=453(96) $ keV which are (unexpectedly) larger than the lowest ground state ones. These features may indicate some new dynamics compared to ordinary $\bar cc$ states. We extend the analysis to the $0^{++}$ states and to the $ΥΥ$ family where the results are compiled in Tables 2 and 3. We critically review some other QCD spectral sum rules (QSSR) results. We suggest that the X(6600) might be a molecule ground state. Using a two-component mass mixing scheme, the X(6900) and X(7100) might be interpreted as a mixture of the $cc\bar c\bar c$ lowest ground state with its 1st radial excitation via an angle $θ=5^0$.

</details>


### [196] [Comprehensive reconstruction of collider events with hypergraph representation learning and graph-conditioned diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18928)
*Lining Mao,Yvonne Peters,Ethan Simpson,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出 VyPER，一个将粒子对撞事件表示为超图（hypergraph）的几何学习框架，统一处理粒子归属与中微子运动学预测两项事件重建（event reconstruction）任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子对撞实验中，事件重建需从探测器记录的稳定末态推断硬散射（hard scatter）中短寿命粒子的运动学信息；该任务传统上被分割处理，缺乏统一框架。

Method: Vyper 将事件重建分解为两个子任务：将测得的喷注（jets）与带电轻子（charged leptons）归属到母粒子，以及预测未测量的中微子（neutrino）运动学。框架将事件表示为具有物理启发式拓扑的超图，结合超边（hyperedge）的监督分类与扩散模型（diffusion model）预测中微子运动学，并通过联合损失函数（joint loss function）在统一框架内优化两项任务。

Result: 在多种质子-质子（proton-proton）碰撞过程中，Vyper 的表现与现有解析方法及基于机器学习（machine learning）的重建技术进行了比较。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在多种标准模型（Standard Model）物理过程中均可实现准确的事件重建，为希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）、电弱（electroweak）及顶夸克（top-quark）领域的精确测量开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In particle collider experiments, event reconstruction is the task of inferring the kinematics of short-lived particles produced in the hard scatter from the stable final states recorded by detectors. We decompose event reconstruction into two primary tasks: assigning measured jets and charged leptons to parent particles, and predicting unmeasured neutrino kinematics. We present VyPER, a novel geometric learning framework that represents collider events as hypergraphs with a physics-inspired topology. VyPER combines the supervised classification of hyperedges for particle assignment with a diffusion model for predicting neutrino kinematics, leveraging a joint loss function to optimize both reconstruction tasks within a unified framework. We showcase VyPER across several proton-proton collision processes, comparing its performance to existing analytical and machine-learning-based reconstruction techniques. In doing so, we demonstrate that accurate event reconstruction is achievable across a diverse range of Standard Model physics processes, opening new avenues for precision measurements in the Higgs boson, electroweak, and top-quark sectors.

</details>


### [197] [Isospin and flavor SU(3) symmetry breaking in doubly heavy baryons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19037)
*Ghil-Seok Yang,June-Young Kim,Hyun-Chul Kim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在π介子平均场方法（pion mean-field approach）框架下研究双粲重子，将三体基态质量劈裂作为预言给出，结果与LHCb观测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb合作组对Ξ_cc^+与Ω_cc^+的观测补齐了双粲重子基态三重态，使其内部质量模式可用于检验轻夸克味（flavor）对称性破缺。

Method: 将双重重子视为N_c-2个价轻夸克在紧致重双夸克（heavy diquark）存在下产生π介子平均场的束缚态；味SU(3)与同位旋（isospin）对称性破缺的动力学参数取自轻重子与单重重子部门，超精细耦合（hyperfine coupling）由单重重子谱确定，重夸克质量取PDG值，仅用已测量的Ξ_cc^++质量锁定轻子系统的集体转动能。

Result: 预言M_Ξ_cc^+=(3620.17±13.08) MeV、M_Ω_cc^+=(3746.62±13.22) MeV；同位旋劈裂M_Ξ_cc^++−M_Ξ_cc^+=(1.43±0.38) MeV，与LHCb观测一致。另给出自旋3/2双粲态与双底（doubly bottom）谱的质量。

Conclusion: 模型参数均由其他部门固定，三重态内质量劈裂为无输入预言，且同位旋劈裂结果支持该框架对双重重子的描述有效性。

Abstract: The recent observations of the $Ξ_{cc}^{+}$ and the $Ω_{cc}^{+}$ by the LHCb Collaboration complete the ground-state triplet of doubly charmed baryons, so that its internal mass pattern can be used to test symmetry breaking in the light-quark sector. We study the doubly heavy baryons within the pion mean-field approach, regarding them as bound states of the $N_c-2$ valence light quarks that generate the pion mean field in the presence of a compact heavy diquark. All dynamical parameters for flavor SU(3) and isospin symmetry breaking were determined in the light and singly heavy baryon sectors, and the hyperfine coupling is fixed by the singly heavy spectrum. With the heavy-quark masses taken from the PDG values, the measured $Ξ_{cc}^{++}$ mass is used only to determine the collective rotational energy of the light subsystem, so that the mass splittings within the triplet are predictions. We obtain $M_{Ξ_{cc}^{+}}=(3620.17\pm13.08)$ MeV and $M_{Ω_{cc}^{+}}=(3746.62\pm13.22)$ MeV. The predicted isospin splitting, $M_{Ξ_{cc}^{++}}-M_{Ξ_{cc}^{+}}=(1.43\pm0.38)$ MeV, is consistent with the LHCb observation of the $Ξ_{cc}^{+}$. We also present the masses of the spin-3/2 doubly charmed states and the doubly bottom spectrum.

</details>


### [198] [Astrophysical Signatures of Fermionic Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17589)
*Vinit D Tyagi,Suhas S S,Arun Kenath*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究由费米子暗物质（fermionic dark matter）通过简并压（degeneracy pressure）支撑形成的致密引力束缚天体，利用微引力透镜（microlensing）巡天限制其质量，并分析其吸积与辐射特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子暗物质粒子是宇宙暗物质的主要候选者之一，但直接探测实验（direct detection experiments）迄今未获阳性结果，因而需要探索其在引力束缚致密天体形式下的可能性。

Method: 系统回顾微引力透镜巡天数据以给出此类致密天体的质量约束，并分析重子物质（baryonic matter）在其上的吸积过程，评估其热学与辐射性质。

Result: 估算得到的爆发辐射（burst emission）能量远低于银河系中心GeV过量（Galactic Center GeV excess）所对应的能量，表明此类致密天体不太可能是该观测信号的来源。

Conclusion: 混合的暗物质—重子物质天体可能解释部分目前未被观测到的重子物质，从而为宇宙中部分缺失重子（missing baryons）提供一种可能解释。

Abstract: Fermionic dark matter particles remain one of the most compelling candidates for the dark matter content of the Universe, yet no positive results have been obtained from direct detection experiments. In this work, we investigate the possibility that such particles form compact gravitationally bound objects supported by degeneracy pressure. Through an extensive review of microlensing surveys, we derive constraints on the masses of these compact objects. We further analyze the accretion of baryonic matter onto these objects and evaluate their thermal and radiative properties. The estimated burst emission is found to be well below the energies associated with the Galactic Center GeV excess, suggesting that these compact objects are unlikely to be the source of the observed signal. Our analysis suggests that admixed dark matter and baryonic matter objects could potentially account for a fraction of the presently unobserved baryonic matter, thereby providing a possible explanation for a fraction of the missing baryons in the Universe.

</details>


### [199] [Collider Detector Observables from Lattice Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17664)
*João Barata,Ying-Ying Li,Bo Wang,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以量子模拟平台（quantum simulation platforms）研究探测器可观测量（detector observables），在量子自旋系统中构造格点探测器算符（lattice detector operators），并以1+1维量子伊辛模型（quantum Ising model）验证其与共形场论（conformal field theory）预期的定量一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测器可观测量具有内禀实时性（real-time）与渐近性（asymptotic），对非微扰格点方法（nonperturbative lattice methods）构成根本性挑战。作者因此寻求量子模拟平台作为研究此类可观测量的理想环境。

Method: 以量子自旋系统（quantum spin systems）作为演示对象，构造格点探测器算符，并建立其与连续统能量流可观测量（continuum energy-flow observables）的联系；随后在1+1维量子伊辛模型中进行有限格点计算。

Result: 在临界点（criticality）处的有限格点计算与共形场论预期呈定量一致（quantitative agreement）。

Conclusion: 结果支持将格点流算符（lattice flow operators）解释为连续统探测器在标度极限与渐近极限（scaling and asymptotic limits）下的正规化对应物，为在桌面量子平台（table-top quantum platforms）上实现高能对撞机物理（high-energy collider physics）提供了具体路径。

Abstract: Detector observables pose a fundamental challenge for nonperturbative lattice methods due to their intrinsically real-time and asymptotic nature. We propose quantum simulation platforms as an ideal setting for studying these observables. Using quantum spin systems as a demonstration, we construct lattice detector operators and establish their connection to continuum energy-flow observables. We demonstrate this framework in the $1+1$-dimensional quantum Ising model, where finite lattice calculations show quantitative agreement with conformal field theory expectations at criticality. These results support the interpretation of the lattice flow operators as regulated counterparts of continuum detectors in the scaling and asymptotic limits, providing a concrete route toward realizing high-energy collider physics on table-top quantum platforms.

</details>


### [200] [Proper Time Shifts in Pulsar Timing Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17668)
*Vincent S. H. Lee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个规范不变的脉冲星计时残差（timing residual）框架，将可观测量定义为脉冲连续到达地球的固有时（proper time）间隔，并在线性化广义相对论（linearized general relativity）下给出其多普勒（Doppler）、夏皮罗（Shapiro）与爱因斯坦（Einstein）延迟分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays）是探测低频引力现象与标准模型之外物理（physics beyond the Standard Model）的有力工具，但需要一个规范不变且可推广的统一描述框架。

Method: 将可观测量识别为连续脉冲到达地球的固有时间隔，在线性化广义相对论中推导该固有时偏移的一般表达式，并将其分解为多普勒、夏皮罗与爱因斯坦三类延迟贡献（其和为规范不变量）；同时给出针对任意给定扰动计算计时残差的方法。

Result: 该分解的总和具有规范不变性，形式类似于激光干涉仪（laser interferometers）与原子干涉仪（atom interferometers）等其他引力波探测器已建立的框架；并以若干具有物理动机的源为例演示了该方法，在文献已有结果之处复现了已知结论。

Conclusion: 所提方法可直接应用于现有数值软件，从真实脉冲星计时数据中搜寻新物理（new physics）。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays are a powerful tool for probing low-frequency gravitational phenomena and physics beyond the Standard Model. We establish a general, gauge-invariant framework for the timing residual by identifying the measurable quantity as the proper time interval between consecutive pulse arrivals at Earth. In linearized general relativity, we derive a general expression for this proper time shift and show that it decomposes into Doppler, Shapiro, and Einstein delay contributions whose sum is gauge invariant, similar to frameworks that have been developed for other gravitational-wave detectors such as laser and atom interferometers. We also provide a recipe for computing the resulting timing residual for any specified perturbation, and we illustrate the method with several physically well-motivated sources, recovering known results where they exist in the literature. This recipe can be directly applied to search for new physics from real pulsar timing data using existing numerical software.

</details>


### [201] [Probing Lorentz-violating scalar forces and dark matter with precision matter and antimatter experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18263)
*Yevgeny V. Stadnik*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨一类洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz-violating）标量场模型，其中标量场对反物质（antimatter）的效应相比对物质可被显著增强。摘要指出，依据标量场在原子尺度上变化缓慢或迅速，光谱学测量的现象学会出现本质差异，并强调实验室精密检验（含暗物质搜寻、光谱学与干涉测量）及特定反物质实验的机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索标量场对反物质与物质效应不对称（反物质侧显著增强）的可能性，从而为洛伦兹破缺相关效应提供更易观测的检验窗口。同时对暗物质搜寻与反物质精密测量的兴趣，推动寻找实验室可及的检验途径。

Method: 采用理论建模的方式，构建一类洛伦兹破缺标量场模型（Lorentz-violating scalar-field models），并分析标量场在原子尺度上随时间变化缓慢或迅速两种情形下的现象学差异。具体分析聚焦于其对光谱学测量（spectroscopy measurements）的影响。

Result: 结果显示，标量场变化快慢的不同会导致现象学上出现非平凡的差异（non-trivial differences），这些差异体现在光谱学测量的表现中。摘要同时指出，此类效应可为实验室精密检验提供机会，包括暗物质搜寻（dark matter searches）以及光谱学和干涉测量（interferometry）。

Conclusion: 该模型的潜在价值在于利用反物质侧增强的标量场效应，拓展洛伦兹破缺的精密检验途径，尤其适用于暗物质搜寻及特定类型的反物质实验。结论强调实验室精密测量（光谱学、干涉测量、反物质实验）是该类模型的可行检验方向。

Abstract: We explore a class of Lorentz-violating scalar-field models where the effects of the scalar field on antimatter can be strongly enhanced compared to matter. Non-trivial differences in phenomenology arise for spectroscopy measurements depending on whether the scalar field changes slowly or rapidly on the atomic scale. We highlight opportunities for precision tests in the laboratory including dark matter searches, in particular with spectroscopy and interferometry measurements, as well as certain types of antimatter experiments.

</details>

### [202] [Self-Supervised Learning for Robust Resonance Mass Regression in Cascade Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17726)
*Ho Fung Tsoi,Alex Yang,Luis Felipe Gutierrez Zagazeta,Shion Chen,Dylan Rankin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该工作采用基础模型（foundation model）范式，以 VICReg 自监督预训练 transformer 编码器，再微调用于重共振质量回归，在 2.5–6.5 TeV、十一体末态的 SUSY 类级联衰变场景中，预训练模型相较同架构从头监督模型可获得更尖锐的共振峰并更稳定地抵抗多种现实扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机新物理搜索中，从含缺失能量的衰变产物重建重共振质量直接决定探测灵敏度。监督学习方法在系统不确定性（systematic uncertainties）与分布偏移（distribution shifts）下泛化能力不足，而穷举标注数据的各种变体计算代价高昂；泛化失败还会破坏峰搜寻（peak-hunting）分析中关键的共振宽度重建。

Method: 遵循基础模型范式，先以自监督方式结合 VICReg 预训练一个 transformer 编码器，学习对多种扰动不变的嵌入（embedding）；随后将其微调用于重共振质量回归。任务设定为质量范围 2.5–6.5 TeV、类 SUSY 级联衰变至十一体末态。对照为使用相同增强数据从零训练的相同架构监督模型。

Result: 与相同架构、相同增强数据从头训练的监督模型相比，预训练模型重建出更尖锐的共振峰，并在多种现实扰动下表现出更稳定的性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，自监督预训练（self-supervised pre-training）结合微调的策略可提升含缺失能量重共振质量重建的泛化性与稳定性，从而有利于峰搜寻分析中的共振宽度重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing the mass of a heavy resonance from its decay products with missing energy is one of the central tasks that directly determine the sensitivity in new physics searches at collider experiments. Supervised learning approaches to this problem often struggle to generalize well due to the presence of various systematic uncertainties and distribution shifts. Exhausting all possible variations in the labeled data can be very compute-intensive, while a failure of the model to generalize can corrupt the reconstructed resonance widths that are critical in peak-hunting analyses. In this work, following the foundation model paradigm, we use a self-supervised approach to pre-train a transformer encoder with VICReg to learn an embedding invariant to various corruptions, then fine-tune it for mass regression on a heavy resonance with masses ranging from 2.5 to 6.5 TeV and a SUSY-like cascade decay into an eleven-body final state. We show that the pre-trained model reconstructs sharper resonance peaks and has a more stable performance under various realistic corruptions, compared to a supervised model of the same architecture trained on the same augmented data from scratch.

</details>


### [203] [Similarity Pairing with Energy Mover's Distance for Self-Supervised Pre-Training at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17738)
*Ho Fung Tsoi,Dylan Rankin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本工作提出一种基于能量搬运距离（energy mover's distance, EMD）的数据驱动事件配对方法，用相似事件的匹配替代手工数据增强（data augmentation），在保持物理内容不变的前提下为自监督学习提供视图，并在 QCD 喷注（jet）预训练中取得与增强式基线相当或更优的下游判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）基础模型（foundation models）的自监督训练中，增强集合的选择自由度大且直接决定下游性能；同时数据增强实现方式存在两难——修改事件可能破坏事件保真度（event fidelity），而模拟更多事件变体则计算代价高昂。因此需要一种无需手工增强、且不损失物理内容的替代方案。

Method: 以能量搬运距离（energy mover's distance, EMD）度量事件之间的相似性，即把一个事件变换为另一个事件所需“功”的多少；据此采样并配对不同事件，用其相似性构成学习不变性（invariance）的视图（views）。该方法无需任何手工设计的失真，从而保持每个事件的物理内容完整，实现免增强（augmentation-free）的事件配对。

Result: 通过自蒸馏（self-distillation）在 QCD 喷注（QCD jets）上进行预训练，验证了该免增强配对方法的可行性；所得语义喷注嵌入（semantic jet embeddings）在下游判别任务上的表现与基于增强的基线（baseline）相当或更优。

Conclusion: 基于 EMD 的事件相似性配对可作为数据增强的替代策略，用于 LHC 基础模型的自监督预训练，在不引入手工失真、保持事件物理内容的同时，获得具有下游判别力的语义表示。

Abstract: Many self-supervised methods for training foundation models at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) rely on data augmentations to encourage the model to embed events into a representation space invariant to certain physical or detector symmetries. A common challenge arises from the large freedom in choosing a proper set of augmentations on which downstream performance depends. The implementation of augmentations involves either modifying existing events, potentially breaking the event fidelity, or simulating more event variants, which is computationally intensive. In this work, we present a data-driven method of pairing events by their similarity via the energy mover's distance (EMD), which measures how similar two events are in terms of the work required to transform one into the other. With this approach, distinct events are sampled and matched by their similarity to serve as views for learning invariance, keeping the physics content of each event intact without handcrafted distortions. We demonstrate this augmentation-free pairing method by pre-training on QCD jets via self-distillation and show that it can yield semantic jet embeddings with downstream discrimination power comparable to or better than an augmentation-based baseline.

</details>


### [204] [Search for Visible Dark Photon Decays at the Future SHINE Facility with the DarkSHINE Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18534)
*Zejia Lu,Huayang Wang,Xiang Chen,Jiahui Wu,Yulei Zhang,Liang Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于8 GeV电子束轰击钨靶的DarkSHINE实验，通过Geant4模拟与图神经网络（graph neural network）算法TrackNet，对位移暗光子（displaced dark photon）衰变A′→e⁺e⁻开展可见衰变搜寻，预期三年数据下背景约0.3事例，可在约30–70 MeV质量区间给出90%置信水平的排除界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估DarkSHINE实验对位移暗光子衰变A′→e⁺e⁻的探测灵敏度（sensitivity），以补充此前主要针对不可见暗光子衰变（invisible dark photon decays）的搜寻。关注质量区间为2m_e<m_{A′}<2m_μ。

Method: 信号产生在树图（tree level）层次用CalcHEP计算，生成事例经详细的Geant4探测器模拟传播。采用专用近径迹探测器（close tracker）、基于图神经网络（graph neural network）的TrackNet算法以及位移顶点（displaced vertex）选择，抑制来自对产生（pair production）与光子转换（photon conversion）的瞬发背景。

Result: 对残余背景顶点分布进行外推，得到三年运行、对应9×10¹⁴个靶上电子（electrons on target）的预期背景为0.3个事例。在该曝光下，90%置信水平的中位预期排除区域覆盖暗光子质量约30–70 MeV、动能混合参数（kinetic mixing parameter）约5×10⁻⁵–2×10⁻⁴，且耦合边界依赖于质量。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了DarkSHINE实验的可见衰变搜寻（visible decay search）策略，与不可见暗光子衰变搜寻形成互补。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the proposed DarkSHINE experiment to displaced dark photon decays, $A'\to e^+e^-$, using an 8 GeV electron beam incident on a tungsten target. The study focuses on masses $2m_e<m_{A'}<2m_μ$ and assumes a unit branching fraction to electron and positron pairs. Signal production is calculated at tree level with CalcHEP, and the generated events are propagated through a detailed Geant4 detector simulation. A dedicated close tracker, the TrackNet algorithm based on a graph neural network, and displaced vertex selections suppress prompt backgrounds from pair production and photon conversion. Extrapolation of the residual background vertex distribution yields an expected background of 0.3 events for three years of operation, corresponding to $9\times10^{14}$ electrons on target. For this exposure, the median expected exclusion region at 90% confidence level spans dark photon masses of approximately 30--70 MeV and kinetic mixing parameters of approximately $5\times10^{-5}$--$2\times10^{-4}$, with mass-dependent coupling boundaries. This study establishes a visible decay search strategy at DarkSHINE that complements searches for invisible dark photon decays.

</details>


### [205] [After 60 years, here comes the Sun](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18799)
*Anatael Cabrera,Mark Chen,Stefano Dusini,Jeff Hartnell,Jorge Martín Camalich,Diana Navas Nicolás,Hiroshi Nunokawa,Aldo Serenelli,Javier Trujillo Bueno,Frédéric Yermia*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文指出太阳中微子研究正从"被中微子物理利用"转向"主动探测太阳"，反应堆反中微子已基本消除振荡参数不确定性这一第一重深渊，但探测精度与标准太阳模型（Standard Solar Model）预测之间仍存在第二重深渊，需要新一轮探测技术革命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六十年来太阳主要作为中微子振荡（neutrino oscillation）研究的工具，而非被探测的对象；随着振荡参数精度提升，太阳中微子有望真正用于研究太阳内部，但探测精度不足构成关键障碍。

Method: 通过反应堆反中微子（reactor antineutrinos）实验精确测定控制太阳中微子味转换（flavour conversion）的振荡参数，并借助太阳地震学（helioseismology）对太阳力学结构的独立测量，结合对核源项（nuclear source terms）的精密中微子测量。

Result: 与传播相关的不确定性已趋于可忽略，第一重深渊基本闭合；但当前太阳中微子探测精度比标准太阳模型对主导 pp 通量（占比 >90%）的预测低约一个数量级，且本征的 ¹⁴C 本底（background）阻隔了 pp 区域，任何反应堆实验都无法弥补这一差距。

Conclusion: 弥合第二重深渊需要革命性而非渐进式的探测技术突破；若成功，结合太阳地震学可实现对太阳内部的多信使太阳层析成像（multi-messenger heliotomography），前提是该领域必须建成与之匹配的探测仪器。

Abstract: For sixty years, the Sun served neutrino physics more than the reverse: its neutrinos established oscillations rather than probing the star itself. That is now changing. Reactor antineutrinos have pinned down the oscillation parameters governing solar-neutrino flavour conversion so precisely that the propagation-related uncertainty is becoming negligible -- closing the first of two abysses. Solar neutrinos can, at last, be turned on the Sun. Yet a second abyss remains: today's solar-neutrino detection falls an order of magnitude short of the precision the Standard Solar Model already predicts for the dominant pp flux ($>\,$90$\%$) -- a gap no reactor experiment can close. The intrinsic $^{14}\mathrm{C}$ background walls off the pp region; only new detection techniques can breach it. Bridging this second abyss demands a revolution, not an increment. Combined with helioseismology's complementary reading of the Sun's mechanical structure, precision neutrino measurements of the nuclear source terms would enable a multi-messenger heliotomography of the solar interior -- reading our star from both sides at once. A new age of solar discoveries may follow, on one condition: that the field builds the instruments equal to the task.

</details>
